nityādisaṃgraha:

।। śrībrahmovāca ।।

suptotthitaiśca bhuktaiśca kiṃ kāryamiha dīkṣitaiḥ ।
sthātavyaṃ kutra vāmaktairidaṃ brūhi maheśvara ।।

।। parameśvara uvāca।।

śivādhyayanaśaktistu kartavyādhyayanārthibhiḥ ।
{śrotavyo mantratattvārthastathā ca śravaṇārthibhiḥ} ।।

gacchaṃstiṣṭhansvapañjāgrandhyāye tattvaṃ paraṃ śivam ।
vāgīśvarīṃ tu nigṛhṇanna śūnyaḥ syāt kathaṃcana ।।

na tu sādhakacaryaiṣā caryāsamayi putrayoḥ ।
vidhau mantrī tu satatamāsakto japa eva hi ।।

ācāryaḥ śivavalloke svatantraḥ samudāhṛtaḥ ।
bhukto vāpyathavābhuktassa tiṣṭhati yathecchayā ।।

gṛhasthāstu svadharmasthāḥ svatantre rakṣaṇodyatāḥ ।
sāmānyaśāstrasaktāśca śivaśāstraratāssadā ।।

arpitaḥ śivasiddhānto vāgīśvaryāśśivena tu ।
kṛtvā vāgīśvarīyāgaṃ śivādhyayanamācaret ।।

yathopadiṣṭaṃ guruṇā paṭale'dhyayanātmake ।
śravaṇārthī ca śṛṇuyādekacittassamāhitaḥ ।।

yathā vyākhyānapaṭale guruṇā samadarśitam  ।
etadadhyayanaṃ proktaṃ dvidhā tu samavasthitam ।।

mantratantrātmakaṃ samyagātmā yaścāpi bhāṣitaḥ ।
ācāryaḥ sādhakaścaiva tulyādhyayanamaṅgalau ।।

samāptavidyau kartavyā gurussādhaka eva ca ।
dīkṣitaḥ samayī caiva na syātāṃ jñānapāragau ।।

pūjāgnikāryamantrajño kāryau samayadīkṣitau ।
(1b)
saṃhitāṃ yastu saṃpūrṇāṃ jñātumicchati dīkṣitaḥ ।।

ātmanaḥ suprabodhāya sopyadhīyata tāmiha ।
śṛṇuyācca prayatnena śāstrapratyaya * * * ।।

śāstrasaṃpratyayāttasya bhavet svapratyayaḥ sphuṭam ।
nādhīyetānyathā śiṣyaṃ gururadhyāpaye * * ।।

* * * * nnadhīyānastirobhāvagatiṃ vrajet ।
jñānotpattipravaktāraṃ tathā jñānāvatārakam ।।

* * * * * * * * jñāne tattvapadagrahaḥ ।
śāstrasambandhamuktvā ca tathā śāstraprayojanam ।।

sūtrasya ja * * * * * * * * * * * * ।
tattve nipātaṃ sūtrasya sūtre sūtrasya caiva hi ।।

padacchede samā * * * * * * * * * * ।
* * ntaranipātaṃ ca tattve prakhyāpya yatnataḥ ।।

tatra vāstvaṣṭakaṃ caiva sū * * * * * * * ।
* * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * ।।

* * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * ।
* * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * ।।

* * * * * * * * nissandigdho hyanākulaḥ ।
tantrayuktibhirevaṃ tu vyākhyeyaṃ tantramācaret ।।

āptāgamaḥ pramāṇaṃ ca prameyasya ca labdhaye ।
padalakṣaṇataḥ siddhiḥ padasya samudāhṛtā ।।

vākyalakṣaṇataścaiva vākyasiddhiḥ pratiṣṭhitā ।
śabdaśa * * samāśca siddhiḥ sarvatra kīrtitā ।।

pramāṇaśāstrasiddhāni pramāṇāni ca sarvataḥ ।
(2a)
na paśujñānasāmarthyāt patijñānaṃ vicārayet ।।

svāmnāyāttadvicāryaṃ ca tu śrotavyaṃ caiva dīkṣitai: ।
paśujñānavicāreṇa patijñānaṃ vicārayet ।।

ācāryo vātha śiṣyo vā tirobhāvagatiṃ vrajet ।
prakriyāṃ vindubhirnyasya sūtraṃ nyasya padaistathā ।।

kuryādvyākhyāṃ daiśikendro kṛtvā manasi vai śivam ।
śiṣyopyācāryavaktraṃ tu vīkṣyamāṇaḥ prayatnadhīḥ ।।

śṛṇuyādyatnamāsthāya sandehe ca punaḥ punaḥ ।
nissandigdhaṃ tu gamayedgurussūtraṃ yathāgamam ।।

pūrvottareṇa vākyena na virodho yathā bhavet ।
vyākhyānasyāvasāne tu sthaṇḍilaṃ pūjayecca tam ।।

guroḥ pādau tu stabdhasya praṇamya viramet tataḥ ।
ukto vyākhyāpyupanyāsastantrayuktiḥ pradarśyate ।।

aṣṭādaśavidhāyuktistantrairvyāpya pratiṣṭhitā ।
utpattireva pūjāyā jñānasya śivadehataḥ ।।

aṣṭādaśavidhābhūya ssaivavaktisamāśritā ।
punaraṣṭādaśavidhāsovatārasamāśrayāt ।।

bhūyastvaṣṭādaśavidhā tattvasya tu parigrahāt ।
punaraṣṭādaśavidhā vyomavyāpi * * grahāt ।।

etāstu navatirjñeyā jñānesmiṃstantrayuktayaḥ ।
śivena gaditāḥ pūrvaṃ tattvārthapratipādakāḥ ।।

ajñātvāya * * * * tatra vyākhyānakṛdbhavet ।
asambaddhapralāpī syāt tantrayuktyāgamojjhitā ।।
(2b)
karoti yasya tantrasya vyākhyāṃ śiṣyasya daiśikaḥ ।
utpattiṃ tasya tantrasya guruḥ śiṣyasya darśayet ।।

idaṃ nāmāmukaṃ jñānamutpannaṃ śivadehataḥ ।
amukasmādavayavāttadbuddhyā pratipādayet ।।

asyāmukasya jñānasya pravaktāmukanāmakaḥ ।
amukāmukanāmā tu jñānasyāsyāvatārakaḥ ।।

avatīrṇamidaṃ jñānamevamasmadvidhe sthitam ।
pāramparyakramāyātaṃ gurusantatisaṃśritam ।।

amukasmādguroretadamukaṃ gurumāgatam ।
yāvadante gururaho vyākhyātā * bhavadbhuvi ।।

utpattiyuktiḥ prathamā dvitīyā vastrasaṃjñitā ।
tṛtīyā tvavasānākhyā caturthī gurusantatiḥ ।।

caturyuktiprabuddhasya śiṣyasya sumahādhiya: ।
muktidvayaṃ samāśritya vyākhyāṃ kurvīta daiśikaḥ ।।

parigrahaṃ tu tattvasya padasya tu śivātmanaḥ ।
ete te dve ca yuktī hi vyākhyādharmaṃ samāśrayet ।।

amukaṃ tattvametattu parigṛhyāmukaṃ tvidam ।
jñānaṃ pravṛttaṃ jñānī hi padaṃ caitacchivātmakam ।।

tattatvaṃ tattvasantatyā vyākhyākāle vijṛmbhate ।
padaṃ ca padasantatyā tantrāvadhinidarśanāt ।।

tatvāntaranipāto yo vyākhyākāle prajāyate ।
tatra sambandhasambaddhastattatvasya vijṛmbhitam ।।

(3a)
padāntaranipātaśca vyākhyāne yotijāyate ।
sambandhastantravākyaistu tatpadasya vijṛmbhitam ।।

sūtrāntaranipāto yaḥ sūtre sūtre prajāyate ।
vyākhyāyāssūtrasambaddhastatsūtrasya vijṛmbhitam ।।

vijṛmbhitaṃ vikāsoktirvikāsaḥ pravimarśanam ।
nātatattva: kaścidarthaḥ syānnāsūtro nāpadastathā ।।

tattvaṃ padaṃ ca sūtraṃ ca vyāviddhaṃ sampravartate ।
avyāviddhaṃ kvacittacca kvaciccaivādhanottaram ।।

kvacidyathā sthitaṃ caiva kvacicchṛṅkhalabandhanam ।
kvacitsopānavandhaṃ syādvyākhyeyaṃ tantrayuktibhiḥ ।।

na tantrayuktirahitāṃ padamekamapi kvacit ।
sūtrasya śakyate boddhuṃ śivajñānamahodadhau ।।

pūrvāstisro yuktayo yāstāstritattvavyapāśrayāḥ ।
śivaḥ sadāśivaścaiva īśvarastattvameva ca ।।

śivastu paramaṃ tattvaṃ niṣkalaṃ samudāhṛtam ।
sadāśivastu sakalamātmākhyaṃ saṃprakīrtitam ।।

īśvaraścaiva vidyākhyaṃ triravasthānadarśanāt ।
adhikārastathā bhogo layaśceti yathākramam ।।

avasthānatrayaṃ hyetadvidyā tattvasya kīrtitam ।
vidyātattvasthitamidaṃ tṛtīyaṃ parikīrtitam ।।

tantraṃ vidyātmakaṃ tattvaṃ vaktā tasya sadāśivaḥ ।
śuddhātmatattvasaṃsthosau soviśuddhovatārakaḥ ।।

śivākhyaṃ paramaṃ tattvaṃ tatprāptiprāpakaṃ smṛtam ।
(3b)
bhavati jñānasaṃyogāttatve prāpte pare śive ।।

ye tu tattvapadāvasthe tattvayukte vyavasthite ।
vidhivattattvasaṃjñāne tadvādhādhigatau matā ।।

yā proktā tantraśaktistu gurusantatisaṃśritā ।
sarvāsāṃ tantrayuktīnāṃ paurvāparyasthitātmanām ।।

santānayuktissā proktā santānaṃ yatra kevalam ।
na tasyā jṛmbhitaṃ tantre kiñcidvyākhyānalakṣaṇam ।।

।। iti tantrayuktiḥ ।।

mūrddhnastu vijayaṃ jñānaṃ lalāṭātparameśvaram (tantraṃ) ।
netrebhyaścaiva niśśvāsaṃ jñānarājamanuttamam ।।

śravaṇābhyāmathodgītaṃ mukhācca mukhavimbakam ।
grīvāskandhāttathāsiddhaṃ kartāraṃ kakṣadeśataḥ ।।

vakṣaso nārasiṃhaṃ tu stanābhyāṃ candrasaṃjñakaṃ ।
hṛdayādvīrabhadrākhyaṃ sā * yaṃ jaṭharāttathā ।।

svāyambhuvaṃ nābhideśātpārśvābhyāṃ visaraṃ tathā ।
rauravaṃ pṛṣṭhadeśāttu tantrajaṃ kaṭideśataḥ ।।

liṅgātkiraṇasaṃjñaṃ tu lalitaṃ coruyugmataḥ ।
jānubhyāṃ saurabheyaṃ tu śivasyātra purābhavat ।।

aṣṭādaśavidhā jñeyā jñānasyotpattiyuktayaḥ ।
vyākhyākāle gurustvetā jñānotpattiḥ pradarśayet ।।

jñānaprakāśakāddevānhṛnmantraṃ śivadehataḥ ।
jñānaissamaṃ sahotpannāḥ kaṣāyadanupūrvaśaḥ ।।

anādirūpo bhagavānvijayasya prakāśitaḥ ।
(4a)
śrīrūpaṃ parameśasya niśśvāsasya daśākṣaraḥ ।।

prodgītasya tu bhadrākhyaḥ praśapomukhasaṃjñitaḥ ।
binduvyāpī tu siddhasya santāno śivasādhakaḥ ।।

saumyaśca nārasiṃhasya candrasyānantasaṃjñitaḥ ।
sarvātmā vīrabhadrasya vyomaścāgneyasaṃjñitaḥ ।।

svayambhuvasya nidhanastejaśca visarasya tu ।
brahmaṇeśo rauravasya śivākhyomākuṭasya tu ।।

kiraṇākhyo devatāpi lali * * layastathā ।
prakāśepi devapatissaurabhe yasya kīrtitaḥ ।।

ityaṣṭādaśa mantavyā jñānaprakāśayuktayaḥ ।
tantrārthāṃstu tathā devāñjñānānāmavatārakān ।।

ācāryaḥ kathayet prājñaḥ śiṣyāya tu sumedhase ।
anādirūpādvijayaṃ bhagavān parameśvaraḥ ।।

śrīrūpataḥ pāramī{tī}śamuśanāḥ prāptavānmuniḥ ।
daśākṣarā tu niśśvāsa * * * vīmupāgataḥ ।।

prodgītaṃ caiva bhadrākhyātkacaḥ samprāptavānṛṣiḥ ।
praśamānmukhabimbaṃ tu dadhīcaḥ prāptavānmuniḥ ।।

binduvyāpyā hvayāddevānsiddhaṃ caṇḍeśamāgatam ।
lebhe śivākhyātsantānaṃ munirvaiśāṃśapāyanaḥ ।।

nārasiṃhe tathā saumyānnṛsiṃhaḥ prāptavānpunaḥ ।
bhadraṃ sarvātmadevā tu vīrabhadrastu labdhavān ।।
(4b)
anantāccandrasaṃjñaṃ tu vṛhaspatiravāptavān ।
āgneyaṃ prāptavānvyomnaḥ skando devastathānalaḥ ।।

svāyambhuvaṃ tu nidhanādbrahmā vai samavāptavān ।
tejaspatestu visaraṃ lebhe devaḥ prajāpatiḥ ।।

rauravaṃ brahmaṇeśānātprandīśaṃ prāptavānmuniḥ ।
tantrajaṃ tu śivākhyānānmahākālāttu prāptavān ।।

vidyālayāttu vilayaṃ bhairavaḥ samavāptavān ।
kiraṇaṃ devapitṛto lebhe saṃvartako muniḥ ।।

saurabheyaṃ devapaterdharmo devaḥ samāptavān ।
iti jñānāvatā * * yuktayo(')ṣṭādaśoditā: ।।

catuṣpañcāśadevaṃ tvaṃ guruṇā tantrayuktayaḥ ।
vyākhyākāle tu śiṣyāṇāṃ vyākhyeyāstu sumedhasām ।।

।। iti tantrayuktinirdeśaḥ ।।

vyākhyātā yuktayaḥ sarvā anādhyāyo nirūpyate ।
sandhyākāleṣu nādhyeyaṃ na cotpāte kadācana ।।

candrasūryoparāge ca rāśisaṅkramaṇe na vai ।
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ pañcadaśyāṃ ca pakṣayoḥ ।।

caturthyāṃ tu prayatnena pakṣamāsāyanādiṣu ।
svādhyāyo parame caiva rājñaśca * * * * * ।।

rājyabhraṃśe tathāriṣṭe sve cāriṣṭe viśeṣataḥ ।
ativātapravṛttau tu sarveṣvevotsaveṣu ca ।।

* * * * * * yenye anādhyāyeṣu teṣu ca ।
vyāpṛto guruvākyeṣu nādhīyeta kadācana ।।

(5a)
anādhyāyaḥ prakartavyaḥ sarvathā paśusaṃsadi ।
jṛmbhāhutkṣīvakāsādīndūrīkuryādguroḥ puraḥ ।।

pramādādanyathā kṛtvā vāmadevaśataṃ japet ।
adhīyānonyathā śiṣyastvanyathādhyāpayedguruḥ ।।

pramādato vismaraṇāttirobhāvagatiṃ vrajet ।।

।। brahmā uvāca ।।

agnīnādhyeyamakhilaṃ śrutvā caiva prayatnataḥ ।
ācāryapramukhairdeva kiṃ kāryamiha dīkṣitai: ।।

।। parameśvara uvāca ।।

samayīputrakaścaiva kuryātāṃ śivapūjanam ।
agnikāryaṃ ca satataṃ sādhako mantrasādhanam ।।

ācāryodhyāpanaṃ vyākhyāṃ parānugrahameva ca ।
bhautikaṃ mauktikaṃ caiva viditvā śivamañjasā ।।

tamabhidhyāya deveśaṃ kāryā sarvakriyā budhaiḥ ।
sarvameva phalaṃ śāstramanyathā saṃprajāyate ।।

।। ityadhyayanavidhiḥ ।।

vidhiḥ proktodhyayanagastantrayuktyādipūrvakaḥ ।
anādhyāyadinopetaḥ śrīmatpauṣkaraśāstrataḥ ।।

śrīkaṇṭhyāṃ tu viśeṣeṇa yathoktamupadiśyate ।
adṛṣṭavigrahāsthāntācchivātparamakāraṇāt ।।

jñānarūpaṃ viniṣkrāntaṃ manasthavadanaṃ mahat ।
kāraṇatvaṃ yadā prāptaṃ sorkatveti anāhatam ।।

kṣarate yatparaṃ yasmādakṣarastena kīrtitaḥ ।
(5b)
yosāvacintya ityāhuḥ prāṇināṃ prāṇadhārakaḥ ।।

cidātmā sarvagaḥ svasthaḥ svasaṃvedyo nirāśrayaḥ ।
jantūnāṃ hṛdayābjastho vadate nādarūpakaḥ ।।

āgopālāṅganābālā mlecchāḥ prākṛtabhāṣiṇa: ।
jalecarāśca ye matvāstepi nityaṃ bruvanti tat ।।

nādarūpaṃ hakāraṃ tu haṃsahaṃseti suvrate ।
tadutpannamidaṃ sarvaṃ caturvargaphalapradam ।।

athātovedavyākaraṇatarketihāsapurāṇavaiṣṇavaśaivasaurabauddhāhatasāṅkhyayogasiddhānta-kulakaulāścākulānyapi ।।

evamādikabhedaistu śabdabrahmapratiṣṭhitam ।
tathānyairbahubhiḥ śubhrairnāmabhedairanīkaśaḥ ।।

ete bhedāssaptadaśa mukhyatve kīrtitā mayā ।
teṣāṃ bhedaṃ pravakṣyāmi śuddhavidyāgamodbhavam ।।

anantamukhaniryātamāgamoditavartmanā ।
vedeti prathamaṃ yacca taccaturdhā prakīrtitam ।।

ṛgvedaḥ pṛthivī brahmā āpo viṣṇuryajussmṛtaḥ ।
rudrastejo bhavetsāma vāyvīśotharva ucyate ।।

adhyāyānāṃ sahasreṇa ṛcāṃ lakṣairanekaśaḥ ।
asaṅkhyaiḥ śāstrasāmānyairbahumantrādipūritaiḥ ।।

yajñavidyāsahasraiśca sāṅgopāṅgādisaṃyutaḥ ।
matprasādajavaktreṇa ṛgvedamavatāritam ।।
(6a)
tataśca vyākṛtaṃ śāstraṃ kālāntaranimittataḥ ।
atharvaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ vaktraṃ guhyamantrālayaṃ śubham ।।

ṛgvedaṃ pūrvavaktraṃ tu śabdabrahmapravartakam ।
smārtaṃ sarvāṅgasaṃpūrṇaṃ kriyābhedādisaṅkulam ।।

paścimaṃ sāmamityuktaṃ cchandodhyayanabhūṣitam ।
hakāraṃ haṃsarūpaṃ ca hauti hāyati gīyate ।।

yajuṛksāmavaktrasthaṃ taccaturdhā tu paṭhyate ।
akāraśca ukāraśca makāraścārdhamātrakaḥ ।।

śaivavaidikaśabdādīñchabdārthapratipattaye ।
prayojananimittaṃ ca dhātuśabdānuśāsanam ।।

vyākaroti yadā sarvaṃ ta- vyākaraṇaṃ smṛtam ।
{navadhā tacca vijñeyaṃ kālāntaranimittataḥ ।।

aindragālavaśākalya gārgyavātsyakṛtaṃ tathā ।
kātyāyanabharadvājaguhātreyakṛtāni ca ।।

navavyākaraṇānyevaṃ sarvaśabdārthakāraṇam ।}
pratyakṣādipramāṇārtha pramāṇaṃ cāṣṭadhā yathā ।।

vācyavācakasambandhamanumeyādilakṣaṇam ।
liṅgālliṅgīti sāmānyaṃ dhūmādagnirghanātmayaḥ ।।

dṛśyo draṣṭā tamorūpo janmī janayitā yathā ।
śuṣkabharkādisattarkanimitto nirahetukaḥ ।।

bhramaḥ parokṣasāmānyaṃ pratyakṣamavinaśvaram ।
arthāpattiprameyaśca sambandho nirahetukaḥ ।।
nimittakāraṇastarkaḥ kṣaṇabhaṅgī parokṣataḥ ।
jñānamātrādisambandha sadabuddhyādilakṣaṇaḥ ।।

jñānamātramidaṃ sarvaṃ yathāvadbhāvahetukam ।
nirnimittāni rūpāṇi nimitto nirahetukaḥ ।।
(6b)
hetuḥ pratyakṣasāmānyaḥ prameyaścārthasiddhaye ।
punarbhavosti nairātmyaṃ nissvabhāvādilakṣaṇam ।।

bauddho pratyakṣasāmānyametadaṣṭavidhaṃ matam ।
itihāsādikaṃ viṣṇossanimittamahetukam ।।

sthityādilakṣaṇaṃ sarvamādhyātmikamataḥparam ।
kriyādilakṣaṇaṃ sarvamupāsyopāsakākulam ।।

prākṛtaṃ vyāptiniyamaṃ karmayogādilakṣaṇam ।
sarvatrātmādhikṛtyaivaṃ svasaṃvedanalakṣaṇam ।।

pravartate sannimittaṃ nirnimittaṃ nivartate ।
tatsarvaṃ vaidikeyaṃ tu  * * daṃ brahmagīyate ।।

yajñakriyādikartavyaṃ guṇasrotādisambhavam ।
karmasanyāsasambaddhaṃ pāñcarātrikalakṣaṇam ।।

taduddiśya tu karmāṇi bhavanti na bhavanti ca ।
ijyāpādānakaṃ karma sa yogaḥ pañcadhā mataḥ ।।

sālokyatāṃ tu prāpya: - paramātmādi sambhavaḥ ।
āyuḥ piṇḍaḥ svatejokto hanyamāno yathā javāt ।।

visphuliṅgāni mumuce tadvadbhūtādisambhavaḥ ।
keṣāṃcidāveśamātraṃ sthitaṃ maṇḍūkacūrṇavat ।।

idaṃ kāryamidaṃ neti idaṃ kṛtvedamāpnuyāt ।
āptopadeśasambaddhamāstīti vastu gauravam ।।

brahmādyairanusaṃsyeyattatsmārtamiti gīyate ।
pūrvajaiḥ pūrvata * * kṛtamityevānuṣṭhitam ।।
(7a)
sargānusargavaṃśāśca manumanvantarasthitiḥ ।
vaṃśānuvaṃśacaritaṃ purāṇamiti kīrtitam ।।

taccāṣṭādaśadhā vakṣye hyavatārakabhedataḥ ।
ādyaṃ brahmāṇḍamityuktaṃ dvitīyaṃ vāyurucyate ।।

ma * * * ṇaṃ tṛtīyaṃ caturthaṃ sūryasambhavam ।
pañcamaṃ viṣṇusaṃjñaṃ ca ṣaṣṭhaṃ vidyāsamudbhavam ।।

somākhyaṃ sāmbasambhūtaṃ bhairavaṃ vijayaṃ tathā ।
ityaṣṭādaśabhedena purāṇaṃ parikīrtitam ।।

cittasya vṛttistattyāgaḥ sākṣātkārādilakṣaṇam ।
ātmanā sahasaṃyogaḥ saṃyogaḥ ṣaḍvidho mataḥ ।।

prāṇāyāmastathā dhyānaṃ pratyāhāraśca dhāraṇā ।
tarkaścaiva samādhiśca ṣaḍaṅgo yoga ucyate ।।

vācakastasya oṅkāro hṛcchāyī puruṣeti ca ।
kleśakarmavipākādyairaparāmṛṣṭa īśvaraḥ ।।

upalabdhipramāṇārthadhāraṇājñānahetukaḥ ।
upāsyo jyotirūpaśca tallavaḥ pariṇāmakaḥ ।।

pātañjalānāmityādi matamātmaviniścayaḥ ।
ghrātā sparśayitā vaktā jyotirātmeti yoginām ।।

sādhyānāṃ saṃkhyayā jñānaṃ pañcaviṃśātmakaṃ na hi ।
pṛthivyādīni bhūtāni śabdādyā bhūtayonayaḥ ।।

antaḥkaraṇaṃ tritveti karmabuddhindriyā daśa ।
prakṛtistriguṇā devi puruṣaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ ।।

sa ca karteti bhokteti saṃyogātphalamaśnute ।
(7b)
niṣpattiḥ paṅgurandheti ayaskānto maṇiryathā ।।

sphaṭikaḥ sūryatejena tadvadbhūtaviniścayaḥ ।
viparyayādguṇānāṃ tu pṛthagbhoktṛtvakarmataḥ ।।

bahūṃstattvānprasiddhāṃśca itimānapramātṛtaḥ ।
avasassamare nityaṃ prakṛtistho na mucyate ।।

pradhānapuruṣāntaraṃ dṛṣṭvā prabhṛti kevalaḥ ।
akartā bhogyaniṣpattau loṣṭavadanukevalaḥ ।।

saṃtaḥ sāṃkhyavidāmevaṃ prākṛtaṃ matamucyate ।
puruṣāndhaparaṃ kiñcitsā kāṣṭā sā parā gatiḥ ।।

yadetatsaṅkhyayā jñānaṃ tripramāṇamudāhṛtam ।
śatakoṭipravistīrṇaṃ pañcarātraṃ pramāṇataḥ ।।

tatra bhedaśataṃ pūrṇaṃ ṣoḍaśādhikameva ca ।
viṣṇunā kathitaṃ devi pañcakālanidarśanam ।।

nāmabhedena vakṣyāmi saṃhitānāṃ samāsataḥ ।
nārāyaṇī saṅkarṣaṇī pradyumnīcāniruddhikā ।।

vaihāyasī ca kapilā ātreyī kālapauṣkarī ।
bhāradvājī ca kālākhyā aindrī baudhāyanī tathā ।।

svarbhānavī jayaksenā kūrmākhyā śāṅkupāyinī ।
nāradī kautukī tathyā prahlādī pauṣkarī tathā ।।

jayā hayaśirā caiva tathā kālavapīyakā ।
padmodbhavā pañcakālyā māyā vaibhavikā tathā ।।

vāsiṣṭhā paramā saṅkhyā aurvā sārasvatī tathā ।
(8a)
sāndarśanā tathā lakṣmyā naranārāyaṇī tathā ।।

hairaṇyagarbhā paulastyā athāsurvadā nāradī ।
jājvalistvatha śāṇḍilyā kaustubhākhyā ca saṃhitā ।।

śeṣānantā sātvatā ca rvārhaspatyātha gālavā ।
sadyākhyā saṃhitā devi tathā kāśyapireva ca ।।

saumantā cāpi viśvā ca tathā trailokyamohinī ।
catuścayāvatārā ca nārasaiṃhī trivikramā ।।

dattātreyi kriyāsāraṃ sanatkaumārikā tathā ।
lauhamarṣiṇikā caiva vihagendramataṃ tathā ।।

pārāśaryā ca kāpitthī tathā sauparṇaketukā ।
cāturātryā ca vāyavyā jaigīśivyā ca māṇḍavī ।।

mālvā kālaparā caiva vaikuṇṭhyādbhutagautamā ।
lāciketā jāmadagnī vaiśampāyanikā tathā ।।

śrīpatnī caiva śākalyā aiśvarī pārthivī tathā ।
hautāśanī dhruvāntā ca mahāmāndārimānavī ।।

navātmikāpyāṅgirasī āruṇā śākaṭāyanī ।
avyaktāpyādi vārāhī tathā pradyumnakāmikā ।।

brahmodbhavā tu kiñjalkā tejosravaṇikā tathā ।
kaṇvakrūrā satyabhāmā mārkāṇḍeyā bhavodbhavā ।।

guhyārṇavā sphoṭasiṃhaṃ tathā pātalakesariṃ ।
mūrtibhā sā pañcamūrtiniruktā viśvasambhavā ।।
(8b)
śatadhārā viśvamukhī viśvaksenā anuṣṭubhā ।
deśāvatārā praṇavā padmagarbhā tu lomaśā ।।

suparṇā caiva deveśi śataṃ vai ṣoḍaśādhikam ।
saṃhitānāṃ varārohe vaiṣṇavaṃ parikīrtitam ।।

pratyakṣādi parokṣādi sāmānyamatalakṣaṇam ।
tadyogapariṇāmaṃ ca na punarjanmalakṣaṇam ।।

karmasambandhi nāstikyā bhāvaśūnyamidaṃ jagat ।
yadgataṃ tadgataṃ ceti jalabudbudasambhavam ।।

kaḥ kartā kotra kriyate mataṃ mānahatāmiti ।
jīvādityeti hṛtsthaṃ yad bhūtākāśo tamonudaḥ ।।

tajjyotiṣprāptilakṣaṇaṃ saurāṇāṃ matamuttamam ।
jyotīrūpamidaṃ sarvaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam ।।

bahudhā gīyate tacca saurabhedādisambhavam ।
saṃhitārtheti niṣpattiṃ tatpravakṣyāmi sāmpratam ।।

mārtāṇḍī saṃhitā pūrvaṃ mahāmārtāṇḍikī tathā ।
tṛtīyī bhargaśikhā ca tathā bhairavaśekharī ।।

vedamantrādisaṅgītā jaiminīyā ca pañcamī ।
saṃhārākhyā tathā ṣaṣṭhī bhāskarī saptamī -tā ।।

parākhyā cāṣṭamī proktā navamī saṃyamā tathā ।
daśamī guhyacakrāhvā mahālakṣmī tathā parā ।।

sahasrā dvādaśī proktā karaṇā ca trayodaśī ।
(9a)
caṇḍa ca vyomagarbhā ca khaśolkā * * * skarī ।।
vāḍavī caiva kāruṇyā yamunā ca tathā jitā ।
tathā sūryaśikhānāma raśmicakrā khageśvarī ।।

maholkā ca maholkākhyā nabholkā vyomamālanī ।
kalmāṣā puṣkarāvartā tathā sāmbhākhyasaṃhitā ।।

vaiśvānarī prakāśā ca saṃvartākhyā ca piṅgalā ।
hanumākhyā yājñavalkī jānakī yajñamekhalā ।।

uccaicśravākhyāpyaruṇā kālavaiśvānarīprabhā ।
caṇḍacetā mahādevā tathā vyājīvatārikā ।।

tārikā tejati * * yākṣikā mokṣatārakā ।
jīvārkā paramārkā ca praśāntārkā ca vaidyutā ।।

dharmadhvajā jñānagarbhā tattvagarbhā * * * hā ।
hairaṇyagarbhā tanmukhyā tathānyā harimardinī ।।

bhacakrā hyātmacetā ca tathā piṅgalacetakā ।
* * * svasāpyātreyārauśyā vyomā ca sāraṇā ।।

revandākhyā hetirājā sahirā draviḍā tathā ।
aurvā ca sāratipaṭābheruspandā tathāntrikī ।।

maheśvetā tejagarbhā mahākairātikā tathā ।
sūryadharmātryodaśātmā sūryadharmottarā tathā ।।

vyomāṅgarā vyomagarbhā pañcāśītistu saṃhitā ।
ityetāḥ saṃhitā devi mukhyatve saurabhedataḥ ।।

lakṣāvalakṣabhedena pādakenāvakena ca ।
granthavistārabhedena kathitāḥ pārameśvare ।।
(9b)
pratyakṣalakṣaṇo nāma saurāṇāṃ tatpramāṇataḥ ।
samyagabhihitaṃ yatra arthaṃ bahvāgamākulam ।।

alpagranthaṃ mahārthaṃ ca saṃhitārthaṃ ca taṃ viduḥ ।
tāntrikaṃ yatsamutpannaṃ smṛtvā yatparikīrtyate ।।

sūkṣmavādavidhānaṃ ca paṭalaṃ tena bheditam ।
ādyantamarthavistāraṃ kramādvādavinirṇayam ।।

sargapūrvaṃ tu taṃ devi granthebhyaḥ pratipādyate ।
pṛthagvastusamūhena bahugranthārthasaṅkulam ।।

ānupūrvyeṇa yo vādaḥ saparicchedapūrvakaḥ ।
pṛthaksiddhastu jīvo vai heyopādeyalakṣaṇaḥ ।।

māyāṇukārmiko bandhaścidacidbodhapūrvakaḥ ।
bhogyonmukhatvavartitvādvairāgyapratipattaye ।।

jijñāsuḥ paramaṃ tattvaṃ śaktipātasya lakṣaṇam ।
dānakṣapaṇahetvādi sūkṣmadehavināśanam ।।

seśvaraṃ yatparaṃ śāntaṃ padaṃ prāpnoti niścitam ।
prasādākhyaṃ tu yanmantraṃ siddhāntaṃ samudāhṛtam ।।

nityoditasvarūpāṇi bhāvāni paramārthataḥ ।
tanmayaḥ prāptisaṃyogaḥ sa śāktaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ।।

kulaṃ śarīramityāhurnāśivaṃ vidyate kvacit ।
svabhāvaphalaniṣpattirnānyatparataraṃ punaḥ ।।

piṇḍādi yaccaturdhānta saṃsthitaṃ tatkulaṃ smṛtam ।
(10a)
draivyādvaitādikaṃ sa tu taccaturdhā vyavasthitam ।।

guhyamantraprakāśādi sarvasaṃhāralakṣaṇam ।
kaulārthamiti taddevi sadyaḥ pratyayakārakam ।।

adhunā daśadhā caiva śaivārthaḥ parikīrtyate ।
daśāṣṭādaśabhedena rudrabhedādilakṣaṇam ।।

etatsaptadaśavidho bhedaḥ śabdādisambhavaḥ ।
mukhyatve kathite devi kiñcicchabdāntarāntaraḥ ।।

īśānaḥ śabdadehastu sarvavidyātmakaḥ smṛtaḥ ।
yatkiñcillaukikaṃ jñānaṃ tatsarvaṃ brahmanirgatam ।।

vaidikaṃ tu tadā viṣṇostejasādhyātmaniścayaḥ ।
īśvaraścāti mārgaṃ tu mantrākhyaṃ tu sadāśivāt ।।

caturvargāstu evaite vyāpitve saṃvyavasthitāḥ ।
atha devātidevasya pañcamantramayasya ca ।।

pañcasrotāṃsi jātāni śāstrotpattinimittataḥ ।
ūrdhvataḥ pūrvataḥ prācyāṃ yāmya uttarataḥ kramāt ।।

srote bhedā bhavejjñānaṃ śivarudrākhyajaṃ purā ।
kāmikaṃ yojakaṃ citryaṃ nākuṭamaṃśumānpunaḥ ।।

dīptaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tu sāhasramajitānantakaṃ tathā ।
śivabhedāssamākhyātā rudrabhedāñśṛṇu priye ।।

vijayaṃ pārameśaṃ ca niśśvāsodgatimeva ca ।
mukhabimbaṃ ca siddhaṃ ca santānaṃ nārasaiṃhikam ।।

candrāhvaṃ vīrabhadraṃ ca āgneyaṃ ca svayambhuvam ।
(10b)
visaraṃ rauravāhvaṃ ca vimalaṃ kiraṇaṃ tathā ।।

lalitaṃ saurabheyaṃ ca tantrāhyāhurmaheśvari ।
aṣṭāviṃśati bhedaṃ tu ūrdhvasroto vinirgatam ।।

aṣṭāviṃśati ye tantrā tūrṇoketuprakāśitāḥ ।
śivādyādṛksamutpannā bhuktimuktiphalapradāḥ ।।

bahugranthārtha bhedaiśca tānpravakṣyāmi sāmpratam ।
aṣṭāviṃśati ye bhedā ssātanuḥ pārameśvarī ।।

śarīraṃ devadevasya śabdabrahmapratiṣṭhitam ।
anugrahārthaṃ bhūtānāṃ nānyathā kāyasambhavaḥ ।।

mantramūrtissabhagavānsādākhyaḥ parameśvaraḥ ।
śarīraṃ rudrabhedaistu śivabhedaiśca pārvati ।।

sarvamantrā * yatvepi yo jānāti sa vedavit ।
śiraḥ prabhṛti pādāntaṃ tatpravakṣyāmi sāmpratam ।।

mūrdhnastu vijayātmānaṃ lalāṭātpārameśvaram ।
netrebhyaścaiva niḥśvāsaṃ jñānarājānamuttamam ।।

śravaṇābhyāṃ ca prodgītaṃ mukhācca mukhabimbakam ।
skandhayossiddhasaṃjñastu santānaṃ kakṣadeśataḥ ।।

vakṣaso nārasiṃhastu stanābhyāṃ candrasaṃjñakam ।
hṛdayādvīrabhadrākhyamāgneyaṃ jaṭharodbhavam ।।

svāyambhuvaṃ nābhideśātpārśvābhyāṃ visaraṃ tathā ।
rauravaṃ pṛṣṭhadeśāttu vimalaṃ kaṭideśataḥ ।।

liṅgātkiraṇasaṃjñaṃ tu lalitaṃ coruyugmataḥ ।
(11a)
jānubhyāṃ saurabheyastu śikhāgrātkāmikaṃ tathā ।।

śikhāyāṃ yogajaṃ caiva jihvāyāṃ cintyasaṃjñakam ।
mākuṭaṃ mukuṭā jjātaṃ bāhubhyāmaṃśumāṃstathā ।।

locanatritayāddīptaṃ sūkṣmaṃ kaṇṭhādvinirgatam ।
dantaireva ca sāhasraṃ hastayorajitaṃ tathā ।।

pādayorantanāmākhyaṃ tantraṃ tārāgaṇodbhavam ।
yonyaḥ parataro bhedaḥ śivamantrasya paṭhyate ।।

saṅgrahassa tu vijñeya ṛṣidevagaṇāgirā ।
tadvadvāmadakṣiṇabhedāvaṣṭau mūrdhnopalakṣitau vyākhyāsyāmaḥ । taścaikādaśabhedabhinnasambandhanimittabhedasaṃjñāpramāṇavaktrā śrotā  pūrvasvarūpaśāstrārthadarśako vaktavyaḥ ।

vācyavācakasambandha sarvabhedādilakṣaṇam ।।
vācyortho vācakaḥ śabdaḥ sa cārtho bahudhā bhavet ।
sannimittaṃ samārambhaṃ vastupūrvamahetukam ।।

pratipatyādisāmānyaṃ satarkamānumeyakam ।
śabdāstu pūrvavaddevi tathā ca bahubhedataḥ ।।

bāhyanaiścāyikābuddhiḥ śuktikārajatabhramaḥ ।
sādṛśyatvātprapatsyedyo hyatītānāgataṃ tathā ।।

pravartate nirākāṅkṣaṃ buddhyādiparihīyate ।
anyabhāvagatatvācca saṃvedyenaiva bhāvyate ।।

ūhādilakṣaṇaṃ devi saśabdo bahubhedataḥ ।
anugrahārthaṃ bhūtānāṃ nimittaḥ parigīyate ।।
(11b)
saṃsāravinivṛtyarthaṃ svatantratvāt sa ceśvaraḥ ।
svabhāvenaiva jantūnāmanugrahakaraḥ paraḥ ।।

māyābandhanimitārthaṃ tadbandhānmocayetpaśūn ।
sarvajñatvādiśāstrasya nimittaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ।।

bhedo bahuvido devi bahutvādgranthavistarāt ।
buddhibhedaṃ tu bauddhānāṃ śaivānāmīśvareti ca ।।

matabhedaṃ matārthānāṃ saurāṇāṃ jñānasambhavam ।
tāntrikānāṃ rudrabhedaṃ vaktrasthānāmanekaśaḥ ।।

śaktibhedaṃ kriyābhedaṃ mantrabhedaṃ ca paṭhyate ।
śabdabrahmaprabhūtattvād brahmacaryādibhedataḥ ।।

avatāraprabhedāśca saṃjñābhedamudāhṛtam ।
ṣaṭpramāṇāśca pūrvoktāḥ pratyakṣādyāśca ye sthitāḥ ।।

svasaṃvedanamanyacca tathā cānubhavaḥ smṛtaḥ ।
pramāṇaścāṣṭadhā devi śaivesminparigīyate ।।

srotonusāraṃ vākyārthaṃ sandehavinivṛttaye ।
devatā bahubhedena avatārādibhedataḥ ।।

vaktrā yadabhisambandhassarvaśāstrapravartakaḥ ।
saṃśayānudito bhūtvā saṃsāravinivṛttaye ।।

kimidaṃ kena kasmācca srotassambandhalakṣaṇam ।
āyātādisamudbhūtaṃ svecchayā nāvatāritam ।।

śivādanantanāthena hyanantānmayupāgatam ।
tanmukhātpatitaṃ bhūmāvatārāṣṭaketi ca ।।
(12a)
ityāgamānāṃ sambandhaḥ kramāyātastadāgamaḥ ।
svarūpaṃ kāraṇatveti sacchāstrasya viniścayaḥ ।।

kimarthamavatīrṇaṃ tu tadarthaṃ bahubhedataḥ ।
śikṣā nītyādikaṃ cārthaṃ yogārthamātmalakṣaṇam ।।

śaivārthamīśvaraprāptirjñānārthaṃ jñeyamucyate ।
vākyārthaṃ vākyaniṣpattirdīkṣārthaṃ mokṣamucyate ।।

ityartho bahudhākārairyorthaścārthādvibhāvyate ।
ayamartha idaṃ śāstramityartho bahubhedataḥ ।।

darśaketi yaduddiṣṭaṃ śāstradṛṣṭyānumīyate ।
aśāstradṛṣṭaṃ yatkarma yaccarvādikalakṣaṇam ।।

śāstradṛṣṭyā kṛtaṃ yacca tadarthaṃ darśako mataḥ ।
ityekādaśa vastūni tatprakāreṇa vacmahe ।।

śambhupakṣeṇa caikena śaktipakṣeṇa vā punaḥ ।
prāpyate yena pakṣeṇa śaktipakṣaḥ sa ucyate ।।

prāpyate yatparaṃ nityaṃ śambhupakṣastadā bhavet ।
aṇutve mandaniṣpatirbhūtānāṃ sannimittataḥ ।।

avatīrthānugṛhṇanti mantrārthaḥ śambhupakṣagaḥ ।
aṣṭāviṃśatibhedaistu gāruḍaṃ hṛdayaṃ purā ।।

sāvityuttarasaṃjñaṃ tu kalāmānaṃ kalāmṛtam ।
harahuṅkāra * binduṃ bindusāraṃ kalottaram ।।

pakṣirājaṃ śikhāyogaṃ śikhāsāraṃ śikhottaram ।
pañcabhūtaṃ tu bhedākhyaṃ śivabhedaṃ ca mānasam ।।
(12b)
nīlakaṇṭhaṃ ca kālākhyaṃ kālakūṭaṃ śatadrukam ।
vākojaṃ kambalaṃ kākaṃ kākatuṇḍaṃ ka * hakamīṃ ।।

suvarṇaledhisugrīvaṃ trotulaṃ trotulottaraḥ ।
ityāṣṭāviṃśatibhedā mayā proktāḥ samāsataḥ ।।

* ṣanirnāśanārthaṃ tu bhūtānāmanukampayā ।
kulanāgādirakṣārthaṃ bahumantrārthasaṅkulam ।।

caturviṃśatibhedaistu harahuṅkārasaṃjñitam ।
prathamaṃ pakṣirājākhyaṃ pañcabhedagataṃ bhavet ।।

hayagrīvaṃ kumāraṃ ca * gormādavameva ca ।
vajratuṇḍaṃ śubhāṇḍaṃ ca pañcabhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ।।

saptadhā tacchikhāyogamānupūrveṇa kathyate ।
śikhāśaiva śikhā caiva yā ca sāraṃ mahābhaṭam ।।

karṇarājaṃ saurabhedaṃ saptamaṃ * hyamālinam ।
śikhāsāraṃ tridhā devi caṇḍasārādilakṣaṇam ।।

tuṇḍigrīvaṃ mahākālaṃ tato vajraśikhottaram ।
pātālaṃ tālacaṇḍaṃ ca aṭṭahāsaviṣolbaṇam ।।

agadhaṃ ghoratuṇḍaṃ ca viṣadharamataṃ tathā ।
holākhyaṃ vegaṣaṇḍaṃ ca vivartakam ।।

gonāsaṃ bhiṇḍicaṇḍaṃ ca śākalaṃ jīvarakṣaṇam ।
daśapañca ca bhedaṃ ca kathitaṃ te śikhottaram ।।

nīladaṇḍaṃ śivākhyaṃ ca tāloghuṣṭaṃ vilambikam ।
(13a)
bhairavākhyaṃ śivāṇḍaṃ ca rājamūlaṃ mahābalam ।।

karṇāmoṭaparādaṃśa indravajraṃ mahāgadam ।
iti dvādaśabhedāstu pañcāśatasamudbhavāḥ ।।

sāvitryotulasaṃjñāstu sarve caikamatāḥ smṛtāḥ ।
bhedāḥ khyātā mayā hyete śiṣyā * * svarūpataḥ ।।

paścime bhūtatantrākhyaṃ śivabhedagataṃ bhavet ।
tatra holā hayagrīvaṃ karaṅkaṃ kaṭakāmayam ।।

karoṭī muṇḍamālākhyaṃ kārkoṭaṃ khaḍgarāvaṇam ।
caṇḍaṃ daṇḍāsidhārākhyaṃ vikaṭaṃ kaṭimaṇḍalam ।।

bhūtrāsaṃ ca śikhārāvaṃ ghargharaṃ siṃhakoṭaram ।
ghorāṭṭahāsamuddiṣṭaṃ yamagha(ṇṭaṃ) ghaṭotkacam ।।

ete duṣṭanivṛtyarthaṃ bhūtānāmanukampayā ।
sarvavyādhivināśārthaṃ puruṣāddhiṃha bheda * ।।

nissṛtaṃ raudramantrorthaṃ mālāmantraistu saṅkulam ।
cikitsādyauṣadho petaṃ duṣṭagrahavidāraṇam ।।

apamṛtyuśataiḥ pūrṇaṃ yena jñātena naśyati ।
helākhyaṃ prathamaṃ yattu hyāyurvedādilakṣaṇam ।।

dvitīyaṃ bālarakṣārthaṃ hayagrīveti viśrutam ।
kaṭaṅkarūpikānāṃ ca nāśanārthamudāhṛtam ।।

ratikāmanivṛtyarthaṃ caturthaṃ kaṭakāhvayam ।
netrāmayavināśārthaṃ śalyakarmapravartakam ।।
(13b)
nītiśāstrādisaṃyuktaṃ karoṭī pañcamaṃ smṛtam ।
rasavādādisiddhyarthaṃ dhātuvādādilakṣaṇam ।।

tadarthaṃ muṇḍamālākhyaṃ ṣaṣṭaṃ tantraṃ pradarśitam ।
khanyavādādikaṃ sarvaṃ nidhānākṛṣṭilakṣaṇam ।।

sarpākarṣaṇayuktaṃ ca karkoṭaṃ saptamaṃ smṛtam ।
grahajvaraviṣādīnāṃ mālāmantraiḥ samākulam ।।

sarvavyādhipraśamanamaṣṭamaṃ khaḍgarāvaṇam ।
caṇḍaṃ rakṣapiśācānāṃ nāśāya navamaṃ smṛtam ।।

caṇḍasidhāraṃ daśamaṃ sārdhalakṣatrayaṃ matam ।
samastauṣadhaprāptyarthaṃ daśanāmā pratiṣṭhitaḥ ।।

lakṣaṇamauṣadhīnāṃ tu pṛthakkalpādibhedataḥ ।
mantrarājasamūhena vikaṭaikādaśaṃ smṛtam ।।

parasainyavināśārthaṃ caṭakādiprasādhanam ।
bhūtānāṃ balibhedāśca amaraṇṭhakamaṇḍalam ।।

dvādaśaṃ ca mahādevi yena jñātena mantrabhāk ।
sarve bhūtā vinaśyanti yoginī śākinī tathā ।।

yathārthanāsamuddiṣṭaṃ bhūtatrāsaṃ trayodaśam ।
rāvoktaścāṣṭadhā yatra bhūtānāṃ bhayavardhanaḥ ।।

mudrā bhedāni sarvāṇi yoginīnāṃ ca lakṣaṇam ।
tathāhusmādi bhedaṃ ca na cchikhā nāvakīrtitam ।।
(14a)
tantraṃ caturdaśaṃ devi guhyamantrārthasaṅkulam ।
ekabhedaṃ tu bhūtānāṃ tathā vyāptyādilakṣaṇam ।।

jvarāvataraṇaṃ yacca tannivāraṇameva ca ।
bhūtayoniṣu sarvāsu tadaṃśānivibhāgaśaḥ ।।

jñāyante yena tantreṇa ghargharaṃ tantripañcamam ।
navalakṣapravistīrṇaṃ bahubhedaistu saṅkulam ।।

mantravādaṃ tu yanmukhyaṃ taduktaṃ siṃhakoṭaram ।
ghorāṭṭahāsaṃ deveśi lūtāpiṭakanāśanam ।।

bhūmikājñānasaṃyuktamṛddhyaṣṭaka samanvitam ।
yatra prayogasāmānya dṛṣṭanaṣṭādilakṣaṇam ।।

indrajālādisaṅkīrṇaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ parikīrtitam ।
devatānāṃ pramāṇaṃ ca yajanamṛddhikarmaṇi ।।

naktayāgaṃ guhyasūtraṃ vāśyoścāṭanalakṣaṇam ।
kṣudrakarmādikaṃ yattu raudramantraparibhṛtam ।।

ucchiṣṭaṃ nāmatajjñeyaṃ tantraṃ saptadaśaṃ smṛtam ।
dhāraṇājjñānabhedaṃ tu plutyādisamalaṅkṛtam ।।

sthiratvaṃ dehapiṇḍādya jarāvyādhivināśanam ।
paracikīrṣaṇṇātvaṃ ca parakāśapraveśanam ।।

oṣadhīnāṃ tu saṃyogaṃ sthitatvaṃ rudraretasaḥ ।
varṇitaṃ yatra devena sarvabhūtahiteṣiṇā ।।

tacchiṣṭaṃ taṃ varārohe tantramaṣṭādaśaṃ smṛtam ।
krīḍakarmāṇi bhūtānāṃ svasthā veśādilakṣaṇam ।।
(14b)
pramāṇaṃ bhūta * niśśeṣaṃ yatra varṇitam ।
granthabhedena sarvatra kriyābhedena niścitam ।।

udayaṃ caiva mantrāṇāṃ saṃhāramatravarṇitam ।
atha pramāṇaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ lokapālādilakṣaṇam ।।

pṛthvī sasāgaravanā yatroktā parameśvare ।
vainateyena yatpṛṣṭaṃ yatpṛṣṭaṃ bhṛgusūnunā ।।

tatsarvaṃ yatra coddiṣṭaṃ yamaghaṇṭaṃ tu taṃ viduḥ ।
ekonaviṃśatitamaṃ sārdhakoṭipravistaram ।।

aparaṃ viṣamaṃ devi sarvamantrālayaṃ śubham ।
rāvaṇādavatīrṇaṃ ya(ttri) * * * dghaṭotkacam ।।

pātālakhaṇḍaṃ prathamaṃ hāṭakeśvarasaṃjñakam ।
daśadhā tatsamākhyātaṃ tatpravakṣyāmi sārataḥ ।।

hāṭakeśaṃ ghaṭākhyāṃ ca tathā viṣamasambhavam ।
sarvasaṃhārakaṃ cānyaṃ tatpātālaśikhottaram ।।

kusumāli mataṃ caiva saptamaṃ kāmapañjaram ।
guhyārṇavaṃ picumataṃ daśasāmanikā smṛtā ।।

dvidhāvatārakhaṇḍaṃ ca tadvravīmi yathārthataḥ ।
kulārṇavaṃ tu prathamaṃ tathākramacatuṣṭayaṃ ।।

kulārṇavaṃ tu daśadhā yathāvatkathayāmite ।
kulaṃ mahākulaṃ caiva mahākāli kulaṃ tathā ।।

viścākulaṃ kāmikule kulaṃ śubhāmaṇiṃ tathā ।
kulaṃ bhairava sāmānyaṃ tathā mātṛkulaṃ śubham ।।
(15a)
viścākulaṃ śaktikulaṃ kulā * * prakīrtitāḥ ।
kramabhedaṃ yathādevi tatsamāsena me śṛṇu ।।

sṛṣṭikramaṃ kālikramaṃ saṃhārakramasaṃyutam ।
avatārakramaṃ cānyaditthaṃ kramacatuṣṭayam ।।

sṛṣṭikramaṃ caturdhā tu bhedairbhavati pārvati ।
rāgāvatāraṃ prathamaṃ lakṣārdhaṃ kathitaṃ mayā ।।

kulāvatāraṃ dvitīyaṃ mantrāvataraṇaṃ tathā ।
te varṇi * vatārākhyaṃ caturdhā sṛṣṭirucyate ।।

kālikramaṃ caturddhā vai yathābhedamudāhṛtam ।
kālikramaṃ tu prathamaṃ mahākāli kramaṃ tathā ।।

kālasaṅkarṣaṇi kramaṃ tathā pīṭhakramaṃ smṛtam ।
saṃhāraṃ ca tathā vakṣye yathāvatparameśvari ।।

saṃhārakramapūrvaṃ vai bhairavī śaktisaṃyutam ।
kālākhyaṃ dvitīyaṃ nāma bhāvasaṃhārakārakam ।।

sarvasaṃhāra saṃjñaṃ ca caturthaṃ parikīrtitam ।
kramasṛṣṭiḥ kulasṛṣṭirbhāvasṛṣṭistṛtīyikā ।।

sṛṣṭirgurusṛṣṭiriti sṛṣṭikramaṃ viduḥ ।
ayodhyākhaṇḍasaṃjñaṃ yattṛtīyaṃ khaṇḍamucyate ।।

aṣṭadhā tatsamākhyātaṃ tacchṛṇuṣvamahātape ।
bhallavijñāradaṣṭiśca vindhyakhaṇḍastṛtīyakaḥ ।।

candrikākhaṇḍasāmānyaṃ tathā tārāgaṇodbhavam ।
(15b)
jayadrathākhya manyaśca bhinnatārakameva ca ।।

nāmnā vai sarvavīraṃ ca hyetatkhaṇḍatrayaṃ viduḥ ।
etadghaṭotkacaṃ tantraṃ koṭibhedairanekaśaḥ ।।

guhyādguhyataraṃ caiva mantraissaṃvṛtamuttamam ।
etattu paścimaṃ vaktraṃ samāsātkathitaṃ mayā ।।

dakṣiṇe dakṣiṇaṃ mārgaṃ caturviṃśatibhedataḥ ।
teṣāṃ madhye mahāghoraṃ ghoraghoraṃ diśāṃ mukham ।।

bhīmakhaṇḍāparākhyaṃ ca mahāvetālasaṃjñakam ।
asitāṅgaṃ mahocchuṣmaṃ krodhamunmattabhairavam ।।

caṇḍākhyaṃ kamalākhyaṃ ca mahābhairavaśekharam ।
siddhayogīśvarītantraṃ yoginījālasambhavam ।।

pañcāmṛtaṃ prapañcākhyaṃ viśvabhairavakaṇṭhakam ।
bhairavāmṛtamutkocaṃ tilakaṃ yāmalaṃ tathā ।।

ete bhedā mahādevi yathāvat kathayāmi te ।
prathamaṃ ghoraghoṇaṃ tu taccaturdhā prakīrtitam ।।

vidyudghoṇaṃ ca vetālaṃ malicaṇḍaṃ tṛtīyakam ।
surāsavaṃ caturthaṃ tu guhyabhūtaṃ mahātape ।।

mahāvetālakaṃ yaṃtu taddvidhā parikīrtitam ।
aṣkāloriṃ tu prathamaṃ ruṣṭasarvasvameva ca ।।

mahāghore pravakṣyāmi dakṣiṇasrotasodbhavam ।
bahukoṭipravistīrṇaṃ caturviṃśatibhedataḥ ।।

mahāghoraṃ tu prathamaṃ tathā cānandabhairavam ।
lākulaṃ cāddhiśrīpūrvaṃ tathā vijñānabhairavam ।।

(16a)
aghorīśvarīsvacchandaṃ vidyāsvacchandameva ca ।
sāraṃ svacchandasaṃjñaṃ ca rasasvacchandakaṃ tathā ।।

rājaputrīyakaṃ caiva yaḥ svacchandaḥ svatantrakaḥ ।
ceṭikā tālukaṃ nāma guhyasāraṃ tathā śubham ।।

bindusvacchandakaṃ nāma nādasvacchandameva ca ।
rauravaṃ kāladaṇḍaṃ ca hyūlakādyaṃ tathaiva ca ।।

kramojjhitaṃ ca sāṭopaṃ candragarbhaṃ surārcitam ।
mṛtyunāmacaturviṃśaṃ mahāghoramudāhṛtam ।।

ghoraghoṇaṃ pravakṣyāmi hyaṣṭamaṃ yatprakīrtitam ।
prathamaṃ ghoraghoṇaṃ ca navakoṭipravistaram ।।

aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi bhairavottaranāyikā ।
candrikākhyā bhagavatī bhedairgītā tu pārvati ।।

lambodarī nityatṛptā hyudayāstamavarjitā ।
mantrabhedena kathitā bhaktānāṃ hitakāmyayā ।।

ataḥparaṃ mṛtyujihvaṃ tataḥ śuṣkākulaṃ param ।
vahnipādaṃ bhīmaśivaṃ heḍanormikulaṃ tathā ।।

cittuvādaṃ kāmadaṇḍamityaṣṭau kīrtitā mayā ।
diśāṃ mukhaṃ cāṣṭavidhamadhunā matabhedataḥ ।।

mahāvetālasaṃjñaṃ ca navamaṃ kaulameva ca ।
adhunā sāmprataṃ devi hyasitāṅgaṃ prakīrtyate ।।

caturdhā prathitaṃ tacca kālapakṣeti viśrutam ।
(16b)
brahmavādyaṃ durākhyaṃ ca kalmaṣaṇḍeti gīyate ।।

mahocchuṣmaṃ tato devi daśadhā paripaṭhyate ।
śikhocchuṣmaṃ tathā kaulī vīrabhedamataḥ param ।।

aṣṭavīreśvaraṃ cānyamṛddhikāmaṃ mahādbhutam ।
śuddhaśikṣāmataṃ cāpi tathā sārasvataṃ matam ।।

kālajihvaṃ kuvīrākhyaṃ siddhasantānakaṃ tathā ।
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi krodhamunmattabhairavam ।।

bhedadvayena cākhyātaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu pārvati ।
anāmakaṃ tu prathamaṃ candracūḍāmaṇiṃ tathā ।।

jayākhyaṃ saptadhā bhadre vaijayaṃ prathamaṃ tataḥ ।
vāruṇaṃ bhadracūḍaṃ ca sārāmṛtamahotkaṭam ।।

parāvartaṃ kākaśi * * * * * * * * * ।
(17a)
* * * * kimanyacca vidyātilakameva ca ।।

tilakaṃ bhairavākhyaṃ dayāmalaṃ paripaṭhyate ।
aṣṭadhā yāmalaṃ devi sūtrānte kathayāmite ।।

rudrabhedaṃ brahmabhedaṃ viṣṇubhedaṃ tathaiva ca ।
yāmalāntargataṃ devi ityetaddakṣiṇaṃ mukham ।।

udaksrotasi vāmākhyaṃ caturviṃśatibhedataḥ ।
teṣāṃ madhye mahātantraṃ nayasūtraṃ nayottaram ।।

mahāsaṃmohanaṃ cānyadvīṇātantraṃ śikhottaram ।
ajitaṃ vijayaṃ tvanyaccintāmaṇimahodayam ।।

vidyābhyudayanāntaṃ ca caturdhāmāmṛtādbhutam ।
* * * * * * * * * * * * * kaṃ tathā ।।
(17b)
kālādbhutaṃ tu prathitaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ guhyādbhutaṃ smṛtam ।
ṣaḍvidhādbhutametattu sarvapūjitakaṃ tathā ।।

lakṣārdha * * * saṃjñaṃ tu kathitaṃ sāramuttam ।
pañcavaktrā smṛtā yatra bhaginyo bhrātṛbhissaha ।।

mahāguhyamidaṃ devi na * * śyamanāmakam ।
ataḥ paraṃ kāmadhenuṃ caturdhā paripaṭhyate ।।

vāmaguhyaṃ tu prathamaṃ śāntikaṃ mānasaṃ tathā ।
ajatuṇḍaṃ caturthaṃ tu tato vakṣye karambakam ।।

saptadhā bhedabhinnaṃ tatprathamaṃ ca karambakam ।
samudrormidvitīyaṃ tu stambhādyaṃ mohanaṃ tathā ।।

siṃharājaṃ vājimukhaṃ naurbandhaṃ saptamaṃ smṛtam ।
anantaṃ tu tato vakṣye pañcadhā gaṇava * te ।।

anāmakaṃ bhinnaśīrṣamekavīraṃ balotkaṭam ।
apsarājaṃ tathā devi śaukraṃ śukrādvinissṛtam ।।

vāmadevena kathitaṃ bhārgavasya hitaiṣiṇā ।
cakre dvādaśasāhasrī śaukrākhyā saṃhitā śubhā ।।

mahāmantragaṇākīrṇā sarahasyā tu pārvati ।
yasyā aṣṭāvṛtaṃ bhūtaṃ devairātmasamaiḥ śubhaiḥ ।।

vāmācāraratairdevi brahmādyaiḥ koṭisaṅkhyayā ।
catuṣkaṃ tu tato vakṣye taccaturdhā prakīrtitam ।।

jayācatuṣkaṃ prathamaṃ vijayākhyaṃ tathaiva ca ।
jayantyākhyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu tathā caivāparājitam ।।
(18a)
catuṣkaṃ pratibhedaśca navadhā yatra kīrtyate ।
mahāmantrālayaṃ gūḍhaṃ tato vai bījapañjaram ।।

taccaturdhā samākhyātaṃ pañjaraṃ śaktipañjaram ।
bījapañjarakaṃ devi tathā sārasvataṃ matam ।।

ānandākhyaṃ saṃhitārthaṃ lakṣagranthārthavistaram ।
sarvamantrādisaṃyuktaṃ bhavarudrākhyajaṃ mahat ।।

sarvākhyaṃ candraśakalaṃ somarājaṃ kalāmukham ।
tathā siddhamataṃ devi pañcadhā paripaṭhyate ।।

kiṅkareśaṃ tato vakṣye yena jñātena siddhyati ।
kṣīrodakamataṃ nityaṃ hāṭakeśaṃ virājitam ।।

dūtīmukhaṃ tattvabhedaṃ mūlavākyaṃ manonmanam ।
etatsaptavidhaṃ devi tathā vakṣye parāṅmukham ।।

siddhakaulaṃ nāgasaṃjñaṃ tathā vidyāṣṭakaṃ śubham ।
kāmeśvaraṃ siddharājaṃ pañcadhā parikīrtitam ।।

caturviṃśatibhedaistu mukhyairvāmamukhodgataiḥ ।
rahasyairgūḍhamantraiśca sarvakāmārthasādhakaiḥ ।।

granthakoṭipravistīrṇaṃ vāmavaktramudāhṛtam ।
anyatsaṃkṣepato vakṣye gītaṃ yatparameṣṭinā ।।

teṣāṃ bhedaṃ pravakṣyāmi catuṣṣaṣṭhivibhāgaśaḥ ।
bhairavaṃ yāmalaṃ devi matākhyaṃ piṅgalaṃ tathā ।।

cakrāṣṭakaṃ śikhāṣṭaṃ ca bahurūpaṃ ca saptamam ।
vāgīśyā cāṣṭamaṃ proktamaṣṭamaṃ vīravandite ।।
(18b)
etatsādāśivaṃ tantraṃ kathayāmi samāsataḥ ।
svacchandabhairavaścaṇḍaḥ krodha unmattabhairavaḥ ।।

asitāṅgaṃ mahocchuṣmaṃ kāpālīśāṣṭamaṃ matam ।
brahmayāmalamityuktaṃ vetālaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ smṛtam ।।

।। viṣṇuyāmalakaṃ tathā ।।

skandamumāruruścaiva ṣaṣṭhaṃ cātharvaṇaṃ smṛtam ।
saptamaṃ rudramityuktaṃ vetālaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ smṛtam ।।

caṇḍabhedāḥ smṛtā hyete bhairave vīravatsale ।
bhairavī prathamā proktā piśutantrasamudbhavā ।।

sā dvidhā bhedataḥ khyātā tṛtīyaṃ ca tathocyate ।
brāhmī kalā caturthī ca vijayākhyā tu pañcamī ।।

candrākhyā caiva ṣaṣṭhī syānmaṅgalā sarvamaṅgalā ।
ete te maṅgalābhedā: krodheśena tu bhāṣitāḥ ।।

prathamaṃ mantracakraṃ tu varṇacakraṃ dvitīyakam ।
tṛtīyaṃ śakticakraṃ tu kalācakraṃ caturthakam ।।

pañcamaṃ binducakraṃ tu ṣaṣṭhaṃ vai nādasaṃjñitam ।
saptamaṃ guhyacakraṃ tu khacakraṃ cāṣṭamaṃ matam ।।

ete vai cakrabhedāstu asitāṅgena bhāṣitāḥ ।
andhakaṃ rurubhedaṃ ca hyajākhyaṃ mūlasaṃjñakam ।।

karṇāmoṭaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ ca jvālinyā mātṛrodanam ।
ete svacchandabhedāśca bahurūpeṇa bhāṣitāḥ ।।

bhairavī tu śikhāproktā vīṇā caiva dvitīyakā ।
vīṇāmaṇistṛtīyaṃ tu saṃmohaṃ tu caturthakam ।।
(19a)
pañcamaṃ ḍāmaraṃ nāma ṣaṣṭhaṃ caivāpyatharvakam ।
kabandhaṃ saptamaṃ khyātaṃ śiracchedamathāṣṭakam ।।

ete devi śikhābhedā unmattena tu bhāṣitāḥ ।
etatsādāśivaṃ cakraṃ maṣṭāṣṭakavibhedataḥ ।।

srotodbhavamidaṃ puṇyamanantaṃ tadvibhūṣitam ।
anantabhāskareṇedamananteśena bhāṣitam ।।

śaktitritayasaṃbhinnaṃ mudrāratnavisarpitam ।
anekākārasaṃbhinnaṃ mahāmaṇḍalanāyakam ।।

(vi)vidyāsahasrasaṅkīrṇaṃ divyavaktrāmbujodbhavam ।
viṃśatkoṭinivaddhāstu hṛditantrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ।।

navakoṭipravistīrṇaṃ siddhayogeśvarīmatam ।
sārdhakoṭitrayoktāni tantrāṇyanyāni bhairavi ।।

lakṣārdhalakṣabhedena paścādbhūtā varānane ।
sahasratrayasaṅkhyātāstathā pañcāśatāni ca ।।

śatārdhaṃ śatikā caiva (kṣa)ttre tatotha bhāmini ।
kecitsādāśive tattve anye cānantasaṃjñike ।।

anye guptāstu rudrasya vidyākhye cāpare tathā ।
prākṛte tu mahātattve gurupaṅktitraye tathā ।।

guruṇādhikaguruṇā buddhāvahaṅkāre tathaiva ca ।
tataḥ śrīkaṇṭhanāthena vismṛtaṃ sṛṣṭisāgare ।।

śivaḥ śikhā tathā jyotirvimalaśca caturthakaḥ ।
ebhyaḥ sakāśādākhyātaṃ tato martyamupāgatam ।।
(19b)
mudrāmaṇḍalapīṭhaṃ tu pañcapīṭhaṃ tathaiva ca ।
vidyāpīṭhaṃ tathā proktaṃ catuṣpīṭhā tu saṃhitā ।।

śrīkaṇṭhasaumakaumāraṃ mausalijājaliṃ tathā ।
vimalaṃ cāṣṭahāsaṃ ca ghorasvacchandamaṣṭamam ।।

dūrvāsamecakaṃ caiva tathā sārasvataṃ param ।
jayadrathaṃ ca phetkāraṃ pañcamaṃ parikīrtitam ।।

raktākhyaṃ lampaṭākhyaṃ ca saptadhā brahmayāmalam ।
haṃsayakṣiṇi mātaṅgamekavīrāmbu pauṣkaram ।।

vāmadevena sahitaṃ saptadhā pārameśvaram ।
haṃsapārameśvaraṃ yattaddvidhā paripaṭhyate ।।

pāṭayakṣiṇi haṃsākhyaṃ lakṣairnavativistaram ।
yakṣiṇī parameśaṃ ca navadhā paripaṭhyate ।।

kiraṇākhyaṃ nandimataṃ haṃsinī brahmamaṇḍalam ।
svāyambhuvaṃ skandamataṃ pratiṣṭhā pārameśvaram ।।

liṅgakalpaṃ sagārgīyaṃ navakoṭipravistaram ।
mātaṅgākhyaṃ ca yattantraṃ tridhā tatparipaṭhyate ।।

kalpaśākhā tu mātaṅgā mṛgendrākhyā ca saṃhitā ।
ekavīraṃ dvidhā bhadre sārdhalakṣatrayaṃ viduḥ ।।

paitāmahamekavīramambākhyaṃ pañcadhā tataḥ ।
ambāsajñaṃ guhyasūtraṃ tathānyatpiṅgalā matam ।।

pratiṣṭhā ṣaṭkadīkṣārṇamityetatpañcadhā sthitam ।
pauṣkaraṃ devi yattantraṃ saptadhā paripaṭhyate ।।
(20a)
puṣkaraṃ brahmavallī ca tathā nārāyaṇīyakam ।
kuberaṣaṭkaṃ kāliñjaṃ tathā hālāhalaṃ matam ।।

yoginīmatamanyacca vāmadevamathocyate ।
vāmadevaṃ catuṣkākhyaṃ tathā tumburupañjaram ।।

sarvajñametamanyacca guhyaṣaṭkaṃ gaṇāmbike ।
tattvaśākhā devavalli sarvamantrārṇavaṃ param ।।

aṣṭadhā kathitaṃ devi saptakoṭipravistaram ।
nīlāmbaraṃ sutāraṃ ca sandhyā yogini ḍāmaram ।।

svāyambhuvaṃ siddhamataṃ gaṇākhyaṃ khecarīmatam ।
aṣṭau kaulāstvamī khyātāḥ sadyaḥ pratyayakārakāḥ ।।

navamaṃ tu mahākaulaṃ yatsurairapi durlabham ।
maulaṃ samudbhavaṃ pīṭhaṃ kaulākhyaṃ tantrameva ca ।।

pūrṇapīṭhaṃ caturdhā tu yogibhiḥ paripaṭhyate ।
yogapīṭhopapīṭhaṃ ca guhyapīṭhaṃ samāttikam ।।

mattapīṭhaṃ ca daśadhā yathāvatkathayāmi te ।
mattapīṭhaṃ tu siddhākhyaṃ jāla * * mathottaram ।।

śrīpīṭhamiṣṭamardhaṃ ca sattipīṭhaṃ saśambhukam ।
nādapīṭhaṃ bindupīṭhaṃ cakkrapīṭhaṃ maheśvari ।।

samyagvaṃ vāmadakṣe tu pañcadhā parikīrtitam ।
ṣaṭsrote samidaṃ śaivaṃ pañcasrotodbhavātparam ।।

śikhākameśvaraṃ pūrvaṃ mīnākhyaṃ kiraṇaṃ param ।
dhātrīmataṃ haṅkṛlori vāsukiṃ pañcamaṃ smṛtam ।।

adhassrotodbhavā ete bhedāḥ pātālasaṃjñakāḥ ।
(20b)
tantraṃ saṃhitasūtraṃ ca saṅgrahaṃ śāstrameva ca ।।

kalpācchandaṃ tathā kalpamāgamaṃ saptadhā viduḥ ।
cakrasthaṃ parivāroktaṃ caturthaṃ pañcakaṃ tathā ।।

ṣaḍyonimatārthaṃ ca devatābahulaṃ tu yat ।
yājyayājakasamyatkṛstāntasteneha kīrtitaḥ ।।

saṃhitārthaṃ tu pūrvoktaṃ saṃkhyaṃtyarthaviniścayām ।
sārānsārataraṃ yattu saṃhitārthaṃ taducyate ।।

gūḍhārthamalpagranthaṃ yadvakṣyamāṇamanekadhā ।
tatsūtrārthaṃ samuddiṣṭamādyantāntaviniścitam ।।

saṅgrahārthaṃ ca vistāraṃ kālāntaranimittataḥ ।
svalpārthamalpagranthaṃ ca saṅgrahaṃ tena kīrtitam ।।

anuśāsayitā yatra anuśāsyādilakṣaṇam ।
śāstraprāmāṇyanirdiṣṭaṃ śā{sanācchā}stramucyate ।।

ekāśaktiśśivaścaiko bahubhedopacaryate ।
bhedaṃ tu kalpanāmātramekadeśādilakṣaṇam ।।

svalpaprajñā prameyandhātkalpasteneha kalpitaḥ ।
kalpānāṃ yatsamūhaṃ tadavatāryāvatārakam ।।

naiścāyikamidaṃ tatsyaṃ kalpaṃ skandhaṃ taducyate ।
iti hyāgamasambandhaṃ saptadhā parikīrtitam ।।

evamuktāni bhedāni sarahasyāni yāni tu ।
tāni syuśśivabhedāni ṣaṭsrotāḥ kathitāni tu ।।

śabdārṇavapramāṇāni mantratantrāṇi yāni tu ।
(21a)
jñānārṇavasamuttāni mokṣārthaphaladāni tu ।।

itthaṃ śivena śrīkaṇṭhyaṃ sroto bhedaḥ pradarśitaḥ ।।
sroto vicāraḥ ।।

adhītyādhyāyamakhilaṃ śrutvā caiva prayatnataḥ ।
ācāryapramukhaindeva kiṃ kāryamiha dīkṣitaḥ ।।

samayī putrakaścaikaṃ kuryāttaṃ śivapūjanam ।
agnikāryaṃ ca satataṃ sādhako mantrasādhanam ।।

ācāryodhyāpanaṃ vyākhyaṃ parānugrahameva ca ।
bhauktikaṃ mauktikaṃ caiva viditvāsanamañjasā ।।

tamabhidhyāya deveśaṃ kāryā sarvakriyābudhaiḥ ।
sarvamevāphalaṃ śāstramanyathā samprajāyate ।।

pauṣkaroktamidaṃ karma jñātvā snānaṃ samācaret ।
labdhadīkṣānaye kuryuravalepācchivārcanam ।।

dīkṣāphalamanarhatvātteṣāṃ vyarthamasaṃśayamiti ।
svacchandadalāyām ।।

।। ityāgamoddhṛte nityasaṅgrahe śrīrājānakatakṣakaracitedhyayanavidhiḥ ।।

athārcanādisatkarma yogyatā janakocitaḥ ।
snānaṃ nigadyate saptavidhamīśoditaiḥ padaiḥ ।।

vāruṇaṃ taijasaṃ bhaumamānilaṃ divyagaurave ।
mānasaṃ ceti saṅkṣepādeṣāṃ lakṣaṇamucyate ।।

nirvighneneśapūtena sābhiṣekaṃ sasaṃyamam ।
vāriṇā vāruṇaṃ snānaṃ sāmānyavidhipūrvakam ।।

tatpūtasyocyamānena vidhinā bhasmacālanam ।
(21b)
gavādisadrajoyogi marutsevanamānilam ।।

gaṅgādisatpayaspṛṣṭamṛtavāpūtayānyayā ।
vidhinā pārthivasnānaṃ sūrvṛṣṭyā tu nābhasam ।।

gurupādāmbujālabdhamūrdhvogā ravamucyate ।
mānasaṃ śāktameteṣāṃ vidhānamadhunocyate ।।

snānoddeśassaptadhāyaṃ mṛgendre saptakīrtitaḥ ।
māntrodakena vāmnāyāddivyena marutāthavā ।।

tatra pūrvaṃ jalaṃ snānaṃ sarvavarṇāśrameṣu ca ।
āgneyaṃ mārutaṃ divyaṃ mantrākhyaṃ tu tataḥ param ।।

jalasnānaṃ vināśastaṃ brahmacarye tu ye sthitaḥ ।
sasarkadoṣanirmuktāstapassthāssaṅgavarjitāḥ ।।

snānapūrvāssarvadharmā ssnānaṃ pāpamalāpaham ।
mahāhradaṃ nimnagā vā sarastīrthamathāpi vā ।।

eṣāmalābhādvrajate pauruṣeyaṃ jalaṃ yataḥ ।
daśasaptasamuddhṛtya mṛtpiṇḍātsnānamācaret ।।

parakīyanipāneṣu nānyathā sasyate kvacit ।
yatassarvagataṃ devaṃ vāmadevaṃ jalātmakam ।।

tathāpi devaviprāgnya * ṣṭheṣu vidhirucyate ।
nolaṅghayeddevakṛtaṃmṛṣiproktaṃ kadācana ।।

mṛdaṃ kuśāṃstilāṃścaiva gṛhītvā snānamācaret ।
śikhākāmeśvaretvirthaṃ vyavasthā vibhunā kṛtā ।।

athādhikārasiddhyarthaṃ snānaṃ vakṣyāmi pūrvata: ।
(22a)
yena bhaktobhiṣiktaśca yāgahomakṣamaṃ bhavet ।।

snānaṃ tu dvividhaṃ kuryānmalasaṅkaraśuddhaye ।
sāmānyavidhinā snātvā viśeṣavidhinā tataḥ ।।

sāmānyaṃ laukikaṃ snānaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ mantrasaṃsmṛtam ।
nadīṣu devakhyāteṣva palvalaplavaneṣu ca ।।

dīkṣitasyottamaṃ snānaṃ puṣkariṇyāṃ tu madhyamam ।
kūpedhamaṃ snānamāhurgṛhe śreṣṭhaṃ prakīrtitam ।।

ācāryapramukhānāṃ tu caturṇāmapi tatsamam ।
snānoddeśo jalasnāne proktāśśrīpauṣkare tvayam ।।

astreṇa mṛdamādāya jalopānte nyasecchucau ।
deśe sadyena vāmena prokṣyāghoreṇa mantrayet ।।

mantreṇālabhya saṃveṣṭya varmaṇā vibhajetridhā ।
malasnāne hastaśuddhau vidhisnānanimittataḥ ।।

pareṇaikaṃ tathoddhṛtya japtvāstreṇa malāpaham ।
mṛtsvīkāre vidhirayaṃ mṛgendre sampradarśitaḥ ।।

pādau jaṅghe kaṭicaiva mṛtsnābhistisṛbhiḥ kramāt ।
tirantaritayogena saptabhiśśuddhyate punaḥ ।।

saptābhimantritāṃ kṛtvā mṛdamastreṇa mantravit ।
prātāpyārkamukhaṃ paścāccharīramanulepayet ।।

vighnaughaśamanārthatvākṣālyadehaṃ samācamet ।
śrīmatmṛtyuñjayoktesmindalasnāne kṛte sati ।।

keśaprakṣālanaṃ dhanyaṃ nityamāmalakādibhiḥ ।
cakṣuṣyaṃ ca malaghnaṃ ca sirorogaṃ vināśanam ।।
(22b)
yavagodhūmacūrṇāni śālipiṣṭyamathāpi vā ।
dhanyānyudvantamārthaṃ tu tilakalkayutāni tu ।।

udvantanaṃ śiraśśaucaṃ tilaṣuktaṃ rasāvaham ।
vighnaghnaṃ ca yatastasmāttilayogaḥ praśasyate ।।

tilairudvartanaṃ snānaṃ tarpaṇaṃ tilabhakṣaṇam ।
tiladānaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ mukhe pāpaharaṃ smṛtam ।।

iti vidyāpurāṇoktaṃ vidhisnānamathocyate ।
taccheṣamapi sampādye tridhāṃśamaparaṃ mṛdaḥ ।।

vibhajya pāṇigaṃ pūrvayāmyasaumyasthamālabhet ।
astrabrahmaśivairvighnānādyenotsārayejjalāt ।।

tadantenāmṛtī kuryāddvitīyenālabhettanum ।
snāyāttatastribhāgena pāṇauśśaucaṃ punaḥ punaḥ ।।

vidhisnānamidaṃ proktaṃ mṛgendrottaraśāsane ।
vāmahastasya pūrveṇa dakṣiṇe cottare kramāt ।।

pūrvabhāgaṃ tatostreṇa saptavārāṃstu mantrayet ।
dakṣiṇasthaṃ tathā vaktrairabhimantrya varānane ।।

uttaraṃ cābhimantryaivaṃ mūlenāṅgayutena ca ।
pūrvabhāgaṃ gṛhītvāṃ tu daśadikṣu vinikṣipet ।।

uttareṇa tu bhāgena jalaṃ caivābhimantrayet ।
bhāhumātrapramāṇena bhairave samanusmaran ।।

ātmānaṃ guṇṭhayitvā tu dakṣiṇenāpi suvrate ।
snāyādrājopacāreṇa sugandhāmalakādibhiḥ ।।
(23a)
śrīmatsvacchandaśāstre ca proktaṃ mṛdabhimantraṇam ।
pūrvācarabhya sarvāsu dikṣupūrvaṃ vinikṣipet ।।

nārā ca śastrasaṅkṣepādvighnogho naśyate dhruvam ।
jale kṣiptena tenāśu kṣayaṃ yānti jalasthitāḥ ।।

udaksthena śivaṃ tīrthaṃ kṛtvā bāhuparibhramāt ।
dakṣasthena nijaṃ kāyaṃ śiraḥ prabhṛti saṃspṛśet ।।

pādānugaṃ ca pāpaghnaṃ snānaṃ kuryācchivāmbhasi ।
ukta(chā)krītu yā proktā tasyāṃ nāpaśca takratuḥ ।।

niyuktassarvadevānāṃ nāyakatvena vedhasā ।
tena taddiggatenāsminvighnaproccāṭanaṃ bhavet ।।

saumyā yā tu kakuptasyāmīśānomṛtasambhavaḥ ।
śivenāloḍitassomassudhāṃ rakṣati kevalām ।।

tattirthamamṛtaṃ yasmāttasminsnātomṛtī bhavet ।
dakṣiṇāśādhipo dharmassa pāpāpahārakaḥ ।।

tena taddiggatāyāṃ ca mṛtatsparśādanena saḥ ।
kṣayamṛdāṃ viśeṣo digbhedātkathitaśśrīparāgame ।।

vartulaṃ kalpayettīrthaṃ hastaṃ vā hastapañcakam ।
nārācāstraprayogena mṛdaṃ tatra parikṣipet ।।

jalamadhye tu vighnā hi makaragrāhapūrvakāḥ ।
palāyante tu vitrastāḥ kṣipramastraprapīḍitāḥ ।।

bhūyopyatra samuddiṣṭastīrthasaṃskāra uttamaḥ ।
vahniyuktaṃ carenmantrī dhārāṇāṃ tīrthaśoṣiṇīm ।।
(23b)
āsaptamyā tu pātālādyāvadaṇḍaṃ prajāpateḥ ।
pūrayedvahninā mantrī jvālāmālākulānmanā ।।

śoṣaṇaṃ bhavati hyeva tīrthasya dahanaṃ tathā ।
saṃhitāṃ vartayenmantrī pāṇinā mārjayettu tat ।।

kuñcikāgreṇa vārāstrīnaṅkuśākārayogataḥ ।
prasahyākarṣaṇaṃ tasya śivena kathitaṃ purā ।।

divyāntarikṣabhaumaṃ ca trividhaṃ tīrthamucyate ।
sañcityā vidhinānena sthāne tatrāvatārayet ।।

anenaiva prayogena * rākhyena vicakṣaṇaḥ ।
sādholāntasamāyuktassa ṣaṣṭhasurabindukaḥ ।।

śūnyatattvāntasaṃyuktaśśi * ssākṣājjalātmakaḥ ।
tīrthabījaṃ bhavedetatsarvakilviṣanāśanam ।।

hṛdayaṃ sarvatīrthāṇi smarenmantrī punaḥ punaḥ ।
pīyūṣavṛṣṭisampātaḥ plavyamānaṃ samantataḥ ।।

turyasthānāraviṣkṣāntaiḥ patitaistīrthagocaraiḥ ।
tuṣārakarasaṅkāśaiḥ kṣīradhārābharopamaiḥ ।।

sampātairamṛtotthaistu tīrthamāplāvitaṃ smaret ।
āpyāyanaṃ ssmṛtaṃ hyetattīrthasya tu na saṃśayaḥ ।।

bhūyastīrthe tu mantrajñe mantranyāsaṃ samācaret ।
uttarābhimukho bhūtvā śivatīrthaṃ vicintayet ।।

tatra nyasedanantaṃ tu dharmajñānādisaṃyutam ।
padmaṃ śaktigaṇaṃ caiva maṇḍalatritayaṃ tataḥ ।।
(24a)
śivaṃ tasyopari nyasyedbrahmagātrasamanvitam ।
sitaṃ candrāyutaprakhyaṃ sudhāsārasamākulam ।।

netraṃ netreṣu saṃyojyaṃ sānusvāraṃ svadhātmakam ।
śivatīrthaṃ samuddiṣṭamidaṃ devena śambhunā ।।

anyathā toyasampūrṇaṃ jaḍaṃ saṃskāravarjitam ।
siddhimuktyorna taddheturna ca pāpāpanodanam ।।

tasmāttatra prayatnena śivīkaraṇamācaret ।
śivīkṛtaṃ tadātīrthaṃ snāyādāyāmasaṃyutaḥ ।।

śivabījamanusmṛtya śivatīrthasya madhyataḥ ।
majjanonmajjane kuryādvāmadevena vā sudhīḥ ।।

utplutyārkaṃ tataḥ paśyenmantrī tīrthātmakaṃ śivam ।
itthaṃ śrīvāmadevoktaṃ dhyātvā tīrthaṃ śivātmakam ।।

saṃyatāsurapāṃ madhye vaktumādhāya cetasā ।
japedaghaughavicchityai tārakaṃ duḥkhatārakam ।।

antarjalavidhiścāyaṃ mṛgendre sampracoditaḥ ।
prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kuryānmanovṛttiṃ nirodhayet ।।

manovṛttinirodhena pāpaṃ yanmānasaṃ haret ।
vācikānmucyate pāpātpāpaghnaṃ praṇavaṃ gṛṇan ।।

kṣayassāmānyapāpānāmevamanyo viśeṣagaḥ ।
viśeṣontarjale cāyaṃ kathitaśśrīparāgame ।।

utplutya parivartyādho vāsaḥ kṣālitahastakaḥ ।
tatobhiṣecanaṃ kṛtvā mantraiḥ kalaśamudrayā ।।

saṃhitā tāliko pāṇī pṛṣṭhāranmūdarauhitau ।
(24b)
suśliṣṭamūlāvaṅguṣṭhau tatkṛtyaṅguliparvagau ।।

ghaṭamudrāmudākhyātā snānādividhipūraṇī ।
śrīmṛgendre nigadito ghaṭamudrāvidhistvayam ।।

kṛtvā snānaṃ yathānyāyaṃ kaupīnaṃ parivartayet ।
dvādaśāṅgulavistīrṇaṃ dairghyāttadvikaraṃ bhavet ।।

aṣṭasūtrakṛtārajjuḥ kaupīnaṃ tryaṃśalambitam ।
kurvīta śuklavastroktaṃ kāṣāyaṃ vā dṛḍhaṃ vratī ।।

kaupīnalakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ vāmadevīyasaṅgrahe ।
mohājjalāvasiktena kaupīnena jalaṃ naraḥ ।।

yo dadyātpitarastasya tatprapātopajīvinaḥ ।
snānārthaṃ tu pravṛttasya devānāṃ nṛgaṇaissaha ।।

vāyavaścānugacchanti tṛṣārttāssalilārthinaḥ ।
nirāśāstasya gacchanti vastraniṣpīḍane kṛte ।।

tasmānna pīnuyedvastramakṛtvā pitṛtarpaṇam ।
devātitarpaṇai prokto niṣedhaḥ kṛtavāsase ।।

devenānalasiddhānte smṛtāvapi nidarśitaḥ ।
ārdravāsāstu yatkuryājjapahomapratigraham ।।

sarvaṃ tadrākṣasaṃ vidyādvahirjānu ca yatkṛtam ।
vastrāntarasya bhāve tu gīrvāṇapitṛtarpaṇe ।।

kurvīta nābhisalile iti smārtodito vidhiḥ ।
upaspṛśya yathānyāyyaṃ śivatīrthaṃ prakalpayet ।।

sa mantrapañcakaṃ mantrī pañcabhirbrahmabhiḥ kramāt ।
(25a)
ātmano hastamātraṃ tu caturdikṣu pratikṣaṇam ।।

īśānaṃ sphaṭikābhaṃ tu vinyasedātmanogrataḥ ।
prācyāṃ tatpuruṣaṃ dhyāye rocanākuṅkumāruṇam ।।

yāmyāyāṃ bahurūpaṃ tu bhinnāñjanacayopamam ।
sadyojātaṃ paścimāyāṃ viśuddharajataprabham ।।

vāmadevaṃ pradīśyāṃ tu dāḍimīpuṣpasannibham ।
svamudrābhirnyaseccaitāṃstīrthaṃ dhyātvābhivāñchitam ।।

digantarikṣabhaumaṃ tu tīrthaṃ dvividhameva hi ।
ākṛṣyāghoradevena samyagaṅkuśamudrayā ।।

saṃhāramudrayā vāpi nikṣipedātmanogrataḥ ।
samantapuṣpayā mantrī śivatīrthaṃ tu tadbhavet ।।

iti pauṣkaraśāstroktaṃ dhyātvā tu śivatīrthakam ।
hastau pādau tu saṃkṣālya śivī kuryātsvakāṃ tanum ।।

astraśuddhaḥ tatassthāne praṇavāsanasaṃsthitaḥ ।
nirmale suśubhe pūte sthale vā śādvalānvite ।।
jalaṃ jalena jvalayacchuddhayā tu karasthacā ।
agnikarmavidhānena mantrānsaṃnyasya tarpayet ।।

agrasavyāpasavyaistu devaviprapitṛnkramāt ।
svāhā namassvadhā ceti kramātteṣāṃ pratarpaṇam ।।

jalena tarpitā nnṛṇāṃ śāntiṃ kurvanti sarvadā ।
devatāḥ pitarassiddhā munayopi varapradāḥ ।।
(25b)
etaddhi tarpaṇaṃ kāryaṃ kṛtvā pādau jale sthale ।
tataśceduddharetpādaṃ tīrthaṃ yāti svapālayan ।।

idamuddeśataḥ proktaṃ vāmadevīyasaṅgrahe ।
pādau prokṣyāgrataḥ kaṇṭhyaṃ śivatīrthaṃ samāhṛtam ।।

sa tu saṃhitayā mūrdhābhiṣecyaḥ kumbhamudrayā ।
svarakṣārthamavicchinnaṃ * * vidhvaṃsanāya ca ।।

astrebhimantritaṃ toyaṃ digvidikṣu vinikṣipet ।
mantranyāse kṛte sandhyāṃ tato vandeta vaiṣṇavīm ।।

mārjanaṃ māghamarṣaṃ ca vidhāya punarācamet ।
svāhāntaśivamantreṇa kuśapuṣpākṣatānvitam ।।

śivāyārghāñjaliṃ datvā gāyatrīṃ śaktito japet ।
yathāsthitopavītena mantrā devāṃśca tarpayet ।।

namaskāreṇa saptarṣīrvaṣaṭkāreṇa mānavān ।
vaṣaṭkārasamāyogātbhūtādīnparvasatvibhiḥ ।।

kaṇṭhasthitopavītena tavyametattrayaṃ kramāt ।
dakṣāṃsasthopavītena svadhākārānvitānpitṝn ।।

tarjanyaṅguṣṭhamadhyena dakṣahastasya tarpayet ।
atra sarvatra hṛnmantrassādhāraḥ praṇavādana ।।

vaktrāṅgaissāvaraṇairyuktaṃ mūlaṃ vadanti mantratayā ।
vidyāvidyeśvarāssatattvatattveśvarāsmṛtā devā ।।

vasuvimbarudrasādhyāmaruto bhṛgavoṅgiroyuktāḥ ।
ādityaprathamā ityaṣṭavidhāṅkacana kīrtitā devāḥ ।।

(26a)
atrivasiṣṭhapulastyakratūrbharadvājasaṃyuktānviśvāmitrasametānmarīcinā saha ṛṣīnvidussapta ।। sanakasanandanasaṃhitaṃ
sanātanaṃ saha sanatkumāreṇa ।। kapilaṃ pañcaśikhaṃ ca smaranti ṛtusaptamānu * * * ।। paśupakṣimṛgasarīsṛpasāgarasaridacalavarṣādyāḥ ।।
saṅkhyānāmavihīnāssarve bhūtā vinirdiṣṭāḥ ।। devapitṛ pretapitṛ pratodatacca dvidhā mataḥ pitaraḥ ।। taṃ kavyavāhananalasamayamā aryamā caiva ।।
agniṣvāntābarhiṣadādyāpayutasomapānavādyāstyuḥ ।। pitaraḥ pitāmahaṣu tāḥ prapitāmaha mātaraścaika ।। mātāmahāḥ praśabdapravṛddhapūrvāsta eva tathā ।।
sodaramātulajāyāmitropādhyāyagurvādyāḥ ।। anye ca jñātryādyā atraivāntaḥ prayoktavyāḥ  ete mantrā devāmunimanujabhūtapitara iti tarpyāḥ ।।

ācamya datvā sūryāya samantramudakāñjalim ।
mūlamantrajapitvāya devāya vinivedayet ।।

tīrthaṃ svahṛdi vinyasya snānavastraṃ prapīḍya ca ।
prakṣālayetri: karau mṛdbhiriti sthānavidhirbahiḥ ।।

devāditarpaṇaṃ proktaṃ śrīviśveṣaśupaddhatau ।
tatastu śītalenātha gṛhe snāyātsuvāriṇā ।।

kintu tatra viśeṣoyaṃ vidhisnānebhigīyate ।
mṛdaṃ prakṣāpya kalaśe bhāvyosau śivatīrthavat ।।
(26b)
tantrastamantrassaṃhārya ityuktaṃ kiraṇāya me ।
āpo hiṣṭhāmayaṃ snānaṃ prājāpatyamudāhṛtam ।।

mantrasnānaṃ brahmabhistu śivasaṃhitayāpi vā ।
navāṣṭapañcabhi: kumbhaiśśivatīrthasvakalpitaiḥ ।।

snānaṃ gṛhepi nirvartya liptāyāṃ tu vidīkṣitaiḥ ।
gṛhasnānavidhiścoktaḥ pauṣkare pārameśvare ।

āpassvabhāvato medhyakampānaṃ vahnisaṃyutāḥ ।
tena santaḥ pra{śa}sanisnānapuṣpena vāriṇā ।।

uṣṇāmbhasāpi snapanaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśanmatacoditam ।
vāruṇaṃ snānamevaṃ tu sādhakānāmudāhṛtam ।।

yasminkṛte bhavacchuddhissarvapāpamahābhayāt ।
manaśśuddhikaraṃ snānaṃ kalidussvapnanāśanam ।।

kṛtyākhārkhodavetālamalājīrṇavināśanam ।
daurmanasyaṃ manobuddhyarviṣādaharaṇaṃ tathā ।।

aṅgānāṃ puṣṭidākāntaṃ kāmabhogasukhapradam ।
na snāne rahitā loke dharmakarmārthapūjitāḥ ।।

bhavanti sādhakendrāśca evamāha maheśvaraḥ ।
snātasya kāyikaṃ pāpaṃ sadya eva praṇaśyati ।।

ṛṣayo devagandharvā nityaṃ snānaparāyaṇāḥ ।
lakṣmīvivṛddhissnātasya devāstiṣṭhanti sammukhāḥ ।।

grahaścaivābhinandanti snātamātraṃ naraṃ sadā ।
niścalatvaṃ mano yāti kṣamā sadyaḥ prajāyate ।।
(27a)
{devānāṃ * la dānena śrī * * * * cākṣayā ।
sarvayajñasukhaṃ snānaṃ yajñe rudreṇa bhāṣitam ।।

sarvayajñasamaṃ puṇyaṃ nityaṃ snānātprajāyate ।}
dine dinepi yaḥ kaścinnarassnānabahiṣkṛtaḥ ।।

snātvāṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ pakṣasnānātphalaṃ bhavet ।
yopi snānarato bhaktyā pūrṇimācandradarśane ।।

tasyānantaphalā nadyo bhavantīti kimadbhutam ।
uṣṇāmbusnapanaṃ puṇyaṃ tacchītaddviguṇaṃ bhavet ।।

nairantaryeṇa saptāhātsnānaśabdaśatārjitam ।
dahatyapuṇyasaṅghātaṃ tūlakāśisikānalaḥ ।।

śrīmatyaba?lasiddhānte jalasnānasya yatphalam ।
uktaṃ parāgame cātha yathā tadupadiśyate ।।

varuṇo mṛtarūpānmāmṛtayuktaṃ jalaṃ śuci ।
tatsnāto mṛtavadbhūtvā tiṣṭhatyāmṛtavartmani ।।

snāne devārpane home bhojane yāgayogayoḥ ।
āvaśyake jape dhīrassadā vācaṃyamo bhavet ।।

।। iti jalasnānavidhiḥ ।।

athavā bhasmanā snāyādvārisnānaṃ vivarjayet ।
śivādyā devatāssarvāḥ snānaṃ kurvanti bhasmanā ।।

bhasmasnānaṃ jalasnānādasaṅkhyeyaguṇairyutam ।
sarvayajñeṣu yatpuṇyaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu yatphalam ।।

tatphalaṃ labhate nityaṃ bhasmasnānānnasaṃśayaḥ ।
mahāpātakasaṃyukto yukto vā sarvapātakaiḥ ।।

bhasmasnānaṃ niśeveta mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ ।
bhasmānniṣṭhā pratiṣṭhasya divasenāpi yatphalam ।।

na tacchakyaṃ gṛhasthena prāptuṃ kratuśatairapi ।
(27b)
tasmādvāruṇamutsṛjya snānamātreyamācaret ।।

śivabhasmeti vikhyātaṃ yatra tiṣṭhati pūjitam ।
na tatra caurānāpyagnirna rogā na tu jaṅgamāḥ ।।

na ca vighnopasarpāssyunna piśācā na rākṣasāḥ ।
na rājabhayapīḍā ca na cānyadaśubhaṃ bhavet ।।

tāḍito bhasmanānena grahassadyaḥ praṇaśyati ।
jvaraiścāturthikādyaiśca sadya eva vimucyate ।।

snāyeta bhasmanā mantrī kāle kālāntareṣu ca ।
prātarmadhyandine caiva tṛtīyasavane tathā ।।

kāla eṣa samuddiṣṭo niśāmadhyaścaturthakaḥ ।
vātamūtrapurīṣāṇāmutsarge snānamācaret ।।

kālānāmantaraṃ tacca vi * taṃ śūlapāṇinā ।
triṣkālaṃ vāriṇā snānātkapharogaḥ prajāyate ।।

śirorogoṅgabhaṅgaśca tasmāttatparivarjayet ।
vāriṇā tu sakṛtsnātvā tasmātsnānaṃ samācaret ।।

tannityaṃ sevyamānaṃ tu sarvarogapraṇāśanam ।
svapnopaghātadussaṅge vāte caivopaghātake ।।

vidhinā vāriṇā snātvā snānamāgneyamācaret ।
rudrāṇāṃ ca gaṇānāṃ ca snānametatsadā priyam ।।

vidyeśvarāṇāmiṣṭaṃ ca śivo vā vallabhaṃ sadā ।
nityaṃ tatsevyamācāryaissādhakaiśca mahānmatiḥ ।।

brahmakṣattriyavicchūdraissevanīyaṃ vratasthitaiḥ ।
(28a)
bhasmāśrayo yato vahnistasminneva layaṃ gataḥ ।।

bhasmasnānena tejatvaṃ vahnivaddṛśyate naghaḥ ।
parāgame pauṣkare ca praśasābhasunāḥ kṛtā ।।

mantrāgnitejasā dagdhaṃ śivayā yatra dṛśyate ।
kuto vā yatra deveśastejorāśissadāśivaḥ ।।

tadbhasmavidhinā dadyādyadāsau na visarjitaḥ ।
athavā bahurūpeṇa śivāgnerulmukaṃ purā ।।

prakṣipedbhasmamadhye tu saṃhitāvartitutuvā ।
śivabhasmabhavedevaṃ snānārthamupayojayet ।।

alābhādvidhinā mantrī sādhayedbhūtimuttamām ।
tayā snānaṃ prakartavyaṃ vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ।।

ādāya gomayaṃ śuṣkaṃ śuddhaṃ doṣavivarjitam ।
praśastamindhanaṃ vātha kṣīravṛkṣādisambhavam ।।

evaṃ bhasmavidhiḥ proktau  vāmadevīyasaṅgrahe ।
bhasmanātha yatissnāyāttadvanyairgomayopalaiḥ ।।

palāśacandanādyairvā kāsthaiḥ pracchambarāhṛtaiḥ ।
vāmadevacitaighoradīpitaiḥ puṣpavījitai: ।।

varmaṇā veṣṭitairastraīkṣitaiśśucibhūtale ।
janitaṃ śāṅkaraṃ bhasma gṛhītveśena mantrayet ।।

śivenāṅgavatācena hṛdāhṛtyagni dhāpayet ।
sadādhārestavamartyāṃ kṛtarakṣe dvijottama ।।

tenottamāṅgavaktrāntadehajaṅghādyanukramāt ।
(28b)
taddhūlayecca sarvāṅgaṃ pāṇipādetaratsudhīḥ ।।

pratyaṅgakarmahastasya śuddhirastreṇa pūrvavat ।
mantrābhiṣekasandhyādi sūryopasthānavāritam ।।

mṛgendreleśataccettaṃ bhasmasnānamudīritam ।
astrābhimantritaṃ bhasma gṛhītvā tadyathāvidhi ।।

malasnānaṃ prakartavyaṃ bhāvitenākaranmanāḥ ।
parivṛtya tato vā sa:sandhyāṃ prāgiva vandayet ।।

vidhisnānaṃ prakurvīta bhairavīśamanusmaran ।
sandhyāyā vandanaṃ kāryaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ varānane ।।

sa vidhisnānakaṃ cetthaṃ proktaṃ svacchandaśāsane ।
raudraṃ tejaḥ paraṃ bhasma dhyātvā snāyātkramāditaḥ ।।

anye tripuṇḍrakā kuryu: kṣatriyādyāsstriyopi vā ।
vakṣagrīvāssabāhūruhṛdayaṃ pārśvameva ca ।।

pṛṣṭhaṃ kaṭyūrujaṅghe ca guhya uddhūlayetkramāt ।
īśādita: kramātsnāyātkalābhivā vicakṣaṇaḥ ।।

iti vidyā purāṇepi proktaṃ candrārdhamaulinā ।
īśena pañcadhā bhasma kṣipecchirasi sādhakaḥ ।।

vaktraṃ caturdhā vaktreṇa bhasmanoddhūlayedbudhaḥ ।
bahurūpeṇa coddhūlya hṛdayaṃ cāṣṭadhā tathā ।।

guhyaṃ vai vāmadevena trayodaśavibhedataḥ ।
uddhūlayettatau pādau sadyojātena cāṣṭadhā ।।

ekaikaṃ sthānanirdeśassaṅgraheṇa kṛto mayā ।
sarvāṅgaddhūlanaṃ caiva nihitaṃ śivaśāsane ।।
(29a)
caturṇāmapi varṇānāṃ naiṣṭhikānāṃ virāgiṇām।
bhasmanoddhūlanaṃ śastaṃ śrutaśīlakriyā{'}vatām ।।

athavā mantraviddhīrastripuṇḍraṃ śivabhasmanā ।
kuryādyatnena mantrajño vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ।।

tarjanīmadhyamānāmākhyāṅgulībhirdvayostu tat ।
sthitamāvartayenmantrī bhasmavāriyutaṃ sudhīḥ ।।

kuryāttripuṇḍramalike vidhinā tadanantaram ।
kaṇṭhe hṛdi śikhāyāṃ ca śirogrīve ca karṇayoḥ ।।

skandhayonnābhikaṭyestu sphijau tūruyuge ।
vāmadakṣiṇayo: kuryājjaṅghayorubhayostathā ।।

vidhinānena mantrajñassatataṃ bhūtivardhanam ।
tripuṇḍraṃ bhasmanā proktaṃ vāmadevīyasaṅgrahe ।।

yadagamyaṃ dakṣiṇasya tadvāmena tu pāṇinā ।
samyaguddhūlayedaṅgaṃ spṛṣṭvānyonyaṃ karāvubhau ।।

śuddhyantarvāsa āvṛtya pāṇī prakṣālayenmṛdā  ।
salilena ca pūtena pādau mṛdbhiśca saptabhiḥ ।।

śrīmanmataṅgaśāstrepi sopapattikamīritam ।।
।। iti bhasmasnānavidhiḥ ।।

pārthivaṃ tu mṛdāsnānaṃ jalasnānaṃ yathoditam ।
āgneyaṃ bhasmanā snānaṃ vidhistasya nigadyate ।।

kapilāgomayaṃ prāpya dahecchivahṛdā budhaḥ ।
abhimantrya yathānyāyaṃ sakṛdāvartyasaṃhitām ।।

mukhe mūrdhni ca dātavyaṃ hṛdi nābhau krameṇa ca ।
(29b)
guhye jānvoḥ pādayośca gāyatryā tu yathākramam ।।

tataḥ pāśupatāstreṇa dikṣu bhasma vinikṣipet ।
tena vighnāḥ palāyanti diśodaśa bhayārtitāḥ ।।

āgneyamaparaṃ snānaṃ dhyāyeddehaṃ sujājvalam ।
vāyavyaṃ kathitaṃ snānaṃ prāṇāyāmairyathoditaiḥ ।।

pañcamaṃ nābhasaṃ snānaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam ।
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ śarmānaṃ ca jagatsmaret ।।

iti snānatrayaṃ proktaṃ hāṭakeśvaraśāsane ।
gaṅgārkatanayāsindhusarasvatyādisandhayaḥ ।।

spṛṣṭāṃ matkṣetrajāṃ vāpi mṛdaṃ saṃhitayā labhet ।
tayā mūrddhādigātreṣu kuryāccaivaṃ tripuṇḍrakān ।।

pārthivaṃ ceti kathitaṃ mṛgendrottaraśāsane ।।

।। iti pārthivasnānavidhiḥ ।।

tadbhūtaṃ yadrajogobhyo mātariśvavimiśritam ।
tena snātaḥ pavitrāṅgo viśuddho vāyuvadbhavet ।।

śrīmanparāgame cetthaṃ mārutaṃ snānamucyate ।

।। iti vāyavyasnānam ।।

puṇyaṃ sārkātapāṃ vṛṣṭiṃ vibhṛyācchivamuccaran ।
prāknnādāni brajetsapta divya * tacchivāmbhasā ।।

śrīmanmṛgendre kathitaṃ vāmadevīyamucyate ।
anabhravṛṣṭisaṃpātā * * ndramījapādbhavet ।।

ūrddhvabāhurniruddhākṣaḥ prāṅmukhassnānatatparaḥ ।
padasaptakamāvartyetyuktaṃ tat sa viśeṣakam ।।
(30a)
।। iti divyasnānam ।। ।।

śucimurddhāguroḥ prādaubhibhṛyācchivamuccaran ।
gauravaṃ tanmanovṛttyā mantramāvilayāt smaret ।।

tatsparśavāriṇā śākte nāplave tadvimānasam ।
iti snānadvayamidaṃ mṛgendrasaṃprakāśitam ।।

।। iti gauravamānase snāne ।।

māntraṃ mantraiśśirassekānkāryaṃ tadghaṭamudrayā ।
iti proktaṃ maheśena māntraṃ snānaṃ parāgame ।।

।। iti māntraṃ snānam ।।

mānasaṃ manasā kāryaṃ tacca sāvartakaṃ matam ।
sarvamantrādimantreṇa prāṇāyāmayutena ca ।।

iti svāyambhuve śāstre mānasaṃ snānamīritam ।

।। iti mānasasnānam ।।

ityuktamaudakaṃ snānamatha māntraṃ nibodhase ।
toyābhāve tu yatkuryāddurge kāleyaśītale ।।

gamane kṣiprasiddhau vā gurukārye svatantratam ।
prāptāyamativiprendra niśābhāge dinasya tu ।।

prakṣālya pādāvācamya proddhṛtena tu vāriṇā ।
sthānaṃ daśadiśaḥ prāgvatsaṃsadhyopaviśettataḥ ।।

astraṃ hastatale yojya kramānnyāsaṃ tatastanau ।
mūlamantrāditaḥ kuryātsarvaṃ mantragaṇena ca ।।

kevalādudakasnānātsaṃskāraparivarjitāt ।
prabhāsādiṣu tīrtheṣu yatphalaṃ snātakasya tu ।।

jñeyaṃ śataguṇaṃ tasmānmantrasnānasya nārada ।
dhyānasnānamato vakṣye dvābhyāmuparigaṃ ca yat ।।

khasthitaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ mantramūrtiṃ vibhuṃ smaret ।
(30b)
tatpādodakajāṃ dhārāṃ nipatantīṃ svamūrdhani ।।

cintayedbrahmarandhreṇa praviśantīṃ svakāṃ tanum ।
tayā saṃkṣālayetsarvamantardehagataṃ malam ।।

tatkṣaṇādvirajā mantrī jāyate sphaṭikopamaḥ ।
idaṃ snānavarānmantrānsnānācchata guṇaṃ smṛtam ।।

tasmādekatamaṃ kuryātsnānaṃ śuddhāsamanvitam ।
snānapūrvāḥkriyāssarvā yatassamyakphalapradāḥ ।।

tasmātsnānaṃ purā kuryādya icchetsiddhimāpnuyāt ।
anena nirmalo jantu: kṛtamātreṇa jāyate ।।

dvijendra nirvivekātsā kiṃ punaryaḥ kriyānvitā ।
etatsnānaṃ śrījayāyāṃ vaiṣṇave bahudhoditam ।।

hṛtpuṇḍarīkamadhye vā kadambakusumākṛtim ।
rātrīmadhyagatāṃ tāṃ ca śivenduhradagāminīm ।।

saṃyamyacendriyagrāmaṃ caitanyalakṣasaṃsthitam ।
kṛtvā smaretsravantīṃ tāṃ sātsopari viśuddhaye ।।

kālāgnitejasā caiva pāśajālaṃ dahedbhṛśam ।
dvidhā cādhyātmikahyevaṃ snānamuktaṃ samāsataḥ ।।

eṣāmekatamaṃ kṛtvā sakalīkṛtya sādhakaḥ ।
śivārcanaṃ prakurvīta na tvasnātaḥ kadācana ।।

vidyāpurāṇepyuditaṃ snānamādhyātmikamidam ।।

।। iti mantrasnāne ।।

।। ityāgamoddhṛte nityakarmaṇi snānaprakaraṇam ।।

athātassaṃpravakṣyāmi liṅgārcanavidhiṃ param ।
(31a)
liṅgamūrtidharaṃ śambhuṃ vyaktāvyaktobhayākṛtim ।।

svarapratiṣṭhite liṅge manujairvā prakalpitam ।
yajettanmadhyagaṃ nāthaṃ mantramūrtiṃ parāparam ।।

yeneṣṭena bhavediṣṭassarvodhvāyaṃ śivāntagaḥ ।
tasmāttameva deveśaṃ liṅgamūrtigataṃ yajet ।।

liṅgasya kīrtanaṃ puṇyaṃ darśanaṃ pāpanāśanam ।
ye spṛśasti śivaṃ yānti ye yajantyapunarbhavāḥ ।।

te syurnarā mahāpuṇyā na kālasya vaśaṃ gatāḥ ।
liṅge devā mahādevāssākṣānnityaṃ vyavasthitaḥ ।।

lokānugrahakṛdyasmāttasmālliṅgaṃ prapūjayet ।
yo na sampūjayenmohālliṅgaṃ suragaṇārcitam ।।

nāsau svargasya mokṣasya na ca rājyasya bhājanam ।
yastu sampūjayedbhaktyā liṅgaṃ tribhuvaneśvaram ।।

sasvargamokṣarājyānāṃ kṣipraṃ bhavati bhāvanam ।
liṅgatīrthāni sarvāṇi liṅge sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ।।

liṅge siddhaḥ parā muktiḥ pūjanātsukhamaihikam ।
liṅgahīnā na siddhyanti na ca yānti paraṃ padam ।।

tasmātpuṣpaiḥ phalairbhaktyā toyairmantrapavitritaiḥ ।
yathāśaktyā tu yaṣṭavyo liṅgastho vidhinā śivaḥ ।।

uktaṃ śrīvāmadevena liṅgamāhātmyamuttamam ।
ye siddhā hyāgame loke svayambhūtā maheśvarāḥ ।।

te rudrā bhuvanādhārāstattvatattve svarāḥ priye ।
pārthivādisamākhyātā mūrtibhūtāstu kāraṇe ।।

anugrahārthaṃ bhūtānāṃ sthāvaratvaṃ gatāstu te ।
(31b)
bāṇāstathā svayambhūtāssaṃsiddhāśca purāgame ।।

siddhairvidyādharairyakṣairmunidevāsuroragaiḥ ।
sthāpitāstepi tattulyā na tu saṃsthāṃ tyajanti te ।।

aghorīpañcaśatake dṛṣṭaṃ liṅgāvatāraṇam ।।

।। iti liṅgamāhātmyam ।।

liṅgamūrte tathā padme bahnāvādityamaṇḍale ।
svadehe cāntarikṣe vā pūjayetparameśvaram ।।

ātmaśuddhisthānaśuddhirdravyaśuddhistathaiva ca ।
caturthī liṅgaśuddhistu mantraśuddhistu pañcamī ।।

yo vetti pañcadhā śuddhiṃ sa pūjāṃ kartumarhati ।
snānaṃ pañcavidhā proktamātmaśuddhistu sā smṛtā ।।

digbandhena mahāstreṇa sthānaśuddhirudāhṛtā ।
apāmātrāmbhasā kuryāddra?vyāṇāṃ prokṣaṇaṃ sadā ।।

dravyaśuddhiriyaṃ proktā yāgahomādisiddhidā ।
prāsādapīṭhaliṅgasthā ye tu vighnā mahābalāḥ ।।

tehamastrairmahāghoraistālāpūrvairnivāraṇam ।
liṅgaśuddhiriyaṃ proktā sādhakānāṃ hitābahā ।।

praṇavādinamonte tu mantraśuddhiḥ prakīrtitā ।
śuddhiḥ pañcavidhā proktā hāṭakeśvaraśāsane ।।

jñātvaivaṃ sādhakaḥ kṣipramatīhaṃ siddhimāvahet ।।

।। iti pañcadhā śuddhiḥ ।।

ādāya suśubhaṃ pātraṃ suvarṇarajatādikam ।
mṛṇmayaṃ ca jalaṃ vāpi pātraṃ padmapalāśayoḥ ।।
(32a)
ebhya ekatamaṃ prāpya yathālābhena daiśikaḥ ।
kṣālayedastratoyaṃ ca candanādyaistu lepayet ।।

arghaṃ saṃyojayettatra śāstroktamastrasaṃskṛtam ।
āpaḥkṣīraṃ kuśāgrāṇi * * lāssumanāstilāḥ ।।

yavāśca sarṣapāścaiva marghoṣṭāṅgaḥ prakīrttitaḥ ।
astreṇa dīpayedarghaṃ kavacena tu mārjayet ।।

āpyāyanaṃ tataḥ kuryānmūlamantreṇa mantravit ।
śaktiṃ saṃyojayettatra satataṃ dharmasaṃyutam ।।

jñānaṃ vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ māyāvidyāsamanvitam ।
īśānaṃ ca tadūrdhve tu puruṣaṃ pūrvamantragam ।।

aghoraṃ dakṣiṇe mantre vāmadevaṃ tathottare ।
paścime tu dalāṃśe tu sadyo devaṃ niyojayet ।।

hṛdayaṃ pāvake koṇe tathaiśāne śirassmaret ।
śikhāyojya tu nainṛtyāṃ mārute kavacaṃ smaret ।।

agradeśe smarettatra mastaṃ ca parito viduḥ ।
dhenumudrāṃ tato baddhvā mūlamantraṃ samuccaret ।।

vyāpakaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ pīyūṣate ca kalpayet ।
sitāścandrapratīkāśāssudhāsārairivonmayaḥ ।।

arghapātraisthitā mantrā dravyāṇi prokṣayettataḥ ।
astreṇa tāḍayettāni varmamantreṇa mārjayet ।।

plāvayenmūlamantreṇa prokṣayecca śivāmbhasā ।
tadā tānyamalāni syuśśivarūpāṇi pūjane ।।

siddhimuktipradāni syuryuktāni śivatejasā ।
(32b)
te hi mokṣaṃ gamiṣyanti māyātītaṃ nirañjanam ।।

।। ityarghapātravidhiḥ ।।

yāvattiṣṭhati mantrātmā liṅge vā sthaṇḍile cale ।
tāvatsarvāṇi dravyāṇi na nirmālyāni tāni vai ।।

devadravyaṃ smṛtaṃ hyetanna deyaṃ yasya kasyacit ।
upekṣā naiva kartavyā nātmano hyupayojayet ।।

arghapātraṃ samādāya namaskṛtya maheśvaram ।
devaṃ devīṃ gaṇeśānāṃ tathā caiva gurutrayam ।।

ātmamūrtidharaṃ paścācchivaṃ puṣpaiḥ prapūjayet ।
ādityaṃ ca tatobhyarcya triśūlaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ tathā ।।

prabhāmaṇḍalake pūjyatvarghapuṣpādivistaraiḥ ।
prāsāde vipraṣaḥ paścādastrajaptā vinikṣipet ।।

sthalāye saṃsthitā (sta)tra bhūtadehā mahāgaṇāḥ ।
tūrṇamutsāritāstābhirvi * * rastayo gataḥ ।।

yāgavighnakarā raudrāḥ palāyantevatāḍitāḥ ।
vāmadevīyametacca kṛtvā dvārādhipānyajet ।।

āśāmātṛgaṇaṃ lakṣmīṃ nandigaṅge prapūjayet ।
mahākālaṃ ca yamunāṃ dehalīṃ pūjayettataḥ ।।

vighnaproccāṭanaṃ kṛtvā digbandhaṃ kavacāstataḥ ।
svāsanārthaṃ prakalpyeta śaktimādhārikāṃ śubhām ।।

upaviśya tata: kuryātprāṇāyāmamanukramāt ।
dhāraṇāmārabhettatra yugapacchoṣaṇādibhiḥ ।।

ṣāṭkośikaṃ tu malinaṃ nirdagdhaṃ tatra bhāvayet ।
(33a)
vijñānaṃ kevalaṃ tatra śūnyaṃ sarvagataṃ smaret ।।

{nāhamasti na cānyosti dhyeyaṃ cātra na vidyate ।
ānandapadasaṃlīnaṃ manassamarasīgatam ।।

paścādādhāraśaktisthaṃ svāsanaṃ paribhāvayet ।
vijñānaṃ kevalaṃ tatra śūnyaṃ sarvagataṃ smaret ।।}

dhātrīpayorṇavaṃ padmaṃ candrabimbārdhabhāsitam ।
kacchātkalākalaporūpedhūṣeṇa tu secayet ।।

mūrtibhūtaṃ tritattvaṃ tu mūlenaiva prakalpayet ।
tatoṅgāni karābhyāṃ tu śarīre kalpayetpunaḥ ।।

māntraṃ caivābhimānaṃ tu cintayeddhyānayogataḥ ।
mudrāṃ caivāmṛtāṃ baddhvā padmamudrāmathāpivā ।।

dhyāyedātmani deveśaṃ candrakoṭisamaprabham ।
sarvaśvetopacāreṇa pūjitaṃ tamanusmaret ।।

atha cintāmaṇiprakhyaṃ bhāvabhedena saṃsmaret ।
saumyaṃ raudraṃ tathā bhīmaṃ vikṛtiṃ bhāvabhedataḥ ।।

sadāśivaṃ bhairavaṃ ca tumburuṃ vīranāyakam ।
evaṃ dhyātvā yajeddevaṃ mānasaiḥ kusumaiśśubhaiḥ ।।

hṛtpadme sarvasiddhyarthaṃ paścādbāhye prapūjayet ।
mānasai: kusumairyārcā sātvikī sā sthirā matā ।।

anirmālyā parāśuddhā mokṣadā siddhidā śubhā ।
ādāveva prakartavyaṃ paścāddravyaissuvistaraiḥ ।।

svagṛhe devatāgāre saṅgame girimūrdhani ।
sapraśastai tu bhūbhāge padmaṣaṇḍe suśobhane ।।

mṛtyuñjaye iti proktaṃ dvārārcādehaśodhanam ।
mūrtyantamāsanaṃ dhyānaṃ śāntyādau yatkramātpunaḥ ।।

tatrāntaretyataścitya tadidānīṃ nigadyate ।
tato dvārasya bāhye tu yāgasaṃrakṣakāḥ parāḥ ।।
(33b)
mātaro daśadiksaṃsthāḥ pūjanīyāśśubhapradāḥ ।
umā durgā bhadrakālī svastissvāhā śubhaṅkarī ।।

śrīśca gaurī lokadhātrī vāgīśī daśamī smṛtā ।
iti diṅmadhuraṃ hyetāśśrīkaṇṭhyāṃ hi śivena tu ।।

yugapadvā pṛthagvāpi pūjyassaṃkṣepavistaraiḥ ।
diṅmātṛbhyo namaskṛtvā dvāraṃ saṃprokṣya yatnataḥ ।।

śivāmbhasāstu mantreṇa vighnaproccāṭanaṃ bhavet ।
dvāraśākhordhato devaṃ gaṇeśaṃ ca śriyaṃ tathā ।।

sampūjya gandhapuṣpādyairdhūpādibhiranukramāt ।
arghapādyopahāraiśca tato dvārasya cottare ।।

nandigaṅge samabhyarcya mahākālaṃ ca dakṣiṇe ।
kālindaṃ caiva sampūjyaṃ dehalīṃ pūjayedadhaḥ ।।

bhairavāstraṃ samuccārya puṣpaṃ saṅgṛhya bhāvitaḥ ।
saptābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā jvaladvahniśikhākulam ।।

nārācāstraprayogeṇa prakṣipedgṛhamadhyataḥ ।
uttānaṃ tu karaṃ kṛtvā tisroṅgulyaḥ prasārayet ।।

tarjanyaṅguṣṭhasaṃyogāccālayeta muhurmuhuḥ ।।
।। ityanantavijaye ।।

tato rakṣārthamastraṃ ca daśadikṣu vinikṣipet ।
huṃ phaṭkāraprayogena tālāśabdaṃ vidhāya ca ।।

pāṣṇyādhohastasaṃyogādvighnaproccāṭanāya tu ।
pārṣyābhūmigatānhanyāttālayā cāntarikṣagān ।।
(34a)
astrairdivyānviśodhyaiva yāgaharmyaṃ viśettataḥ ।।

।। iti dvārapūjā ।।

madhye sampūjya brahmāṇaṃ gandhaiḥ puṣpairanukramāt ।
dakṣiṇāyāṃ tato mūrtau praṇavāsanasaṃsthitaḥ ।।

upaviśyāsanaṃ baddhvāsvabhyastaṃ yatpurāsthitam ।
gandhadigdhau karau kṛtvā hyastreṇa pariśodhayet ।।

kavacenāvaguṇṭhau tau plāvayedamṛtena tu ।
parāṃ śaktiṃ tu saṃkṣobhya tatonantaṃ prakalpayet ।।

mūrtiṃ nyasyānuvaktrāṇi svacchandaṃ parikalpayet ।
aṅguṣṭhādikaniṣṭhāntaṃ vinyasedaṅgapañcakam ।।

bhairavānapi saṃkalpya paraṃ tattvamanusmaran ।
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kāryaṃ dehasaṃśuddhikāraṇam ।।

aśuddhassvamarudreśyaśśuddhenāpūrayettanum ।
kumbhakaṃ recakaṃ kṛtvā vyomnātmānaṃ nidhāpayet ।।

khadyotakanibhaṃ sūkṣmaṃ karaṇaistu vivarjitam ।
kāryeṇaiva vihīnaṃ * māyāpradhvastagocaram ।।

śivīkāryastadātmā vai yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu ।
paraṃ bhāvaṃ tu saṅgṛhya tataścoṣyā tanuḥ priye ।।

saṃhāreṇa yabhinnena rudrabījayutena ca ।
tenaiva dahanaṃ kāryaṃ mūrdhvadhogniyutena tu ।।

adhoviṣṇusamāyukte vāyuvarṇassavindukaḥ ।
tatpūyanakaro hyeṣa plāvane vāruṇasmṛtaḥ ।।

bindumastakasaṃbhinnaḥ ।।
iti dhāraṇāmantrāḥ śaktinyāsastato bhavet ।
(34b)
ānayettaṃ yathānītaṃ plāvayedamṛte(na) tu ।।

malapradhvastacaitanyaṃ kalāvidyāsamāśritam ।
rāgeṇa rañjitātmānaṃ kālena kalitaṃ tatra ।।

niyatyāyamitaṃ bhūyaḥ pu * * * pa bṛṃhitam ।
pradhānāśayamāpannaṃ guṇatrayasamanvitam ।।

buddhitattvaṃ samāsīnamahaṅkārasamanvitam ।
manasā buddhikarmākṣaistanmātraissthalabhūtakaiḥ ।।

praṇavena tu sarvaṃ taccharīrotpattikārakam ।
nyasetkrameṇa deveśi triṃśadekaṃ ca saṅkhyayā ।।

ṣaṭtattvījñātavyātra varānane ।
pradhānāvaniparyantaṃ śarīraṃ vinirmitam ।
caturviṃśatitattvāni caitanyarahitāni tu ।।

draṣṭavyāni varārohe puruṣādhiṣṭhitāni tu ।
sa cetanāni sarvāṇi jñātavyāni sadaiva tu ।।

pañcaviṃśakametaddhi prākṛtaṃ samudāhṛtam ।
tato mūrtiṃ nyaseddevi pūrvatramu * lakṣitam ।।

itthaṃ samāsataḥ proktaṃ svacchande bhairaveṇa tu ।
baddhvāsanaṃ yathābhīṣṭaṃ svastikaṃ padmameva ca ।।

ardhacandraṃ {ca} vīrākhyaṃ yogapaṭṭaṃ prasāritam ।
paryaṅkaṃ ca yathāsaṃsthamāsanāṣṭakamucyate ।।

dviguṇaṃ pṛṣṭhato nītvā vāmapādasya dakṣiṇam ।
tiryaktaṃ dviguṇaṃ kṛtvā jaṅgālagnaṃ {tu} svastikam ।।

nyaseddviguṇamanyonyaṃ pādamekaṃ tathetaram ।
(35a)
padmamevaṃ samākhyātaṃmardhacandraṃ śṛṇuṣva tat ।।

tadvatpādadvayaṃ kṛtvā svatalānyonyasaṃsthitam ।
ardhacandraṃ  * vedevaṃ vīrākhyaṃ yacchṛṇuṣva tat ।।

dviguṇaṃ pātitaṃ vāmaṃ pādaṃ dakṣi * murddhvataḥ ।
kṛtvānyonyatalasthaṃ tu vīraṃ paṭvāmathocyate ।।

kṛtvādau dviguṇo * dau tiryagūrdhvaṃ manākṣiga ।
nyasetpāṇī yathāpaṭṭaṃ śitaśliṣṭāṅgulī * kau ।।

pādadvayāṃ prasāryātha mukhaṃ mukhaprasāritam ।
pṛṣṭhato hyu * dhānasthaṃ prasārya caraṇadvayam ।।

yathāsaṃsthaṃ yathāsthitiḥ ।
eṣā * kata maṃ baddhvā vitanyorassthalaṃ samam ।
haste hastatalaṃ kṛtvā vāmahastasya * kṣiṇam ।।

tatsvakīyāṅkagaṃ vāryaṃ dīrghaṃ vāmakaraṃ karam ।
vāma(jā)nusamāyuktaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ cāṅkagaṃ nyaset ।।

añjaliṃ vā parātṛptissa - dṛṣṭissamānanaḥ ।
anāṣṭakametaddhi kiraṇāyāṃ pracoditam ।।

।। āsanāṣṭakam ।।

baddhvāsanaṃ kramātkuryātkaradehādiśodhanam ।
padmāsane sthitassthairyāttannośśuddhiṃ samācaret ।।

yatassā prākṛtai- vairaśudddhai: karmabhiryuktā ।
nirdahettena tāṃ sarvā mārṣisyāntargataṃ dhana ।।

taddhanaṃ vartate jīvassacānilasamāśrayaḥ ।
gṛhṇīyātpūrakennaiva kumbhakena sthirīkṛtam ।।
(35b)
recakena samutkṣiptaṃ sthāpayecchaktidhāmani ।
kriyeyaṃ kalpitā tena viśuddhatanuyogataḥ ।।

tayā saṃśuddhayeśuddhaṃ śuddhamantraphalaṃ labhet ।
tena tatsthāpayejjīvaṃ śaktisthāne malātmake ।।

tacchūnyaṃ tadvapuḥ kuryānnirdahettena kāṣṭhavat ।
śastreṇāgninibhenedaṃ nirdagdhaṃ bhasmavartitam ।।

kṣālitaṃ vāriṇā bhasma nirālambaṃ tamomayam ।
kṛtvā śaktyāśrayaṃ jīvaṃ śuddhamaśmakaṇopamam ।।

śrīmatparāgame ce tumupapattirnirūpitā ।
tatrādāyāstramantreṇa kālānalasamatviṣā ।।

aṅguṣṭhāgrāttanuṃ dagddhvā sa bāhyābhyantaraṃ smaret ।
vikīryamāṇatadbhasma dhyātvā kavacavāyunā ।।

śive bindusamākāramātmānamanucintayet ।
śrīpūrvaśāsane proktaṃ dehadāhādilakṣaṇam ।।

aśuddhirvāmanārūpā pa * vamanusarpiṇī ।
sarvadṛkkriyayośśaktiniruddhā parvatastayā ।।

tasyā vicchitiheturvai dehedāhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ।
avidyā vāsanā kutsā na tyajanti yathā paśum ।।

udānta: karaṇe śuddherupāyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ।
yathā hemne viśuddhatvaprāptiheto vidhīyate ।।

tāḍanacchedadāhādirasavedho hi kenacit ।
(36a)
evaṃ mantrādhvayajanātmudrāmaṇḍaladarśanāt ।।

āyāmadhāraṇāyogānpa(śuṃ) * ñcanti vāsanāḥ ।
nityaśuddhamidaṃ sarvaṃ yathāmanyeta sādhakaḥ ।।

tadākhi: * kriyāstasya patitvaṃ pratipādakāt ।
proktā śrīmaṅgalāśāstre dāhayu - śśarīrake ।।

aṅguṣṭhāgrātkālavahnijvalābhāsuramutkṣitam ।
astra - * tacchikhābhirbahirantardahettanum ।।

dāhaśca dhvaṃsa evoktau dhvaṃsakaṃ ma - * jñitam ।
tejastathābhilāpākhya svavikalparasombhitam ।।

tena * ntrāgninā dāho dehe puryaṣṭake tathā ।
dehapuryaṣṭakā hantā dhvaṃsādeva * jāyate ।।

na hi sambhāvamātreṇa dehemābandhadehavat ।
ahantāyaṃ  hi dehatvaṃ sādhvastātaddaheddhruvam ।।

taddehasaṃskārataro bhasmatvenātha * ssthitaḥ ।
taddharmavāyunādhūya tiṣṭhecchuddhacidātmani ।।

tasminvruve * staraṅge samāpattimupāgataḥ ।
saṃvidassṛṣṭidharmitvādādyāmeti ta * ṅgitām ।।

saiva mūrtiriti khyātā tārasedvinmahātmikā ।
tantrālo * pyevamuktamatha mūrtivicāryate ।।

।। iti dehadāhādyupapatti ।।

plutenoccārya dhāmoccaiśśivatatve nivedya ca ।
tadbhāḥ parimalākarṣā * ṅgaissarvajñatādibhiḥ ।।

kṛte patāṃ parāṃ mūrtiṃ vinyaseddehayugmake ।
(36b)
ekoccāreṇa vā śuddhirdvātriṃśadakṣarātmanaḥ ।।

tena nyāsassakṛtpāṇau sakṛddehe tathā bhavet ।
niṣkalā sakalā mūrtiḥ parā caivamudāhṛtā ।।

upādānaṃ pravakṣyāmi mūrterbhedasamanvitam ।
sumanā ca adhodeśe śuddhavidyā kalāgaṇaḥ ।।

heyo vidyeśasaṃhāre bhaktaśiṣyasya cātmanaḥ ।
aharmukhe bhogabhoktussamanodaravartinaḥ ।।

pralayākalakāśśuddhā jñeyā heṣātirasthitāḥ ।
samanāyāssadā heyāśśuddhavijñānanirmalāḥ ।।

tadūrdhvo conmanaśśaktistadadho * * na sthitāḥ ।
śuddhātmatatvaṃ tairjñeyaṃ tatkṣobhādātmasaṃjñitā ।।

sā mūrtiniṣkalā tasyā tūrdhve yā sotmanābhidhā ।
tatkṣobhātsakalāmūrtiśivakṣobhātparātanuḥ ।।

yadotmanātmanākrāntā niṣkalā svayamunmanā ।
sakalā sā śivonmeṣā sā parākhyā parācchivāt ।।

upādānaṃ tridhā tasyā itthaṃ guruparamparā ।
unmanā yā parāśaktissā mūrtiḥ parikīrtitā ।।

na śivosyā upādānaṃ nānmātāvadhikāriṇau ।
sā ca ṣaṭkāraṇārūḍhā viśvaṃ vyāpya vyavasthitā ।।

saikadhā ca catu(rtha)dhā tu saptadhā bahudhāṣṭadhā ।
āyyāśivānmikā sādhvī mūrtitvenopacaryate ।।

śrīmanmataṅgagītaṃ yaṃ tacchlokaiḥ procyatedhunā ।
atha patyuradhiṣṭhānaṃ svaśaktikiraṇātmakam ।।
(37a)
tasyāṃ divi sudīptātmā niṣkampo calamūrtimān ।
kāṣṭhā caiva parā sūkṣmā sarvadikkāsitātmikā ।।

pradhvastāvaraṇā śāntā vastumātrātilālasā ।
ādyantoparatā sādhvī mūrtitvenopacaryate ।।

sa liṅgā niṣkalā jñeyā nirliṅgā piṇḍaśodhane ।
vidyākhyā ca śivākhyā ca caturdhā saptadhā tataḥ ।।

vācyātra prasarārambhavyāptibodhaprakāśikā ।
sthitissaṃvitsvabhāvāntāḥ ārṇanackasabindukāḥ ।।

ardhendunādaśaktyantā unmanā mūrtivarṇagā ।
prasaro hṛdaye prokto vyāptiḥ kaṇṭhe prakīrtitā ।।

bodhākhyastāluni proktaḥ prakāśo bhrūyugāntare ।
sthitirmūrdhani saṃvittiṃ śśakyante samudāhṛtā ।।

svākhyaṃ śivapade cetthamityuktaṃ mūrtisūribhiḥ ।
bahudhā mūrtisandehadehabhe{dā}nmiliṅgikā ।।

vidyāṣṭākhyaprakārākṣmavahniyajjvārkavāribhiḥ ।
anilendunabhobhiśca tayā te samadhiṣṭhitā ।।

cidviśiṣṭātmamūrteśca tattvasattātmikā paraḥ ।
sarvānugrahakṛtkhyātaśivonmeṣācca sā parā ।।

anuktamūrtimantrāṇāmekavīrā hi yoginām ।
vyāptitritayamāśritya tridhaikasminprakalpanā ।।

dhyātvā tattvasya hṛdaye svakarmaproktavigraham ।
japopakramapūrvoktaṃ kṛtvā karmādibhedanam ।।
(37b)
indhanādinirodhānte karmaṣaṭkaṃ prakalpayet ।
indhanaṃ hṛdayākāśe jñānaśakteḥ prabodhanam ।।

hṛdayāttāluparyantaṃ purāṇaṃ karmukākṛti ।
tanmadhye cintanāttasya dīpanaṃ nijatejasaḥ ।।

tāḍanaṃ śaktimārgeṇa dahanānilamadhyataḥ ।
śodhayettādamārgeṇa mantraṃ sādhakadūṣitam ।।

bodhayeddhṛdayāntasthaṃ bindunādāntasaṅkaṭe ।
nirodhaṃ kārayetpaścādacale śāśvate pade ।।

yogaṃ tattvapadātītaṃ kṛtvā śivaguṇaissaha ।
śrīmaddharmaśivaproktaṃ mūrtiṃ jñātvā tu sādhakaḥ ।।

।। iti mūrtisvarūpakathanam ।।

tato mūrtiṃ nyaseddevi pūrvamantrasulakṣitam ।
sakalaṃ bhairavaṃ nyasya dvātriṃśāhaṃ sulocane ।।

mukhāni vinyasetpaścānmurddhādicaraṇāvadhi ।
vaktrāṇi kārayetpaścādūrdhvaṃ pūrvaṃ ca dakṣiṇam ।।

uttaraṃ paścimaṃ caiva yathāvatpravibhāgaśaḥ ।
kalābhedaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ śodhyadhvānaṃ prakalpayet ।।

navatattvaṃ tritattvaṃ ca vidyāṅgā locanatrayam ।
vargātītena kṣurikā mūrdhvādhognipradīpikaḥ ।।

ṣoḍaśāntahaṭhā sā tu rakṣikā vighnanāśikā ।
navakaṃ kalpayet pūrvaṃ mūrdhni vaktre ca kaṇṭhake ।।

hṛdaye nābhideśe ca guhye ūrdhvośca jānutaḥ ।
aṅghryantaṃ caiva vinyasya sudhyānaguṇasaṃyutam ।।

kriyā jñānī tathā cecchā dakṣe vāme ca madhyataḥ ।
(38a)
vidyārājasmṛto hyevaṃ bhairavo mantranāyaka ।।
svacchandebhihito devaścāntyādau punarucyate ।
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśahimaku * ndusannibham ।।

śāntyarthaṃ cintayenmantraṃ pauṣṭike kanakaprabham ।
vaśyākarma * kārye ca raktavarṇaṃ vicintayet ।।

abhicāreṣu sarveṣu kṛṣṇaṃ dhyānaṃ prakīrtitam ।
hāṭakeśvara evaṃ hi niśśvāsā cātha kathyate ।।

।। darśate vibhoḥ * ti pāṭhaḥ ।।

cintayetsarvabhūteśaṃ jyotīrūpaṃ tu  śaṅkaram ।
taṃ devā(di) * rvyassiddhāyakṣā vidyādharoragā ।।

rudramātṛgaṇeśānānityaṃ dhyā(ya)nti suvratāḥ ।
tasya rūpāṇyanekāni nīlapītāruṇāni vai ।।

upāsyante tapoyuktaiḥ phalabhedena paṇḍitaiḥ ।
tatra yallohitaṃ rūpaṃ nityaṃ lākṣārasaprabham ।।

dhyeyaṃ tadrājyakāmena yogena cānupūrvakam ।
tatra pī * prabhāyuktaṃ saumyaṃ kāntaṃ śriyānvitam ।।

taddivyāsuranārīṇāṃ nitya - karṣaṇātmakam ।
atha pāpaviśuddhyarthaṃ śuddhasphaṭikanirmalam ।।

cintayetsatataṃ mantrī yatra yatrāśrame rataḥ ।
ādityodayasaṅkāśaṃ dharmārthaṃ * vicintayet ।।

naśyanti ca sadā rogā ghorā dehasya nāśakāḥ ।
vinā * kavināśārthamātmano vijayāya ca ।।

astaṃgamatisūrye ca yadrūpaṃ tādṛśaṃ smaret ।
(38b)
jyotiṣkāñcanavarṇābhaṃ sutīptaṃ sarvatomukham ।।

dhyāyeddharmasamṛddhyarthaṃ yadvā puṣṭyarthamātmanaḥ ।
visphuliṅgaiścalaṃ śyāmaṃ cintayecchaṅganāśane ।।

krūramantravidhānena krodhassaṃraktalocanaḥ ।
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ smarejjyotissarvakāryeṣu siddhaye ।।

varṇānāmānupūrveṇa yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ ।
nandīśvarāvatāre hi nandinaitadudīritam ।।

īśānaṃ mūrdhni vinyasya bhadrayā liṅgamudrayā ।
vaktre ca vaktrayā vaktraṃ bahurūpaṃ hṛdāhṛdi ।।
