guhyayā vāmadevaṃ ca guhyadeśe niveśayet ।
pādayostu samārabhya sadyojātaṃ niveśayet ।।

sarvāṅgikaṃ śiro yāva{dbha}drayā sūktrimudrayā ।
aṅguṣṭhakena saṃyogāttarjanīprabhṛti kramāt ।।

bhadrā mudrāstu vaktrādyā liṅgamudrā tu muṣṭinā ।
etadvaktreṣu mudrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ puṣkaroditam ।।

yadivārtāni varṇāni saṃhāreṇa yutāni ca ।
bindumastakabinnāni bhairavasya mukhāni tu ।।

brahmabhaṅgyāni yojyāni mūrddhādicaraṇāvadhi ।
punaścordhvaṃ mukhyaṃ kalpyaṃ prākdakṣiṇamathottaram ।।

aparaṃ kalpayitvā tu kalābhedena vinyaset ।
pūrvaṃ ca dakṣiṇaṃ caiva uttaraṃ paścimaṃ tathā ।।

ūrdhvaṃ mūrdhādisaṃyuktaṃ kṣakāraṃ tvīśarūpiṇam ।
evaṃ vaktraṃ caturdhā tu vaktreṣveva niyojayet ।।


(39a)
pañcamaṃ yadbhavedvaktraṃ kṣakāreṇaiva nirdiśet ।
hṛdigrīvāssapṛṣṭhe ca nābhau ca jaṭhare tathā ।।

pṛṣṭhe corasi vinyasyedaghoreṇa yathākramam ।
guhye tathā gude caiva tathordhorjānunorapi ।।

jaṅghayośca sphiṭo: kaṭyāṃ pārśvayorubhayorapi ।
vinyaseccaiva vāmena śarīre tu yathākramam ।।

tāsāṃ vakṣyāmi nāmāni yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ ।
tārā sutārā ta - ṇī tārayanti sutāriṇī ।।

īśānasya kalāḥ pañca nirañjanapadānugāḥ ।
nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyāśāntistathaiva ca ।।

puṣasya ca kalā hyetāścatasraḥ parikīrtitāḥ ।
tamāmohā kṣudhā nidrā mṛtyunmāyā tayā jarā ।।

aghorasya kalā hyevamaṣṭau ca varavarṇini ।
rajā rakṣā ratiḥ palyā kāsyā tṛṣṇā matiḥ kriyā ।।

ṛddhikāyā ca rātrī ca bhrāmaṇī mohanī tathā ।
manonmanīti vikhyātā vāmadeve trayodaśa ।।

siddhi ṛddhirdyutirlakṣmīrmedhā kāntissudhā sthitiḥ ।
sadyojātakalāstvevamaṣṭau saṃparikīrtitaḥ ।।

samantako vaktrakalānyāsassvacchandaśāstragaḥ ।
pāṇibhyāmapi tannyāsastena mātraṃ karadvaye ।।

kāryā tābhyāṃ kriyā yasmānmantranyāsādipūrvikā ।
kriyāṃ ca kurute sarvassarvamantraviketanam ।।
(39b)
kanyasādyāsvajātādīnaṅgulīṣu kramānnyaset ।
sarvāṅgaṃ tathāṅgāni paścāttābhyāṃ nyaseddvija ।।

piṇḍavadvinyasenmūrtimudgītenāmalatviṣā ।
sā bhūtirkāyasaṃkliptirajādyaḥ pañcabhissthitaiḥ ।।

sadyaḥ kṣitirjalaṃ vāmo ghorāstejānaro marut ।
īśaḥ khamupasaṃhāro nyāsaḥ pādādikāntagaḥ ।।

mokṣassarvopasaṃhārānnyāsasteṣu tathāvidhaḥ ।
īśādārabhya sadbhyojyo nāso bhogārthamiṣyate ।।

yatassaṣṭau bhavedbhogassṛṣṭiryāmo yatasthitā ।
tenordhvatassamārabhya nyasaḥ pādavasānagaḥ ।।
atāḥ prāguttamāṅgasya kliptirīśena tattanoḥ ।
tāni pañca yatheśasya pratyaṅgāni niyogatā ।।

pūrvato dakṣiṇaṃ saumyaṃ paścimaṃ madhyamaṃ purā ।
vaktrāṇyaiva nareṇaiṣāmeṣāṃ mūrtiṃ vibhāvayet ।।

ghoreṇa hṛdgalāṃsoraḥ pṛṣṭhanābhyudarāṇyatha ।
pāyuliṅgerucānusphigjaṅgākaṭyaśca pārśvagāḥ ।।

vāmenājena pādau dvau pāṇīnāsāṃ śirobhujau ।
kalpayetpañcabhirmantraissvakāyaṃ tu tavatsthitaiḥ ।।

māṃsasya pārthivassadyassarvāṅgavyāpakassthitaḥ ।
tasminnaṅge sthito deva ātmavatsarvabhūtagaḥ ।।

parāgamena kathito nyāso bhogāpavargadaḥ ।
punaśca sādhako devi sarvāṅgeṣu yathākramam ।।

navatattvaṃ tritattvaṃ ca navakaṃ bhairavābhidham ।
(40a)
navakaṃ kalpayetpūrvaṃ mūrdhni vaktre ca kaṇṭhake ।।

hṛdaye nābhideśe ca guhye ūrdhośca jānutaḥ ।
aṭhyūntaṃ caiva vinyasyet svadhyānaguṇasaṃyutam ।।

bhairavānāṃ sutattvānāṃ nyāsaṃssvachandaśāsanai ।
yaḥ proktastatra navakaṃ śivasya suśivasya ca ।।

īśvarasya ca vidyāyā māyākālaniyāmike ।
puruṣaprakṛtīnāṃ ca yāni sthānāni tāni tu ।।

bhairavānāṃ vicintyāni tritatvīke gale hṛdi ।
eṣa dravyaiśca vinyāsaḥ proktaśśrīpauṣkare yathā ।।

nyāsa eṣa śivegnau ca pāśāvātmani caiva hi ।
gandhaiḥ patraiśca puṣpaiśca dhūpaiśca pariśodhitaiḥ ।।

hṛdayādīni yathāsvaṃ svastikabandhena pārśvayorastam ।
vyāptikaraśśiva eṣā sā pādāṅguṣṭhamūrdhvagataḥ ।।

nandīśvarāvatārasthityetra sāṅgamūlavinyāsaḥ ।
praśasanī śivā caiva jvalinī stambhinī tathā ।।

ghātinī jñānanetre ca hṛdādīnāṃ prakīrtitāḥ ।
aṅguṣṭhamunnataṃ hṛtvā muṣṭiṃ baddhvā hṛducyate ।।

puṣṭau nikṣipya cāṅguṣṭhaṃ śirodeśyucchritā śikhā ।
tiryaktadeva kavacamastraṃ cātra trirucchritam ।।

lalāṭe tarjanīṃ kṛtvā baddhvāmuṣṭimadhomukhīm ।
netramityucitaṃ vidyā purāṇākhye mahāgame ।। ।।

।। pratodo bhagavānvasiṣṭha uvāca ।।
(40b)
aṅgapratyaṅgataṃ pūrvaṃ tatkliptaṃ brahmapañcakam ।
kimebhiḥ kalpitairaṅgairhṛdayādyabhidhāyakaiḥ ।।

।। prakāśo bhagavānsūrya uvāca ।।

udaṅgānāṃ yadaṅgatvamupacāravidhisthitam ।
yato dharmakṛpāṇākhye tepīhāṅga(tvamā)gate ।।

nācateṅgeṅgabhāvatvaṃ gate tenāṅgahetuge ।
hṛdayaṃ hṛtparitrāṇe dharmapaṭṭāvadāhitam ।।

śiraśca tacchirastrāṇaṃ śiraśaḥ pariveṣṭanam ।
śūlikā mastakatrāṇamaṅgatrāṇaṃ ca kaṅkaṇam ।।

kṛte śastragrahe mantrī bhīṣaṇo bhaṭavatsthitaḥ ।
kṣudrāṇāmupadhruṣyassyāttārkṣyo dṛkśruvasāmiva ।।

yathā śarīrasaṃkliptistathā tadgātrakalpanā ।
kāryastadā dṛḍhobhāvassādhakasyāvalepagaḥ ।।
sontarbahiśśivībhūtaśśivo bhūtvā śivaṃ yajet ।

।। pratoda uvāca ।।

pūjyapūjakabhāvoyaddṛṣṭonyūnānyayoriva ।
śivībhūtaśśivassākṣātkathamārādhako bhavet ।।

।। prakāśa uvāca ।।

patiśśuddhādhvaharmyasthatatprāptistatsamāsyasā ।
yāvantatatsamo jātastāvanārādhane kṣamaḥ ।।

yatassamānaśīlānāṃ dṛṣṭaṃ sakhyaṃ phalāntikam ।
ityuktaṃ tu parāyāya dārurukṣujanaṃ prati ।।

ārūḍhānāṃ punarvakṣye prameye cāṅgaṣaṭkagam ।
sarvajñatā tu hṛdayaṃ paritṛptiṃ śiro viduḥ ।।

anādibodhaṃ tu śikhāṃ svātantryāṃ kavacaṃ vibhoḥ ।
aluptaśaktinnayanamastraṃcānantaśaktigam ।।
(41a)
ṣāḍguṇyametadevasya hyaṅgaṣaṭkamidaṃ smṛtam ।
iyaṃ sāmānyato vyāptī rājatantreṅgaṣaṭkagā ।।

svairarcibhiryathāvahnirbhānubhirbhānumānyathā ।
samartho duṣpradharṣyaśca tadvadaṅgairnijaiśśivaḥ ।।

viṣādānandabhāvābhyāṃ vyāpakatvācca vedakaḥ ।
sarvajñatvaṃ maheśasya hetunānena dṛśyate ।।

tadasya hṛdayaṃ proktamaṅgaṃ prathamamucyate ।
paritṛptirmaheśasya dvitīyaḥ prathito guṇaḥ ।।

bhogābhogeṣu madhyastha audāsīnyānmaheśvaraḥ ।
paritṛptastadā proktastasthiraḥ kathyate vibhoḥ ।।

pradhānabhāvātsa bhavedyodhaścānādimānvibhoḥ ।
sārvalakṣaṇyabhāvena sarvadṛksarvavedanāt ।।

apaśyantī pa(śṛṣṭhṛ)tvā bodhasyāpi tu bodhanāt ।
guṇastṛtīyaḥ kathitassā śikhā samudāhṛtā ।।

svātantryaṃ sarvadā śambhoḥ sthūlasūkṣmavibhedataḥ ।
sarvānugrahakartṛtvādabhedyatvāttaducyate ।।

svātantryaṃ varmadevasya caturthaḥ kathito guṇaḥ ।
aluptaśaktirbhagavānnityameva maheśvari ।।

sāmarthyaṃ parameśasya na lupyate kadācana ।
tena tatra samuddiṣṭaṃ prakāśatvānmaheśvare ।।

anantaśaktirbhagavānnāsyānto dṛśya * yadā ।
patitāyānmaheśāni pāśānāṃ ca vidāraṇāt ।।

sāmarthyātpāśaviśleṣaṃ kṛtvāsya samatāṃ nayet ।
tadasyānantaśaktitvādastraṃ tenaha kathyate ।।
(41b)
etatprameyaṃ kathitaṃ dehanyāsāṅgaṣaṭkagam ।
śikhātantredhunā vakṣye śrīmṛtyuñjayamantrataḥ ।।

pūjāyāṃ kriyamāṇāyāṃ kramaṃ saṅkṣe prapūjakam ।
ityeṣa nyāsavidhisiddhāntanayāśritaḥ kathitaḥ ।।

sarve srotassaṅgrahasārāṃśayatopi kathyate solpaḥ ।।vinyasya mūrtimantraṃ vyāpakabhāve {na} sakaladehasyaṃ śivavidyātmākhyāni trīṇi śikhāhṛdayakaṇṭhadeśeṣu ।
tattvānyaṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ prakṛtihṛdādīni vinyaset । astraṃ ca hastamadhye svasthānasthāni vapuṣi tānyeva ।। astraṃ prāgiva karayordehe ca vyāpakatvena vinyasya
mūrtimantraṃ dhyāyeddevaṃ nibaddhakarāmudrasiddhāntadarśanoktaṃ nyāsaṃ kṛtvā yajeta mṛtyujitam । mṛtyuñjayoktamathavā nyāsaṃ kṛtvā sadāśivo । mṛtyusañjayākhyaśāstre (ta)thā hi kathitaṃ maheśena ।

pratiṣṭhāyāṃ pūjane ca tathā vidyārghapātrayo ।
agnisaṃskaraṇe caiva nyāsadhyānādike tathā ।।

vikalpo naiva kartavyassarvasādhāraṇo yatra ।
saṅkaro naiva jāyate tasmācchaṅkāṃ parityajet ।।

śrīviśvānta evaṃ hi jagāda nijapaddhate ।
niṣkalaṃ tu samāvāhya aṅgānyeva yathākramam ।।

hṛcchiraśca śikhāvarmalocanāstraṃ ca kalpayet ।
eṣa bhairavagaṇastu sarvakāmārthasādhakaḥ ।।

gandhaiḥ puṣpaistathādhūpairvividhairbhakṣyabhojanaiḥ ।
(42a)
pūjayeddevadeveśaṃ manasaiva prakalpitaiḥ ।।

ātmānaṃ bhairavaṃ tato hṛdyāgamā * * nabhet ।
nābhau kandaṃ samāropya nālaṃ tu dvādaśāṅgulam ।।

hṛdayaṃ kalpayetsyāvattatra padmaṃ vicintayet ।
aṣṭapatraṃ mahādīptaṃ kesarālaṃ sakarṇikam ।।

kandaṃ śaktimayaṃ tatra nāle vai kaṇṭhakāstu ye ।
bhuvanāni tu tānyeva rudrāṇāṃ varavarṇini ।।

śaktyādhāramanantaṃ ca dharmādicaraṇāni ca ।
gātrakā nityadharmādyāstathā sandhānakīlakān ।।

adhacchadanamurdhvaṃ ca padmakesarakarṇikāḥ ।
puṣkarāṇya * śaktiśca maṇḍalātmaṇḍalādhipān ।।

śavāntamāsanaṃ dadyātpūrvarūpaṃ dhruveṇa tu ।
mūrtiṃ brahmakalāvyūhaṃ navatattvaṃ tritattvakam ।।

dvātriṃśadakṣaraṃ devaṃ bhairavāṣṭakameva ca ।
vidyāṅgakṣurikāṃ caiva tathā vai locanatrayam ।।

śaktitrayaṃ paraṃ devamaṅgapaṅkasamanvitam ।
mudrā mantrāśca dravyāṇi yathāsthānaṃ niyojayet ।।

sakalpya ca yathānyāyyaṃ yathāyogaṃ prakalpayet ।
sadyojātaṃ ca vāmaṃ ca aghoraṃ ca yaduktavān ।।

puruṣe gau ca devasya dalasthāṃścopakalpayet ।
hṛdādīni tataḥ pañca diśāsu vidiśāsu ca ।।

pūrvato yāvadīśāntaṃ bhairavāvaraṇaṃ bahiḥ ।
lokapālāstatostrāṇi pūrvādīśānakāvadhi ।।
(42b)
astrāṇi lokapālāṃśca bhairavāṣṭakameva ca ।
pañcabrahmāṇyathāṅgāni etānyāvaraṇāni ca ।।

krameṇoccārayenmantraṃ yāvattadgarbhamīśvaram ।
mantrasandhānametaddhi paramīkaraṇaṃ śṛṇu ।।

uccārayettato devaṃ hrasvadīrghaplutānvitam ।
tāvaduccārayenmantraṃ yāvannirvāṇagocaram ।।

adhaścakreryāvadūrdhvaṃ somasūryapathāntare ।
piṅgalāmadhyamārgeṇa varṇoccārakrameṇa tu ।।

devatāpañcakaṃ śaktiṃ vyāpinī samanonmane ।
bhedayitvātkramātsarvaṃ yāvadvai nidhanāntikam ।।

nistaraṅgaṃ niradhvānaṃ sakalavyāpi conmanam ।
tadadhyasyānulomyena hṛtpadme viniveśayet ।।

sarveṣvāvaraṇeṣvevaṃ devi tadvyāpakaṃ nyaset ।
tena cādhiṣṭhitāssarve śivadhāmaphalapradāḥ ।।

yathāsvarūpasaṃsthāśca mantrā ye kathitā mayā ।
tathā te viniyoktavyā mānase mānasaiva tu ।।

karṇikāyāṃ śivassyāpya dvidhāvasthaśca bhairavi ।
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśassarvamantrairalaṅkṛtaḥ ।।

tatrāpi parato jñeyastvanirdeśyo gatāmayaḥ ।
yatra nāsti dvidhābhāvo varṇamantraprakalpanā ।।

oṃkārabindunādānāṃ vilayaṃ te vinirdiśet ।
tatsthānaṃ durlabhaṃ dhyātā sambhavetra kadācana ।।

yasya nordhvaṃ ca mūlaṃ ca na diśo vidiśo na ca ।
śabdasparśo raso rūpaṃ nākāśaṃ nāsti gandhatā ।।
(43a)
taṃ devi devadeveśa jñātvā yogī vimucyate ।
itthaṃ ca mantrasandhānaṃ nāḍisandhānameva ca ।।
paramīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt vyāpakeṇa pareṇa tu ।
naivedyaṃ vividhākāraṃ datvā mudrāṃ pradarśayet ।।

praṇipātaṃ japaṃ kuryāddaiśiko yāgatatparaḥ ।
svadehe sthaṇḍile liṅge maṇḍale caruke tathā ।।

jalaṃ cāgnaṃ ca labhate sadīkṣāphalamuttamam ।
akṛtvā mānasaṃ yāgaṃ yonyaṃ yāgaṃ samārabhet ।।

aśivassa tu vijñeyo na mokṣāya vidhīyate ।
ātmayāge kṛte devi dehaśuddhivijāyate ।।

adhiṣṭhitaṃ śivenaiva tamācāryaṃ vinirdiśet ।
ātmasaṃśodhanāyaiva mānasoyaṃ prakīrtitam ।।

taṃ jñātvā samyagācāryaḥ pāśahā bhavatī suraḥ ।
yatra yatra sthito deśe yaścaivaṃ mānasaṃ tyajet ।।

brahmaghnopi vimucyeta kiṃ punaryodine dine ।
etanyantroktavidhinā yaduktaṃ vidhipūrvakam ।।

ijyādi cānyatantrepi labhatvā kāmikaṃ phalam ।
nānāsiddhiguṇairyuktaṃ nānābhogaphalapradam ।।

yogasiddhimavāpnoti muktiṃ ca labhate dhruvam ।
taṃ jānanti surassarve prajānāti śivovyayaḥ ।।

hṛtpradeśe vibhonnyāsaṃ manasyarcā ca tatstutiḥ ।
śrīmatsvachandādidaṃ proktaṃ yatpūjāṅgaṃ taducyate ।।
(43b)
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi sadbhaktānāṃ gaṇāmbhike ।
yathāyānti vināyāgaṃ durlabhaṃ tu mamāntikam ।।

karomi vividhairbhāvairdevi devendrapūjite ।
teṣāṃ svātmani pūjārthaṃ svayameva samadyute ।।

tadarcādravyasambhārairyatsthairyaṃ sādharā smṛtā ।
dravā hlādasya rūpeṇa nityamāpassvasaṃsthitā: ।।

samudya tasya pūjārthaṃ tadā hlādaḥ pravartate ।
tejo rūpaṃ ca yattatra tadahaṃ parikīrtitam ।।

sparśadharmaścalatvaṃ yattadvāyavyaṃ hi tatsthitam ।
teṣu ye ssuśirābhāgyaśūnyatvācca sumadhyame ।।

tadākāśaguṇastatra madīyassavijṛmbhate ।
āpyāyatvena tenaite dṛśyante ca yathā punaḥ ।।

poṣyante devi vardhante tau candrārkau tu tatsthitau ।
etāssapta maheśāni rasyaḥ poṣyanti nityaśaḥ ।।

sā cāṣṭamī tanundevi yajamāneti saṃsthitā ।
iti pūjādivastūnāṃ pādyādīnāṃ svareśvari ।।

vīryaṃ madīyaṃ deveśi dṛśyate sarvajantuṣu ।
yato madvīryasambhūtā mattulyā matparākramāḥ ।।

pūjopakaraṇe bhāvā mama pūjopabhoginā ।
tānsamāśritya madbhaktā matpūjā tatparāyaṇāḥ ।।
(44a)
pūjayanti sureśāni te yānti paramāṃ gatim ।
pūryaṣṭakānusāreṇa pūjā niśśvāsutasmṛtā ।।

vyāsaṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvoktaṃ pradadyādaṣṭapuṣṭikām ।
sā proktairaṣṭabhiḥ puṣpairmānasai śṛṇu tadyathā ।।

vārijaṃ saumyamāśreyaṃ vāyavyaṃ pārthivaṃ punaḥ ।
vānaspatyaṃ tathā ṣaṣṭhaṃ prājāpatyaṃ tu saptamam ।।

aṣṭamaṃ śivapuṣpaṃ tu śṛṇu teṣāṃ vinittriyam ।
vārijaṃ salilaṃ jñeyaṃ sapughṛtaṃ payaḥ * * ।।

* * yaṃ dhūpadīpādya vāyavyaṃ candanādikam ।
pārthivaṃ kandamūlādyaṃ vārkṣaṃ puṣpaphalādikam ।।

prājāpatyaṃ padattrādyaṃ śivapuṣpaṃ tu vāsanā ।
evaṃ sadāśivo devaḥ pūjyaḥ puṣpai sadāṣṭabhi ।।

rubhayatrāṣṭapuṣpīyaṃ śivoktā kiraṇāgame ।
avikalpena cittena mantradehamupāharan ।।

mantranyāsaṃ prakurvīta tanmāyībhāvasiddhaye ।
saṅkalparūpā vyāpārāḥ sudṛḍhā hi śubhāvahāḥ ।।

yatra sarvamidaṃ devi manasyantaravasthitam ।
manorbaddhaśca mokṣaśca dharmādharmo ca śāśvatau ।।
(44b)
nāmarūpaṃ prapañcaśca jīvanaṃ maraṇaṃ tathā ।
anādyavidyāmalinaṃ manassaṃsāra ucyate ।।
tadeva kevalībhūtaṃ nirvāṇāya prakalpate ।
tasmānnyāsaṃ viniṣpādya yogayāgaṃ samācaret ।।

ayātayāmairasitairamāyīyairanaśvaraiḥ ।
pūjayenmānasairbhogai daivamākāradhāriṇam ।।

itthaṃ cakreśvaramate hṛdyāgāssarvasiddhidaḥ ।।

।। nāradaḥ ।।

jñānavijñānasahitaṃ hṛdyāgaṃ sarvasiddhidam ।
kimarthaṃ bāhyataḥ pūjā kāryā vai maṇḍalāntare ।।

।। bhagavānuvāca ।।

bāhyorthavāsanā yeyaṃ bahujanmārjitā dṛḍhā ।
lolīkṛtonayā hyātmā śuddhośuddhasvarūpayā ।।

yā mantraviṣayā śuddhā kriyāśāntasvarūpadā ।
samutthānavināśārthaṃ tasyāssā parikīrtitāḥ ।।

sabāhyābhyantarābhyāṃ vā kriyābhyāntanmayo bhavet ।
dṛḍhorthavāsanānāṃ tu tānavaṃ syācchanaiśśanaiḥ ।।

yatmayassāmprataṃ vipra dehasthassādhako bhavet ।
tatmayo dehapātātsyādityetatkathitaṃ mayā ।।
(45a)
dvidhārcanopapattiśca śrījayāyāmudīritā ।
abhāvānbhogapūgasya mānasīṃ sannidhiṃ śrayet ।।

ye tu lobhādādriyante dhaninaṃ mānasaṃ kramam ।
vṛthādīkṣā vṛthāyāgasteṣāmupahatātmanām ।।

tasmāccakreśvaramatadvidhāyā samprakīrtitaḥ ।।

।।  ityāgamoddhṛte nityādisaṅgrahe manoyāgaprakaraṇam ।।

(45b)
sakalīkṛtya kartavyo yāgaḥ kena kathaṃ ca saḥ ।
kadā kutra ca kairdravyaiḥ kimarthaṃ ca maheśvara ।।

itthaṃ saṃcodito devyā jagāda parameśvarāḥ ।
athātassaṃpravakṣyāmi śivayāgamanuttamam ।।

yaṃ jñātvā dehinaḥ kṣipraṃ mucyante bhavabandhanāt ।
daiśikassādhako vāpi samayī putrakopi vā ।।

* * * * * * * * yogapīṭhaṃ śivaṃ yajet ।
yāgadravyāṇi cāhṛtya yāgayogyāni mantravit ।।

kṛtarakṣaḥ prasannātmā kṛtadravyādiśodhanaḥ ।
astrajaptena toyena spṛṣṭvā vā bhasmanāpi vā ।।

sthānaśuddhiṃ prakurvīta tālairvā mudrayāpi vā ।
śivodakapavitrāṅgaiśśaktibhiśca śivena vā ।।

ku(ryā)darghādi saṃśuddhyai dravyāṇāṃ prokṣaṇāya ca ।
pṛthivyastramadho dikṣu prākāraṃ daṇḍamudrayā ।।

bhūtamaṇḍalabandhaṃ ca sarajālaṃ samudrayā ।
uparyadhordhvaṃ vahnyarkau madhye somasya maṇḍalam ।।

tatrāsīno yajeddevaṃ karmanā manasā girā ।
yāgadravyāṇi saṃśodhya śivāstraṃ manasā smaret ।।

suratyāpyāyayetpaścādgāyatryā cāmṛtaṃ sravan ।
yathākarma yathākālaṃ dravyairdravyāṇi yojayet ।।

āvāhanārghapūjāsu snāne dhūpe vilepane ।
naivedye ca visarge ca puṣpamaṣṭasu yojayet ।।
(46a)
arghe vilepane snāne triṣu gandhaṃ prayojayet ।
puṣpadānārghapādyeṣu snāne cācamane tathā ।।

kṣālane prokṣaṇe caiva vāri saptasu yojayet ।
dhūpassarvatra dātavyaśśeṣaṃ jñātvā prayojayet ।।

dhūpavacca smṛtā devamudrāssarvakriyāsu ca ।
aṣṭapuṣṭāṃ trigandhādyāṃ saptavāripariplutām ।।

(ma)ntradhūpavidhivyāptāṃ kriyāṃ jñātvārcayecchivam ।
āgamasthitisaṃrodhāt sānnidhyaṃ pādyameva ca ।।

arghaścāmanīyaṃ ca pūjāde ** ditaḥ kramāt ।
snānaṃ vastrādidānaṃ ca punaḥ pūjārghameva ca ।।

japastu vyādikaṃ karmasanyāsotha japārpaṇaḥ ।
punaḥ pūjārghadānaṃ ca tathā devi visarjanam ।।

aṣṭapuṣpādikaṃ sarvaṃ kriyāścāvāhanādikāḥ ।
yatra yatra smṛtā etāssa sayāgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ।।

vidyā purāṇe saṅkṣepādeṣa yāgavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ।
puṣpādilakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ yaistu sampūjayecchivam ।।
(46b)
evaṃ tu śivatāṃ prāpto yadāsau daiśikottamaḥ ।
tadā puṣpādikaṃ sarvaṃ nirjīvaṃ śivasaṃskṛtam ।।

sajīvatvaṃ tu labhate śivaniṣṭhaṃ yadā bhavet ।
anyathā mṛtadehecca kusumādyaḥ prapūjitaḥ ।।

na bhavet pūjito devo paṣṭhvākalpaśataṃ yadi ।
evaṃ sampūjya vidhivannānākusumavistaraiḥ ।।

dhūpaissugandhaissaghṛtairmadhuguggulumiśritaiḥ ।
dīpairghṛtāntaraiḥ kāntai: prabhūtaistārakopamaiḥ ।।

gandhaiśca vividhairhṛdyairbalyaissvāduyutaiśśubhaiḥ ।
pānaiśca vividhairdevi susvādaiśca sugandhibhiḥ ।।

muktāphalairindranīlaissarvaratnaiśca pārvati ।
netraiśca vividhairvastrai sampūjya vidhivattataḥ ।।

itthaṃ śrīkaṇṭhaśāstroktaṃ sarvatraivāmitātmikām ।
- rāṃvṛttimanudhyāyandravyāṇyādau vilokayet ।।

tatrārghapātramādau vai sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tathā ।
śaṅkhaśāmbūkaśauktaṃ vā tāmraṃ mṛṇmayameva vā ।।

padmapatrapalāśorthaṃ gṛhītvākṣālya vāriṇā ।
astrajapteśadeveśi pra{lipyā} gurucandanaiḥ ।।

mṛṣṭadhūpena sandhūrya kariṇā pūrayettataḥ ।
vastrapūtena śubhreṇa svachanda iti kīrtitam ।।

ebhya ekatame pātre hyarghamaṣṭāṅgamāharet ।
puryaṣṭakavidhānena hyāpaḥ kṣīraṃ kuśādadhi ।।

taṇḍulākṣatasiddhārthāstilā iti vibhāgaśaḥ ।
(47a)
āpastasomadaivatyāḥ kṣīraṃ sārasvataṃ smṛtam ।।

kuśāgre caiva sāvitrī dadhni viṣṇurnirāsate ।
taṇḍulāḥ kṣitidaivatyāstilāstu pitṛdaivatāḥ ।।

yavācca brahmadaivatyāssiddhārthā rudradaivatāḥ ।
ityaṣṭāṅgārghapātraṃ tu jñātavyaṃ daiśikottamaiḥ ।।

brahmāṇḍamudaraṃ padmaṃ pārthivaṃ tattvamucyate ।
śoḍaṣārthadalopetaṃ digaṣṭakavibhūṣitam ।।

tadarghapātramādāya vidyeṣāṣṭakapuṣṭakam ।
vāthule bhāvapūjāyāmuktauṣṭāṅgārghanirṇayaḥ ।।

tena cābhyukṣayetsarvaṃ puṣpādyaṃ yadupasthitam ।
recidhitvārghamaṣṭāṅgaṃ recayeddhenumudrayā ।।

abhiṣiñcedathātmānaṃ mūrdhni tattoyabindunā ।
tatrasthaṃ yāgasambhāraṃ prokṣayedastravāriṇā ।।

abhimantryaṃ hṛdāpiṇḍaistanutreṇaiva veṣṭayet ।
darśayitvāmṛtāṃ mudrāṃ puṣpaṃ datvā nijāsane ।।

vidhāya tilakaṃ mūdhni puṣpaṃ mūlena yojayet ।
darśitassamayastvevaṃ tilake somaśambhunā ।।

vartulaṃ cārdhacandraṃ ca caturaśraṃ bhagākṛtim ।
īdṛśaṃ tilakaṃ kuryādūrdhvarekhāṃ tu pañcamīm ।।

vartulaścārthadaḥ prokto dharmadaścaturaśrakaḥ ।
mokṣadaścārdhacandraśca kāmadastu bhagākṛtiḥ ।।

sarvasampadvahāṃ caivamūrdhvarekhāṃ tu kārayet ।
vartulaṃ jālapīṭhe tu kāmākhye caturaśrakam ।।
(47b)
pūrṇagiryāmardhacandramoḍyāne tu bhagākṛtim ।
vyantareṣu tu pīṭheṣu hyardharekhāṃ tu kārayet ।।

tarjanī śatruvijaye madhyamā śāntikarmaṇi ।
anāmā śoṣaṇe proktā kaniṣṭhā buddhakarmaṇi ।।

aṅguṣṭhaśca bhavecchrāddhe viniyogoṅguliṣu ca ।
candanaṃ candramityāhussūryaḥ kuṅkumamucyate ।।

sindūraṃ vahnidaivatyaṃ tilakassyāttridhāmajaḥ ।
kṛtvaivaṃ sādhakaḥ kṣipramiṣṭasya nnatanurbhavet ।।

lakṣaṇaṃ viṣayo dravyaṃ tilake varṇitaṃ kvacit ।
brahmasthānasya pūrveṇa gurūnsaṃpūjayettataḥ ।।

gaṇeśaṃ vāyudigbhāge gandhadhūpādibhiḥ kramāt ।
avighnārthaṃ namaskṛtya dattājñamiti bhāvayet ।।

svacchandabhairave tantre gurupūjāditaḥ smṛtā ।
ācāryamūrtimāsthāya śivo jñānaṃ prayacchati ।।

tasmātsarvaprayatnena pūjanīyaśśivassadā ।
sādhakānāṃ hi sarveṣāṃ tatra siddhiḥ pratiṣṭhitā ।।

gurupūjāṃ vinā siddhissiddhānāmapi durlabhā ।
tasmātpūrvaṃ guruḥ pūjye bhaktiyuktena cetasā ।।

yasmānmaheśvarassākṣāt kṛtvā mānuṣavigraham ।
kṛpayā gururūpeṇa {mantrāḥ} proddhariti prajāḥ ।।

proktaṃ vājisaneyāyāṃ mukhyaṃ tu gurupūjanam ।
pūjayitvā gurūnevamekadeśe śilātale ।।

vijñapya tāṃstato mantrī caṇḍayāgaṃ samārabhet ।
vaidyāmantargatāssūkṣmā mantradehā mahāgaṇāḥ ।।
(48a)
yāgavighnakarā raudrāḥ pro * ccajyāpi yatnataḥ ।
astrajaptena toyena vi(pru)rbhistāḍitā budhaḥ ।।

tyajanti dehadhātpīṭhaṃ paścāt pūjāṃ prapūjayet ।
pañcabhirbrahmabhirmantrī tāṃ yā paryuṣitā tataḥ ।।

padyādikusumairdravyaiścaṇḍarudrasya pūjanam ।
nyāsaṃ kṛtvā purā mantrī śuddhe * * rayordvayoḥ ।।

śiraḥ prabhṛtipādāntaṃ tābhyāṃ dehaṃ tu saṃspṛśet ।
netrasthāne smaredbhūyo netracaṇḍaṃ sadā budhaḥ ।।

nirmālya bindubhirmūṣyairaṅgaṃ lagnairna duṣyati ।
sādhako yāgakāle tu śuddho bhavati sarvadā ।।

svayastvādimaye liṅge caṇḍarudraṃ na kīrtayet ।
manuṣyakalpite liṅge mantraissaiddhāntikairapi ।।

pūjite teṣu vinyāsaḥ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ padatrahi ।
tatpūjanavidhāveva caṇḍapūjārpaṇaṃ budhaḥ ।।

kuryādibhīdamatrerthaṃ tadvidhānaṃ samācaret ।

oṃ śuddhendudhautāya svāhā । oṃ dhunicaṇḍa huṃ phaṭ । netracaṇḍāya namaḥ ।

arghapātraṃ tataḥ kāryaṃ caṇḍamantrairdvitīyakam ।
āsanaṃ kalpayettatra hyanantaṃ praṇavādinā ।।

hṛnmantreṇa namontena sa{kṛ}duccāritena tu ।
tanmadhye saṃsmareccaṇḍamaṅgaṣaṭkasamanvitam ।।

taṃ sampūjyā tataḥ kāryaṃ caṇḍasyāsanamuttamam ।
ardhacandraṃ puraṃ dhyāyedeśānyāṃ diśisaṃsthitam ।।

hṛdānantaṃ namontena tanmadhye kalpayedbudhaḥ ।
(48b)
dharmaṃ jñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ ca catuṣṭayam ।।

āgneyādiṣu koṇeṣu sitapadmaṃ tadūrdhvataḥ ।
karṇikāyāṃ smarenmūrtiṃ caṃkāreṇeṅkṛtātmanā ।।

mūlamantrayutenaiva namaskārāntayoginā ।
hṛdayaṃ hṛdaye tasya śiraśśirasi saṃsmaret ।।

pādyādyaiḥ pūjayet paścāt pañcavaktryaṃ sitaṃ prabhum ।

daśabāhuṃ viśālākṣaṃ divyābharaṇabhūṣitam ।।

dhyāyeccaṇḍeśvaraṃ devaṃ paścāt pūjāṃ samarpayet ।
cakārākārasaṃyuktaścaṇḍanāma namontagaḥ ।।

mūlamantro bhavedeṣa caṇḍarudrasya vācakaḥ ।
cakāro dīpasaṃyukto hṛdayādiṣu vācakaḥ ।।

sānusvāraṃ bhavettatra mastraṃ tu savisargakam ।
evaṃ mantragaṇaṃ nyasya mahāmudrāṃ pradarśayet ।।

liṅgamūrtisthitāṃ pūjāṃ paścāttasmai samarpayet ।
puṣpaṃ vāmakare kṛtvā pūjāṃ dakṣiṇapāṇinā ।।

liṅgamūrdhnastu saṃgṛhya pīṭhapaṣṭhe nidhāpayet ।
tūrṇaṃ vāmakarātpuṣpaṃ savyenādāya pāṇinā ।।

aspṛśaṃ liṅgaśirasi dadya - kṣepato budhaḥ ।
liṅge śūnyaśiraḥ kṛtvā na tiṣṭhet sādhakaḥ kvacit ।।

vighnaprajāyate ghoro yadā syācchūnyamastakam ।
pīṭhapṛṣṭhā tu tāṃ pūjāṃ caṇḍamantrayutāṃ budhaḥ ।।

gṛhītvā caṇḍarudrāya pradadyānmūrdhnisaṃyataḥ ।
tadā tatkathitaṃ tantre nirmālyaṃ mantrasaṃskṛtam ।।

liṅgavedī viniṣkrāntaṃ caṇḍarudre samarpitam ।
arghaṃ datvā punastasya kṣamasveti vivarjayet ।।
(49a)
saṃhāramudrayā mantrī pūrakeṇa hṛdaṃ nayet ।
upacārastu kartavyo hṛdaiva praṇavādinā ।।

āsanaṃ kalpayettasya tvanenaiva prapūjayet ।
pañcamudrāṃ tato mantrī pīṭhamiṣṭvāttadarcayet ।।

nirmālyaṃ preṣayet sarvaṃ śiṣyahaste visarjayet ।
vicakṣaṇaḥ * * * * * * * * * * * *  ।।

nadyāṃ caivātha vahnau vā nopekṣeta kadācana ।
tatsthānaṃ śodhayet kṣipraṃ tribhirgomayagolakaiḥ ।।

hastau pādau ca saṃkṣālya liṅgasnānaṃ samācaret ।
saptavārāstrajaptena toyenaiva sugandhinā ।।

pīṭhaṃ prakṣālayet paścāt prāsādaṃ tadanantaram ।
parṣayedgomayenaiva mṛnmayaṃ śailajaṃ punaḥ ।।

kṣālayeddhastapādau tu jaṅghe caiva viśeṣataḥ ।
uttamāṅgaṃ yadāsiktaṃ tadā snānaṃ samācaret ।।

nākramedvāhunā liṅgaṃ hastau kṣālyau na tatra ca ।
pīṭhapṛṣṭhena caṇḍeśaṃ na kuryāddhastadhāvanam ।।

bhūmiṃ tu śodhayedyuktyā saṅkaraṃ naiva kārayet ।
vipruṣo tūtthitāḥ pīṭhe pātayenna kadācana ।।

nirmādyadūṣitāṃ pūjāṃ gṛhṇātīśo na jātucit ।
caṇḍayāgassamuddiṣṭo vāmadevīyasaṅgrahe ।।

puṣpahastastato mantrī praviśecchivamandiram ।
phaḍityanena devasya sammukhaṃ tu srajaṃ kṣipet ।।

liṅgarūpadharā raudraceṣṭitā ye vināyakāḥ ।
prapalāyanti vitrastānniraste tanulolupaḥ ।।
(49b)
mahādevaṃ sanirmālyamarghayitvā tu sādhakaḥ ।
samaṇḍalaṃ yathāvastrairbrahmabhissurasattamam ।।

ādau caṇḍīśvare yāge īśāne viniveśayet ।
āgneyalāñchanārūḍhaṃ caṇḍarudraṃ mahotkaṭam ।।

sphuradvimboṣṭhacibukaṃ ṣaḍbhujaṃ ṭaṅkadhāriṇam ।
khaḍgacarmadharaṃ raudraṃ jaṭāmaṇḍalamaṇḍitam ।।

akṣamālādharaṃ divyaṃ vyāghracarmāmbaracchadam ।
mukuṭānaddhaśīrṣāntaṃ diśīśānyāṃ samāśritam ।।

caṇḍarudranamastestu mantreṇānena dāpayet ।
tasmai tacchaivikaṃ mālyaṃ naivedyaṃ ca nivedayet ।।

sa devadevena purā tapasā toṣitena tu ।
vṛto vai varadānena tathā nirmālyajena * ।।

yattasmai dīyate mālyaṃ tatpavi(tra)taraṃ param ।
pareṣāmeva taddattamapavitramamedhyavat ।।

svabhāvato nāviśuddhaṃ nirmālyaṃ pāpanāśanam ।
aśuddhayonyathā devaṃ caṇḍīśamavamānayet ।।

samahāpāpabhāreṇa saṃyuktassyānna saṃśayaḥ ।
tasmānnivedayedyā - nnaivedyamavatāritam ।।

ādāyadeyamīśānavāge caṇḍeśvarāya ca ।
dhyātamāyatanaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvavighnavināśakṛt ।।

tatra devāya taddatvā sadya eva visarjayet ।
prītassabhagavāndevaścaturvargamatandritaḥ ।।

dadāti paralokāya tasmāddevaṃ samāśrayet ।
kṛtvaivamastrajaptena vāriṇā snapayecchivam ।।

sapīṭhaṃ sa ca nirmālyaṃ sapraṇā- sagarbhakam ।
(50a)
evamekatame sthāne nirmālyaṃ yadi tiṣṭhati ।
caṇḍīśvaraprabhāveṇa na taddoṣo bhavetkvacit ।।

uktaṃ pavitraṃ nirmālyaṃ siddhānte nilasaṃjñake ।
dhārayecchivanirmālyaṃ bhaktyā lobhānna bhakṣayet ।।

bhakṣaṇānnarakaṃ gacchellaṅghanādapyadhogatim ।
na tasya pratigṛhṇāti snānāpūjādiśaṅkaraḥ ।।

yo hi naivedyanirmālyamalabhukpūjayecchivam ।
bhūpradeśaṃ yadā kaściducchiṣṭānnasamākulam ।।

nāśrayeta tathā rudro naraṃ nirmālyadūṣitam ।
asaṃspṛśyo bhavettasya yo yasyāṅgamalaṃ pibet ।।

tasmānna saṃspṛśedbhasmānaro nirmālyadūṣitam ।
yaistu naivedyanirmālya saṅkīrṇakalaśādibhiḥ ।।

snānādyaṃ kurute tasya na tadgṛhṇāti śaṅkaraḥ ।
itthaṃ nirmālyadoṣoyaṃ śivadharmeṣu coditaḥ ।।

nirmālyaśuddhiṃ mantrajñaśśṛṇuṣvāvahito nagha ।
sadāśivādyā ye mantrāstāṃstu sampūjya pūrvataḥ ।।

paścāttu sagaṇaṃ devaṃ pūjayenmātṛnāyakam ।

(arudraḥ)।।

nirmālyaṃ kīdṛśaṃ deva sādhake vighnakārakam ।
kathayasva jagannātha devadevasya cāgrataḥ ।।

।। bhagavānuvāca ।।

kaivalyānna bhaveddoṣo nirmālyaṃ parivarjayet ।
yāvattiṣṭhati mantrātmā liṅgegnau sthaṇḍile jale ।।

tāvatsarvāṇi dravyāṇi na nirmālyāni tāni vai ।
liṅge vā sthaṇḍile mūrtau jale vā kamalopari ।।
(50b)
gurupādārcane caiva sarvaṃ nirmālyatāṃ vrajet ।
vinā kaivalyabhāvena bhāvayet kaśyapātmaja ।।

nirantaraṃ ca cinmārgaṃ manovuddhīndriyātmajam ।
svarūpasthaṃ svasaṃvedyametatkaivalyamacyute ।।

kaivalyapadasaṃsthāne nirmālyaṃ naiva gacchati ।
anyathā caṇḍadoṣaistu devaṃ gṛhyeta nārada ।।

calaliṅge catūre ca citta * * tathāgniṣu ।
sphāṭikeṣu na nirmālyaṃ mantraliṅgesu dehataḥ ।।

nirādhāraṃ kramaṃ jñātvā na nirmālyāni tāni vai ।
dāhasthāne tataḥ kṛtvā mantraṃ nabhasi vinyaset ।।

uddharet prakṛtaṃ mantraṃ manurūpaṃ tato * * ।
ṛkṣareṇurivākārā brahmacakre vyavasthitā ।।

bhūpadme dhyānamālambya nābhasaṃ ca * tatassa ।
anyathā kevalāmantryanirmālyaṃ nāharanti tāḥ ।।

parabrahma padācchuddhirnadāhaṃ * * * * *  ।
yātayāmaṃ ca yaddravyaṃ devatānāṃ purassthitam ।।

visarjite mantryanāthe yā havirdevatāśrayā ।
amantrābhāvarahitāssarvaṃ nirmālyatāṃ vrajet ।।

pārthivaṃ caiva pāṣāṇaṃ hematāraharītijam ।
netrarekhāṅkitaṃ liṅgaṃ sarvaṃ nirmālyatāṃ vrajet ।।

uddhṛtya bhāgaṃ mantrajñāssthāpayenmantrarakṣitam ।
agādhembhaṃsi nikṣipya vāyurecakavedhataḥ ।।

pūrvasrotaḥ kramāyātaṃ sarvaṃ nirmālyatāṃ vrajet ।
ityuktaṃ hi maheśena haṃsākhye pārameśvare ।।

yo hyavyaktasya liṅgasya caṇḍīśassamudāhṛtaḥ ।
(51a)
tena dṛṣṭaṃ tu yatkiñciddattamātraṃ tu pārvati ।।

liṅgaspṛṣṭopyaparyantaḥ puṣpādikabalistathā ।
dattaṃ vā yadi no dattaṃ tannirmālyamihocyate ।।

vyaktarūpasya liṅgasya dhātujasya viśeṣataḥ ।
pṛthakprapīṭhasaṃsthasya yannadattaṃ na tadbhavet ।।

viśeṣāddhātujānāṃ tu nirmālyaṃ naiva vidyate ।
yaddattaṃ caṇḍarudrāya dhaliśittaṃ tu pārvati ।।

tannirmālyamiti jñeyamanyathā naiva vidyate ।
nirmālyaṃ laṅghayedyastu jñātvā jñātvā tu dīkṣitaḥ ।।

tasya dīkṣāsāmayikī pavitrārohaṇaṃ tathā ।
kartavyaṃ guruṇā samyaganyathā narakaṃ vrajet ।।

nirmālyaṃ prāśayedyastu dīkṣito mohaghaṭṭitaḥ ।
api koṭiśataṃ jāpī rauravaṃ pratipadyate ।।

na tasya siddhiṃ paśyāmi kenāpi vidhinā śubhām ।
ajñātvā prāśayedyastu hyekavāraṃ tu darbhagaḥ ।।

candrāyaṇatrayaṃ kṛtvā puraśuddhiṃ sa gacchati ।
itthaṃ caṇḍeśanāthasya baliṃ bhuktvā tu pārvati ।।

laṅghanātsparśanāddānānmahāpāpaṃ prapadyate ।
nakhāgralagnaṃ liṅgārthaṃ likāmātramathāpi vā ।।

sa kulātpātayenmūḍho daśapūrvāndaśāparān ।
na tadyadevā gṛhṇanti nātithibrāhmaṇastathā ।।

liṅgopajīvinaṃ dṛṣṭvā hyaghorasyāyutaṃ japet ।
(51b)
pramādāttena dṛṣṭena lakṣajāpādviśuddhyati ।।

kaṭhadhūpaṃ maithunaṃ ca chāyāṃ caiva vijātiṣu ।
liṅgopajīvinaṃ dṛṣṭvā sadyassnānaṃ vidhīyate ।।

brahmahā śuddhyate kalpaṃ bhruṇahā pitṛghātakaḥ ।
śivadravyāpahārī ca kalpakoṭiśatairapi ।।

rauravastho na śuddhyeta satyametanna cānyathā ।
śrīkaṇṭha itthaṃ śrīkaṇṭhyāṃ+++ nirmālyavidhimādiśet ।।
devasvaṃ devatādravyaṃ naivedyaṃ ca nivedakam ।
caṇḍadravyaṃ ca nirmālyaṃ nirmālyaṃ ṣaṭvidhaṃ smṛtam ।।

grāmādīśasya devasvaṃ devadravyaṃ paṭādikam ।
naivedyaṃ kalpitaṃ tasmai devocchiṣṭaṃ nivedakam ।।

caṇḍadravyaṃ tu taddattaṃ nirmālyaṃ preritaṃ bahiḥ ।
piṇḍikāsthaṃ ca nirmālyamapi deve visarjite ।।

kravyādo jāyate dānānmātaṅgastasya bhakṣaṇāt ।
laṅghanātsiddhihāni syāt gatvopādānato vṛkaḥ ।।

caṇḍālasva yathādāne vikraye śavano bhavet ।
sparśanājjāyate strītvaṃ nirmālyasya na saṃśayaḥ ।।

yannirmālyena saṃspṛṣṭaṃ tadbhūjā puruṣeṇa vā ।
pādonaṃ tatra pūrvoktaṃ sparśalaṅghanabhakṣaṇaiḥ ।।

anuṣaṅgapraviṣṭaṃ tu candanādiprasādataḥ ।
tannirmālyacaturthāṃśe siddhaliṅgādidhāmasu ।।

gurupustakanāgānāṃ vahneryogigaṇasya ca ।
pārvatīyakṣamātṛṇāṃ na nirmālyaṃ śive yathā ।।

ratnahemādiliṅgeṣu cale citre pradakṣiṇe ।
(52a)
guruṣvapi na nirmālyaṃ divyāsu pratimāsu ca ।।

pūjāyā mantrapūjādi gandhe dīpa pra * * *  ।
nadī pravarte nirmālyaṃ na duṣyati kadācana ।।

sadyaḥ pratiṣṭhite liṅge cale vā yadi vā cale ।
caturthīkarmaparyantaṃ nirmālya ca na duṣyati ।।

bāṇaliṅge cale rohe siddhaliṅge svayambhuvi ।
pratimāsu ca sarvāsu na caṇḍodhikṛto bhavet ।।

advaitabhāvanāyukte sthaṇḍileśa vidhāvapi ।
evaṃ saṃskṛtya caṇḍeṣu caṇḍadoṣaṃ nivartayet ।।

nirmālyalakṣaṇaṃ ṣoḍhā proktaṃ śrīsomaśambhunā ।
daivadravyaṃ smṛtaṃ yatu na dadyātaddhi kasyacit ।।

nopekṣyaṃ nātmanāgrāhyaṃ devasvaṃ bhūṣaṇādikam ।
caṇḍīśadattamaśuci śuddhaṃ liṅgavyavasthitam ।।

pariṇāmavikāraṃ hi liṅgāddravyaṃ cyutaṃ bahiḥ ।

śuddhendudhautāya dhuni oṃ svāhā caṇḍāsvarāya huṃ phaṭ svāhā namaḥ ।

netrasthāne smaretkrodhaṃ netracaṇḍamanuṃ tathā ।
sadā nirmālyabhispṛṣṭhaṃ mukhādyaṃ ca na duṣyati ।।

svenaivāsanamantreṇa daṇḍā pūjyoṅgasaṃyutaḥ ।
vṛṣaṃ śūlaṃ tathā dvāsthāṃstanmantrārgheṇa pūjayet ।।

trivighnoccāṭanaṃ prāgvadbhūtaśuddhihṛdarcanam ।
pūrvavaccārghapātraṃ ca pūjā pranayanaṃ tataḥ ।।

dīkṣitānāmatra kaścidviśeṣaḥ procyate manāk ।
yadā visarjayenmantraṃ gacchelliṅgaṃ tu caṇḍatām ।।
(52b)
liṅge sannihite śambhau pūjāmapanayettu yaḥ ।
tasyāpanaya{nā}dyoṣo liṅgabhedasamo bhavet ।।

tasmādajātādīśāntaṃ brahmabhaṅgyātra pūjanam ।
liṅgamadhye vinikṣipya prabhuṃ bindutanuṃ smaret ।।

liṅgātpūjāmīśadiśi pīṭhapṛṣṭhe niveśayet ।
puṣpamādāya vāmena pūjāṃ dakṣiṇapāṇinā ।।

tūrṇaṃ vāmakarātpuṣpaṃ dadyā savyena pāṇinā ।
* * * * lliṅgamūrdhāno dadyāt saṃkṣato budhaḥ ।।

vighnairāvāryate ghorairliṅgaṃ yacchūnyamastakam ।
pīṭhapṛṣṭhāttu tāṃ pūjāṃ tyajeccaṇḍīśamantragām ।।

puṣpaṃ datvā mahārgheṇa kṣamasveti visarjayet ।
tatoktamantraliṅge vā bāṇaliṅge calepi vā ।।

vahnau kṣipedagādhe vā na caṇḍīśaprakalpanam ।
astrāmbhasāssavasatau gomayena sthale śucim ।।

kuryāttredhā yadā dehe bindusparśaśirovinā ।
prakṣālayettadantaṃ tu śirasparśe tu majjanam ।।

liṅge nivedayet paścāt sakṛtsaṃhitayā budhaḥ ।
arghapātrodake naiva tālā yoge * vipruṣaḥ ।।

liṅgamūrdhaṃ nidātavyā recanaṃ samudāhṛtam ।
sthūlasūkṣmaparādvighnāṃlliṅgapīṭhagṛhāśritān ।।

yadyaddravyaṃ bhavettatra tāṃstadrupatayā sthitān ।
nāśayenmanasā mantrī hetisnānaṃ samācaret ।।

karmodyetairvinirdiṣṭo vidhirnirmālya vastuni ।
(53a)
dvividhāstu gaṇāḥ proktā liṅgamāśritya saṃsthitāḥ ।।

teṣāṃ protsāraṇaṃ kuryādyena śuddhirbhaviṣyati ।
bhūtajā mantradehāstu muktidehāstathā pare ।।
triṣu sthāneṣu tiṣṭhanti liṅgavighnā mahābalāḥ ।
prāsāde bhūtajā jñeyā mantradehāstu pīṭhagāḥ ।।

muktidehāstu vai liṅge hyupajīvanti śaṅkaram ।
bṛhatkālottare śāstre nirdiṣṭamiti śambhunā ।।

dvidhāstu gaṇāḥ proktāste bṛhadvāthule yathā ।
rajotkaṭā tu prathamaṃ caṇḍarudraṃ dvitīyakam ।।

raudrarūpaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu caṇḍāgraṃ ca caturthakam ।
pañcamaṃ tu mahācaṇḍaṃ sitavaktraṃ tu ṣaṣṭhakam ।।

saptamaṃ sitanādaṃ tu grasanaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ viduḥ ।
umārūpadharāvighnaḥ pīṭhamārge vyavasthitāḥ ।।

piṅgalo harahuṅkāraḥ kṛṣṇapiṅgalabhāsuraḥ ।
kālāntagaḥ kapālīśastathā saṃhārakārakaḥ ।।

liṅgarūpadharāvighnā liṅgasyoparisaṃsthitāḥ ।
ete vighnā mahāraudrāssarve te yāgahārakaḥ ।।

pūjanīyā prayatnena śivāstreṇāgrarūpiṇā ।
liṅgaṃ prakṣālya medhāvī śivāstreṇa na saṃśayaḥ ।।

mahāpāśupadastreṇa pīṭhaṃ prakṣālayediti ।

sarveṣāmeva mantrāṇāṃ netrassādhāraṇassmṛtaḥ ।।

tathaivāvyaktaliṅgānāṃ bālaṃ liṅga praśasyate ।
ādhārādheyayorbāṇaliṅgamṛtyujitorapi ।।
(53b)
sarvasādhāraṇatvaṃ hi mahālakṣmīmate sthitam ।
yatra śrīmṛtyujinmantrassādhyāssādhāraṇopi vā ।।

pratiṣṭhitastatra nāsti nirmālyaṃ na ca saṅkaraḥ ।
tathā ca dṛśyata idaṃ * * pañcāśikāgame ।।

tannirmālyaṃ na jāneva mantreṇānena jāyate ।
pratiṣṭhāyāṃ pūjane ca * * * rarghapātrayoḥ ।।

agnisaṃskaraṇo caiva nyāsadhyānādike tathā ।
vikalpo naiva kartavyassarvasādhāraṇo yataḥ ।।

saṅkaro naiva jāyeta ta * * * parityajet ।
mṛtyuñjayavidhānepi proktametacchivena tu ।।

।। iti trividhavighnaśāntaye strastrapanama ।।

tatrāstrakṣālite kuryāt prāgvadāsanakalpanam । sakusumamarghaṃ datvā kālāgreḥ prabhṛti cintaya * ptim ।  ādhāraśaktimādau taduparikandārhatiṃ pṛṣṭhīm ।
māyāsroto garbhita pañcāśadbhāvakitanālam । tadadhiṣṭhānatva sthita māyāgratyūvavanti susitarucividyeśvaracchadāṣṭakaniyatamantraughakesarapracayam ।
karṇikayaiva parigrahavartitā sobhitāntaraṃ śaktyā vāmādiśaktinavakaskuṭanavabījaṃ prakalpya vidyābjam ।
(54a)
praṇavārambhakamātrāṃ tritayaṃ sūryenduvahnibimbanirupama tejaḥ prasaradalakesarakarṇikāsu saṃcintya hlādai prakāśahetuṃ vinyasennilāṃ parāṃ mūrtim ।
saṃkṣiptāsanametaddhi tataṃ punarucyate kramaśaḥ । saptarasātalaniṣṭhā samāvāhya kālāgniśivam । pṛthivī dugdhāndhiratastaduparikamalaṃ sahasradalaṅkṛṣṭha
agneyyādiṣu tasmiṃcchadeṣu dharmādikaṃ nyaset । uttaradakṣiṇapaścimapūrvāmbhoruhada * * dharmādyam । anayordharmādigaṇo vivṛddhaye nyastva bādhārthaḥ ।
sitaraktapītākṛṣṇāḥ krame * rmādiriha caturvargaḥ yo madhyamastayoreva varṇaniyogatva{sau} śabalaḥ । dharmādyāni  * rmo jñānaṃ ca vairāgyasahitamaiśvaryam ।
na ca uttarāṇyadharmādīni tu tānyeva catvāri । pī * pādabhūtaṃ dharmādyaṣṭakamudāhṛtaṃ yasya catvārastasya yugāḥ kṛtādayo daṇḍavaddhyeyāḥ ।
prāgastadadhasta eva sitākta kṛṣṇarucaḥ hastadvaya dhṛtadaṇḍa * * * * । yastadupari vedāsyuḥ * * * * divarṇayuktāśśivonmukhā viracitāñjalayaḥ ।
kiñca yugānyatra kṛtaṃ tretādau dvāparakaliyugeti ṛgyajuṣī vedā api sāmātharvākhyayā gītāḥ ।
(54b)
pīṭhasyopari māyātiryatkṛtvā kṛti * tāgneyyāḥ ravirūpendunibhānyā vidyaiśānyāḥ prabhṛtyevam । etatpīṭhabhūyassakarṇikaṃ saṃ * tu patraśatapatram ।
kādicatuṣṭaya bhāntairyaśavargaiśkliptakiñjalkam । dalakesara * * * * kuṇḍalakaṭisphuṭitacchāyāruṇakāntagaṇḍa * * mukhanirjite bimbāssunitambagā
svaroddeśāḥ āgneyyāḥ prabhṛti daleṣu śaktayoṣṭhau ca । vāmādyā vāmā jyeṣṭhā raudrī śaktiḥ kālī kalavikaraṇī ca balavikaraṇī balaprathanī sarvabhūtadamanī ca ।
āsāṃ {ca} pṛṣṭhadeśe raśanāvadasthitā parā devī nāmnā manonmanīti jñeyā śaktyuttamā navamī । ityāstaṃ na varamuktaṃ vibhunā nandīśvarāvatāranaye rurusūtreṣu ca
yenoktamukulyā cāsanākṛti āsanaṃ vibhajenmantrī kramaśaḥ padmamudrayā । ityāsanasya dve mudre । badhnīyādaṃṣate kramāt ।

lakṣaṇaṃ ca tayoktaṃ tatraiva vibhunā yathā।
añjaliṃ niyataṃ kṛtvā kṛddeśesu samāhitaḥ ।।

mukulīṃ tu vijānīyāt kiñci{t} saṅkucitāṅgulim ।
prasārayedubhau hastau hṛddeśe tu niyāmitau ।।

aṅgulyo visṛtāḥ kāryāḥ padmamudrāṃ tu darśayet ।
nyāsastu siktaiḥ kusumaiḥ kāryo * gandhagolakaiḥ ।।

ityāsananyāsavidhau dravyabhedosti pauṣkare ।
(55a)
saprameyo paro nyāso darśyate sādhakādiṣu ।।

śaktinyāso bhavet pūrva karmaṃ tu tadanantaram ।
aṅkuranā * * * * * * * parikalpayet ।।

tejomayaṃ mahāśubhraṃ sphuratkiraṇabhāsuram ।
itthaṃ jñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ ca kramānnyaset ।।

sitaraktapītakṛṣṇā āgneyyā īśadiggatāḥ ।
pādakāssiharūpāste trinetrā bhīmaviśrutaḥ ।।

śivaśaktimayā mantryā nyastavyā vīravandite ।
adharmo jñānavairāgyamanaiśvaryaṃ tu prāgdiśaḥ ।।

uttarāntaṃ niveśyaṃ tu gātrakāsmitavarṇakāḥ ।
sandhānakīlakāścaiva hyatasī puṣpasannibhā ।।

vedā yugāśca te śete jñātavyāḥ kramaśāḥ priye ।
adhacchadanamūrdhaṃ ca raktaṃ śuklaṃ vicintayet ।।

sa * tamo vijānīyāḥ kesarāṇi vicintayet ।
sitaraktaprapītāni mūlamadhyāgradeśataḥ ।।

karṇikā hemasaṅkāśā bijāni haritāni tu ।
vāsāṃ pūrvadale nyasejjyeṣṭhāṃ vahnidaliśritām ।।

raudrīṃ dakṣiṇapatre tu kālīṃ nindati gocaram ।
kalavikaraṇīṃ devīṃ vinyasedvāruṇe dale ।।

balavikariṇīṃ nyasedvāyavyadalamāśritam ।
balapramathinīṃ devīmuttare viniveśayet ।।

sarvabhūtadamanīśaktimaiśānyāṃ viniveśayet ।
madhye manonmanīṃ devī vāyavyaṃ śaktimaṇḍalam ।।

(55b)
madhye sūryasahasrābhāṃ cintayettu manonmanīm ।
sūrya huṃ maṇḍalaṃ pattre somaṃ vinyasya kesare ।।

va * maṇḍalakaṃ devi karṇikāyāṃ na veśayet ।
brahmaviṣṇurharaścaiva maṇḍaleṣvabhidhāssmṛtāḥ ।।

mahāpretaṃ nyaset paścāt prahasantaṃ sa cetanam ।
praṇaveṇa nyasetsarvamāsanaṃ bhairavasya tu ।।

gandhaiḥ puṣpaissamabhyarcya tato muktiṃ prakalpayet ।
kadā kusumākāraṃ * tuṣāra nikara dviṣam ।।

mū{rja}rdhve bhairavaṃ devaṃ sakalaṃ parikalpayet ।
dvātriṃśadvarṇaracitaṃ sphurattaḍidiva jvalam ।।

vaktrāṇi kalpayeddevi sudhyānena maheśvari ।
mūrdhādicaraṇaṃ yāvat praṇavādi namontataḥ ।।

aṣṭātriṃśatkalābhedaṃ śodhyādhyānaṃ prakalpayet ।
navatatvaṃ tritatvaṃ ca na(va)kaṃ bhairavābhidham ।।

vidyāṅgācalanaṃ caiva kṣurikāṃ ca prakalpayet ।
śaktitrayaṃ tato nyasyeddakṣadigvāmagocare ।।

madhyapradeśe deveśi tato rūpamanusmaret ।
svacchandabhairavaṃ devaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam ।।

dhyāyedvai yastu yuktātmā kṣipraṃ siddhyati mānavaḥ ।
yā sā pūrvaṃ samākhyātā aghorī śaktiruttamā ।।

bhairavaṃ pūjayitvā tu tasyotsaṅgagatāṃ nyaset ।
yādṛśaṃ bhairavaṃ rūpaṃ bhairavyāstadvadeva hi ।।

tataḥ paramabījena paraṃ paramakāraṇam ।
svaśāntaṃ niṣkalaṃ śuddhaṃ sarvavyāpinirañjanam ।।

(56a)
niṣkampaṃ karaṇātītamāvāhya parameśvaram ।
saṃsthāpyavidhivaddevamaṅgaṣaṭkaṃ tato nyaset ।।

pādyamācamanaṃ cārghassvāgataṃ tadanantaram ।
sannidhānaṃ ca deveśi niṣṭhurāyāṃ nirodhayet ।।

gandhaiḥ puṣpaistathādhūpairdhūpayitvā tamarcayet ।
nyāsaścāvāhanavidhisvacchande suprakāśitaḥ ।।

hṛtpuṇḍarīkamadhyasthaṃ caitanyaṃ jyotiravyayam ।
kadambakusumākāraṃ viśvarūpaṃ maṇiprabham ।।

ratnadīpamivākāraṃ sadbhinnaprasaraṃ mahat ।
śrotrapūgaiḥ karandhraiśca radmavastuvinirgataḥ ।।

chidrapūrṇānyaṣātumbhānmahaddīpayutāddvija ।
yāti bhāsā gaṇaṃ bādhye śarīrādevameva hi ।।

ityevaṃ mantrasāmarthyaṃ purā jñātvā yathārthataḥ ।
sannidhānaṃ bhavedyena pūjākāle hyupasthite ।।

svaprabodhaṃ mahānandaṃ sarvabhāveṣu bhāvayet ।
bahirucchalitaṃ devi darpaṇapratibimbavat ।।

vyajanena yathāvāyussarvagopyanuruddhyate ।
tathā sarvagato devo yāgādiṣvanuruddhyate ।।

anapekṣasya va * vaccitrāthasya mahātmanaḥ ।
parispandodayagrāmamāvāhanavisarjane ।।

dīpāddīpana * * tvatsva caitanyaṃ tathā vahiḥ ।
bhinnābhinnaṃ bhāvayecca tarpaṇapratibimbanam ।।

etadāvāhanaṃ samyakchrījayā yā nirūpitam ।
cakreśvaramate devyā etadeva prakāśitam ।।
(56b)
।। ityāvāhanaprayojanam ।।

paścādaśeṣatatvavyāpitvāmāhuyecchivaṃ mūrtau । uccāryamūlamantraplutāntamudyantṛtāvalambanataḥ । brahmādikāraṇeśvaraniṣevitebhyaḥ padebhya udgamya  dhvaninidhanadhāmni
manāṅgrilīya sṛṣṭikrameṇa vinivṛtya ciccittapavanavācaka vācyānāṃ sāmarasya manubhūya । hṛtpuṇḍarīkamadhyāt sphuradanupamadhāmatārakākāram । sauṣumṇena pathātaṃ
saṅgṛhya ca śāntamudrayā nyaset । śrīnārāyaṇakaṇṭhe neṭyadiṣṭaṃ nijapaddhatau ।

savyasuṣṭhutratāṅguṣṭhā śānteyaṃ samprakīrtitā ।
sthāpane sarvamantrāṇāṃ prayoktavyā vicakṣaṇaiḥ ।।

iti svāyambhuve śāstre śāntamudrāṃ śivo vadat ।
uttānau tu karau kṛtvā aṅguṣṭhātalamadhyagau ।।

āvāhanī tviyaṃ proktā mantrāvāhanakarmaṇi ।
muṣṭhībaddhordhvagāṅguṣṭhau mudreyaṃ sthāpane parā ।।

muṣṭhībaddhvā punaḥ kṛtvā aṅguṣṭhau karamadhyagau ।
niṣṭhureti samākhyātā devadevasya rodhane ।।

kaniṣṭhāṃ kuñcayitvā tu aṅguṣṭhenākramedvudhaḥ ।
śaktimudreti vikhyātā sānnidhyakaraṇī matā ।।

।। iti kiraṇāgamagaditaṃ baddhvāmudrācatuṣṭayaṃ kramataḥ ।।
(57a)
āhvānasaṃsthāpanasannirodhasaṃyuktasannidhītkuryāt । atra prayogaḥ ।
oṃ bhagavannāgaccha 2 । oṃ bhagavansantiṣṭha 2 । oṃ bhagavansanniruddho bhavata । oṃ bhagavansannidhatsva ।

aṅguṣṭhamunnataṃ kṛtvā muṣṭhiṃ pāṇau tu dakṣiṇe ।
prakṣipeddhastamadye tu vāmahastasya mantravit ।।

dakṣiṇe tu tathā pāṇau śeṣāḥ kuryāttathopari ।
liṅgamudrā mahāvīryetyuktvā rauravaśāsane ।।

baddhvāliṅgābhidhāmudrāṃ vidadhyādavaguṇṭhanam ।
ānandāyatanaṃ loke dṛṣṭaṃ gupta{ta}raṃ yataḥ ।।

tasmādānandarūpatvātsyācchivasyāvaguṇṭhanam ।
iti śrībrahmaśambhurhi jagāda nijapaddhatau ।।

brahmakalaṅgaiḥ prānvat svatanunyāsānusāreṇa sakalīkṛtyekatvaṃ nirantaraṃ sārdhasaṅgināyansyāt । amṛtīkaraṇaṃ tadidaṃ kartavyaṃ mūlamantreṇa ।
āsanavinyāsaḥ prāksakalīkaraṇaṃ tathāhvānam । iti yatkarmatritayaṃ pratipāditamarcane śambhoḥ। vidyātsaśivābhikhyasti tatvanyāsa uktosau ।
ūce tathā ca devaśśrīpauṣkare pārameśvare śāstre ।

vidyātatvaniveśoyamāsenākhyaśśivasya tu ।
ātmatatvasya vinyasya sassakalīkaraṇaṃ tataḥ ।।

āvāhayettato devaṃ sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ param ।
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ śivatatvaṃ tu yatsmṛtam ।।

śivātmavidyātatvāni trīṇyetānyanupūrvaśaḥ ।
(57b)
viditvā sādhakaḥ kṣipraṃ mantrasiddhimavāpnuyāt ।।

।। iti rurusiddhānte ca tritatvabodhātphalaṃ jagau bhagavān ।।

hastāṃbhyāṃ saṃspṛśetpādau urdhvaṃ yāvacca mastakam ।
eṣā mudrā mahāmudrā sarvakāmārthasādhakī ।।

iti kālottaraśāstre kathitā yā śambhunā pūrvam ।
tadanu mahāmudrāyā: kuryādbandhaṃ prarocanaṃ karam ।।

bhagaliṅgī karaṇaṃ syāddeśinyaṅguṣṭhapīḍanataḥ ।
oṃ tvaṃ gaurī tathā devī liṅgaṃ caiva bhagādinā ।।

oṃ gauri padmadhāriṇi 11

oṃ bhagasya hṛdayaṃ liṅgaṃ liṅgasya hṛdayaṃ bhagaḥ ।
tasmai tai bhagaliṅgāya rumārudrāya vai namaḥ ।।

iti pauṣkaraśāstroktavidhānenuṣṭhite sati ।
pādyādīnāṃ pradānasya vidhiryassobhidhīyate ।।

āvāhanerghapādyādidhūpāntaṃ ca prayojayet ।
itthaṃ rauravamastre ca devenoktaṃ yatastasmāt ।।

pādyācamanīyārghāḥ pādāsya śirassu dātavyāḥ। sammukha dakṣiṇa hastenordhvaṃ mūrdhani tathaiva puṣpaṃ ca । atra prayogaḥ । oṃ bhagavanpādyaṃ gṛhāna namaḥ ।
oṃ bhagavannācamanīyaṃ gṛhāṇa svadhā । oṃ bhagavannarghaṃ gṛhāṇa svāhā । oṃ bhagavanpuṣpaṃ gṛhāṇa vauṣaṭ ।

āvāhanādi pragoyaṃ śrīviśvāvarta bhāvitaḥ ।
(58a)
āvāhanyāhṛdāvāhya sthāpanyā sthāpya śaṅkaram ।।

sannidhānāntikaṃ nītvā niṣṭhurāyā nibodhayet ।
kālakarṇyā phaḍantena vighnāśca * * riṣṭhinā ।।

lliṅgamudrāṃ namaskāraṃ hṛdā baddhvāvaguṇṭhayet ।
sādaraṃ sammukhībhāva āvāhanamihocyate ।।

bhaktyā niveśanaṃ tatra sthāpanaṃ kathitaṃ budhaiḥ ।
jagannātha tavāsmīti yatsvīkāro ca darśanam ।।

vibhutvepi tadeśasya sannidhānaṃ na cānyathā ।
ākarmakāṇḍaparyantaṃ sannidhāyo paritvayaḥ ।।

saśambhorvyāpakatvena nirodhaḥ parigīyate ।
śivasyānandabījasya bodhānandamayasya ca ।।

abhakteṣvaprakāśo yastadbhavedavaguṇṭhanam ।
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā mantraiṣṣaḍbhirathaikatām ।।

* * nāmaṅginā sārdhaṃ vidadhyādamṛtī kṛtim ।
yathādhṛṣyassamarthaśca mārtāṇḍaścaṇḍaraśmabhi ।।

tadvadaṅgairnijairdevo dhyātavyaḥ parameśvaraḥ ।
hṛdayaṃ tasya cicchanti śśiraścaiśvaryamaṣṭabhiḥ ।।

nānyādhīnna vaśitvaṃ yat sā śikhoparigīyate ।
abhedhyaṃ parameśasya bhejaḥ kavacamucyate ।।

dāhāpyāya prakāśārthaṃ tejo yattaddhi netragam ।
pratāpo dussahaśśāstramantarāyāpahārakam ।।

namassvāhā tathā vauṣaṭ huṃ vaṣaṭphaṭkrameṇa tu ।
(58b)
hṛtpurassaramuccārya pādyādīni nivedayet ।।

pādyaṃ pādāmbujadvandve vaktreṣvācamanīyakam ।
arghaṃ śirasya devasya puṣpadūrvākṣatāni ca ।।

evaṃ saṃskṛtya saṃskārairṛṣibhiḥ parameśvaram ।
yajot pañcopacāreṇa vidhināṃ kusumādibhiḥ ।।

śrīsomaśambhunādiṣṭamāhvānāditi paddhatau ।
pāpātpāśamalairgrastānaṇuvai mocayatti yāḥ।।

muñcanti mantravṛkṣasya phalāni vividhāni ca ।
puṣṇanti bhavaduḥkhāni muhyato bodhayanti ca।।

rakṣo yakṣoragādīṃśca grahātkrūrānvināyakān।
anyāni duṣṭasatvāni drāvayanti sahasraśaḥ ।।

dravanti sādhakavaraṃ saukhyāmṛtasaśīkaraiḥ ।
ubhayārthaprasiddhyarthaṃ muktāḥ proktāśśivena tu ।।

vighnoghamudraṇānmudrāḥ kathyante haraśaktayaḥ ।
yato na vastato nyasya dṛśyate kṛtyamīdṛśam 2 ।।

mudrā tu trividhā jñeyā manovākkāyasambhavā ।
manojñā guruvaktrasthā vāgbhavā mantravigraha ।।

dehodbhavāṅgavikṣepairmudreyaṃ trividhā smṛtā ।
kāyakī karabandhena vācikī mantrapūrvikā ।।
jvalacchaktisamuccārānmudrā jñeyā tu mānasī ।। 3 ।।

itthaṃ mudrānirvacanamumāyāmalasaṃjñake ।
mṛgendre vāsaneyāyāṃ tridhā samupadarśitam ।।
(59a)
namaskāro dhvajaścaiva tathā ca śaśikarṇikā ।
muktālī padmamudrā ca tathā cāvāhanī bhavet ।।

niṣṭhurā kālakarṇī ca liṅgamudrā tathaiva ca ।
etā nava mahāmudrā prayoktavyāssadā vidhau ।।

etā sā lakṣaṇaṃ brahmankīrtyamānaṃ nibodhamat ।
caturvidhānāṃ mantrāṇāṃ parivārasthitātmanām ।।

mudrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ jñātvā navānāṃ yathāsthitam ।
avaikalyamavāpnoti sarvatraivaṃvidho budhaḥ ।।

karābhyāṃ sampuṭaṃ kṛtvā saṃhataprasṛtāṅguli ।
namaccharo bhavenmudrā vāmāṅguṣṭhopavītinaḥ ।।

gṛhītvā tarjanī vāmā muṣṭinā dakṣiṇena tu ।
ūrdhvaṃ kṛtvā tathāṅguṣṭhaṃ vāmaśliṣṭāṅgulidhvajaḥ ।।

tarjanyaṅguṣṭhayoragramāśliṣya karayordvayoḥ ।
utānau maṇibandhau ca mudrāsyācchaśikarṇikā ।।

ākāśe mukulākārau kārau tu mukulī smṛtā ।
padmamudrā bhavetsaiva sarvatra prasṛtāṅguliḥ ।।

uttānau tu karau kṛtvā saṃhataṃ prasṛtāṅgulī ।
aṅguṣṭhau tiryagānīya mudrā tvāvahanī bhavet ।।

aṅguṣṭhagarbhamuṣṭhī tu saṃhitau niṣṭhurā smṛtā ।
saivordhvasammukhāṅguṣṭhā kālakarṇī prakīrtitā ।।

vāmahastāṅgulicchatrā saṅkhyārdhvāṅguṣṭhadakṣiṇam ।
liṅgamudrā bhavedeṣā sarvamudrāgranāyikā ।।

kṛtvā tu karayo nyāsā śaktīnāṃ tu yathoditam ।
(59b)
bandhassvahṛdaye kāryo mudrāṇāṃ sādhakena tu ।।

saṃyuktassarvamantrāṇāṃ hṛddeśe bandha iṣyate ।
anye yathopadiṣṭāstu karaṇīyo vijānatā ।।

mudrābandho na prakāśaḥ kartavyo mantravādinā ।
gurunivāse mudrāṇāṃ bandhamevaṃ prayojayet ।।

pratyakṣaṃ samayajñānāṃ mudrābandhaprakāśanam ।
anyatra nopadeśā tu prakurvīta kadācana ।।

ṣaṭaguptau karau kṛtvā mantrī mudrāviśāradaḥ ।
badhnīyādanyathā vighnā jāyantūdrāvakā vidhau ।।

āsane mukulī yuñjyet padmamudrāṃ ca sādhakaḥ ।
namaskārasamāstisrāḥ pādeṣvāvāhapaścimāḥ ।।

mukulīṃ karṇikāyāṃ tu padmāṃ padmadaleṣu ca ।
kesareṣu ca yuñjīta tathā bījeṣu mantravit ।।

prabhuśaktyā niyuñjīta dvajaṃ vidyādhipepi ca ।
āsaneṣu ca gātreṣu caturṣvapi vidhānavit ।।

āvāhanīmajātasya vāmasya śaśikarṇikām ।
niṣṭhurāmapyaghorasya kālakarṇīṃ mukhasya ca ।।

īśasya liṅgamudrāṃ tu śivasya ca niyojayet ।
namaskāraṃ ca śiraso dhvajaṃ ca hṛdayasya tu ।।

śaśikarṇī śikhāyāṃ ca niṣṭhurā kavacasya tu ।
kālakarṇīmathāstasya bhujayorubhayorapi ।।

śaktīnāṃ padmamudrā syānmukulyantāḥ prayojayet ।
hṛdādīnāṃ dhvajādyāsu namaskārāvasānikāḥ ।।


(60a)
mūrtimānāṃ kramādaṣṭau namaskārapurassarāḥ ।
ūrdhvādhassarvamūrtīnāṃ liṅgamudrāṃ prayojayet ।।

āvāhyādyāstu padmāntāścakreśānāṃ prayojayet ।
nandino dhvajamudrāṃ tu mukulīṣaṇmukha * * ।।

kālasya niṣṭhurāṃ yuñjya * ṅgenāvāhanī tathā ।
vṛṣasya padmamudrāṃ tu namaskāro gaṇendrake ।।

īśānaliṅgamudrāṃ tu kuberasya tu karṇikām ।
dhvajamudrāṃ samīrasya mukulīṃ varuṇasya tu ।।

nivṛtenniṣṭhure yuñjyāḥ kālakarṇīṃ yamasya tu ।
agrerāvāhanī yuñjyādbrahmaṇo dhvajameva ca ।।

padmamudrāmanantasya yojayenmantravittamaḥ ।
astrajātasya sarvasya kāśakarṇī prayojayet ।।

mātṛbhūtagaṇānāṃ tu noktānāṃ caiva mantravit ।
uttiṣṭhadhvajamudrāṃ hyāntardhāye tu niṣṭhurām ।।

āvāhane pādacāre pātāle kālakarṇikām ।
padmamudrāṃ tu saubhāgye ākarṣe vāhanīṃ tathā ।।

uktā * kteṣu sarveṣu liṅgamudrāṃ prayojayet ।
namaskāreṇa vandeta dhvajena dhyānamācaret ।।

śaśikarṇāṃ niyamyeta mukulyā cāsanākṛtiḥ ।
āsanaṃ vibhajenmantrī kramaśaḥ padmamudrayā ।।

āvāhanīmarghapādye dhūpe caiva prayojayet ।
niṣṭhurāyānirundīta vigrātsaubhāgyameva ca ।।

kālakarṇyāpracāṭeta sarvasthānaprayojitām ।
(60b)
padmamudrāṃ prayuñjīta patreṣu kusumīṣu ca ।।

annapāne namaskāraṃ bhakṣajāte prayojayet ।
yojanāliṅgamudrāyāśśivarūpe hyudāhṛtā ।।

bandho mantraprayogaśca karmayogavidhistathā ।
proktaṃ navānāṃ mudrāṇāṃ puṣkare pārameśvare ।।

pādyādācamanī{yā}ccetthaṃ śivasamabhyarcya ।
abhyajyodvartya ca śananirvicchamayedvidhānena ।।

koṣṇasu (gga)nda payasā pṛṣṭvā prakṣālyaṃ ukṣayeccūrṇaiḥ ।
bandhodakartatodadyātsaharidrā priyaṅgukam ।
uśīracandanopetaṃ nityameva prayojayet ।।

itthaṃ gauriyāmalaśāstre gandhāṣu coditaṃ snānaṃ tena hṛdā saṃsnapayedasinā dhūpotha varmaṇārghasthāt ।

pracchanne śubhabhubhāge sahasreṇa śatena vā ।
ardhārdhena tadardhena daśabhiścāṣṭabhiśca vā ।।

liṅgapramāṇitassnānaṃ sauvarṇādivikalpitaiḥ ।
akālamūlaiḥ kalaśairabhāvānmṛṇmayairatha ।।

iti saṅkhyāvidhiproktaḥ śrīgaurīyāmalāgame ।
mṛṇmayaiḥ kalaśaindevi yajanaṃ ye prakurvate ।।

brāhmaṃ varṣāśataṃ devi rudraloke vasanti te ।
tathā śataguṇaṃ tāmre raupye lākṣādhikaṃ bhavet ।।

hemajaiḥ koṭiguṇitaṃ rudraloke mahatsukham ।
yathā puṇyaphalaṃ devi śivadharmeṣu coditam ।।

adbhiścatuṣpalaiḥ pūrṇaṃ gaṇḍukaṃ hemajādikam ।
mudgādyaṅgulamātrasya calasya snapane hitam ।।
(61a)
śeṣāṇāṃ cāṅgulairbuddhyā vṛddhistripalakādiṣu ।
hastamātre sthire {liṅge} gantukaṃ prasthasasmitam ।।

anyeṣāṃ karavṛddhyāsyādvṛddhistvaṣṭapalaiḥ kramāt ।
prāha vairocanācāryo gaḍukānāmamuṃvidhim ।।

kaḍukānāṃ pradānasya vidhiśśrīsomaśambhunā ।
svapaddhatau yobhihitostacchlokaissa nirūpyate ।।

anantaramanuṣṇaiva mantrajaptena vāriṇā ।
yathecchaṃ snapayettatra vidhiryassobhidhīyate ।।

vāmahastatale kṛtvā gaḍukaṃ liṅgamastakāt ।
kusumaṃ pūrvavinyastaṃ tadargheṇāpanīya ca ।।

dhautapāṇissvamantreṇa śaṅkhamudrāpariplutaḥ ।
jalairanucchaladbhiśca snapayedasamastakaiḥ ।।

payasā dadhisarpibhyāṃ madhunā sitayāpi vā ।
dadyātpañcabhiramṛtairebhirdatvā snānaṃ statodakāntaritaiḥ ।।

evaṃ vyāptiṃ dātuṃ kṣīrodādītsmaret kramaśaḥ।
ghṛtodaṃ tu ghṛtasnāne mantravitparikalpayet ।।

kṣirodaṃ tu payaḥ snāne mantravitparikalpayet ।
dadhyambhodadhisecane madhudaṃ ca madhusnāne।।

svādūdovārisecane ।

pañcāmṛte phalamidaṃ proktaṃ pauṣkaraśāsane ।
yāvaḥ pṛthivyāṃ yāvatyastaddānātphalatulyatām ।।

yātiliṅge jalasnānāgatyaiśśataguṇaṃ tataḥ ।
gandhasnānācchataguṇaṃ kṣīrasnānaṃ tatastathā ।।

dadhisnānaṃ śataguṇaṃ tadvājyena tataśca tam ।
(61b)
sadyaḥ pītitadṛddhīkārasenaitatsamaṃ phalam ।।

sādhakasya vinirdiṣṭaṃ śrīmaddharmaśivena tu ।
advaitamantrayuktāye tathā bāṇāssvayambhuvaḥ ।।

vāmāmṛtādibhirdravyaiḥ proktaṃ teṣāṃ tu tarpaṇam ।
na hi ta{ta} sthāpite liṅge siddhānte mantrakalpite ।।

jalācchataguṇaṃ gāndhaṃ gandhācchataguṇaṃ payaḥ ।
kṣirācchataguṇaṃ dādhnaṃ dadhnaśśataguṇaṃ smṛtam ।।

ghṛtācchataguṇaṃ vāma dakṣiṇaśāsane ।
ghṛtatulyaṃ madhusnānaṃ drākṣācekṣurasaṃ samam ।।

tasmācchreṣṭhataraṃ snānaṃ surayā{vā}śavena vā ।
{tṛ}ṇāgrabindumātreṇa liṅgasnānetha tarpaṇe ।।

kṛtaṃ bhavati tattulyaṃ maśvamedhaśataissamam ।
vāme kuryāttrisiddhānte vihitaṃ bhairave sadā ।।

bhairave tu prapannānāṃ snānaṃ snānaṃ phalādhikam ।
aghorīpañcaśatake proktametaddhi śambhunā ।।

ambhasā payasā dadhnā ghṛtena madhunā tathā ।
vrīhibhiścoṣadhībhiśca phalaiḥ puṣpaissamāsataḥ ।।

siddhārthakaistathālājairgandhāmbhobhirmahādhiye ।
evaṃ dvādaśadhā proktaṃ mahāsnānaṃ maheśvari ।।

svayasthīrakalpeyaṃ mahāsnānavidhismṛtaḥ ।
snānaṃ palaśataṃ jñeyamabhyaṅgaṃ pañcaviṃśatiḥ ।।

palānāṃ dve sahasre tu mahāsnāne vidhīyataiḥ ।
(62a)
śivadharmeṣviti snānadravyāṇāṃ mānamīritam ।।

kanyasottamamadhyaṃ ca snānaṃ trividhamucyate ।
dadhighṛtaṃ payaḥ kṣaudraṃ śatenāṣṭottareṇa tu ।।

ekaikasya tu suśroṇi snānaṃ madhyamamuttamam ।
aṣṭottarasahasreṇa kanyasaṃ tu palāṣṭakam ।।

ityuktaṃ tu maheśena devyāyāmalaśāsanam ।
kuryādaharahaścāddhamantrādye nodakena vā ।।

tṛptāssvargaṃ pradhāntyasya pitaro narakārṇavāt ।
śrutyeta * śivassnāpyaḥ pittṛddelena gaḍukaiḥ ।।

gandhāmbhaḥ phalakusumaiḥ pūrṇaṃ sampūjya saṃhitāmantraiḥ ।। abhimantrya śubhakala{śaṃ} karayugalasampuṭe vidhṛtam । hṛtkaṇṭhatālubindubrahmabilānāṃ vibhedanakramataḥ ।
svadvādaśāntalīnaṃ satraṃ saṃcintya pūrṇaśaśitulyam । tadamṛtadhārāsāraṃ nipatitamitayā vibhāvya tasyāntaḥ । bindusthānaṃ yāvante notkṣiptena devamabhiṣi * ।
itthamībhiṣiśyaṃ sitamṛdusugandhivāsasā sapīṭhaṃ tat । utpuṃsya candanādyairvilipya datvātha muktapuṣpāṇi nitvicchasanakakumbhaḥ parito trāsyāstreṇa bāhyataḥ kṣepyaḥ।

tato nirbhatsanaṃ kāryaṃ mantreṇānena mantravit ।
gṛhṇantu śivasaṃbhakṣyaṃ bhūtāḥ prāsādagā balim ।।

pañcamantrāśca bhūtāśca teṣāmanucarāśca ye ।
(62b)
svaya(ssvi) rakalpayamantrau nirbhatsanātmakaḥ ।।

pūjācchatrākṛtiḥ kāryā sitapītaiśca varṇakaiḥ ।
raktairasitajātīyairardhacandramayi tu vā ।।

amalā svastikā vāpi hyatha śṛṅgāṭakā priye ।
vicitraiva prakartavyā varṇakaiścitravarṇakaiḥ ।।

kusumaracanāviśeṣāniti devyāyāmale jagāda śivaḥ । sragalaṅkāradugūlai ghaṇṭādhvaniyuktasuratidhūpena satpātrapratibodhitadīpakṛtārātrikavidhānena ।
abhyarcya devadevaṃ praṇamya vijñāpya bāhyapūjārtham । labdhānujñāstasmācca bhāvaraṇānyathākrameṇa yajet ।।

anāvṛto na śobhārhaḥ prabhoryenānugāvṛtiḥ ।
prabhoryeḥ parivāryāḥ prāksamantraiśśaktilakṣaṇaiḥ ।।

ityuktavānmaheśena parākhye sahitāntare ।
parameśasya kartavyaṃ bahirāvaraṇārcanam ।।

bhogasthāneṣu tatastvīśaprāgdakṣiṇottarāpyeṣa brahmāṇi pañcahṛdayādyagrīśaniśācarānilāgrastham ।
astro bahiraṣṭāsu dikṣuthābjacalāntareṣu tatpūjya viśvarāgaṇeśādikpālā mātaro grahānāgāḥ ।
vajrādyastrāṇīti krameṇa sā sa * dhā bahiḥ pūjā tadidaṃ garbhāvaraṇaṃ saptāvaraṇaṃ ca tatpare parāṇi ।
(63a)
prācyāḥ prārabhyāṣṭakayutāni vidyaiśvarasukhāni  tatrānanteśaḥ prāksūkṣmonu ca śivottamekanetrau ca ।
ekatrirudramūrtīśrīkaṇṭhaśikhaṇḍinau prathamā  devī tadanucarantī bhairavaparameṣṭhiviṣṇuvighnakṛtaḥ  bhṛṅgīskandaitī me gaṇeśvarāstadvadevāṣṭau 2 ।
caṇḍeśvaravṛṣarudrau sthānaniyogāttu cintayenmantrī । indrānalayama(kṣi)tijalapavanadhanādhipeśvarāstebhyaḥ । tebhyo jayātha subhagā
vija{yā}nyādurbhagā yantyaparāntanityaparājitayā saha ca karālīti mātṛgaṇaḥ । tasmādraviśaśikujabudhaśukraśanassauravi * rīrāḥ ।
tebhyopyanantakulikau vāsukiśaṅkhau tato mahāpadmaḥ । takṣakakārkoṭāviti sapta tathāṣṭaḥ padmaḥ ।।(3 )।। atha kuliśaśaktidaṇḍā:
kṛpāṇapāśāṅkuśagadāśśūlam । ityāstrāvaraṇa * prāśyāḥ prārabhya saṃyojyam ।। nandīśvarāvatāre niśśvāse navasahasrasaṅkhyāke ।
saptāvaraṇārcāyaḥ kramo vibhāgaśca kathita ityeṣaḥ । siddhayogīśvarī tantrodbhūte gaurīśvarāgame śrīhārakeśvaram । evāvyati kramaḥ ।
saṅkṣepe sati sāyudhalokeśaiścaiva kartavyaḥ । śrīhāṭakeśvaram । * thā ca kathitaṃ maheśena
(63b)
gurukramaṃ purā pūjyā gaṇeśaṃ mātṛkā tathā ।
lokeśāssāyudhaścānte pūjanīyaḥ prayatnataḥ ।।

ityekāvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradam ।

nandīśvarāvatāraparivārārcāvidhiśca vikhyātaḥ । devasya prāsādo bhavatu prāgādikāṣṭayā kāmam । tatpuruṣādimukhāni tu na jahati pūrvādidiksaṃsthām ।
yatrāpyarcāyāṃ kila tatpuruṣādīni na svadiksthāni । pūrvādiksthānyeva hi manasā tatra kalpyāni । dharmādiḥ parivāroyaṃ snānto niyatadiṅniviṣṭhosau ।
prabhumukhadikparivṛtyā tasyāpi bhavatkuto niyama । iti ।।

maheśābhimukhānyasyāssarve mantragaṇāsvime ।
iti proktaṃ maheśena pauṣkare pārameśvare ।।

maheśe tu maheśābhimukhyānyasya iti śrutam ।
sarvopacāravidhinā saṃpūjyai tānyathāśakti ।।

nānāvidhanaivedyairbhakṣya saṃpūjya parameśam ।
uktasparśendraśāstre tathā kiñcinmaheśena ।।

sarvābharaṇaṃ ca cāmaraṃ dīpatarpaṇe divyāyudhāni mālyaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ pānabhojana vitānasāt । dhvajaṃ cotra tamujjvalam ।

hṛdodgīthena vā dadyādyāgāṅgamanunāpi vā । iti ।।
itthamāmantryapūṭādau śrīviśvāvartabhāṣitā ।।
(64a)
siddhāntadṛṣṭyātvadhunā vacmi bhairavaśāsane ।
tatasthā * * * karma kṛtvā bhairavaśāsane ।।

paridhāpya suvastrāṇi netrapaṭṭodbhavāni tu ।
vilipyāgurukarpūramuktajādyairvibhūpayet ।।

puṣpairnānāvidhaiśśubhrairarcayeddhūpayetpunaḥ ।
arghaṃdatvā maheśāni punarmudrāṃ pradarśayet ।।

praṇamya bhairavaṃ devaṃ svacchandaṃ viśvanāyakam ।
tato hyāvaraṇaṃ bāhye viniveśyaṃ varānane ।।

aiśānyāṃ pūrvato yāmyā muttarāpyāvasānikām ।
vinyaset pañcavaktrāṇi pañcavaktrayutāni tu ।।

agnīśarakṣau vāyavya caturdikṣu gataṃ yajet ।
hṛcchiraśca śikhāvarmalocanāstraṃ vibhāgaśaḥ ।।

hṛdayaṃ raktavarṇābhaṃ śiro gorocanaprabham ।
taḍidbalayasaṅkāśāṃ śikhāṃ devi vicintayet ।।

ādhūmraṃ kavacaṃ vidyāt kapiśaṃ cāstrameva ca ।
jyotīrūpapratīkāśaṃ netraṃ madhye ca saṃsthitam ।।

bhairavādyāssmṛtāmantrāḥ pīṭheśāḥ pīṭhamandakāḥ ।
dvitīyā varaṇe caiva vinyasedbhairavāṣṭakam ।।

kapālīśaṃ tu pūrveṇa āgneyyāṃ śikhivāhanam ।
dakṣiṇe krodharājānaṃ vikarālaṃ ca nairṛte ।।

manmathaṃ paścime bhāge meghanādeśvaraṃ tathā ।
(64b)
vāyavye caiva vinyaset somarājaṃ tathottare ।।

vidyārājamatheśānyāṃ vinyasyettu subhāvitaḥ ।
tṛtīye caiva lokeśātsāstrātsaṃparikalpayet ।।

nāmāni teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ ।
indrāgniyamanivṛtivaruṇācca samīraṇaḥ ।।

somarājaḥ kubero vā īśānaḥ parameśvaraḥ ।
bhairavāṣṭakarūpeṇa dhyātavyāstravarānane ।।

vajraṃ śaktistathādaṇḍaḥ khaḍgapāśastathaiva ca ।
dhvajaṃ gadātriśūlaṃ ca lokapālāyudhāni vai ।।

vajraṃ cānekavarṇāḍhyaṃ śaktiṃ hemasamaprabham ।
daṇḍaṃ bhinnāñjanābhāsaṃ khaḍgaṃ nīlotpalaprabham ।।

kiṃśukābhaṃ tathā pāśaṃ dhvajaṃ śuklaṃ vicintayet ।।

gadāṃ vai vidrumābhāṃ tu śūlaṃ vidyunsamaprabham ।
sampūjyāvaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ sandhānaṃ mantranāyake ।।

mūlamantreṇa kartavyaṃ nāḍīsandhānameva ca ।
astrāṇi lokapālāṃśca bhairavāṣṭakameva ca ।।

pañcabrahmāṇyathāṅgāni etānyāvaraṇāni ca ।
krameṇoccārayetsarva yāvattadgarbhamaiśvaram ।।

mantrasandhānametaddhi paramīkaraṇaṃ śṛṇu ।
uccārayettato devaṃ hrasvadīrghaplutānvitam ।।

tāvaduccārayenmantraṃ yāvannirvāṇagocaram ।
adhaścakreryāvadūrdhvaṃ somasūryapathāntare ।।
(65a)
piṅgalāmadhyamārgeṇa varṇoccārakrameṇa tu ।
devatāpañcakaṃ śaktirvyāpinī samanonmane ।।

bhedayitvā kramātsarvaṃ yāvadvainnidhanāntikam ।
parāntaṃ yāvadābhāvya naivedyā vinivedayet ।।

ghārikā vaṭakāṃścaiva śaṣkulī modakāni ca ।
khaḍgaṃ tattu śarāvāṇi bhakṣyāṇi vividhāni ca ।।

śālyodanaṃ mudgasūpamā(jyā)ktaṃ sampradarśayet ।
avadaṃśānyanekāni kaṭūni madhurāṇi ca ।।

ramālāṃ ca dadhiṃ kṣīramāsavaṃ vividhaṃ tathā ।
martyamāṃsānyanekāni lehyapeyāni yāni ca ।।

agramā pūrayecchambhorvittaśāṭhyavivarjitaḥ ।
paścādarghaḥ pradātavyassurayā susugandhayā ।।

uktvā sā vṛtikāṃ pūjāṃ naivedyaṃ ca prakāśitam ।
svacchandaśāstrādatrāntaḥ pūjā proktā tu yā vṛteḥ ।।

asyāṃ bhaira * sāmmukhya pūrvāśāparikalpanāt ।
vidheyamarcanaṃ bhaktai na prājāpatya pūrvataḥ ।।

āśvāsanārthaṃ saṃvādaśśrīlakṣmī kaulaśāsanāt ।
kathyate trocitastatra yāgapūjāprasaṅgataḥ ।।

dvāratrayaṃ pidhātavyaṃ paścimaṃ na pidhāpayet ।
sā tu pūrvaṃ vijānīyāddevyā devasya sammukham ।।

pūrvaṃ tu paścimaṃ jñeyaṃ paścimaṃ pūrvameva ca ।
dakṣiṇaṃ cottaraṃ jñeyamuttaraṃ dakṣiṇaṃ tathā ।।

sāmānyassarvamantrāṇāṃ kramoyaṃ śivabhāṣitaḥ ।
(65b)
gopanīyaṃ prayatnena śiṣyāṇāṃ duṣṭacetasām ।।

iti naivedya *  dhunā vacmi siddhāntaśāsanāt । pūrvasmindadhidugdhapāyasaghṛtairmāṃsasavairdakṣiṇe bhakṣyaiḥ pā{ṇi}satonaivadyāracanāvaidyasya hṛdyāspadaiḥ ।
vāmeṅge gurukhaṇḍaphālitaphalakrīḍopabhogairbaliṃ kuryādatra śivasya sarvamukuṭanyāsena ūrdhe mukhe ।।

naivedyaṃ bhakṣyabhojyādi yacchivāya niveditam ।
tatsa{ma}staṃ bhavetpūtaṃ vāmadakṣiṇaśāsane ।।

bhakṣaṇe dhāraṇe dāne laṅghanepi na doṣabhāk ।
caṇḍeśvaraṃ vināyasmānsiddhāntepi śubhāvaham ।।

gaurīyāmala ityuktaṃ naivedya vaktrapañcake ।
aprasā{dā} ca varaṇāddvārāgrādyanniveditam ।।

nirmālyaṃ tadvinirdiṣṭaṃ caṇḍarudrāya dāpayet ।
ayantvanalasiddhānte viśeṣassamprakīrtitaḥ ।।

atha mudrāṃ pravakṣyāmi śivoktadravyarūpiṇīm ।
rūpaṃ dravyastu dravyasya yā vibhaktavidhau smṛtā ।।

dravyarūpī tu sāmudrā ekānekā ca kīrtitā ।
aṅgulassaṃhitāḥ kṛtvā prottānakarasaṃsthitāḥ ।।

mudraiṣā tu vidhau yojyā dakṣiṇasya karasya tu ।
kṛtvā karataleṅguṣṭhaṃ mudreyaṃ gandharūpiṇī ।।

anāmāṅguṣṭhakau śliṣṭau hastayoragradeśagau ।
aṅgulyaḥ prasṛtāśśeṣā mudraiṣā rūpiṇī srajaḥ ।।

(66a)
suśliṣṭamaṇibandhau tu hastayorubhayorapi ।
* kauśā viralāṅgulyo mudrā kusumarūpiṇī ।।

aṅgulyassaṃhatāḥ kṛtvā prottānaka * * * tāḥ ।
mudraiṣā tu vidhau yojyā dhūpadravyasya rūpiṇī ।।

tarjanyaṅguṣṭhakau śliṣṭau hastayorubhayorapi ।
aṅgulyo visṛjaśśeṣā mudrā yajñopavītinī ।।

uttānāṃ tu karāvagre śliṣṭāvaṅguṣṭhakuñcitā ।
mudraiṣā darśanīyā tu vastradravyasya rūpiṇī।।

tarjaṇyaṅguṣṭhakāvagre saṃśliṣṭau karayordvayoḥ ।
aṅgadānāmiyaṃ mudrā vidhau rūpaṃ bibharti hi ।।

kanyasāṃ prasṛtāṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇasya karasya tu ।
śeṣā aṅguṣṭhakā krāntā aṅgulīyakarūpiṇī ।।

aṅguṣṭhāvagrasaṃśliṣṭau sakaniṣṭhau karadvaye ।
prasṛtaḥ karajāśśeṣā mukuṭasya ca rūpiṇī ।।

baddhvā cāṅgadamudrāṃ tu samāviśya parasparam ।
ubhābhyāmapi pāṇibhyāṃ kuṇḍalasya tu rūpiṇī ।।

{baddhvā tathaiva mudrāṃ tu kaṭisūtrasya rūpiṇī} ।
anāmike sampraktañcya mudrā(grai)veyarūpiṇī ।।

aṅguṣṭhamūlaparvasthā tarjanī dakṣiṇasya tu ।
cūḍāmaṇerbhavenmudrā hāramudropavītinī ।।

annapānasya sarvasya dhenumudrā tu rūpiṇī ।
mudrāstvetāssamākhyātā dravyarūpābhidhāyakāḥ ।।

sadyo jātasya yā mudrā tasyā nāma dvayaṃ punaḥ ।
agre saṃcchādayedvidvānaṅguṣṭhena tu parvaṇā ।।

sarveṣāmeva dravyāṇāṃ rūpameṣā vibharti hi ।
(66b)
ekaiva mudrā tu vidhau jñeyā dravyasya rūpiṇī ।।

dravyarūpāmimāṃ mudrāṃ baddhvāmantrī vidhau sthitaḥ ।
sarvāṇyeva manasvāni dravyāṇi tu vicintayet ।।

dhenumudrāsahāyeyaṃ sampādayati puṣkalam ।
vidhisthaṃ mantriṇāṃ dravyaṃ dravyaṃ mānuṣyameva ca ।।

oṃ dravyarūpādhipataye śivāya ।।

iti pauṣkaraśāstroktasthitāsthitadravyavargasya mantro mudrāyuktaḥ pūraṇasampādanāyoktaḥ । ācamanīyaṃ dadyātpādyarthaṃ praṇatimudrayā tiṣṭhet ।

iti naivedyamudrāvidhiḥ ।।

praṇipātaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā japaṃ paścāt samārabhet ।
mantrasiddhyucitāṃ yāṃ tu tāṃ gṛhītvākṣamālikām ।।

nirārambho japaṃ kuryāḥ karaśaṅkhakṣamālayā ।
gṛhasthena ca kartavyaḥ kartavyaścākṣamālayā ।।

tāmasī rājasī caiva sātvikī sā tridhā kramāt ।
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāraṃ tadardhaṃ vā samarpitā ।।

guruṇā grāhayedakṣānnirguṇānguṇayojitān ।
bhasmanāstrāhijaptena vāriṇāntaritena ca ।।

pañcagavyena gandhaiśca sūpayecchivavāriṇā ।
yāgaṃ vidhāya limpe tu mantravatprārthayettataḥ ।।

upoṣitena śuddhena bhaktiplāvitacetasā ।
rudrākṣaśaṅkhasphaṭikaiḥ padmākṣapatrajīvikaiḥ ।।

maṇimuktāsu varṇādyairvidrumairiṣṭhakaiḥ kacit ।
daśākṣamālāgṛhiṇāṃ vratināṃ puruṣā sthitaḥ ।।
(67a)
tatsūtraṃ puṣyalohā {*} śāṇaṃ kuśamayaṃ tridhā ।
triguṇaṃ tatra sandhāya karpāsamathavā dṛḍham ।।

śivaṃ sūtraṃ tathātmānamakṣāṇi ca parasparam ।
jyotsnā jāla mayānīti cintyānyekatayāthavā ।।

nādaśaktisamāyuktānyetāni karayaḥ sphuṭam ।
sphuraddīpapradīptāni sakalāni vicintayet ।।

śiṣyāya paramaprodyattejorūpāṇi cintayet ।
vinyasya dakṣiṇe hastaṃ pṛṣṭhe tasyāṃ japeściram ।।

sāṣṭaṃ śataṃ hṛdādīnāṃ daśāṃśenābhimantrayet ।
toyena secayettena candracandanagandhinā ।।

mudrāṃ pradarśayenmantrī nijakalpakramoditām ।
bhagavanparameśāna mālikāṃ svāṃ prayaccha me ।।

bhaktinamraścādadīta prāptānujñetha dīpayet ।
mantraṃ vā japato nityaṃ na laṅghyaṃ merumastakam ।।

gacchetpucchācchiro meroḥ kṛtvā tasya pradakṣiṇam ।
pramā{dā}llaṅghanānmerordarśanatrayalaṅghinaḥ ।।

jīrṇecchitre tathā traste hastapādācchataṃ japet ।
adīkṣitasparśanena sahasraṃ japamācaret ।।

naṣṭātrastā yadā mālā japassyādayutaṃ tadā ।
visarjayeccārcayecca nyasenmantraṃ dine dine ।।

paśyeddevaṃ śivaṃ sākṣāt pañcavaktraṃ trilocanam ।
tarjanīmadhyamānāmā karṣaṇāduttamādikā ।।

siddhirbhavetgrattho * ṣṭhātāṃ samudgāntare nyaset ।
sa siddhārthe dhūparamye vastracchatrestrarakṣite ।।
(67b)
dānahomajapābhyāse yāvannābhyudito raviḥ ।
tatassnānaṃ prakurvīta ravipūtena vāriṇā ।।

{tiṣṭhañjāgransvapangacchanbhuñjāno maithune rataḥ ।
manasā saṃsmarenmantraṃ pīḍyate vyādhibhinnasaḥ ।।

tatassnānaṃ prakurvīta ravipūtena vāriṇā ।}
akṣamālāvidhihyeṣa karmoddyai te nirūpitaḥ ।।

adhunā sampravakṣyāmi akṣasūtrasya lakṣaṇam ।
sahaśaṅkhamayaṃ kuryācchaṅkhotthaṃ pautrajīvikam ।।

raudrākṣaṃ hemaraupyaṃ vā anta - - -vālajam ।
padmākṣendramayaṃ vātha sphāṭikaṃ rājavartakam ।।

vimalā(rci) samopetairmaṇibhistu śubhaiḥ kṛtam ।
kuryādetaccākṣasūtraṃ tu - - -divarjitam ।।

vartulākṣaṃ susampūrṇaṃ nirbhraṇaṃ tu samaṃ śubham ।
saṃsavedhaṃ na vaktraṃ tu kārayensādhakottamaḥ ।।

na sthūlasūkṣmamaṇibhi samaisnigdhaissu vartitaiḥ ।
kārayedakṣasūtraṃ tu śobhanaṃ tadyathā bhavet ।।

mahāśaṅkhamayaṃ yattu ūrdhvavedhaṃ samaṃ bhavet ।
srigdhaṃ tu varjitaṃ śubhraṃ varṇato mayapiṅgalam ।।

tattu * tatsamākhyātaṃ niyuktaṃ siddhasādhane ।
pautrajīvi *? padmākṣa sarvasiddhiprayojanam ।।

vinyāsabheja * pyanyadakṣasūtraṃ bhavediha ।
vaśyādisanniyojyaṃ syādakṣasūtrapravālajam ।।

vidveṣoccāṭanadyeṣu * * * ktaṃ praśasyate ।
māraṇādividhāneṣu rājavartamayaṃ smṛtam ।।

dahane tāpane caiva padmarāgamayaṃ smṛtam ।
sauvarṇaṃ pārthive bhoge sumbhanādiṣu yojayet ।।
(68a)
rājataṃ śāntike jñeyamṛddhyarthaṃ ca vipaścitā ।
pauṣṭike caiva saṅkhotthaṃ mṛtyuñjayavidhau tathā ।।

sphāṭikaṃ sarvakṛtproktaṃ muktāphalasamudbhavam ।
sarvarogavināśāya hyakṣasūtraṃ varānane ।।

evaṃ sthitāni sūtrāṇi praguṇānyakhilāni tu ।
lābhālābhavibhāgena kartavyāni yaśasvini ।।

nitye naimittike kāmye yathākalpoktakarmaṇi ।
vinyāsaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi hyakṣasūtrasya śobhane ।।

gopucchākṛtisaṃsthānaṃ yavamadhyaṃ tathaiva ca ।
tathāpipīlikāmadhyaṃ samaṃ caiva tathā bhavet ।।

viṣamaṃ ca tathā proktametadvinyāsalakṣaṇam ।
ādau sthūlaṃ krameṇaiva hyante sūkṣmaṃ yadā bhavet ।।

etadgopucchavatkhyātamakṣasūtraṃ suśobhane ।
antayossūkṣmamaṇibhiḥ madhye sthūlaṃ tu yadbhavet ।।

yavamadhye bhavedetadakṣasūtraṃ suśobhanam ।
sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tathā sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ sukṣmataraṃ tathā ।।

etadviṣasamākhyātaṃ pādisthūlagatergatam ।
sūkṣmamadhyaṃ tathā vṛddhimante yāvansamācaret ।।

etatpipīlikā madhyaṃ samaṃ cakṣaissamaissthitam ।
pautrajīvikapadmākṣaṃ mukhapṛṣṭhakrameṇa tu ।।

gopaṭṭinyāyayogena rudrākṣotthaṃ samācaret ।
ubhayoḥ pārśvayorgranthiṃ sammukhākṣaṃ tu vinyaset ।
(68b)
ardhatulyepi tannāma śubhakarmapraśasyate ।
yugmaṃ yugme sthitaṃ hyetatsammukhaṃ tu parāṅmukham ।।

granthisutrāntike sūtraṃ stambhane tu prayojayet ।
granthyantārdhasamānsūtrādviparītaṃ niyojayet ।।

granthisammukhapārśvaṃ tu maraṇādiṣu yojayet ।
vidveṣoccāṭanaṃ caiva hyunmade mohane tathā ।।

kalahe roṣaṇe caiva pātane nāśane tathā ।
parasainyavimarde ca tarjanī tu praśasyate ।।

muktau tu nāmikā proktā akṣasūtrasya lakṣaṇam ।
mehanaṃ kārayeddevi kṛtaṃ vātha na laṅghayet ।।

uttamāsiddhikāmena madhyamā ca varānane ।
vaśyākarṣaṇasaubhāgye nigaḍānāṃ ca bhañjane ।।

śāntike pauṣṭike caiva mṛtyuñjayavidhau tathā ।
melake siddhisampattau svasainyasya ca rakṣaṇe ।।

kopopaśamane caiva hyanyasminvā śubhe dine ।
akṣasūtraṃ prakarṣeta madhyamā yā varānane ।।

piṇḍakarṣādikotthānāmakṣasūtravidhismṛtaḥ ।
nitye naimittike kāmye siddhau kalpoktake vidhau ।।

pūrvamevāvidhāne ca śāntike pauṣṭike tathā ।
mṛtyuñjayavidhāne ca prakopaśamanī tathā ।।

utsāde trāsane caiva pātane stobhakarmaṇi ।
rakṣaṇe nijasainyasya yavamadhyaṃ prakalpayet ।।

bhāgyarājyapravṛddhau tu sṛṣṭā vāpyayanādiṣu ।
(69a)
melake ca mahābhāge gopucchaṃ parivartayet ।।

vidveṣoccāṭane caiva roṣaṇe pīḍane tathā ।
unmāde trāsane caiva pātane stobhakarmaṇi ।।

viparitākṣamālāṃ ca tāṃ tu mantrī vicintayet ।
māraṇe mohanonmāde parasainyasya mardane ।।

kalahe cakṣuṣi bhraṃśe viṣamākṣaṃ vivartayet ।
stambhane sarvadevyānāṃ vāyutoyavicetasām ।।

parasainye tu vā kumbhe bhede garbhādike tathā ।
śastaṃ pipīlikākāramakṣasūtraṃ varānane ।।

sarvakarmapravṛttasya sarvasiddhyabhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ ।
samākṣamakṣasūtraṃ tu sarvaśāstrārthakṛtparam ।।

uktāni cākṣa sūtrāṇi sarvakarmocitāni tu ।
yathākramāt prakurvāṇo kāmikaṃ siddhimāpnuyāt ।।

mantranyāsaṃ yathānyāyyaṃ karmabhedena saṃsthitam ।
pūrvaṃ ca kṣālanaṃ kṛtvā vastropari niveśayet ।।

tatra nyāsaḥ prakartavyassvayāgavirato yathā ।
sarvāṣvayojanaṃ kṛtvā pūjayitvā yathākramam ।।

nitye naimittike kāmyepyakṣasūtravidhissmṛtaḥ ।
ākarṣaṇavidhānena guṇadoṣānnibodhata ।।

āndolati japaṃ sūtre vibhramo jāyate priye ।
lavanaṃ ca tathoccāṭe kaliṃ cāpi vinirdiśet ।।

atidīrghaikṣasūtre yāpyarthanāśaḥ prajāyate ।
prāyaścittaviśuddhyarthamāhnikaṃ kārayejjapam ।।
(69b)
saṃsphoṭe ca tathākṣāṇāṃ ślathe lambe ca suvrate ।
vedhānāṃ cakravṛddhyā ca prāyaścittaṃ tu kārayet ।।

dine dine yadā kuryādāsphoṭaṃ mantravijjape ।
pañcaviṃśasahasrāṇi prāyaścittaṃ prakīrtitam ।।

mṛtyuñjayasya devasya svayāgavidhisaṃyutam ।
prāyaścittaṃ na kurvita sādhakastu yadā priye ।।

siddhihānirbhavettasya vyādhirvāsumahānbhavet ।
mṛtyurvāsya cireṇaiva nātrakāryāvicāraṇā ।।

sphoṭaṃ caivāhnikaṃ kuryādyadā vai jñānadurbalaḥ ।
mṛtyuñjayasya devasya svayāgavidhisaṃsthitam ।।

ūnāntenākṣasūtreṇa karmahānistu jāyate ।
ajñāte tu kṛte jāpyaṃ daśasāhasrikaṃ japet ।।

na sthūlamaṇibhirkuryānna ca sūkṣme varānane ।
samaissnigdhaistathā vṛtairakṣasūtraṃ tu kārayet ।।

catuṣpañcāśataṃ caiva saptāviṃśatireva ca ।
kārayeccākṣasūtraṃ tu evaṃ bhairava uktavān ।।

vidhānamakṣasūtrasya samya(gvi)purāṇaṃ sphuṭitaṃ śīrṇaṃ svārūḍhaṃ parasādhitam ।

a{rgha}pātrākṣasūtrādiśātrā ca tadvivarjayet ।
purāṇaṃ pauruṣaṃ hanti sphuṭite tu kulakṣayaḥ ।।

śīrṇe vyādhissadā jñeyaśśvāca mṛtyuñjayasya ca ।
daśasāhasriko japaḥ sakṛcchṛtvā tathā sphoṭaṃ ।।

kṛtvā sūtraṃ japettu yaḥ hastena cāhnikaṃ kṛtvā bhaveddaśa * ca daridratā ।
(70a)
anyasādhakajuṣṭaṃ hi prakuryātsiddhināśanam ।
mānaso mokṣasiddhau tu hyaṇimādiprasādhane ।।

va * sya karṣaro pāśuḥ khecare melakādiṣu ।
maraṇoccāṭadīni vācikena prasādhayet ।।

maṇisphaṭikarudrākṣairhemamuktāphalaistathā ।
padmarāgamahāśaṅkhairmālāhyuttamasiddhidā ।।

trapuruṣyairīti sīsaistāmrapadmākṣavidrumaiḥ ।
rājāśmaputrabījaiśca madhyamā siddhimicchati ।।

āriṣṭhakī tathā kācī mṛṇmayī śaṅkhapīlukā ।
phalajā darbhagratsyurthā hastajā ca kanīyasī ।।

tarjanyaṅguṣṭhamākṛṣṭi hyuttamā sā prakīrtitā ।
madhyamāmadhyamākṛṣṭi narāsāsu kanīyasī ।।

aṣṭottaraśataṃ mātrā tadardhaṃ pādameva vā ।
ūrdhvaṃ cātra na kartavyaṃ yadīcchetsiddhimuttamām ।।

kāmyeyamakṣamālānāṃ bhedo maṅgalaśāstragaḥ ।
vaiṣṇave tu viśeṣeṇa dṛṣṭo sāvabhidhīyate ।।

akṣasūtraṃ śubhaṃ kāryamadṛṣṭamitarairjanaiḥ ।
akṣā(sthi)mātrairmaṇibhirjyeṣṭhaṃ kāryaṃ suvartulam ।।

dhātrī phalāṃ ca garbheṇa pramāṇaṃ madhyamaṃ smṛtam ।
badirāsthipramāṇena kanīyasamudāhṛtam ।।

aṣṭottaraśataṃ pūrṇaṃ tadardhaṃ pādameva vā ।
kuryāttacca vidhānoktavidhinā sarvakarmasu ।।

sauvarṇaṃ dravyasiddhyarthamuttamaṃ tatra kārayet ।
puṣṭyarthaṃ rūpalābhāya rājataṃ pitṛkakarmaṇi ।।
(70b)
medhā vīryaṃ tathā tejo lipsunā tāmrameva ca ।
madhyamaṃ muniśārdūla kāryamevākṣasūtrakam ।।

yakṣāṇāmatha yakṣīṇāṃ sādhane tripujaṃ smṛtam ।
rākṣasānāṃ piśācānāṃ vaśe sīmamayaṃ tathā ।।

pātālasādhanārthaṃ tu harītimayamucyate ।
pannagānāṃ ca nāgānāṃ sādhane syāttu kāṃsyajam ।।

kanīyasāṃ tathā kuryā * yasaṃ kṣudrakarmaṇi ।
iti dhātumayānāṃ ca sūtrāṇāṃ viṣayasmṛtaḥ ।।

vikṣepaṇimayānāṃte vibhāgaṃ sthaṇḍisattama ।
sarvaṃ maṇimayaṃ tāvadāyurārogyavardhanam ।।

mokṣadaṃ tu viśeṣeṇa sphāṭikaṃ śāntikarmaṇi ।
mūlajā nāmato vakṣye bījānāṃ vidhimuttamam ।।

saubhāgyai vaidrumaṃ kāryamuttamādyaṃ sadātrayam ।
muktyarthaṃ jīvestu hyaṣṭairdoṣa{pra}śāntaye ।।

vakṣye jalotthitānāṃ ca viṣayaṃ sarvasiddhidam ।
padmabījaiśca śaṅkhaiśca śrīkāmo japamācaret ।
āyuḥ prajā yaśaḥ kāmā mauktikaissarvasiddhidaiḥ ।।

uttamādividhānena tritayaṃ sarvasiddhidam ।
teṣāṃ madhyādekatamaṃ grāhyaṃ caiva śubhe dine ।
jayāyāmiti nirdiṣṭaṃ phalaṃ rūpaprabhedataḥ ।।

dakṣiṇena tu hastena gṛhṇīyādakṣamālikām ।
sthāpayeddakṣiṇe naiva na vāmena kadācana ।।
(71a)
ūrdhvagranthiṃ sadāmālamuttamānāṃ prakalpayet ।
tiryaggranthiṃ madhyamānāṃ madhogūnthimanuttame ।।

pradakṣiṇamupānī{ya} mantravāndakṣiṇaṃ karam ।
sammukhā kṣāṃtutāṃ mālāṃ gṛhṇīyādgranthisaṃsthitām ।।

sarvavidyādhipaṃ dhyātvā ucchvāsairagragāmibhiḥ ।
kanyasā mūlavartinyā rekhayā tāmasaṃ smṛtam ।।

japaṃ kṛtvā tu matimānmadhyamāṅguṣṭhasāraṇāt ।
anāmā sāraṇā bhāvādanāmā sarvakāmadā ।।

ākarṣe pauṣṭike caiva praśastā madhyamā jape ।
māraṇādiṣu sarveṣu tarjanyā karmamācaret ।।

parivartaśca kartavyā nāgendraparivartavat ।
sarveṣāmeva mantrāṇāṃ sarvasiddhikaraśśubhaḥ ।।

niyamo darśito hyevaṃ pauṣkare pārameśvare ।
vidhānamakṣasūtrasya lakṣaṇaṃ kāmanānugam ।।

evaṃ jñātvā japaṃ kuryāt sādhakassarvasiddhibhāk ।।

।। akṣasūtraprakaraṇam ।।

akṣamālāṃ tu saṅgṛhya gandhaiḥ puṣpaissamarcitām ।
vāṅniruddhassa śuddhātmā rājīvāsanasaṃsthitaḥ ।।

mūlamantraṃ samuccārya nāde līnaṃ vicintayet ।
unmīlyākṣāṇi sañcintya tatastu japamārabhet ।।

akṣarākṣarasantānaṃ na drutaṃ na vijṛmbitam ।
japaḥ prāṇasamaḥ kāryo dinastho muktikāṅkṣibhiḥ ।।

saṃhārastu sa vijñeyaśśivadhāmaphalapradāḥ ।
(71b)
vyomni prāpto yadā nādaḥ punareva nivartate ।।

sarvarī sā tu vijñeyā hṛdabjaṃ yāvadāgataḥ ।
sṛṣṭireva samākhyātā sarvasiddhiphalodayā ।।

ātmano bhairavaṃ rūpaṃ sadābhāvyaṃ varānane ।
pūrakena prayogena * ṣṭhaṃ ca tritayānvitam ।।

trisiddhi siddhidaṃ devi sarahasya mudāhṛtam ।
śāntike manaso japtvā upāṃśuḥ pauṣṭike smṛtaḥ ।।

saśabdaścābhicārosāvudakprāgdakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ।
ātmānaṃ śṛṇute yastu mānasosau prakīrtitaḥ ।।

ātmanā śṛṇute yastu tamupāṃśuṃ vijānata ।
parāśśṛṇvanti yaṃ devi saśabdassa udāhṛtaḥ ।।

itthaṃ japavidhiḥ proktaśśrīmatsvaccha{nda}śāsane ।
videhā api vadhyante * cādau guṇavāsitāḥ ।।

śarīriṇopi mucyante māntrī bhūmimupāṃgatāḥ।
mananātrāyate yasmāttasmānmantraḥ prakīrtitaḥ ।।

jñātaścintāmaṇirasā yajñataḥ kāṣṭhaloṣṭhavat ।
na mātrā nā * * * pi svaraṃ vyañjanadumbaraḥ ।।

na kūṭavarṇo na padaṃ na padānāmanugrahaḥ ।
na vāndhaṃ nāpi vijñānaṃ nārtho na pratibhodayaḥ ।।

na ca vyakto dhvanirmantro laukikaḥ karaṇāśrayaḥ ।
parasparānukūlyena yatrā{gni}ṣo mayo ssthitiḥ ।।

tatra pratiṣṭhāṃ labhate bhāratī brahmavādinī ।
(72a)
sā mantrabhūḥ parā sattā saṃvinsāmānyavigrahā ।।

unmeṣastatra yaḥ pūrvaṃ svālokālokanirbharaḥ ।
nityānandassphuradrūpo deśakālakalojjhitaḥ ।।

saṅkalpapūrvakoṭistho nādosau mantra ucyate ।
sa eva vyavahāreṣu krameṇa sthūlatāṃ vrajet ।।

yatkiñcillaukikaṃ karma vyavahārāssa kathyate ।
pravṛttirvyavahārasya sthitā cārthanibandhanī ।।

grāhyagrāhakarūpeṇa sa cārtho dvividhaḥ priye ।
vikalpakalpanādhīna jīvitonantaśaktibhiḥ ।।

artho vikalpanilayassa ca saṃkalpajīvitaḥ ।
saṅkalpo vākyaviśrāntaṃ vākyaṃ padasamāśrayam ।।

padāni varṇarūpāṇi te varṇāstetvarūpiṇaḥ ।
bījayonisamāpatyā mantraiśvaryamupāgatāḥ ।।

iti ye rūḍhasaṃvitti sāraṃ mantramupāsate ।
asaṅketamaye śuddhe pare dhāmni viśanti te ।।

puṃrūpassyādyadāmantrastadā bhavati niṣkriyaḥ ।
tadrūpādmūḍhatāṃ yāti tasmācchaktau niyojayet ।।

śaktyā sarvamidaṃ proktaṃ mantrajālamupāhṛtam ।
trikoṇakandamadhyasthā sarvalomapravāhinī ।।

nīvāraśūkavattanvī vidyullekheva cañcalā ।
prasarpatkuṭilākārā sūkṣmatantrīriva svanāt ।।

ānandarasasaṃbhinnā kulakuṇḍalinī {parā} ।
(72b)
sā varṇānāṃ catuṣṣaṣṭhiṃ sūte sañjīvanī kalā ।।

tato mantraḥ prabuddhyante tata indriyaśaktayaḥ ।
tato manastato haṅkṛttato dhīścetanā tataḥ ।।

tantrārthaḍho japaṃ kuryādanyathāraṇyabhāṣitam ।
aṃśakaiḥ parihīnā ye mantrāssyuścaradabhravat ।।

ye dagdhā ye ca saṃbhinnā cchinnāgurvāgamojjhitāḥ ।
kīlitāssumbhitā hīnā grathitāśca vidarbhitāḥ ।।

suptāssaṃmūrchitā rūḍhyāḥ khaṇḍitā muṇḍitā hatāḥ ।
etadbalasamāveśātte bhavanti phalapradāḥ ।।

paśubhāve sthitā mantrāḥ kevalā varṇarūpiṇaḥ ।
te patitvāya kalpante śaktibṛṃhitamūrtayaḥ ।।

paśyantyādyāstrayo bhedāssa tvādyāḥ prakṛtā guṇāḥ ।
turyastṛtīyopyathavā bhāgomātraṃ nijaṃ vapuḥ ।।

sthānena karaṇenāpi prayatnena nirūpaṇam ।
mantrasya vyaktarūpasya kramamuktaṃ padatrayam ।।

niścityārthaṃ dhiyā dehī mano yuṅkte vivakṣayā ।
kāyāgnimāha ca tatprāṇatāṃ pratipadyate ।।

sa nābhideśāt prāṇākhye vāyuratra balāhṛtaḥ ।
śaśvadurdhvaṃ vrajansthānānyaṣṭā vā sādya bhidyate ।।

tataḥ koṣṭhābhighātertha * * * * prapadyate ।
ekaiva mūrtinmantrasya jāgradādipadāśrayāt ।।

vibhinnarūpā bhavati bhūtiścintāmaṇeriva ।
(73a)
indriyairarūdhīrjāgratsvapnastābhyāṃ vinā smṛteḥ ।।

pratyekameṣāmanyonyaṃ caturdhābhedamattatoḥ ।
meyaṃ mānaṃ pramāṇaṃ ca pramātātra yathākramam ।।

sadehaśca videhaśca nirdehaśca cidātmakaḥ ।
jāgradādi padānāṃ ca pra * tāraḥ prakīrtitāḥ ।।

nirābhāsaṃ niṣprapañcaṃ cidghanānandanirbharam ।
turyātītaṃ samākhyātaṃ prakhyopādyavivarjitam ।।

cidānandeśanājñānakriyaḥ pañca yathākramam ।
turyātītādirūpeṇa prasṛtaḥ pañca śaktayaḥ ।।

ābhirevocyate devaḥ pañcaśaktimahātanuḥ ।
pañcapañcakarūpeṇa sā ceyaṃ tatvapaddhatiḥ ।।

tamarcanarasāveśādahamityucyate ca sā ।
ananyājīvarūpasya vibhoreva vyatirekinī ।।

taddharmadharmiṇīpūrvā śaktirastyakulābhidhā ।
ānandeccheśitvayogairunmeṣādūnatāṃ vrajet ।।

icchānalakṣapāyogātkuṇṭhatvamupagacchati ।
icchonmeṣātisandhe nādakulānandayormithaḥ ।।

caturdhā prasaro bindurālokasyopasaṃhṛtiḥ ।
visargeṇa tridhā devi mudritā kulamālinī ।।

akulātpañcaśaktyātmā dvitīyo varga udgataḥ ।
anārūṣitarūpāyā icchāyāśca tataḥ paraḥ ।।

vahnikriyājuṣastasya taṭābhyāṃ ca dvayaṃ tataḥ ।
(73b)
pādirunmeṣato jāta iti sparśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ।।

vibhinnā śaktisaṃyogādicchā yaralatāṃ gatā ।
unmeṣaśaktirāyāti vatvameva varānane ।।

icchādyantāḥ praveśena tentastha iti kīrtitāḥ ।
icchaiva soṣmaṇākrāntā kālatrayasamāśritā ।।

visargasthitimāsādya svātma eva pralīyate ।
ṣaḍevete svarā hrasvāḥ kaśitāmūlakāraṇam ।।

te ca prakāśarūpatvādvijñeyāssūraraśmayaḥ ।
antassūryakalājāle ṣaṭkalāssomajāssthitāḥ ।।

etatsatparamaṃ jñānameṣayogaḥ sanātanaḥ ।
iyaṃ sā śakticakrasya cakreśvarapadasthitiḥ ।।

atra śaktasya deveśi mantrāmantreśvaraṃ yathā ।
vayamāyanti satataṃ cillā cakreśacoditāḥ ।।

adīkṣitopi yaḥ kaścidevaṃ vetti varānane ।
sa mukto modakaścāpi pareṣāṃ paśuhāvataḥ ।।

mantravyāptiḥ parā hyeṣā cakreśvaramato yathā ।
evaṃ jñātvā tu mantrāṇāṃ mantratvaṃ kurute sadā ।।

śaktisthāśśaktidāssarve bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।
na vindati yadā mantrī sṛṣṭiṃ saṃhāravartmanī ।।

udayāstamarūpeṇa mantrā alpaphalapradāḥ ।
bhogaṃ mokṣaṃ na yacchanti śivājñāsaṃpracoditāḥ ।।

yadā tu vai vetti mantrī hyutpattisthitisaṃhṛtīḥ ।
(74a)
udayāstamarūpeṇa mantrāṇāmamitaujasām ।।

tadā kiṅkaratāṃ yānti tadā jñānavidhāyinaḥ ।
sammukhāśca bhavantyete sādhakasya varānane ।।

śrīmatmṛtyuñjayepyevaṃ japavyāptiḥ prakāśitā ।
yathā japaṃ prakurvīta tathā vyāptirihocyate ।।

saṃyamyākṣāṇi manasā rājīvāsanasaṃsthitaḥ ।
mūlamantraṃ samuccārya nāde līnaṃ svakāraṇe ।।

dhyātvā hṛdi svamantrasya svakarmaproktavigraham ।
sūkṣmadehasya hṛtpadme śivadehe viśecchivam ।।

bindutvaṃ rūpakaṃ tasmānnādatvaṃ śaktitāṃ gatim ।
tadākramya meḍhrakandaṃ pīḍayayadbhedayatkramāt ।।

vāmāvartena tu paraṃ nābhikāraṇaṣaṭkagam ।
tathā bhedyaṃ kramāttvirthaṃ nādalīnamanurbhavet ।।

na puṃsi na pare patve śaktau mantraṃ vibhāvayet ।
tadakṣarāṇi maṇivacchaktiprotāni bhāvayet ।।

sā śaktiḥ pañcadhā jñeyā sthūlā sūkṣmā parā tataḥ ।
vyomātmikā śivā tatra hṛdākāśasamudbhavā ।।

ādhāratve sthitā sā ca somasūryāgnināḍiṣu ।
tāṃ samīkṛtya viṣuvatyāsīnaḥ karaṇadvayam ।।

kṛtakākṛtakaṃ kṛtvā nayetkuṇḍalinībalam ।
mantraṃ pūraṇayantreṇa hṛdi vā vyaktimāśritam ।।

pādāṅguṣṭhau dharārūḍhau karāṅguṣṭhau tadantare ।
(74b)
kāyaṃ samunnataṃ kuryāddivyaṃ karaṇamāśritaḥ ।।

itthaṃ vā bhairavākārāṃ dṛṣṭiṃ vā pārśvapīḍanāt ।
nāsāprāntaniviṣṭena tatstho vā pūrayedbahiḥ ।।

tatraiva pūrayecchaktyā viṣuvajjāyate kṣaṇāt ।
dhyātvā hṛtpadmamadhyasthaṃ mantreśaṃ binduvigraham ।।

kadambagolakākāraṃ brahmanāḍipathāntaram ।
vyāpnuyaṃstālurandhrāntaṃ prevāhabalarodhanāt ।।

tatastālāśate pūrṇe viṣuvajjāyate kṣaṇāt ।
tadā na vāyunā vāpi tadevaṃ kṛtakaṃ smṛtam ।।

vijñānaṃ bhairave śāstre yaduktaṃ bhūmikāśatam ।
sadvādaśaṃ tadekāsthaṃ svecchayā viṣuvadbhavet ।।

ekanāḍipravāhaṃ vā yadīcchetpārśvapīḍanam ।
kuryāttasya baheścauro yāvaddehasya saṃsthitaḥ ।।

savyātsavyetaraṃ veśātgrīvayorbalavena saḥ ।
kāryābhāvaparāvṛttirdattapattyūrdha cālanāt ।।

prāṇātipūraṇaṃ kāryaṃ vahetprāṇaḥ pujāntare ।
ityevaṃ khecarī sthūlā sūkṣmā bindusvarūpikā ।।

sā mayūrāṇḍarasavadanti ssañjalpakātmikā ।
granthidvayaviniṣkrāntā tālurandhrapadānugā ।।

vaikharyaṅgārasadṛśī hutabhukkaṇabimbitā ।
madhyamā sā mantrayutā tasyā tatpatikāraṇam ।।

parā nādātmikā jñeyā nirvibhāgā tu sā sadā ।
pāvakārcissamākārā vaktrapadmopyanāhatā ।।
(75a)
paśyantī bodhamātrā sā vidyāśuddheti vyomagā ।
bhūrasau sarvamantrāṇāṃ kriyāvāśyādikāstataḥ ।।

suṣumṇā prāpyalabhūtervyāpinī brahmarandhragām ।
śivākhyāsamanāmeva śuddhavidyodayātmikā ।।

bhūra * * dhvanāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ śivamārgānusāriṇī ।
parātmako yadā mantrastadā bhavati niṣkriyaḥ ।।

puṃyogātmūḍhatāṃ yāti tasmācchaktau niveśayet ।
karmaṣaṭkamindhikādyaṃ kārayedvakṣyamāṇavat ।।

ābhiścatasṛbhinnityaṃ sthūlavāksamadhiṣṭhitā ।
śaktyā tayā japaṃ kuryātprāṇavātānusārataḥ ।।

śītalā padmasaṃnābhāgamāgamavidhāyinī ।
tatstho japassiddhimeti projjhito yopi lakṣaṇaiḥ ।।

lakṣaṇaṃ sarvadā proktaṃ paraṃ prāṇaśikhānugam ।
sthitāssadāmantrāssarvajñādiguṇānvitāḥ ।।

krūrakarmādisiddhyādau paraṃ dehapraveśane ।
preraṇe caiva satvānāṃ paraḥ proktastu baindavaḥ ।।

aparo nādajo jñeyo bhuktimuktipradāyakaḥ ।
duḥkhaghnaśśāntijananassaumyakāryaprasādhakaḥ ।।

ārogyāpyāyanakarassukhasaubhāgyavardhanaḥ ।
sarvasiddhiprado nityanādā * japassmṛtaḥ ।।

pūrvāhṇe cātha madhyāhṇe dinānte ca samāgataḥ ।
devayakṣasamāṃsānāṃ japahomādikakriyā ।।

kandaśākaphalaiḥ patrairagṛhītapratigrahaiḥ ।
(75b)
pūjāṃ nyāyārjitairdravyairhomaṃ gṛhṇāti * * * ।।

bhikṣāśī juhuyādbhikṣāṃ ya icchetsiddhimātmani ।
nyāyye dravyaviśuddhistu japādau nopayujyate ।।

na * * na tvaritaṃ kuryādyogī sadā japam ।
prāṇavṛttinirodhena japo yasmātvamāśritaḥ ।।

yo dhamokṣaprāptyai taṃ kuryātsatatoditam ।
ātma vai karṇayatyekaṃ novātmā budhyate param ।।

yatparaiśśrūyate japyaṃ tattuṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam ।
upāṃśuṃ mānasaṃ * * puṭviśāntyabhicāradam ।।

* * bhānave jalasaṃyuktaṃ vahnaye ṣāyutam ।
sapuṣpaṃ śambhave nityaṃ sthūlasthāyasarecakam ।।

mānasaiḥ kusumairhṛdyaiḥ pūrakena niyojayet ।
brahmāṅgānāṃ daśāṃśena japaṃ kṛtvā nivedayet ।।

dvaite proktamidaṃ jñānaṃ sadvaitārthe tato nyaset ।
pañcaṃ srotasi śaivasmiñjapaḥ proktaḥ kulenyathā ।।

yāvacca taccintayati mantrotpattirbhavetkramāt ।
* tvaṃ sthiratvamāpannaṃ mantrāṇāṃ rodhanaṃ bhavet ।।

upakṣīṇaṃ yadā cittaṃ bhāvābhāvavivarjitam ।
* tuṃ mantrāṇāṃ mitthaṃ jñātvā tato japet ।।

prāyaścitte tathā nitye prasādāya śatatrayam ।
karmo * * dhārma śivepyevaṃ japavidhismṛtaḥ ।।

japedekāgracittastu śrāntaścedviramejjapam ।
(76a)
