yadaharnaikacittassyāttadaharna japedbudhaḥ ।।

jṛmbhākṣutkṣivakāseṣu hyupaspṛśya japetpunaḥ ।
vātamūtrapurīśeṣu snātvā tu japamārabhet ।।

snānaṃ pūjāṃ japaṃ homamajīrṇe parivarjayet ।
nivedayejjapaṃ kṛtvā śivāyāśivahāriṇe ।।

tribhiroṅkārapūtaistu śūlakairgandhavāriṇaḥ ।
japadyaniyamaḥ kāryo mantriṇā pauṣkaroditaḥ ।।

oṃ guhyātiguhyagoptā tvaṃ gṛhāṇāsmatkṛtaṃ ja{pa}m ।
siddhyai bhavatu me yena tvatprasādātvayi sthitam ।।

yatkiñcitkurmahe deva sadā sukṛtaduṣkṛtam ।
tanme śivapādasthasya hūṃ kṣaḥ kṣapaya śaṅkara ।।

iti mantradvayaṃ proktaṃ śrīmatsvayambhuvādiṣu ।
japārpaṇavidhau śambhorbhuktaye muktaye kramāt ।।

nivedyaivaṃ japaṃ vidvānakṣamālāṃ nidhāpayet ।
samudgake manojñe tu śrīparṇīdātvanirmite ।।

dugūlapaṭvakauśeyavastragaṇḍopadhānake ।
gāyatryā kṛtarakṣe tu tanmaye vā prasevake ।।

adīkṣitenākṣamālā na sampravyā kadācana ।
tathānyamantriṇā caiva prāyaścittaṃ śataṃ śriyaḥ ।।

anyamantrajapācchuddhiḥ punassaṃskārakarmaṇā ।
sthāpanādividhānaṃ ca proktaṃ pauṣkaraśāsane ।।

।। japaprakaraṇam ।।

(76b)
prabhāmaṇḍalake dvāre gurāvāhākarmaṇi ।
dhūpe naivedyake cānte dūtīṃ sampūjya cādayet ।।

dūtīśaktissamuddiṣṭā kriyākhyā parameśvari ।
tadyodhikā jñānaśaktyā mantramātāmaheśvarī ।।

dhvaniniṣṭhāyato mantrā avyaktāḥ parameśvarāḥ ।
dhvaniḥ kriyātmako nādo nādaśaktirato hi sā ।।

śabdavyaktyai sthitāsatvakriyāśaktirato dhruvā ।
atasmājñānaśaktestu tādātmyaṃ pratipadyate ।।

abhiṣṭamānaṃ yaddevi tacca ṣoḍaṣadhā kuru ।
pañcavedāgnisaṅkhyākaṃ prākkumbhāvayavatrayam ।।

yadvā taddaśadhā proktaṃ catustritritayātmakam ।
avyaktānāṃ tathāstrāṇāṃ sākārāṇāmataśśṛṇu ।।

trivicchedaṃ vibhajyādau bhāgamānaṃ kaṭāṇḍajam ।
bhāgaḥ karāgrato prokte devīrbhāgena kalpayet ।।

astrarājaṃ tu vā sākṣādekapādaṃ trimuṇḍakam ।
ṭaṅkādiyāgasaṃrūḍhaṃ kevalaṃ vā kajasthitam ।।

devīrūpaṃ yathāproktamastrarājaṃ maheśvari ।
triguṇaḥ pariṇāhaśca padmapattrāṅkitaśśubhaḥ ।।

karṇikā vā viniṣkrāntaḥ karagrāho vidhīyate ।
muktajālādiracanā nirmitaṃ śabdaveśma ca ।।

kṣetrāvaṅguliniryātastīkṣṇalohavinirmitaḥ ।
śabdavyañjaka ityukto madhyamaprāṇasaṃjñakaḥ ।।
(77a)
evaṃ vinirmitā ghaṇṭā sarvasiddhikarīśubhā ।
tiṣṭhatīyaṃ gṛhe yatra tatra sākṣācchivassthitaḥ ।।

saṃkṣālitakṛtāsthā tu kṛtārcāsu śubhadhvaniḥ ।
ubhayārthapradāghaṇṭā dūtīmantrāraṇissmṛtā ।।

dūtiḍāmaraityuktā viśeṣo darcyatenyataḥ ।
śaktiryābindunādākhyā vibhośśaktīśvarasya tu ।।

saiṣāghaṇṭāsmṛtādevi śivādhvānapathānugā ।
nādastasyā{ssthi}taśśabde binduścaivākṛtau sthitaḥ ।।

anekarūpabhedena tathā tadvakṣyatedhunā ।
kālāgneśśaktiparyantaṃ vyāptistasyāḥ pratiṣṭhitā ।।

mahāvidvīti sā śakti ghaṇṭārūpeṇa saṃsthitā ।
sṛṣṭissaṃlāpanaṃ proktaṃ saṃhāraḥ parivartanam ।।

pañcatatvamayaṃ cāsāḥ prameyaṃ samudāhṛtam ।
* * vyādīni tatvāni bhedatrayagatāni ca ।।

jñeyāni khāvasānāni pauruṣāntaṃ tathā viduḥ ।
pañcapañcaprabhedena saptapañcakameva vā ।।

prameyamasyā ityuktaṃ pañcopakaraṇānvitam ।
pañcalohamayīghaṇṭā tīkṣṇaśabdavivarjitā ।।

nātitīkṣṇasvanāṃ kuryānnātihrasvāṃ tathaiva ca ।
tīkṣṇaśabdena mṛtyusthasūkṣmenaiva dhanakṣayaḥ ।।

atidīrghasvanenaiva dviṣṭhassarveṣu jantuṣu ।
prayatnātkārayedghaṇṭāṃ pañcā * * padmāṅkitāṃ ।।
(77b)
* * * * cakkracihnāṃsthalāṅkāṃ vā śubhadhvanim ।
śaktiśśūlatathāvajraṃ vāgīśī vṛṣabhaḥ khagaḥ ।।

kamuṇḍalurgaṇeśeva yādṛśīceṣṭadevatā ।
dīkṣitānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ vajrāstrādimayaṃ vapu ।।

pūrvoktasusvanāṃ kuryāt prayatnena susiddhidam ।
pūjitāṃ dhūpitāṃ liptāṃ mantravinyastavigraham ।।

ekīkṛtya svaśabdena hṛdgatenāntarātmanā ।
trailokya drāviṇīghaṇṭā sarvaduṣṭanivarhaṇaḥ ।।

dūtīhyeṣā tu mantrāṇāṃ suptānāṃ ca prabodhinī ।
mantraughamudgirantī ca mantrajvālāsamanvitā ।।

devīsaṃsthayamānā ca brahmādyairbrahmavādibhiḥ ।
ṛṣibhirmunibhissiddhai lokānugrahakāribhiḥ ।।

nāsāsaṃsmaryate cāpyā pūjākāle sadaiva hi ।
oṃ hrīṃ śuddhavidyodbhavāyai mantraṃ mātre hūṃ phaṭ svāhā ।।

dhvaniniṣṭhāyato mantrā mantrānādasamudbhavāḥ ।
nādaśśrotravaho nityaṃ śrūyate parameśvari ।।

tadabhikṣā * kīhyeṣā saptamantrāraṇīśiva ।
āvāhane sthāpane ca tathā devi visarjane ।।

sarvathaiva prayoktavratasaptamantraprabodhanī ।
vādayeddvāmahastena vinā dhūpaṃ na cālayet ।।

itthaṃ prasaṅgato dūtaṃ jñātvā prastutamucyate ।

।। iti dūtī prakaraṇam ।।
(78a)
viśeṣapūjanaṃ homaṃ yathāśaktyā prakalpayet ।
vādyagītapranṛttādyaisstutibhiḥ pūjayecchivam ।।

aṣṭāṅgapatanaṃ kṛtvā vijñāpya parameśvaram ।
bhagavanbhogamokṣārthaṃ dharmayā kheditaḥ prabhuḥ ।।

tatkṣantavyaṃ sadādeva vidhisthasya mama prabho ।
vidhinyūnākṛtādeva pūjāśāstroditā mayā ।।

na sampannā ca bhūyastvāt prārthanairsravyasañcayaiḥ ।
ālambya bhaktimātraṃ tu vidhānaṃ yatkṛtaṃ mayā ।।

tatsarvaṃ sa phalaṃ mestu suprasanne vibhau tvayi ।
prasannavadanaṃ hṛṣṭaṃ varaṃ dattaṃ vibhāvayet ।।

upanyasya tato yāgaṃ saṃhareta kramātpriye ।
agraṃ saṃprārthyagṛhṇīyātsthāpayedastrarakṣitam ।।

viśeṣapūjāmarghaṃ ca datvā kuryānnamaskṛtim ।
tato devinirodhārghaṃ datvā savyāpasavyataḥ ।।

tato visarjayeddevaṃ dhāmamantramanusmaran ।
ātmano recakaṃ kṛtvā puṣpaṃ devāya nikṣipet ।।

saṃhāriṇyātha saṃhṛtya mantrātpārśvāgratassthitān ।
bidyudvaścalitāṃllīlānvāmadehe tu bhāvayet ।।

vidyāndehaṃ maheśasya vilīnaṃ binduvigrahe ।
binduṃ ca nādaśaktisthaṃ śaktirūpaṃ ca vāhayet ।।

vyāpakena svarūpeṇa praṇavo bhayasampuṭam ।
saṃhāriṇyātha saṃhṛtya dvādaśānte niyojayet ।।

pūrakeṇa hṛdinyasya svasthānasthaṃ vibhāvayet ।
(78b)
sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ devaṃ tathā sakalaniṣkalam ।।

bhinnāvasthaṃ svamantreśaṃ hṛdisthaṃ saṃsmaret priye ।
visarjanavidhistvevamiti svacchandaśāstrataḥ ।।

evaṃ vyaktādiliṅgeṣu kintusaṃhṛtyatānaṇūn ।
bhogasthayasthitānmūrtau vidadhyānnopasaṃharet ।।

parorghesteṣviyattāyai pūjāyai na visṛṣṭaye ।
āvāhanaṃ na sānnīdhyai pūjānujñā(pta)ye tvaham ।।

vidyāpīṭhepi matvaivamekoccāreṇa pūjayet ।
sraggandhadhūpanaivedyaissamāsarucirīśvaram ।।

sūryendumaṇḍale vyomni gurau tatpādakāsu ca ।
jale tejasi mūrdhnisve śraddhāvān kalpavicchide ।।

visarjane viśeṣoyaṃ mṛgendre samprakāśitaḥ ।

।। visarjana vidhi prakaraṇam ।।

na hyāgamosthimantrāṇāṃ naivāsthi ca visarjanam ।
sādhakohyaṃ svatantraśca nityaṃ yasmācca tanmayaḥ ।।

āgaccheti ca yadvrūte ātmanyāmantraṇaṃ ca tat ।
yājanārthamahaṃ samyagbhavāmi bhagavanmayaḥ ।।

etadāvāhanaṃ sadyaddhṛdayātsvaraṇaṃ bahiḥ ।
pūjāvasānakāle ca gacchetyuktvā hṛdi nyaset ।।

gacchāmi bhagavaccāhaṃ manyakarmaṇi me kṣama ।
etadvyāpakaśaktīnāmāvāhanavisarjanam ।।
(79a)
sarvagānāṃ tu mantrāṇāmuktaṃ na tvitareṣu vai ।
teṣāṃ na kāryamāgaccha kṣamasveti yathāsthitam ।।

jñātvaiva ca yajetpaścāddehacakreṣu bhūgate ।
ayaṃ vyaktāttathāsiddhavibudhaiśca pratiṣṭhitān ।।

ete viprādikairvipradevabimbaṃ niveśitam ।
vinyastaṃ tatra yanmantramaṅgamantrassa lāñchanaiḥ ।।

sahaprayāti svaṃ sthānaṃ tyaktvā bimbaṃ kriyāṃ vinā ।
śambhvācāryaṃ ca kartāraṃ yadarthaṃ ca pratiṣṭhitam ।।

piśācabhūtavetālāssayakṣāścaiva rākṣasāḥ ।
sthānaṃ pīṭhaṃ ca bimbaṃ ca dhvajāntaṃ saṃśrayanti te ।।

evaṃ jñātvā mahābuddhe tathā kāryaṃ śubhepsunā ।
pratiṣṭhitasya bimbasya {ya}thā sampadyate śubham ।।

pūjāhomapradānaiśca vādiraissahapītakaiḥ ।
svagṛhe calabimbasya puṣpadhūpādikīsthitiḥ ।।

sarvakālaṃ yathāśaktyā bhaktyaiḥ kāśca śubhāptaye ।
yāvajjīvāvadhiṃ kālaṃ tatvamantraiḥ pratiṣṭhitam ।।

traikālaṃ calabimbasya mantraṃ mantrīsvakaṃ yajet ।
ityavicchedavidhinā pūjanāhvānakādikam ।।

darśitaṃ pauṣkare śāstre viṣṇunā padmajanmanā ।
arghapātraṃ purā yadvadvidhāya sveṣṭamantrataḥ ।।

tena maṇḍalayāgādi sarvaṃ saṃprokṣayedvudhaḥ ।
tatastatraiva saṅkalpya dvārāsanagurukramam ।।
(79b)
pūjayecchivabhāviṣṭassvadehāntaḥ purassaram ।
tatastatsthaṇḍilaṃ sarvaṃ vyomasphaṭikanirmalam ।।

bodhānmakaṃ samālokya tatassvaṃ devatāgaṇam ।
pratibimbaṃ tathā paśyedvidhitvena prabodhataḥ ।।

etadāvāhanaṃ mukhyaṃ vyajanānmarutāmiva ।
sarvagopi marudyadvadvyajanenopajīvitam ।।

arthakṛtsarvagaṃ mantracakraṃ rūḍhestathā bhavet ।
catuṣpañcāśikāyāṃ ca yadetattatvamucyate ।।

śrīmatsvacchandaśāstrepi tadeva vibhunoditam ।
devassarvagato deva nirmaryādaḥ kathaṃ śivaḥ ।।

āvāhyattekṣasya deveśyevaṃ pṛṣṭho vravīdvibhuḥ ।
vāsanā vāhyate devi vāsanā ca pratiṣṭhitaḥ ।।

pūjitastuta ityevaṃ hṛdi devaṃ visarjayet ।
āgatasya tu mantrasya na kuryāttarpaṇaṃ ca yaḥ ।।

haratyadhvaṃ śarīrāt sa tasyeti śiva * diśet ।
tantrālokepi guruṇā proktamāvāhanādikam ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi lokapālā yathā sthitāḥ ।
herukaṃ pūrvapīṭhe tu āgneyāṃ tripurāntakam ।।

dakṣiṇe tvagnivetālā agnijihvaṃ tu nairṛte ।
kālākhyaṃ vāruṇe pīṭhe rājarājeśvarassmṛtaḥ ।।

eṣāmaṅgatayā ca * ṣṭāṣṭakamahābalaḥ ।
upapīṭhe tu sandehe tathānye sarvatassthitāḥ ।।

kṣetre kṣetre ca nagare grāme grāme vasanti te ।
(80a)
adhikāraṃ śivādiṣṭāstepi kurvanti pālakāḥ ।।

vighnarūpāssurāṇāṃ ca vīrāṇāṃ siddhimicchatām ।
upacchidrāṇi kurvanti dhvaṃsayanti tu pūjitāḥ ।।

ajara āpabhūtiśca iḍādhārastathaiva ca ।
īśamūrtistatholkālyaraṃṣmādonṛpumardanaḥ ।।

ṛrdhamūrtiḥ (ktya)ptakāyaśca lṝphapādaikadaṃṣṭriṇau ।
airāvataśca ojoghna auṣadhaghnastathāparaḥ ।।

añjanaḥ aḥ havāpaśca kambalotha kharānanaḥ ।
gomukhaścaiva ghaṇṭākhyoṭasaṃjñaḥ caṇḍarāvaṇaḥ ।।

phaṭāṭopo jaṭālākhyo jhāṅkāro ñabhaṭeśvaraḥ ।
ṭaṅkapāṇirathānyaśca dhānubandhaśca ḍāmaraḥ ।।

phaḍphakarṇoṇarīkāntastapanassthavirastathā ।
danturo dhanadaścaiva nāgakarṇaḥ parātmavān ।।

ḍhakāraścaiva bībhatso bhaṅgurākṣo mahāsuraḥ ।
yugānto rauravaścaiva lamboṣṭho vasanastathā ।।

śaṅkukarṇaṣṣaḍālākhyassurādho hūhukastathā ।
kṣayāntaśceti pañcāśatkṣetrapālā udāhṛtāḥ ।।

kṣetragrāmākṣarādyante madhye vāliṅgya saṃsthitāḥ ।
yathā kṣetre tathā grāme ādimadhyāntalakṣaṇam ।।

yasya nāmnā tu yatkṣetraṃ tatkṣetrasya tamarcayet ।
tatkṣetrabhraṣṭanāmnāṃ tu nāma teṣāmataśśṛṇu ।।

ekaliṅgeṣu huṅkāraśśmaśāne ca bhayāvahaḥ ।
mahālakṣmī vane ghore vaśejjvālāmukhassadā ।।
(80b)
ekavṛkṣe tathā jvāle biḍālavadano bhavet ।
ghaṇṭākarṇe guhāvāsī padmaṣaṇḍe pralambanaḥ ।।

saṅgrāme bhūridhāraughaḥ parvate mārutastathā ।
nirjare pravahaścaiva nidhāne maṇibhadrakaḥ ।।

rasakṣetre parādhyakṣa yajñavāṭe tu kroṣṭakaḥ ।
araṇye śavaraṃ vidyādudyāne madhupassthitaḥ ।।

catuṣṣaṣṭirmahāvīryā jagadvāpya vyavasthitaḥ ।
yatra kṣetrastha ācāryo balipūjāṃ nivedayet ।।

catvārastatra bhūpālā dvijādyā grāhakā balim ।
bhūkhagastarupakṣī ca śvacārī balikāṅkṣiṇaḥ ।।

nityādhikāriṇaścaite kravyādāntetarejanāḥ ।
barvarāḥ piṅgakeśāśca piṅgabhrūśmaśrulocanāḥ ।।

daṃṣṭrākarālā atyugrā kapālābharaṇojjvalāḥ ।।

।। sarveṣāṃ balimantrāḥ ।।

aṣṭāvanye mahāvīrā mātṛvaṃśasamudbhavāḥ ।
nivasanti hi sarvatra nagare catvare gṛhe ।।

kulajāyā gṛhe vāso divyāyā jñānamātaraḥ ।
upadeśāddhijāyante paramajñānasasphuṭaḥ ।।

vasanti tai tatra nityaṃ svāge yatrāgatāpi vā ।
daṇḍādhāraḥ pracaṇḍaśca * jaṭaśśaktipāṇikaḥ ।।

śaktituṇḍo gadāhastau daṃṣṭrālassūkarānanaḥ ।
vajratuṇḍastu kāpālī triraśmiścāṣṭamassmṛtaḥ ।।
(81a)
brāhmyādyassaṃsthitāścaivamaṃśakairdevatālaye ।
viditvā lakṣaṇaiścihnaissvabhāvairdevatā gṛham ।।

tasyāṃ śetu tataḥ kṣetre kṣetrapālastuto bhavet ।
svayambhūryatra vai liṅgaṃ tathānyaśca śivasya yat ।।

ekaliṅgaikavṛkṣe vā parvate girigahvare ।
nirjhare saṃsrave naśyossaṅgame lokaviśrute ।।

padmaṣaṇḍe taḍāke vā hrade caivātha catvare ।
catuṣpathaikaśṛṅge vā śmaśāne kānanepi vā ।।

mahālakṣmī ca ghoreṣu sukṣetre siddhabhūmiṣu ।
rasāyanavidhānāni siddhadravyāṇi yatra ca ।।

ārāme * * jvāle bhagnarūpe sarasyatha ।
mātṛṇāṃ nilayo yatra yajñavāṭe mahāhave ।।

gokule nagaradvāre mahārājñaṃ pure tathā ।
yakṣanāgaśriyo yatra caṇḍikāyā gṛhepi vā ।।

mahādevālaye viṣṇorambikāyāravestathā ।
pātālagāminisvāre hyasurāṇāṃ pure tathā ।।

kalpavṛkṣo yatra tiṣṭhennyagrodhādinmahādrumaḥ ।
udyānāni sugandhīni sthānaṃ yatra manoramaḥ ।।

yatra yatra svayambhūśca śivādyanyāpi devatā ।
nityaṃ rakṣanti tatsthānaṃ vighnāstatra mahābalāḥ ।।

niyuktāste mahādevi sthāneṣveteṣu rakṣakāḥ ।
tapaḥkāle nṛṇāṃ vighnānnārabhante sadā hyamī ।।

na siddhyanti surāścāpi kṣetrapālairupadrumāḥ ।
upadravāṃśca kurvanti vighnā hyetetra sādhake ।।
(81b)
sādhakaistu yadā devi na jñātā na ca pūjitā ।
tadā te tu durā rakṣāḥ sthānabhaṅgaṃ prakurvate ।।

aghorī pañcaśatakātkṣetrapālavidhimamum ।
jñātvā samyaksādhakendrāssiddhiṃ yāsyantyabhīpsitām ।।

paścādbaliḥ pradātavye mātṛbhūtagaṇāya ca ।
bhūteśvarebhyo deveśi kṣetrapālāya bāhyataḥ ।।

oṃ bhūtāye vividhākārādivibhūmyantarikṣagāḥ ।
dhruvādisarvalokāntā aindrādyāmāsthitāśca ye ।।

sarvesu prītamanasaḥ pratigṛhṇantvimaṃ balim ।
svāhākārasamāyogāttṛpyantyuścārayatkṣipet ।।

namaskāreṇa saṃpūjya gandhaiḥ puṣpairanukramāt ।
pūrvādīśānaparyantamadhaścordhvaṃ samantataḥ ।।

koṇasthāne kṣetrapālāśca patitāśśvapacā api ।
baliṃ datvāttatsutebhya ācamya tu varānane ।।

snāyāttatotha voddhulya yatheṣṭāṃ sthitimācaret ।
samantrako bhūtabaliśśrīsvacchandādudīritaḥ ।।

teṣāṃ svarūpamuktaṃ śrīdevyāyāmalaśāsane ।
gṛgdhragomāyubhāsāśca haṃsakārā purāstathā ।।

cakravākastathā krauñcāḥ kākolukāstathā pare ।
sāraṅgāssanasāṭāśca mārjārāvṛkavānarāḥ ।।

māyārūpadharāssarve devyo dūtyaśca yogikaḥ ।
kṣetrapālāstathaivāsminnevaṃ rūpadharāssadā ।।

(82a)
mānavāstānpaśyanti devyaḥ paśyanti tatra tān ।
tathāpyevaṃvidhānsṛṣṭā baliṃ teṣāṃ nivedayet ।।

।। bhūtaparijñānam ।।

niṣkrānto yāgabhavanādvāhye na balinā tataḥ ।
diṅmātṛsskandamātṛśca bhūcarīḥ khecarīstathā ।।

kākinīśśākinīścaiva ḍākinī * lāsikāḥ ।
śmaśānāni ca bhūteśāndevaprasthānaharmyagān ।।

krameṇa kṣetrapālāṃśca śuddhe deśe pratarpayet ।
tataścotsṛjyavastrāni sarvaṃ sajjanamācaret ।।

ambhasā bhasmanā samyakkurvītā cāntimeva ca ।
naiṣṭhikabrahmacaryastha bhautikasya gṛhe sthitiḥ ।।

punarvastrādisaṃvītastatonyāstādikarmakṛt ।
agnikāryādhikārāya śivaṃ sthaṇḍilapūjitam ।।

paripūjyāratetātha karmaviśvānarāśrayam ।
bāhyettatra baliṃ prāha śrīmāndharmaśivābhidhaḥ ।।

itthamarcanavidhinirūpitaśśambhunāgamayaṇe yathoditaḥ ।
sostu nirmalahṛdaṃ śubhāptaye vakṣyategniyajanaṃ tathādhunā ।।

।। ityāgamoddhṛte nityādisaṅgrahercanaprakaraṇam ।।

(82b)
oṃ namaśśivāya ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vahnikāryaṃ śivoditam ।
bhūmibhāge same śuddhe sarvaśalya vivarjite ।।

vediṃ kuryātsurāmantrī santāścakuraśrikām ।
prāṅmekhalānusāreṇa rudrāṇī sā prakīrtitā ।।

tatpṛṣṭhe kārayet kuṇḍaṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam ।
vartulaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ sarvakāryaphalapradam ।।

yāto vanermadhyabhāge mekhalāḥ kaṭisūtravat ।
samantataḥ * * tavyāḥ kuṇḍasānuprasārataḥ ।।

kuṇḍavediriyaṃ proktā vāmadevīyasaṅgrahe ।
pūrvato yogapīṭhasya vijayāyāgnimārabhet ।।

dakṣiṇena tu sāntyarthaṃ śrīkāma paścimena tu ।
uttareṇa śāntyarthaṃ dhanalābhārthameva ca ।।

āgneyāṃ jñānalābhāya yogasiddhyarthameva ca ।
āgneyyāṃ śatrunāśāya vijayāya dhanāya ca ।।

nairṛtyāṃ tu kulotsande śatrupakṣasya sarvataḥ ।
uccāṭane tu vāyavyāṃ vaśyākarṣakriyāsu ca ।।

agrato vāpi devasya sarvakāmātprasādayet ।
idamuktaṃ maheśena yakṣiṇī pārameśvare ।।

caturaśraṃ bhavetpūrvaṃ āgneyyāṃ tu bhagākṛti ।
yāmyāyāmardhacandraṃ tu nairṛtyāṃ tryaśrakaṃ matam ।।

vartulaṃ vāruṇe jñeya ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vāyugocare ।
(83a)
padbhābhaṃ saumyadigbhāge hyaṣṭāśramīśagocare ।।

uttarābhimukhaṃ jñeyaṃ prāgāgneyaṃ varānane ।
dakṣiṇaṃ nairṛtaṃ caiva prāṅmukhaṃ parikīrttitam ।।

paścimaṃ vāyukuṇḍaṃ ca saumyavaktraṃ vidurbudhāḥ ।
saumyavaktraṃ tathaiśānyaṃ prāṅmukhaṃ parikīrtitam ।।

trimekhalaṃ suyoniṃ ca taṣṭavedisamanvitam ।
sthaṇḍile vā śubhe ramye ālikhet kuṇḍamutta{ma}m ।।

dṛṣṭamitthaṃ digvidikṣu śrīvṛhatpiṅgalā mate ।
atha lakṣaṇaśāstroktaṃ kuṇḍaṃ muṣṭyādisaṃmitam ।।

vedāgraṃ yonikhaṇḍendu tryaśraṃ vṛttaṃ ṣaḍaśrakam ।
padmamaṣṭāśrame teṣāṃ pūrvādikkramaśassthitiḥ ।।

vijayo mandaṃ śāntiḥ kṣayaścāpyayanaṃ gamaḥ ।
puṣṭijñānaphalīprāptistanavaddiṃṅmukhasthitiḥ ।।

suṣṭyanantyekahastaṃ dvicatuṣpañcāṣṭahastakam ।
homadravyabalaṃ jñātvā kāryaṃ caiva naladraham ।।

śatārdhaṃ śatasāhasraṃ mayutaṃ lakṣameva ca ।
daśalakṣaṃ koṭisaṅkhyāmeteṣāṃ homakalpanam ।।

ṣaṭpañcaśadbhavatyevaṃ prāha dharmaśivābhidhaḥ ।।

trimekhalaṃ bhavetkuṇḍamekamekhalameva vā ।
muṣṭhimātrasya kuṇḍasya mekhalādvyaṅkuladvayam ।।

mekhalā dvitayaṃ cānyadaṅgulāṅgulahāritaḥ ।
hastamātrasya kuṇḍasya pūrvānemiṣṣaḍaṅgulā ।।

(83b)
parāṅgulacatuṣkeṇa dvitīyā cāṅgulaisstribhiḥ ।
caturhastasya kuṇḍasya pūrvāsyādaṅgulāṣṭakam ।।

dvitīyā ṣaḍbhirevasyāccaturbhiraparāṅgulaiḥ ।। 5 ।।

kartavyā mekhalā pūrvā ṣaḍhastasya daśāṅgulā ।
aṅgulaiścāṣṭabhiścānyā tṛtīyā ṣaḍbhiraṅgulaiḥ ।।

aṣṭahastasya pūrvaikā mekhalā dvādaśāṅgulā ।
daśāṅgulā dvitīyā syāttṛtīyāṣṭabhiraṅgulaiḥ ।। 7 ।।

kuṇḍasya yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ mekhalānāṃ tathā bhuve ।
caturviṃśāṃśamānetha kāryāmadhyoṣṭhakalpanā ।।

kuṇḍamadhyagatānābhiḥ kartavyā mekhalānugā ।
aśvarūpatrasadṛśī vistāroṣṭāṅgulotra tu ।।

dvādaśāṅguladairghyeṇa tatoṣṭāṅgulavistṛtiḥ ।
śeṣāṇāṃ dvyaṅgulāvṛddhivistārāya matopi hi ।।

na trayaḥ kuṇḍayonīnāṃ proktastallakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu ।
pūrve sūtraṃ purākṛtvā tatra matsyadvayaṃ bhavet ।।

sarveṣāṃ caturaśre tu kṣetraṃ kṣetrasamantataḥ ।
kṣetrārdhakoṇako grāhyaḥ kṣetrārdhaṃ bhrāmayettataḥ ।।

tatkṣetrasyāvadhiṃ yāvattathānyaṃ bhrāmayet punaḥ ।
pañcabhāgī kṛte kṣetre bhāgamekaṃ bahirpayet ।।

tasmāttatsaṅgamaṃ kṛtvā {tva}śvarthadalavadbhavet ।।
pañcabhāgīkṛtaṃ kṣetramanyasyārdhaṃ parityajet ।
tathetarānyabhāgasya tyaktvārdhaṃ tatsamaṃ punaḥ ।।

(84a)
kṛtvā sūtrabhramaḥ kāryo bhavettatrārdhacandrakam ।।

pañcabhāgīkṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ dvaubhāgau bāhyate nyaset ।
taddvayoḥ pārśvayostyaktvā tasmānmadhyaṃ vigṛhya ca ।।

lāñchayette tu mānena hyubhayoḥ koṇayorapi ।
tatra sūtratrayaṃ datvā trikoṇaṃ tyajyate sphuṭam ।।

navabhāge kṛte kṣetre sārdhaṃ bahirnayet ।
tasmānmadhyaṃ gṛhītvaiva tāmyaṃ tadvartulaṃ bhavet ।।

akṣabhāgī kṛte kṣetre bhāgamekaṃ bahirnayet ।
tasmānmadhyaṃ gṛhītvaivaṃ vigṛhya ca * * * * ।।

lāñchayetsarvakoṇeṣu tatraṣaṭsūtrapātanam ।
kṛtvā tattu bhavetkuṇḍaṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam ।।

padmaṃ padmasamaṃ kāryaṃ karṇikādalasaṃyutam ।
gambhīrataṃ tatkuryāddalāgrāddarśayedbahiḥ ।। 7

caturviṃśati bhāgāṃstu kṣetraṃ kṛtvā purā khaga ।
ekaṃ bhāgaṃ ca yaḥ paścāt sarvatraiva vināśayet ।।

madhyātkoṇaṃ gṛhītvā tu paścāttadikṣu lāñchayet ।
tatra tatsūtrasaṃyogādaṣṭakoṇaṃ bhavet khaga ।।

evaṃ kuṇḍaṣṭake jñeyā vartanā kiraṇopitā ।। 8

ekamekhalakuṇḍasya pañcamekhalakasya vā ।
ṣaḍbāṇāḥ kṛtripakṣairvā kramaśaḥ pañcamekhalāḥ ।।

aṅgulaiṣṣaḍbhirekā vā kuṇḍakārā ime matāḥ ।
kvacicchāstro mekhalānāṃ dṛṣṭoyaṃ bheda īdṛśaḥ ।।

ayadoṣo yathā na syātkuṇḍavṛddhitathārpayet ।
hastādikeṣṭahastānte tadvatsruksruvayorapi ।।
(84b)
aṅgulenādhikaṃ hastaṃ guruśiṣyostu kāmadam ।
caturviṃśāṅgulaṃ devi prayatnāgreva kārayet ।।

vāstudoṣo bhavedyasmāttasmādekāṅgulādhikam ।
aṅgulādhikyamāyārthamuktaṃ bhairavaśekhare ।।

evaṃ lakṣaṇata(tkū)ṇḍaṃ jñātvābhiṣṭamavāpnuyāt ।।

।। iti kuṇḍalakṣaṇam ।।

sruksruvau tu dṛḍhau kāryo kṣīravṛkṣasamudbhavau ।
śasyete śāntipuṣṭyostu praśastadrumasambhavau ।।

anyatra bhāvabhedena kāryau karmānusārataḥ ।
ṣaṭtriṃśāṅgulamānena sruksruvāhupramāṇataḥ ।।

ṣaḍaṃśapariṇārhe na daṇḍaḥ kumbhātsamutthitaḥ ।
caturaṅgulapīṭhāgrastatpīṭhaṃ kamalodaram ।।

kartavyaṃ daṇḍamadhyettadvyaṅgulāyatavartulam ।
ardhāṅgulasamutsedhaṃ vicitraracanākulam ।।

vedikāṣṭhāṅgulā kāryā caturaśrā suśobhanā ।
adhaḥ padmaniviṣṭhāttu ūrdhvaṃ pañcāṅgulāyatam ।।

khātaṃ tu tryaṅgulaṃ kāryaṃ tadūrdhve vartulaṃ bhramāt ।
ardhāṅgulapramāṇaiśca tilakairupaśobhitam ।।

kaṇṭha ekāṅgulaḥ kāryastatribhāga vivartitaḥ ।
pārśvayośca tathā kāryaṃ mukhaṃ sastāṅgulaṃ śubham ।।

tairghyāttatpārśvayoṣṭhau ca dvaubhāgau hrāsayet kramāt ।
mukhāgraṃ tatribhāgaṃ tu dvau bhāgau tasya pārśvataḥ ।।

vartayedvedhayettaṃ tu kaniṣṭhāṅgulimānataḥ ।
nimnanimnataraṃ kuryātmūlapīṭhaṃ triśākhinam ।।

(85a)
madhyāgrapiṭhaṃ padmāṅkaṃ kaṇṭhāṅgulasuvartulam ।
caturaṅguladīrghaṃ tu dvipuṭāgraṃ suvartitam ।।

vartayedracanāyuktaṃ ka(kṣā)pūrita(rva)trakam ।
catuṣpalā bhavetpūrṇā srukchaktistu sruvaśśivaḥ ।।

kriyāśaktirbhavetkuṇḍaṃ jñānaśaktistathānalaḥ ।
itīrthaṃ mṛtyujitkuṇḍe lakṣaṇaṃ samudīritam ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yāgāṅgānāṃ ca lakṣaṇam ।
samidho yajñavṛkṣātsāssatva(do) nirvraṇāśśubhāḥ ।।

prādeśamātrāṃ sthaulyena kaniṣṭhātyadhikānayāḥ ।
iti sāmānyataḥ proktāssamidho mayasaṅgrahe ।।

samidho yajñavṛkṣotthāḥ kṣīradrumavinirmitāḥ ।
pālāśākṣoṭakāpi bhrūvailvā arjunajā api ।।

deśajātiviśeṣeṇa etā vā manasaḥ priyāḥ ।
nātisthūlāśca sruṅmanāstavīkāṭṭakavarjitā ।।

vacahīnatvacaḥ prāṇi doṣaniṣkuṣitā api ।
nātiśuṣkāgranthicitāścinvīyātkāryasiddhaye ।।

agrapallavasaṃvītāstathākṣataviśodhitāḥ ।
tāsāṃ dviguṇamānena paridhītparikalpayet ।।

deśabhedaśca samidhāduktāssiddhānta ānane ।
na sthūlā na kṛṣāścāpi samidhoṅgulivāhataḥ ।।

ṛjvyovyaṅgaśśubhāḥ kāryāssādva * śśubhakarmaṇi ।
aniṣṭhe sphāṭitā vaktrāstvaṅghīnāssamidhassmṛtāḥ ।।

tadvadeve(dhsa)dārūṇi dviguṇā triguṇānvitaḥ ।
(85b)
vidyāpurāṇe dvividhamiti lakṣaṇamaiśvaram ।।

vistīrṇā dvidalā hrasvā vakrā cātikṛśā tathā ।
dvimukhasthūladīrghā ca varjanīyā prayatnataḥ ।।

vistīrṇā āyuṣaṃ kuryādvidalā rogadā smṛtā ।
prajāṃ nāśayate hrasvā vaktrāsaubhāgyameva ca ।।

kṛṣṭhā sarvatranāśāya dvimukhā siddhināśinī ।
vighnānkaroti vai sthūlā dīrgha * śayati dhruvam ।।

viśeṣeṇātha kathyante niyamāssaptadhā tu tāḥ ।
kāmataḥ ssaṃpravṛttānāṃ bhagavatsarvasevinā ।।

homadravyeṣu havanātphalabhedamataśśṛṇu ।
śaśvadeva {hi} ṣaḍvaktra agnikāryaratātmanām ।
saptānāmeva samidhāṃ samadhāyāśca yatsthitam ।।

phalaṃ saptavidhaṃ tāvatsāmānyaṃ śaśvadeva hi ।
śāntiḥ kāṣṭhāsamiddhoyannityamārādhakasya ca ।।

saubhāgyaṃ kusumairdivyairdivyalokasamaṃ matam ।
gandhaiśca{nda}nadhūrvaiśca sṛṣṭadhūpasamanvitaiḥ ।।

sājyairguggulasaṃmiśrairārogyaṃ sarvakāmikam ।
nindastadadhihomā tu puṣṭirvai mahatīṣu ca ।।

manasobhīṣṭhitānkāmānsiddhānnena hutena ca ।।

āyurārogyamaiśvaryaṃ putrapautrasamanvitam ।
prāpnotyājyāśca taddhomānmantrīsatkanyakāgaṇam ।।

leśato bhedabhinnasya punarevāsya ṣaṇmukham ।
(86a)
samidhā saptakasyātta icchābhedotthitaṃ phalam ।।

sāvadhānena manasā sāmprataṃ vinibodhatam ।
madhukṣīrājyasaṃyuktā hotāyassaddrumotthitā ।।

dvādaśāṅgulamānena hyatha prādeśasammitaḥ ।
phalamasvatthavṛkṣottha samidhā bhayavatpadam ।।

chedapaṃ ye tu tāmohānnacchindyātsvayameva hi ।
śrayādinyāyataḥ prāptā anyasmādyaśca yatnataḥ ।।

svayaṃ kālaṃ vaśāccaiva pātitā vā gajādibhiḥ ।
mahanmarujave nātha na dīkthalakṣate na ca ।।

anyathāsyānmahāndoṣassvayaṃ tacchedanāntavai ।
prāyaścittaṃ na vidyeta bhaktānāṃ tannikṛntanāt ।।

nyagrodhasamidhāmeva havanānmahatīṃ śriyam ।
tadumbaramayānāṃ tu hayanādamalekṣaṇa ।।
parāsu * tānṛṇāmacirādeva jāyate ।। 3 ।।

pālāśīnāṃ tu samidho havanāścaiva mantriṇām ।
phalaṃ bhogotthavibhavo bhū{mi}lābharipukṣayaḥ ।।
brahmāṇyatāvivekaśca prasādo mantrauttamaḥ ।। 4 ।।

phalaṃ kha * * *  tānāṃ samidhāṃ śṛṇu ṣaṇmukha ।
vārāśvamahiṣaprāptirabhimānākṣayo bhavāt ।। 5 ।।
vanavāraṇakāmastu homayeccandanodbhavāḥ ।। 6 ।।

vaśyārthaṃ naranārīṇāṃ kanyākāmastathaiva hi ।
raktacandanavṛkṣotthā homayetsamidhassute ।। 7 ।।

siddhayekhiladhātūnāṃ rājayakṣanivṛttaye ।
prāptaye maṇḍalānāṃ tu grāmāṇāṃ kramaśassuta ।।
(86b)
phalguvṛkṣamayaścaiva drakṣekṣukramukodbhavāḥ ।
apamṛtyujayārthaṃ ca homyaustvamṛtaba(lyi)jāḥ ।।

lānakhyādhijayārthaṃ tu hyapāmārgodbhavāssmṛtāḥ ।
sālatāladrumorthāśca vidhisaṃsiddhaye hitāḥ ।।

kṣatakṣatavināśārthaṃ madhūkābhayavṛkṣajāḥ ।।
vaṃśadrumamayāścaiva śabdabrahmaphalāptaye ।।
plakṣavṛkṣodbhavā sohamālasyaṃ tyājayanti ca ।। 9 ।।

suradāradrūmorthāśca samidhaḥ pārvatīsuta ।
brahmakṣayādikāndoṣāndhvaṃsayanti ratigrahaṃ ।।

kumārākhyaṃ tathā bāla grahaṃ pṛthum ।
madanuvṛkṣajāścaiva bhīmadrumamayāstathā ।।

yāḥ piśācatatatthatthāvai hutāstāsāṃ kramātphalam ।
durlabhāvaivarīsiddhiryakṣiṇī siddhireva ca ।।

vetālotthāpane caiva ।। samitkṛṣṇāgurūdbhavā ।
śiṃśipākhyāmahāseva rocanāñjanasiddhaye ।।

nārikoroḍamāṇotthā akṣoṭadrumajāstathā ।
hotavyāścāpyanāvṛṣṭi śāntaye nityameva hi ।।

avṛṣṭāvupaśame sarvāśani nivāraṇe ।
hotavyā trāptaye caiva āpyāyanavidhāvapi ।।
akṣadoṣajaye homā mātulaṅgatarūdbhavāḥ ।।

mūrtovātaprakope ca sāraṅgadrumajāstu vai ।
āprā āpratakīyāśca karamandakasambhavāḥ ।।

prasanākanyakāveśasvapnamānavake vidhau ।
(87a)
kapitthaṃ ca sasidvṛndaṃ bhavyavṛkṣodbhavaṃ tathā ।।

ṭaṅkapālevatīyaṃ ca kakṣikāradrumasomam ।
vairaṃ sarvajvarāṇāṃ ca nāśane vihitaṃ suta ।।

kādambaṃ vākpaṭutve ca cāṇpakaṃ palitakṣaye ।।
śrīparṇīdrumasambhūto valighnaśca samidgaṇaḥ ।।

viddhisarvāmayaghnaṃ ca vākkumbha na nivāraṇam ।
haridraṃ ca samidvṛndaṃ dhanañjayatarūtthitam ।।

putrajīvakasambhūta samidhāṃ nicayaśca yaḥ ।
karoti manasaḥssthairyaṃ sūryaṃ vṛkṣodbhavastavai ।।

dadāti paramāṃ medhā tejodussahameva ca ।
kṣīravṛkṣodbhavāssarvāssarvaṃ yacchanti sarvadā ।।

palāśakhadirāścatūnyagrodhoḍambarodbhavāḥ ।
sarvakāmapradāstvetā mantriṇāṃ saphalārthinām ।।

lākṣāyutasaphasrākhya śatasaṅkyastu ṣaṇmukho ।
hita icchāvaśāddhomassāmidhassarvakāmikaḥ ।।

prathamākāṣṭhasamitahavanānmitapadmānāṃ rājyaṃ yacchanti mantrarāṭ ।
lakṣmīṃ raktāmbujānāṃ tu havanānmahatīṃ suta ।।

maṇḍalīkanarendrāṇāṃ vadheyatva sakṛttrimam ।
homāntilotpalānāṃ tu mantranāśaḥ prayacchati ।।

tarpitaḥ kusumairevaṃ dadāti vibhavaṃ mahat ।
phalaṃ raktotpalānāṃ tu havanācchikhivāhana ।।

dāsīdāsagaṇaissārdhaṃ nagaraṃ vā puraṃ mahat ।
(87b)
kheṭakope sacirānmantramūrtiḥ prayacchati ।।

susitānāṃ sugandhānāṃ puṣpāṇāṃ sitapadmavat ।
havanāttu phalaṃ statraraktānāṃ raktapadmavat ।।

sasiddhānāṃ sugandhānāṃ manojñānāmumāsuta ।
nīlānāmasitānāṃ ca jñātānāṃ havanāttu vai ।।

puṣpāṇāṃ ca parijñeyaṃ phalaṃ nīlotpaloditam ।
haritānāṃ ca pītānāṃ karmaṇyānāṃ sugandhinām ।।

havanātkusumānāṃ ca phalaṃ syātkusumoditam ।।

।। dvitīyāṃ puṣpasamit ।।

candanāgurukarpūrahavanātkusumasya ca ।
āyurārogyamaiśvaryavibhavassumahadbhavet ।।

jijñāsūnāṃ bhayārtānāṃ bhruṇatāmarthināṃ suta ।
saṃyacchati ca mantrātmā nityaṃ yanmanasepsitam ।।

।। tṛtīyā gandhamasit ।।

śrīvāsādghṛtasaṃsiktāttadvarmajjarasātsuta ।
gugguloḥ kramaśoviddhi paśuputradhanaṃ bahu ।।

।। caturthī dhūpasamit ।।

dadhikṣīrarasālānāṃ havanātparameśvaraḥ ।
dadāti mahatīṃ suṣṭiṃ tuṣṭiṃ janmaphaloditām ।।

aihikaṃ sukhamārogyaṃ lakṣmīlābhamakṛtrimam ।।
।। paṃcamī kṣīrasamit ।।

prāpyate tilahomā tu ṛṇamokṣaṃ kulotthitam ।
anyeṣāṃ śiṣṭibījānāṃ haryakṣacchikhivāhana ।।

āyuṣaśca parāṃ vṛddhimapa * gaṇakṣayam ।
pakṣavṛddhiparāṃ caiva hyamitrakṣayameva ca ।।
(88a)
yavagodhūmapūrvāṇāṃ śālīnāṃ havanāpta vai ।
mantrīmantravarāṃścaiva hyacirātsarvamāpnuyāt ।।

guḍodanasya havanātpāyasasya ca ṣaṇmukha ।
yāvanmantrasya sājyasya mudgamāṣodanasya ca ।।

akṣayā labhate kīrtiṃ pūjāṃ vai sārvakāmikīm ।
prāpnuyāccharavanmantrakarmatantraparonataḥ ।।

bilvāmalakapūrvāṇāṃ phalānāṃ parameśvaraḥ ।
havanādakhila * mātmalakṣmīkārmayacchati ।।

।। ṣaṣṭhīpyatra samit ।।

gavyasyājyasya havanājjñānasiddhiḥ prajāyate ।
māhiṣasya mahāsena śarīrasthairyamuttamam ।।

kāgājya havanāścaivamojastejo balaṃ mahat ।
mantravitprāpnuyacchasva dvibhavena samanvitam ।।

sasarpissaktahavanācchamaṃ vyādheḥ prajāyate ।
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ samidhāsaptakaṃ suta ।।

sahajātīphalādyaistu prāgukṣairapi ṣaṇmukha ।
ekīkṛtya ca ya * * pratyahaṃ mantratarpaṇam ।।

sahadūrvāṅkuraiḥ putrasasitai haritaistathā ।
tasyāyaḥ paramaiśvaryaṃ balamojovṛtiryaśaḥ ।।

kāśasaubhāgyamatulaṃ dharmasañcayamuttamam ।
madamohāvivekānāṃmupaśāntiṃ ca śāśvatīm ।।

dadāti bhagavānśaśvatparituṣṭastu cācirāt ।।
।। ājyasamitsaptamī ।।

evaṃ sandarśitaṃ sarvaṃ śivenāśivahāriṇā ।
(88b)
kāṣṭhādisamidho homātṣaṇmukhasya phalaṃ mahat ।।

aiśvare saṃhitāśāstre kāmanādyanusārataḥ ।
vacmi etatsamāsena viṣṇunā darśitaṃ yathā ।।
।। garuḍa uvāca ।।

bhagavankamalākāntaśrīnivāsajagatpate ।
homyānāṃ parimāṇaṃ ca na jñātaṃ tatvato mayā ।।
pravruhi tridaśeśāna kramadravya vinirṇayam ।।

।। bhagavānuvāca ।।

sādhusādhumahābuddhe kathayiṣyāmyahaṃ tava ।
kramaṃ mānavidhāne ca satrassatyāvadhāraya ।।

prathamaṃ kāṣṭhasamidhaṃ sāṣṭadhāparipaṭhyate ।
pālāśakhadirāścarūśamīlohitacandanaiḥ ।।
bilvākṣoṭaiśca kathitā abhāvātkṣīravṛkṣajā ।।

puṣpākhyāṃ mallikājāti priyaṅgu bakulāḥ jakaiḥ ।
mālatī caṇpakodārakundaiścātha dvitīyakaiḥ ।।

abhāvāccharkarāyuktalājābhistu prakīrtitā ।
dhūsarasya tṛtīyā sā guggulvagurubhāvitā ।।

mṛgajendraturuṣkaiśca śuktikāśmīrasaṃyutā ।
candanena samāyuktāmaṣṭadhātvaparāṃ śṛṇu ।।

śarkarāghṛtamākṣīkadadhikardūrabhāvitā ।
sitailābhiryutā proktā hyaṣṭadhā pañcamī śṛṇu ।।

tilākṣatārāṇyabīja saptagrāhyastathaiva ca ।
padmabījamapāmārgasarṣapāścāṣṭadhā tataḥ ।।

ṣaṣṭhīghṛtasitā yuktā ṣaṣṭikānnena bhāvitā ।
(89a)
saptamīcābhyasaṃpannā phalākhyā tadanantaram ।।

karajābilvajānyākhya pūrvāmalakabhāvitā ।
madanākṣoda vai bhaṅgaiḥ kunduraṃ tadanantaram ।।

guḍūcīdugdhasaṃyuktā navanītaṃ sitānvitam ।
māṃsī ghṛtāktā tadanuktaṣṭamājyatilānvitam ।।

vacāharidradvitayaṃ tathaivājyatilānvitāḥ ।
kharjūramadhukāścāpi mṛdvīkā ghṛtabhāvitā ।।

tathā sarjarasādeyā patrāṇi tadanantaram ।
cūtajātī bilvapūganyagrodhaścātra saṃjñakāḥ ।।

kāryaṃ śatāṃvarī ceti ghṛtāktaḥ paridāpayet ।
bhūtakeśī dugdhayutā muktāni tadanantaram ।।

sahadevā pṛṣṭhaparṇī vṛhatī kaṇṭhakārikā ।
tathā garuḍavegā ca ghṛtākṣassamudāhṛtaḥ ।।

pañcāmṛtaṃ ca ta{da}lutilataṇḍulabhāvitam ।
ghṛtakṣīraṃ ca mākṣīkaṃ pañcāmṛtamudāhṛtam ।।

padmakośīrabadiranagakesarasaṃmitam ।
gokṣīraṃ kunduradūrvāsarṣapā sahadāpayet ।।

sarṣapāśca tato dadyātsarveṣāmanutaḥ khaga ।
anye prasiddhyā hotavyā pāvanā bhogasiddhaye ।।

mokṣārthamakṣatairhomaṃ kevalaistu samācaret ।
homadravyagaṇasyāsya pramāṇaṃ kathayāmite ।।

tilāghṛtasamājñeyā akṣatā dviguṇāssmṛtāḥ ।
grāmyadhānyā ghṛtasamā āraṇyadviguṇā smṛtāḥ ।।
(89b)
lājā ghṛtārdhena samā ghṛtatulya phalāni ca ।
puṣpāṇyāhutimānena dhūpaṃ tanmānameva ca ।।

madhuparkaṃ phalādīni hyevaṃ mānena homayet ।
ghṛtaturyāṃśamānena guḍācī kunduruṃ vaca ।।

tathaiva kuṣṭaṃ mṛdvī vā hyanye dravyāstadardhataḥ ।
abhāvāśca yathāśaktyā homayedvihageśvara ।।

homānte tarpaṇaṃ kuryādbhūtagrāmyasya vāhyataḥ ।
dikpatīnāṃ tathānyeṣāṃ pātālākāśagāminām ।।

lājādadhyodanayutaṃ nirmalaṃ śītalaṃ jalam ।
siddhārthakākṣatayutaṃ dātavyaṃ tarpaṇāya ca ।।

saṅgraheṇāṣṭadhābhedassamidhāsaptakasya ca ।
vihagendre pārameśe viṣṇunetthamudāhṛtaḥ ।।

sarvakāmaprado homastilaiścastoghṛtānvitam ।
dhānyairdhanārthasiddhyarthaṃ ghṛtagugguluhomataḥ ।।

jāyate vipulāsiddhiradhamāmadhyamottamā ।
śvetāravindairājyārthaṃ bilvaiśca śriyamāpnuyāt ।।

kṣīrāktatilahomena śāntikarma varānane ।
sitakṛṣṇo pītakṛṣṇaiścamanākṛṣṭipauṣṭikam ।।

māruje ca varārohe krameṇa parikalpayet ।
kundapuṣpaissutārthāya tvaśokaiḥ priyasaṅgamaḥ ।।

jātikuṭmalakaikkāryā gandharvī vakulodbhavaiḥ ।
nāgaistu nāgakanyā vai siddhakaissiddhakanyakāḥ ।।
(90a)
caṇpakaiścāpyapsaraso narendraḥ phalguṣeṇa tu ।
ghṛtāktena varārohe samantrīsapurohitaḥ ।।

rājñīputrasamopeto vaśaṃ yāti varānane ।
yakṣiṇīvaśamāyāti puṣpaiścaiva kadambajaiḥ ।।

vidyādharīṃ kuvyakaiśca sādhayennātra saṃśayaḥ ।।
iti svachandaśāstrepi homadravyaphalaṃ smṛtam ।।

dadhihomādbhavetpuṣṭiśśāntiḥ kṣīreṇa jāyate ।
havyaiṅgavī na homena paraṃ sāpyayanaṃ bhavet ।।

raktapuṣpājya homena paramāpyānaṃ bhavet ।
raktapuṣpājya homena paraṃ saubhāgyamāpnuyāt ।।

kṣatajājyāddharellūtā atraṃ vṛddhiṃ yavaikṣataḥ ।
samasaktaghṛtaṃ hutvā nāśayeta bhagandaram ।।

ghṛtaṃ madhutilaṃ hutvā kṣatajvararujāṃ haret ।
chāgasya piśitenāśu rājayakṣma vinaśyati ।।

nāreṇa kaṭutailena bhūtebhyo mocayetkṣaṇāt ।
modomajjāvasāhomātkṣatajāṃjyena bhairavi ।।

hyaṣṭottarasahasreṇa hyapasmṛtivinaśyati ।
kevalaghṛtahomena kāpilena sugandhinā ।।

aṣṭādhikasahasreṇa sarvānvyādhinvinaśyati ।
iti te kathitaṃ tubhyaṃmaghorasya prapūjanāt ।।

dravyabhedātphalāvāpti bhakṣyabhairavacoditā ।
vakṣyātetha viśeṣeṇa khabhūpātālasiddhiṣu ।।

uttamānmadhyamāścaiva kanyasā nitarāṇyapi ।
(90b)
uttamā nāma lābhena madhyamāṃścaiva homayet ।।

madhyamā nāma lābhena kanyasā nitarāṇyapi ।
homayedvidhivanmantrī yathālābhānurūpataḥ ।।

uttamaṃ nā ramākhyātaṃ madhyamaṃ kṣmā bhavaṃ vidu ।
bhāgaṃ phalgukanīyāṃstu phalavrīhītarasmṛtam ।।

dhvajāhataṃ tripyahatamudvaddhaṃ haṭhasādhitam ।
māsamityuttamaṃ jñeyaṃ vīrasiddhipradāyakam ।।

akālamṛtyusphuṭitaṃ vajrāśanihataṃ tathā ।
śītavātātapahataṃ jñeyamuttamamadhyamam ।।

jīrṇaṃ ca vyādhināgrastaṃ kṣutpipāsārditaṃ mṛtam ।
śavarīmatsyahavanaṃ māṃsamuttamakanyasam ।।

yavānaṃ vṛṣabho bhūyaḥ kapilaṃ vā suśobhanam ।
nāklinnaṃ na ca rogārtaṃ na śītātapakarṣitam ।।

sthirendriyasamudbhūtaṃ kṣmā bhavaṃ madhyamottamam ।
dantikesarisambhūtaṃ māhiṣāśvaṃ ca gardabham ।।

mārgaṃ maiṣaṃ ca gavalaṃ māṃsaṃ madhyamamuttamam ।
klinnaṃ śuṣkasuṣṭhumayaṃ jarāvyādhivināśitam ।।

māṃsamānandarahitāmetanmadhyamakanyasam ।
vividhaṃ śvetaśāraṅgaṃ pībaraṃ śṛṅginaṃ navam ।।

dhātubhiḥ paripūrṇāṅgaṃ jñeyaṃ kanyasamuttamam ।
meṣāvikasamudbhūtaṃ māṃsaṃ kanyasamadhyamam ।।

śvasṛgelokṣamārjāraśaśamūṣakasambhavam ।
(91a)
yathālābhānusaṃpannaṃ vidyātkanyasakanyasam ।।

puṣpāṇāṃ homasaṅkhyātaḥ padmotpalasavoduhām ।
sthalajānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ vijñeya itarottamaḥ ।।

bilvādīnāṃ phalānāṃ ca homaṃ itarakanyasaḥ ।
nityaṃ pitṛvane homyā uttamā vīrasiddhidāḥ ।।

ekaṃ liṅge parvatāgre madhyamāścottamā api ।
catuṣpathaikavṛkṣe vā gṛhe homyāstu kanyasāḥ ।।

itarāssarvato homyāḥ pratimedhena vidyate ।
uttamaiścottamāsiddhiraṇimādiguṇāṣṭakam ।।

madhyamaiḥ khecaratvaṃ hi yoginībhiśca melakam ।
bhūcarī siddhimāpnoti vilayantraprabhañjanam ।।

kanyasairvaśyamāveśyaṃ vidveṣoccāṭanādikam ।
sarvakāmasamṛddhissya - dyadicchatisādhakaḥ ।।

sumano daśasāhasrāṅgandharvāṇāṃ priyo bhavet ।
nīlotpalaiḥ kṛte home priyo bhavati sarvaśaḥ ।।

jātupuṣpairvarākanyā vaśamāyāti tatkṣaṇāt ।
tilairyavaihutaissiddhirvyādhināśaḥ prajāyate ।।

vrīhīnāṃ vrīhibhirvṛddhirghṛtakṣīraplutaissadā ।
yadya tu homayenmantrī tattadakṣayatāṃ vrajet ।।

ghṛtabhārasahasreṇa yatphalaṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ ।
nārastyadaśamāhutyā samaṃsyāttantravāsamam ।।

idaṃ rahasyaṃ paramaṃ na śrāvyaṃ ca kadācana ।
apavādaṃ prayacchanti garhayanti ca paṇḍitaḥ ।।
(91b)
tena sodhyassadācāro * * * * * * * * ।
na vinā śaktipātena vuddhiścātra prajāyate ।।

phalamuttamasiddhyādau proktaṃ sārasvate mate ।
uttamadravyahomāśca taddaśāṃśena tarpaṇam ।।

mahākṣmājapalānyāhuruttamādīni tadvidaḥ ।
madhyame dviguṇaṃ kuryāttriguṇaṃ kanyasepi ca ।।

ājyaguggulunṛsteha mahāmāṃsasamāsatāḥ ।
tilādyairatha kurvīta navaṣaṭtriguṇaṃ kramāt ।।
mahākṣmājasamohomo mālinīvijaye smṛtaḥ ।

।। saptasamidhādihomavidhiḥ ।।

yadā tu mṛtyumāghrātaḥ kālena kalitaḥ priye ।
dṛṣṭastatpratighātārthamamṛteśaṃ yajettadā ।।

yasya nāma samuddiśya pūjayenmṛtyujidvibhum ।
mṛtyoruttarate śīghraṃ satyaṃ me nānṛtaṃ vacaḥ ।।

sitaśarkarayā yuktairghṛtakṣīrāplutaistilaiḥ ।
puṇyadāvindhane vahnau kuṇḍe vṛtte trimekhale ।।

mahārakṣāvidhānena juhuyādyasya nāmataḥ ।
mahāśāntibhavetkṣipraṃ yatasyāpi yamālayam ।।

athavā śarkarāyuktapayasā kevalena tu ।
sugandhighṛtahomena kṣīraṃ vṛkṣamaye nale ।।

tarpito nāśayetmṛtyuṃ mṛtyuntānna saṃśayaḥ ।
।। mṛtyuñjayahomaḥ ।।

kṣīravṛkṣasamiddho sā jvaraṃ nāśayate kṣaṇāt ।
tilataṇḍulamākṣika sājyakṣīrasamanvitam ।।
(92a)
eṣa pañcāśato homassarvaduṣṭanivāraṇaḥ ।।

।। iti jvarādyupaśamavidhiḥ ।।

guggulo guḍikābhiśca tyaktābhiścaṇa * trayā ।
homānpuṣṭibhavatyāśukṣīṇadehasya suvrata ।।

।। iti muṣṭiḥ ।।

vrīhisaptakahomena dhanārthīdhanabhāgbhavet ।।
tilataṇḍulamadhvājya kṣīraṃ śarkarayā saha ।

homayet pūrvavat kuṇḍaḥ praśastendhanadīpite ।।
mahāntiḥ prajāyeta satyaṃmena nṛtaṃ vacaḥ ।।

।। śāntiḥ ।।
praśastasamidho homāt praśastarujenale ।
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti satyametannacānyathā ।।

rohitaṃ kāmamuddiśya īhitaṃ homamācaret ।
iti mṛtyuñjaye proktaṃ phalaṃ karmaprabhedataḥ ।।

atha tairnaśyatevyādhistilaiśśāntiḥ prajāyate ।
punarāgamanaṃ lakṣmyāssarvadravyairbhavettataḥ ।।

vaśyādikaṃ vidrumaiśca taṇḍulaissaptavrīhibhiḥ ।
sājyairakṣatasaṃmiśrairastrasiddhiḥ prajāyate ।।

vandhyāprasūyate devi padmākṣaiḥ puṣpamiśritaiḥ ।
eṣu sarveṣu homeṣu saṃyogaighṛtamiśrite ।।

ājyahīnastu yo homassacoccāṭanamāraṇe ।
kartavyaḥ kāmate devi nānyathānvājyavarjitaḥ ।।

abhāvātsarvadravyāṇāṃ sājyahomaṃ praśasyate ।
jvarāpasmārabhūtādilūtāpiṭakanāśane ।।

putrajīvikahomassyātsarvaduṣṭanivarṣaṇaḥ ।
(92b)
tilātsa * śca kṣaudrāñjuhuyājjñānasiddhaye ।।

uccāṭanārthaśatrūṇāṃ kaṇṭhakārī phalāni tu ।
vaśīkaraṇasiddhyarthadadhighṛtayutaṃ hunet ।।

siddhidolājajohomo mokṣajastilasambhavaḥ ।
maraṇārthaṃ ca śatrūṇāṃ * * anantahūyate ।।

ūrdhvādhassaṃvahninā ca phaṭkārāntu samanvitam ।
nyasenmadhye sādhyasaṃjñāṃ niśihomaṃ tvakārayet ।।

tena merasamaṃ siddhārthajuhuyāttataḥ * * ।
śatrumande mahādevi śatrumetannacānyathā ।।

śrīkaṇṭhyāmiti śarvoktaṃ pramāṇamadhunocyate ।।
pañcaśākhāhyutaṃ vruhi yavaṃ mṛgyātilādikam ।
sruvāvadhimadhukṣīrasarpibhikṣudravādi ca ।।

dadhipāṇinikuñjeṣu na mūlajānaṃ trikhaṇḍakam ।
bhakṣyavastuyathāsiddhaparvavarmavalakṣitam ।।

dṛgaṅgulalatāvallyaḥ phalapuṣpamakhaṇḍitam ।
mantraṃ ca samidhassarvaḥ pradeśāya tilakṣitāḥ ।।

vakṣyoyavīyasīḥ sthūlāssatvaconirvraṇāśśubhāḥ ।
badireṅgudadrākṣādeḥ pañcapañcāhutīḥ kramāt ।।

dadhikṣīrādisaṃpṛktasodanaṃ grāsalakṣitam ।
sruvodgataśikhaṃ vāpi kaṇasaktaṃstilādivat ।।

kāsāraṃgano kṛtpiṇḍī piṇyākāṇyodanādivat ।
(93a)
guggulonmāhiṣākṣasya guḍikābadaropamāḥ ।।

kalkasya madanādeśca madādeścaṇakopamāḥ ।
।। iti homadravyaparimāṇam ।।

triṃśattantu kṛtāveṇī varhimnāṃ viṣṭarasmṛtaḥ ।
na tulyā haritā hṛdyāḥ kuśāśca prakṛtiṃ vinā ।।

darbhāṇāṃ tālamānena kṛtā ṣaṣṭtriṃśatādalaiḥ ।
saptajaptaśivāstreṇa veṇīṃ bodhāsimuttamam ।।

prādeśāya tadarbhāgradviguṇagrathitaṃ mithaḥ ।
pavitraṃ vighnadoṣaghnaṃ pradīptaṃ guṇatejasā ।।

tripañcasaptakāṇḍoktaṃ kuryādevaṃ karadvaye ।
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ bhaumaṃ vā pāṇipūrakam ।।

śivārghaṃ pātrasabjārkāṃ vṛttaṃ vraṇavivarjitam ।
sacchaṅkhāśuktiśṛṅgādipalāśābjadalāni vā ।।

gavyapātrāṇyapīmānisyurviṣāṇādikaṃ vinā ।
ṣaḍaṅgulārdhaviṣkambhatramāsiddhāni bhūtaye ।।

pātrāṇi mṛccaturthebhyaḥ praṇītātolasarṣapāt ।
mṛgendre lakṣaṇamidaṃ vrīhyādīnāmudāhṛtam ।।

yatheṣṭamatraiva vibhunā ya(tho)ktannirūpyate ।
tadarghapātraṃ vistārādvitasti parimaṇḍalam ।।

uparyadhastādardhena samaṃ talamudāhṛtam ।
yathā ca vibhuvinyastaṃ tiṣṭhate supratiṣṭhitam ।।

patatyanyatra yadyarghaśśivāya parikalpitaḥ ।
nīcānāmunnatistatra rāṣṭre bhavati nityaśaḥ ।।
(93b)
tadā cājya pariskande vipa * * ti nityaśaḥ ।
praṇītā sthitibhaṅgasya rājño rāṣṭrasya caiva hi ।।

tasmāttrīṇi tataḥ kuryāt sthiramūlāni sarvadā ।
suvarṇanāsikākārā ṛjvagrā tu praṇālikā ।।

ājyasthālī tathā dhanyā nāsikaikasamanvitaḥ ।
grīvākāragrahopetā praṇītā tadvinā bhavet ।।

kumbhavṛtaṃ prakurvīta ka(rṇa)śaṃ cāśrisaṃyutam ।
kaṇṭhonnatistu pādāṃ sā vistarāddviguṇaṃ mukham ।।

unmattakusumākāraḥ karake kaṇṭha eva ca ।
kaṇṭhādadhocchritākāñcī mukhavistārasaṃmitā ।।

kalaśasyātha vardhakyā dīrghāthāḥ padabhogataḥ ।
dṛḍhasthādekavarṇā tu tadvannaivedyabhojanam ।।

arghogandhāśca puṣpāṇi siddhārthāstaṇḍulā yavā ।
kuśadūrvānvitaṃ dhanyā yathā saṃbhavatopi vā ।।

puṣpāṇi susugandhīni hyakhaṇḍitadalāni ca ।
pratyagrāṇi pavitrāṇi nirgandhānyapi varjayet ।।

durgandhapariṇāmīni sugandhānyapi varjayet ।
tathā caivagragandhīni kṛṣāni mukulāni ca ।।

nīlotpalādikaṃ grāhyaṃ tatrāpyanyatsukhāvaham ।
baddhabaddhānikā tadvatpatrāṇyapi phalānyapi ।।

hṛdyāmadhuragandhāśca gandhāḥ prāṇyaṅgavarjitāḥ ।
tathā dhūpā vicitrāṇi vastrāṇyapi tathaiva ca ।।
(94a)
daiśikānāṃ catuṣṭaṃ ca svevāyāmocchritaṃ bhavet ।
dvipādaṃ sādhakādīnāmaṅguladvaya hāritaḥ ।।

śivāsanasamaṃ vāpi daiśikasyāsanaṃ bhavet ।
viṣṭaracchattramupari tatsamaṃ śubhavāsasā ।।

caturaṅgulavistīrṇo yāgapaddhātsukhāvahaḥ ।
nissandhirgranthihīnaśca sarvasiddhikaraḥ paraḥ ।।

raudrākṣamakṣasūtraṃ ca pautrajīvakameva ca ।
samamāsphuṭitaṃ dhanyaṃ pauṣkaraṃ vā suśobhanam ।।

sphāṭikaṃ nirmalaṃ vātha uṣatrāśādivarjitam ।
śataṃ tadardhaṃ pādaṃ vā sāvitrī granthasaṃyutam ।।

bhakṣyabhojyaṃ tathā cūṣyaṃ lehyaṃ peyaṃ ca yatsmṛtam ।
pavitraṃ vidhivanmantraṃ naivedyaṃ tatprakīrtitam ।।

kṣīrājyamantrasiddhaśca carussaṃpātito bhavet ।
avidagdhaṃ śritaṃ caiva puroḍāśaṃ tathaiva ca ।।

śamaṃ ca śaphayā pakva kapālasthaṃ ghṛtaplutam ।
gavyamājyādikaṃ yacca yāvakaṃ tilasaṃyutam ।।

ājyapūrṇāvicitrāśca dhānyadhānta mayāśśubhāḥ ।
yaṣṭhyāvalambino vāpi dīpā dhārāśśubhā matāḥ ।।

tilā lājā yavassarponmadhudadhyodanaṃ payaḥ ।
havirmaṇḍaladevānāṃ paraṃ tṛptikaraṃ smṛtam ।।

tailaṃ māṣodanaṃ māṃsaṃ kṛsaraṃ matsyamākṣikam ।
sarvabhūtapriyo hyeṣa valibhaktassamāsataḥ ।।
vidyāpurāṇataścedaṃ yāgasadbharalakṣaṇam ।।

(94b)
yathāsamaya tatrādau homamātrā vidhīyate ।
aṣṭottaraṃ palaśataṃ ghṛtasya yadi hūyate ।।

tadā taddviguṇāḥ kāryāstaṇḍulāśca yavāstathā ।
tilāghṛtasamājñeyā śarkarā tu tadardhataḥ ।।

dugdhaṃ tu tatsamaṃ vidyāttadardhena ghṛtaṃ punaḥ ।
pañcāmṛtamidaṃ bhadre śāntipuṣṭikaraṃ nṛṇām ।।

lājānāṃ tu palāni syu * * saṅkhyāni bhāmini ।
saptavrīhipalānyatra pratyekaṃ sapta pārvati ।।

māṃsīcātha haridre dve kuṣṭaṃ śaṅkhā * * tathā ।
bilvamajjādevamunirnāgakesarameva ca ।।

śaileyacaṇpakasurāvacākharjūramustakam ।
* * cāriṇi dūrvākhyāḥ sarvauṣadhyaḥ prakīrtitā: ।।

pratyekaṃ tu palāssapta homayejjātavedasi ।
sa * * * tathaiva syādgurūśyāssaptaviṃśatiḥ ।।

palāni sarṣapānāṃ tu triphalāyā maheśvari ।
palāni * * * * śi pratyekaṃ sapta sapta ca ।।

tilavacchrīphalānisyuranyacchālisamaṃ phalam ।
pūgāstu śrīphalasamā jātīphalamatordhataḥ ।।

madanākhyaṃ tathā deyamanyatkākolakādikam ।
palāni trīṇi saptāni candanasya kṛṣodari ।।

palaṃ sakarṣaṃ hotavyaṃ tataḥ kuṅkumakesare ।
sitaśarkarayā yuktaṃ navanīta palatrayam ।।
(95a)
guggulormahiṣākṣasya dvikarṣaṃ dviguṇaṃ palam ।
paścāddhomyaṃ tu juhuyāddadhibhaktuṃ tataḥ priye ।।

tataḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ dadyādvahusambhārasamvyatām ।
dugūlakṛtaśobhāḍhyāmupavītādi saṃyutām ।।

tāmbūlena samāyuktā puṣpārghādi samanvitām ।
yadyadicchati vā prāptaṃ tattu ttatra samarpayet ।।

itthaṃ yastarpaddevaṃ vahnisthaṃ parameśvaram ।
socirācchivamāpnoti divyānbhogānsamaśnute ।।

homadravyeṣviyaṃ proktā vāthule saṃpradarśitā ।।

।। iti yāgāṅgaprakaraṇam ।।

tarpaṇaṃ tu gajendrāṇāṃ dine sūryasya siddhyati ।
puṣṭyārogyavivṛddhyarthamoṣadhīṣu prayuñjanam ।।

dine - - sya kurvīta śāntisvastyayanāni ca ।
nikhane doṣadhīścitrā nidhayo dravyasaṃñcayān ।।


bhomasyāhni viśeṣeṇa siddhiratra pratiṣṭhitā ।
budhena vidyādhigamaṃ bilasiddhirgurordine ।।

rasaṃ rasāyanaṃ vādamaṣṭadhā yatprakīrtitam ।
bhārgavasya dine sarvaṃ siddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

kṛtvā vetālakhaḍgādimāraṇoccāṭanāni ca ।
sūryātmajasya ca dine siddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

śikhākāmeśvaraproktaṃ jñātvā karma prasiddhyati ।।
itthaṃ dravyādisambhārassādhāro yatra dāhyataḥ ।
nityopayogitatmadhyādyāgabhūmāvathā haret ।।
(95b)
sthaṇḍilādau śivaṃ cekṣvā yathā vibhavavistaraiḥ ।
labdhānujño vrajetsārghaḥ kuṇḍā tanmantratṛptaye ।।

vighnanutsārya dikṣvaṣṭhau kāṇḍanāmaṣṭakaṃ nyaset ।
tacchiro varmaṇāveṣṭya sūtreṇa triguṇena tu ।।

teṣu saṃpūjayedastra dikpālānāṃ samantataḥ ।
vighnasaṅghāpanodāya kratossaṃrakṣaṇāya ca ।।

dhyāyaṃśca svāyudhākāre jvaladbhāsvarasannibham ।
siṃhāsanopaviṣṭastu tato dvārādhipānyajet ।।

kurvīta bhūtasaṃśuddhi tilakaṃ svāsanātmanoḥ ।
śivamātmani vinyasya sṛṣṭāśvānamabhīpsitam ।।

niṣkalaṃ ca śivaṃ nyasya śivohamiti bhāvayet ।
mūlābhimantritaṃ śvetasuṣṇīṣaṃ śirasi nyaset ।।

uṣṇīṣaṃ dhārayennyāsādūrdhvaṃ tatvapapaṭṭavat ।
dvādaśāṅgulamutsedhātsuśiśuṃ pañcamena tu ।।

alaṅkuryānnijaṃ dehaṃ gandhaiḥ puṣpairvibhuṣitaiḥ ।
gandhairmaṇḍalakaṃ svīye vidadyāddakṣiṇe kare ।।

vidhinā bhrāntayedīśamitthaṃ syācchivahastakam ।
taṃ nyasya śivamantreṇa bhāsvaraṃ nijamastake ।।

śivādabhinnamātmānaṃ kartāraṃ bhāvayedyathā ।
maṇḍale karmaṇāṃ sākṣī kalaśe yajñarakṣakaḥ ।।

homādhikāraṇaṃ vahnau śiṣyepā * * mocakaḥ ।
(96a)
svātmanā sugṛhīteti pañcādhāroya īśvaraḥ ।।

sohameveti kurvīta bhāvaṃ sthitataraṃ guruḥ ।
dhānyapañcakṛtānmantrānsavastrānsragvibhūṣitān ।।

vinyasya kalaśānaṣṭau praṇavāsanasaṃsthitān ।
jñānakhaḍgakaro mantrī vidye śānte prapūjayet ।।

kuṇḍanābhiṃ puraskṛtya kuṇḍasaṃskāramācaret ।
karmakāṇḍakramāvallyāmityuktaṃ somaśambhunā ।।

।। iti śīvīkaraṇam ।।

kuṇḍasyārambhakāle tu saṃskārānukṛtāḥ puraḥ ।
niṣpannasya ca vai sarve vidheyāśca krameṇa tu ।।

niyamo darśito hyevaṃ jayāyā mata eva hi ।
kuṇḍaṃ tu lakṣaṇopetaṃ prokṣayedastravāriṇā ।।

kavacenāvaguṇṭhetadastradarbheṇa collikhet ।
uddhṛtya prokṣayetpaścādastramantreṇa bhāmini ।।

pūraṇaṃ tena kartavyaṃ samīkaraṇamatra ca ।
secanaṃ kuṭṭanaṃ caiva hyastramantreṇa kārayet ।।

prokṣaṇaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ cāpi taścāstreṇaiva kārayet ।
pūjanaṃ gandhapuṣpādyairasinā cābhimantraṇam ।।

vajrīkaraṇamastreṇa rekhāḥ pūrvāparāstridhā ।
yasya saumyamukhīcaikā vajrametatprakīrtitam ।।

asinaivāgnikuṇḍaṃ taddarbhaḥ pūrvāgrasaṃstaraiḥ ।
sabāhyābhyantaraṃ kādyaṃ gṛhahetvarthamīna tu ।।
(96b)
।। iti kuṇḍasaṃskārāḥ ।।

kuṇḍasya dakṣiṇe bhāge śuṣkagomayamānasam ।
darbheṇa viṣṭaraṃ puṣpaṃ praṇasyatha prakalpayet ।।

svanāma madasaṃyuktaṃ sa dhyānena namontagam ।
āmantraṇapadenaiva brahmāṇaṃ sthāpya pūjayet ।।

puṣpādibhissadhūpādyairvruveṇa tu yathākramam ।
catuṣpathaṃ kuṇḍamadhye darbhābhyāṃ praṇavena tu ।।

pūrvaṃ yamyāgrabhāgābhyāṃ viṣṭaraṃ tasya copari ।
puṣpaṃ tasyopariṣṭhā tu hradaye naiva pūjayet ।।

yāgīśīṃ tu samabhyarcya praṇavādinamontagām ।
nīlotpaladalaśyāmāmṛttamaścārulocanām ।।

mantralakṣaṇasamproktaṃ sarvāvayavibhūṣitām ।
dhyātvā caivaṃ vidhāṃ devīṃ sthāpayet kuṇḍamadhyataḥ ।।

ṛtukāle ivotthānāṃ śirasena saṃsthitām ।
pū{ja}yedgandhapuṣpādyairbhavamantramanussaran ।।

mudrāṃ pradarśayeddevi sannidhānāya mantravit ।
kuṇḍasaṃskārato yāvadvāgeśapūjanāntikam ।।

śrīsvacchandādidaṃ proktaṃ vahnerjanmasya tedhunā ।।

।। iti vāgīśyājananam ।।

vasvarkavṛṣukotsedha vistārāyāmalakṣitā ।
śamījāśvarthajā sā * * kramaṇyadharāraṇiḥ ।।

ṣaḍaṅgulaprajananaṃ catre vṛtte bhujādhike ।
sā sanddhāyāya sā madhye tayorgāvṛtā ।।
(97a)
gāyatrī japtayā śambhumuccaranbhramarairdṛḍham ।
jātaṃ pātre nidhāyāgnisaṃskuryāttu yathāvidhi ।।
* * * raṇitaḥ proktamidaṃ janmavibhāvayoḥ ।।

।। ityaraṇividhānam ।।

ādāyāraṇijamagnisūryakāntodbhavaṃ tu vā ।
taijase tāmrapātre vā mṛṇmayeti navepi vā ।।

aiśānyāṃ diśi kuṇḍasya sthāpayeta samakṣitam ।
itthaṃ jayāsaṃhitoktavidhinā {vidhinā} vahnimāharet ।।

navo māhānasaṃścaiva dvividhognirudāhṛtaḥ ।
mantrātmakastathā mantrā dvividhogninavasmṛtaḥ ।।

māhānasastu saṃskāryo navasyatu na saṃskṛtiḥ ।
mantrātmakastvaraṇyuktāśśamīgarbhāttu pippalāt ।।

yadyastatopi vā kāṣṭhātsamantrobhinavoparaḥ ।
agnihotragṛhānītaḥ papretāgnirmahānasāt ।।

vaisyādvādhanasampannātsvovāpi vidhinā hṛtaḥ ।
amantro yo navo vahniryaśca māhānasassmṛtā ।।

kartavyāḥ pañca saṃskārāstasya garbhāṃdayaḥ kramāt ।
pauṣkarepi vinirdiṣṭā viśeṣo vahniyastvayam ।।

ānītasya punarvahneḥ kāryaṃ saṃskārapañcakam ।
nirīkṣaṇādiśāstreṇa kavacenāvaguṇṭhanam ।।

praṇavenāhutiḥ pañca datvā kravyādaśuddhaye ।
viśvāgnyāpādanaṃ paścātkurvīta tramaye tridhā ।।

etacchrīmṛtyujitproktaṃ kuryātsaṃskaraṇaṃ budhaḥ ।
(97b)
jvalitasyāthavā vahneścitiṃ vāsena vāyunā ।।

ākṛṣṭahṛdisaṃsthasya dakṣiṇena punaḥ kṣipet ।
pūrṇāṃ ca pūrvavaddadyācchivāgneraparo vidhiḥ ।।

śivarūpaṃ tamālokya tasyātmāntaḥ kṛti kramāt ।
kuryādantaḥ kṛtiṃ mantrī tato homaṃ prakārayet ।।

śrīpūrve darśito vahne vidhissaṃskāravarjitaḥ ।।
mukhodbhūtā nilaissāstrairagnimudvodhayettataḥ ।
na śūrpeṇa na pātreṇa na kareṇa na vāsasā ।।

tatognipātramādāya śivāmbhostreṇa prokṣayet ।
kavacenāvaguṇṭhyātha praṇave naiva pūjayet ।।

araṇyādisamudbhūtaṃ lokādyantaṃ vidhānavit ।
agniṃ tu śukravaddhyātvā caitanyaṃ praṇavena tu ।।

ṣaḍaṅge naiva saṃpūjya amṛtatvaṃ dhruveṇa tu ।
ātmānaṃ bhairavaṃ dhyātvā vahniṃ dhyātvā tu vījavat ।।

śivena kuṇḍabāhye tu tridhā bhrāmyāvatārayet ।
yonauttabījavatkṣiptvā bhairaveṇa śivāmbhasā ।।

astramantraṃ samuccārya yonipracchādayedvudhaḥ ।
darbhena dhruvamantreṇa tvakṣavāṭaṃ tato nyaset ।।

astreṇaiva caturdikṣu darbheṇaiva prakalpayet ।
saptavārāstu japtena darbheṇaiva tu kaṅkajam ।।

dakṣahaste tu badhniyādastramantraṃ manusmaran ।
(98a)
rakṣārthamagnigarbhasya garbhādhānamato bhavet ।।

hṛdā vai jalavindaṃ tu darbhāgreṇātra pātayet ।
gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ pūjāṃ śikhayā kārayetpunaḥ ।।

trirāhutiṃ cottareṇa śikhāyāmitirāhutim ।
puṃsaḥ kalpanamevaṃ hi na strīgarbheṇa janyate ।।

sīmantaṃ dakṣiṇānyena darbhāgreṇa prakalpayet ।
grīvāmaṃsau kaṭiṃ caiva bāhūjaṅghe ca kalpayet ।।

pratyaṅgāni ca saṅkalpya sīmantonnayanaṃ bhavet ।
gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ pūjā śirasā cāhutitrayam ।।

pūrvamadhyāparānvahnau trīrbhāgānparikalpayet ।
mukhahṛtpādadeśāṃstu homāntaśca tritatvakam ।।

śirāṃsi pañcāhutibhirūrdhvāsyena tribhistribhiḥ ।
pañcavaktraṃ tu saṅkalpya madhyaprāgyāmyasaumyakam ।।

aparaṃ cāpyāhutibhiḥ pūjasyena trisaṅkhyayā ।
netraṃ netreṇa saṅkalpya mukheṣveva trayaṃ trayam ।।

tutibhitritaye naiva tilaissarvaṃ tu kārayet ।
aṣṭāṅgāni tathā trīṇi daśacāṣṭāvanukramāt ।।

śeṣāsyaisamprakalpyaistu tilāmūrtistato bhavet ।
aṅgāni vinyaset paścāddhṛdasyāni yathākramam ।।

trirāhutiṃ dakṣiṇena śirasā cāhutitrayam ।
sīmantonnayanaṃ - vaṃ ।। jātakarma tvathocyate ।

astreṇa vījayedagni mantreṇaiva tu pūjayet ।
(98b)
trirāhutiṃ tu pūrveṇa vastreṇaivāhutitrayam ।।

evaṃ mantradvaye naiva jātakarmakṛtaṃ bhavet ।। 2 ।।

astreṇa prokṣayetkuṇḍaṃ sadyassūtakaśuddhaye ।
vaktrāṇyuccāṭayetpaścādvaktraiṇaivāhutitrayam ।।

vaktrāṇi śodhyānyasinā āhutitraya yogataḥ ।
vaktrābhighāre vaktraṃ tu vaktre vaktre trayaṃ trayam ।।

prokṣayetkuṇḍapārśvāni tvastreṇaiva śivāmbhasā ।
da - - stīryapūrvāgrāndakṣiṇottarasāṃpratān ।।

saumyāgrātpūrvāvāruṇyaḥ paridhīnviṣṭharāṃstathā ।
astramastreṇa sarve te brahmāṇaṃ pūrvaviṣṭare ।।

rudraiśca dakṣiṇasthāpyo viṣṇuḥ paścimaviṣṭare ।
sadāśivaścottare ca svanāmapadacihnitam ।।

ādau dhruvaṃ smareddevi nāma śānte prakalpayet ।
gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ pūjyassarūpaṃ teṣvanasmaran ।।

mekhalopari lokeśāt pūjayet praṇavena tu ।
rakṣārthaṃ jātabālasya brahmādyāḥ pūjitāstu te ।।

tataḥ kaṅkaṇakaṃ muktvā dakṣahastavyavasthitam ।
puṣpaṃ saṅgṛhya devena śivāgrennāma kalpayet ।।

kavacenoktacāraṃ tu gandhapuṣpādidhūpakaiḥ ।
ūrdhvāsyenāhutīstisraḥ kavacena trayaṃ punaḥ ।।

śivanāmāṅkitaṃ vahni janayitvā suraṃstataḥ ।
visarjayettu svasthānaṃ sāvitrīṃ praṇavena tu ।।
(99a)
puṣpādibhissamabhyarcya homenaiva tribhitribhiḥ ।
dhāmnai vedhmāstu hotavyā hastamātra pramāṇataḥ ।।

caturviṃśatisaṅkhyātāśśivāgrestarpaṇāya tu ।
saṃskārapañcakamidaṃ proktaṃ svacchandabhairave ।।

ghṛtābhāvāktilaiḥ patraissadbhirvātakuśaiḥ phalaiḥ ।
etena kilasaṃpannaṃ jātakarma prakalpanam ।।
nāmamūrtena mantreṇa ।। oṃ namaḥ śivāya vahnaye ।।

ityuccārya kṣipetpuṣpaṃ kavacenārcanādikam ।
prayogaṃ nāmakaraṇe īśamantreṇa kārayet ।।

homamīśānavarmabhyāmitthaṃ saṃskārapañcakam ।
japādvā pañcasaṃskārānhomābhāvāttu kārayet ।।

karmo * te viśeṣeṇa dravyālābhaṃ tadarcitam ।।

।। ityagnisaṃskāraḥ ।।

sūksruvau sampratāpyāgnau śivāmbhostreṇa prokṣayet ।
kavacenāvaguṇṭhye tau śivāgnau bhramayettridhā ।।

astreṇa mārjayedadbhirdarbhāgreṇātha saṃspṛśet ।
punaragnau paribhrānta prokṣayettau śivāmbhasā ।।

dambhamanyena saṃspṛśya bhūyognau bhrāmya tāpayet ।
śivāmbhasā mārjayitvā darbhamūlena sampṛśet ।।

sruksruvābhyāṃ tato mūlaṃ sthāpayettāvadhomukhau ।
darbhāṇā pṛṣṭhataḥ pūjyo dakṣiṇegneḥssadāvudhaiḥ ।।

sthāpite paritāpyāgnau prokṣayenmārjayetkuśaiḥ ।
agramagreṇa madhyaṃ ca mūlaṃ mūlena mārjayet ।।

(99b)
iti vyāsasamānābhyāṃ svacchande hāṭakeśvare ।।

icchādiśaktimayatā tritvādevopajāyate ।
sruksruvau ca tridhā tasyā * * * stāpanātpunaḥ ।।

tāpayettejasā tridhā ।
sañjātau tena sa(ntuṣṭau) kāṣṭhabhāvaparāṅmukhau ।।

trayo ye kalpitā bhāvāste śaktitraya kalpitāḥ ।
sthānatrayaṃ spṛśaṃstaistu sarvasmiñśāntabhāvanā ।।

sruksruvānyupadeśena śaktipiṇḍassadāśivaḥ ।
sarvādhvaṃ vyāpikāśaktirevāsrugi(ri) kalpitā ।।

purāśāstrena yoreva viśeṣassaṃskṛtau kṛtaḥ ।

।। iti sruksuvasaṃskāraḥ ।।

ājyasaṃskaraṇaṃ kuryādājyādi śravaṇādivat ।
ājyaṃ saṃprokṣya cāstreṇa kavacenāvaguṇṭhayet ।।

śivāgnau tāpyamastreṇa prodvāsyaṃ kavacena tu ।
kuṇḍasya parito devi tridhā bhrāmya tu sthāpayet ।।

yonisthaṃ cājyapātraṃ tu tatplavaṃ saplavaṃ tataḥ ।
darbhāgradvayamādāya prādeśaṃ madhyagranthi tat ।।

pavitrametadvihitaṃ suplutaṃ tena kārayet ।
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tu gṛhītvā tatpavitrakam ।।

parāṅmukhaṃ triṃdvārātsaṃmukhaṃ trīṃstathaiva ca ।
astreṇaiva tu mantreṇa avaśyotaśśivāgninā ।।

darbholmukaṃ tu saṅgṛhya ājyapātraṃ nirīkṣayet ।
nīrājanaṃ tataḥ kuryāt paryagnikaraṇaṃ tataḥ ।।
(100a)
dhāmnāstramantramuccārya tadagnāvulmukaṃ kṣipet ।
dhāmnaiva manutāṃ mantrī prokṣayedastravāriṇā ।।

abhimantrya ṣaḍaṅgena vyasṛtatvaṃ dhruveṇa tu ।
sakṛduccārayogena pūjayedbhairaveṇa tu ।।

ājyasya saṃskṛtiḥ proktā śrīmatsvacchandaśāsane ।
agninā caiva mantreṇa pavitreṇa ca cakṣuṣā ।।

caturbhirapi yatpūtaṃ tadājyamitaradghṛtam ।
karmoddyote viśeṣoyaṃ śrīmaddharma śivoditaḥ ।।

।। ityājyasaṃskāraḥ ।।

vaktrasandhānakaṃ vaktrairāhuti tritayena tu ।
aparādyena tadvaktrasandhānaṃ tu samācaret ।।

evaṃ saumyasya vaktrasya sandhānaṃ tu kṛtaṃ bhavet ।
trirāhuti prayogeṇa vaktrasandhiḥ prakīrtitaḥ ।।

।। iti vaktrasandhānam ।।

mukhyamūrdhnisthitāṃ vaktraṃ guṇatvamitareṣu ca ।
muktikāmasya dīkṣāyāmurdhvavaktrasya mukhyatā ।।

pāñcāle pāñjanādyā vai siddhistu vividhāścayā ।
sadāśivāntagāssarvāḥ pūrvavaktreṣa homayet ।।

māraṇoccāṭanādau tu vidveṣe stambhane tathā ।
dakṣiṇe caiva vaktre tu homātsiddhiḥ parābhavet ।।

śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ caiva saubhāgyākarṣaṇāni ca ।
bhāgyārohaṇasiddhiṃ tu uttare homayet sadā ।।

paścime nityakarmāṇi viniyogaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ।
ājyabhogo hi hotavya ūrdhvavaktre tu paścime ।।
(100b)
ājyapātrasya madhye tu darbhotha bhairaveṇa tu ।
nyasitavyo varārohe tato vai karmakalpanā ।।

nāḍītrayeṇa yugapatmātre bhāgatrayaṃ nyaset ।
saṣumṇāṃ madhyabhāgasthāṃ dakṣe piṅgāṃ prakalpayet ।।

iḍāmārgeṇa yattejo vāme saumyāṃ prakalpayet ।
evaṃ tribhāgaṃ saṅkalpya druvamāpūrya homayet ।।

bhairaveṇaiva mantreṇāgnaye svāhāntameva ca ।
agnibhāgaṃ tu saṅgṛhya druveṇājyāhutiḥ kṣipet ।।

somabhāge tu somāya svāhetyantaṃ samuccaran ।
dhāmādipraṇavādyaṃ ca dhruveṇājyāhutiṃ kṣipet ।।

agnīṣāmeti dve saṅkhye svāhāntaṃ dhāmacāditaḥ ।
praṇavādyājyamadhyāntaṃ sruvamāpūrya homayet ।।

śuklapakṣe vidhiktvevaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣenyathā bhavet ।
somabhāge bhavetsūryo hyagnisaṃjñā tu pūrvavat ।।

agne (sū)ryasya madhyādvai āhutiṃ pratipādayet ।
yataḥ ssūryasya madhye vai amāvasyā viśecchaśī ।।

।। iti cākṣuṣīhomāḥ ।।

prāśanārthamato homo vaktrāṇāṃ bhairaveṇa tu ।
cūḍādhyāye tu saṃskārā vahne kṣālāntasaṃsthitāḥ ।।

prāśanārthaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ pūvyāmekāṃ pra * bhayet ।
bhairavaṃ tu samuccārya śivāgnissarvasiddhidaḥ ।।

agniṃ tu proddharet paścāt pātre saṃsthāpya rakṣayet ।
vaktrasandhistriva * di kliptissvacchandaśāstrataḥ ।।
(101a)
ityuktaṃ kṛtasaṃskāraṃ pṛthakkuṇḍe hutāśanam ।
carvādisiddhaye vidvānvidadyādvarmakṣitam ।।

pratyahaṃ vā kriyāhetossāmāsikavidhau sthitaḥ ।
śrīmṛgendrepyeva vahneśca{ya}rthaṃ vihito vidhi ।।

tasminno paratajvāle na dhūmavyākulārciṣā ।
juhuyānmandadīpte vānvarā vā napi śāstravit ।।

śāntyāpyāyananauyānparyaṣṭi mṛtyuñjayādiṣu ।
snidhaḥ pradakṣiṇajvālastiraskṛta himadyutiḥ ।।

saubhāgyākṛṣṭisantāna phalamūlādisiddhaye ।
va * * dāḍimīpuṣpa vidruma(ssa)rvirocanaḥ ।।

vidveṣoccāṭaruktāpaśoṣamohakṣayādiṣu ।
śyāmanīlāsitajjvālassadhūgo * svanohitaḥ ।।

rasāyanarasārogya sthairyarājyādisiddhaye ।
haridrā kuṅkumakṣoda karṇikāra himadyutiḥ ।।

tatvajñānamahāyogi divyasiddha pravṛttaye ।
sphāṭikānukṛtajvālo miśromiśrārtha sādhakaḥ ।।

candarāje payogandhi mallikā gandhagandha vā ।
śāntyādau sādhanānī ke karpūrāgurukā sṛjām ।।

karmaṇyariṣṭabhūyiṣṭhe * * ṅmajjamedasām ।
kadambaketakīvimbacaṇpakādeśca pauṣṭike ।।

kapigugguladantīśa vacāyadmācchadātmanām ।
mahodaye kriyāyogya vastusaṃsthāna śabdadṛt ।।
(101b)
rūpāmodāvimau vahnermṛgendre samprakāśitau ।।
lakṣaṇaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi vahnessarvārthasiddhidam ।
va{rṇe}rthapaśikhādhūma dhvanigandhasamanvitam ।।

ṣaḍvidhaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vahnervijñātavyaṃ prayatnataḥ ।
śveto raktastathā pīto haritaḥ kṛṣṇa eva vā ।।

varṇaḥ praśasyate sthāyī srigdha eko manoharaḥ ।
sitānāṃ puṣpajātīnāṃ śaṅkhasya sphaṭikasya ca ।।

mṛṇālasya śaśāṅkasya tulyo varṇassitaśśuciḥ ।
raktānāṃ puṣpajātīnāṃ kalyāṇaganakasya ca ।।

tālabhogo ca nābhaśca pīto varṇaśśubhasmṛtaḥ ।
haritkusumajātīnāṃ tathā marakatasya ca ।।

śukābhaśceṣyate varṇo haritastu vibhāvasoḥ ।
kṛṣṇānāṃ puṇyajātīnāṃ nistriṃśāñjanayostathā ।।

indranīlamahānīlatulyavarṇassitaśśubhaḥ ।
liṅgaprasādapadmādridhvajena tu samākṛtiḥ ।।

siṃhatārkṣyadvipavṛṣaiśśubhastu sadṛśaśśubhaḥ ।
snigdhāḥ pradakṣiṇāvartāḥ protaṅgā saṃhatāśśikhā ।।

avicchinnāḥ praśasyante cāmarasya cchaṭā iva ।
kṛṣṇā ye kīrtitā varṇā dhūmastadgandhaśaśśubhaḥ ।।

kokilālinibhartreva varhikaṇṭhacchastathā ।
veṇuvīṇadhvanirvahniśśaṅkhatūryasamasvanaḥ ।।

vṛṣadviṣaninādaśca siṃhanādāśca śamyate ।
(102a)
sadgandhadravyagandhognighṛtagandhaśca siddhidaḥ ।।

sugandhipuṣpajātīnāṃ tulyagandhaśca siddhaye ।
vahnirviva * * * * sadhūmotha caṭaddhvaniḥ ।।

ākulognirasahyaśca jāyate vighnacoditaḥ ।
tato homaḥ prakartavyo bahurūpeṇa mantriṇaḥ ।।

* * kāramīpitenā hyephaṅkārasahitena tu ।
hūyamāno hyaghoreṇa savikāraśca pāvakaḥ ।।

tadā pāpātmako * * yajamānasamudbhavaḥ ।
brahmahatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vāmadevo vināśayet ।।

svavarṇasteyajaṃ pāpaṃ bahurūpaḥ praṇāśayet ।
gurvāṅganāgamaṃkṛtvā pāpaṃ tatpuruṣo dahet ।।

samastavyastasaṃparkasaṃbhavaṃ pātakaṃ mahat ।
yadājya * * * * mena tadīśānaḥ pramārjayet ।।

śvetavarṇo vivarṇaśca durgandhīrbhakṣa eva ca ।
brahmahatyāṃ sa kathayet surāpānaṃ tu lohitaḥ ।।

steyaṃ tu pītavarṇābho haritaścāṅganā kṛtam ।
kṛṣṇaḥ saṃparkajaṃ pāpaṃ nivedayati vahnigaḥ ।।

homairebhiryathoktaistu hūyamāno vibhāvasuḥ ।
prakṛtistho yadā nasyāttadahantaiva homayet ।।

bahuhavyendhane vahnau susamiddhe sutejasi ।
vidhumele lihāne ca hotavyaṃ karmasiddhaye ।।

apravuddhe sadhūme ca juhuyādyo hutāśane ।
karmahānirbhavettasya hotā caiva vinaśyati ।।

sugandhikuṅkumāmodī valyakīveṇunissvanaḥ ।
pradakṣiṇaśikhāvartaḥ snigdhavarṇoti dīptimān ।।
(102b)
sadā jvalatiniṣkampassa hyuddidhitireva ca ।
padmendīvaravarṇaśca vahnissarvārtha sādhakaḥ ।।

vahnervarṇādikaṃ cihnaṃ proktaṃ pauṣkaraśāsane ।
herākāre śubhaṃ vidyātsphaṭikābhe prajāḥ śriyam ।।

padmarāganibhe rājyaṃ vidhu me sukhasaṃpadaḥ ।
haritālanibhe lakṣmīrvivṛddhiḥ kāntivardhanam ।।

ājyagatrye sutāḥ saukhaṃ mantrasiddhiḥ prajāyate ।
yasya yasya tu dravyasya garbhaṃ dhatte hutāśanaḥ ।।

kṣipraṃ tatphaladaḥ prokto dhanadhānya vivardhanaḥ ।
varṇahīnaṃ saśabdaṃ ca śreyogatrya vivarjitam ।।

dṛṣṭvānalaṃ prajvalitaṃ tryahaṃ yāvadvivarjayet ।
havanaṃ mantratṛptiṃ tu āhāraṃ tu tathaiva ca ।।

caturthehani saṃprāpte tatrāgnau homamārabhet ।
mukharūpamavijñāya juhuyādanyastu yaḥ ।।

savinaśyatyakālena dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalacyutaḥ ।
mūrdhnicedvahnisaṃpāto hṛdaye tu dhanakṣayaḥ ।।

pārśvayorbandhunāśaḥ syātpṛṣṭhe caiva kulakṣayaḥ ।
tasmātsarvaprayatnena saṃsmṛtya manasā sukham ।।

tatraiva juhuyātpūrṇāṃ prāyaścitta kṛtādaraḥ ।
sārasvatamate proktaṃ phalarūpa prabhedataḥ ।।

evaṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ jñātvā vahnau santarpayecchivam ।
hotavyaṃ patitaṃ kuṇḍātprasādādyadi tatpunaḥ ।।

lobhānna nikṣipedvahnau vāntaṃ tattena garhitam ।
kuṇḍasya cottare bhāge viṣṭa{ra}sya tu vāmataḥ ।।
(103a)
praṇītāṃ kalpayettatra camasaṃ vāripūritam ।
puṣpākṣatatilairyuktaṃ pavitraṃ tatra vinyaset ।।

praṇavādisamāvāhya viṣṇunā satato namaḥ ।
āmantraṇapadenaiva viṣṇuṃ saṃsthāpya pūjayet ।।

svāgatāsanapādyārghaistato vijñāpayettu tam ।।
paśvarthaṃ yajña ārabdha ātmārthaṃ sādhakena vā ।
bhagavaṃstvatprasādena {yā}bhāge nicchidratāstu me ।।

praṇītā sthitivijñapti śrīsvacchande prakīrtitā ।।
uccaratpraṇavaṃ paścāt pūrṇakumbheṣu cāṣṭasu ।
śikṣvaṣṭāsvambudhivyāptyā loke śāṣṭakamarcayet ।।

kuryāddipāṣṭakaṃ tadvadvahnidiggocarāñśivam ।
kalaśāṣṭakamuddiṣṭaṃ śrīmaddharma śivena tu ।।

tatognau yajanaṃ devi bhairavaṃ tu prapūjayet ।
sthaṇḍile tu vidhānena hyanantādinprapūjayet ।।

dhyātvā vaktrāṇi pañcāgnau yena yatkarmavāñchitam ।
tanmadhyavaktraṃ saṅkalpya mukhaṃ kuṇḍapramāṇataḥ ।।

bhāvayennavajihvāstu vaktre vaktre pratiṣṭhitāḥ ।
prācādyaṣṭau madhya ekā kāmyārthe diggatāstutāḥ ।।

rājyadā * * jananī mṛtyudā śatrukārikā ।
vaśikartyuścāṭanīsyādarthadā muktidāyikā ।।

sarvasiddhipradā madhye tasmānmadhye tu homayet ।
pūrṇaṃ tu bhairaveṇaiva jihvānāṃ kalpanāya ca ।।

tataḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ caiva bhairaveṇa prapātayet ।
(103b)
jvālāgraṃ tu hṛdā gṛhya vahnicaitanya kalpitam ।।

ātma{kṛ}tsthaṃtu saṅkalpya yogapīṭhaṃ tu kalpayet ।
* dhyajihvānusāreṇa agninābhau tu karparam ।।

nālaṃ hṛdavadhiṃ jñātvā padmaṃ tatra vicintayet ।
patrāṣṭakasamopetaṃ sitavarṇaṃ sutejasam ।।

anantaṃ kalpayettatra dharmādicaraṇādikam ।
oṃkārādiśivāntaṃ ca hyagnimūrtiṃ prakalpayet ।।

śikhāhṛdasthitāyā tu dhruveṇotkīlayet punaḥ ।
recakena kṣipedvahnau sā mūrtibhairavātmikā ।।

mūrtibhūtaṃ prakalpyaivamaṣṭatriṃśat kalānvitam ।
śodhyādhvānaṃ tu vinyasyeddikṣākāle varānane ।।

bhairavaṃ pūjayitvā tu śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā ।
vaktrasandhiśca vaktrābhyāṃ nāḍīsandhirato bhavet ।।

mūlamantraṃ samuccārya tvagnināsau vinirgatam ।
sthaṇḍilastha śikhālīnamekārthaṃ caiva mantrayet ।।

śuddhājye nāhutiśatamaṣṭotkṛṣṭaṃ varānane ।
bhairavasyātra hotavyaṃ vaktrāṅgānāṃ daśāṃśakam ।।

bhairavāṣṭakalokeśān daśamānena homayet ।
mūlamantraṃ samuccārya pūrṇāmekāṃ prapātayet ।।

puna(rnya)nātiriktārthaṃ nicchidrakaraṇāya ca ।
paścāddhomaḥ prakartavyo yathecchaṃ ca varānane ।।

nityamevaṃ svatantroktaṃ kṛtvā śāntyādikarmaṇi ।
krameṇānena hotavyā sugatyājyapariplutāḥ ।।

brahmavṛkṣadrumādyantātsamitkāṣṭhamayī purā ।
kausumīgandha * pākhyā madhuparkodarā bhidhā ।।
(104a)
ājyasaṃjñāṃ tu hutvānyāṃ dadyātpūrṇāhutiṃ śubhām ।
sahasraṃ śatasaṅkhyā ca śraddhaye vā svaśaktitaḥ ।।

dravyaiḥ pūrvoditaiḥ kāmyaistarpaṇīyaḥ pṛthakpṛthak ।
miśrībhūtaistavāsarvaistrimadhuplāvitaiḥ punaḥ ।।

tataḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ dadyāt pratyekadravyatarpaṇe ।
ekāṃvākhilahomyaistu sambhāre sati ṣaṇmukha ।।

evamicchānugo homo dṛṣṭastviśvarasaṃhitau ।
homastathāvidhaḥ kāryo yathā karmānusārataḥ ।।

dūrvāho sastu śāntyarthaṃ pauṣṭike ghṛtameva tu ।
gandhadravyāṇi juhuyādvaśyārthe tilasaṃyutān ।।

uccāṭe kākapakṣāṃśca māraṇe viṣamādutīm ।
ulūkasya kākasya pakṣānvidveṣa karmaṇi ।।

ākarṣaṇe tu siddhārthānkaṭutailasamanvitān ।
yakṣiṇī sādhane caiva tathā gatryarcayoṣitām ।।

nāgapuṣpahutaiścaiva vaśyāḥ syurnāgakanyakāḥ ।
padmotpalāni juhuyāt sādhanepsarasāṃ tataḥ ।।

sastavrīhaṃstu juhuyāccaturvarṇa prasādhane ।
vilahomena viprendra śriyaṃ paśyati sādhakaḥ ।।

ayutena tu lakṣyeṇa sādhayet padmamālinī ।
gulikāyutahomena guggulossādhakottamaḥ ।।

savātkāmā na vāpnoti tyaktena ca viśeṣataḥ ।
madhukṣīraghṛtāktebhyo homaṃ kuryādaśeṣataḥ ।।

tyaktaṃ yattatsamākhyātaṃ krūrakarmasu varjayet ।
sthūlo mantrātmakaḥ proktaḥ sūkṣmo binduśarīravān ।।

paraḥ śaktyātmakaḥ proktaḥ śivadhyānaṃ parāparaṃ ।
(104b)
bindunādavibhāgajñaḥ śivaśaktivicārakaḥ ।।

prāpnotyabhimatāṃ siddhimityāha bhagavāñśivaḥ ।।
hāṭakeśvararakte tu kṛtvā homaṃ yathocitam ।
ācāryo juhuyāt paścāt prāyaścitaṃ śivena tu ।।

vidhettha nātiriktasya cittavikṣepa karmaṇi ।
aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā prāyaścittādviśudhyati ।।

pūrṇāhuti prayogaṃ tu kathayāmyadhunā tava ।
ūrdhvakāyā ṛjugrīvassamapādo vyavasthitaḥ ।।

nābhisthāne srucomūlamuttānāgrasukhasamam ।
srucoparisruvaṃ devi kṛtvā caivamadhomukham ।।

puṣpaṃ datvā srugagre tu darbheṇa sahitauttatau ।
muṣṭibhyāṃ saṅgṛhītaṃ vai tattāvuka{ra}yogataḥ ।।

tato ghṛtena saṃplāvya abhimānaṃ tu kārayet ।
ahameva paraṃ tatvaṃ parāparavibhāgataḥ ।।

tatvamekaṃ tu sarvatra nānyadāvaṃ tu kārayet ।
yaḥ kumbhe dhvāntavinyastaṣṣaṭprakāre varānane ।।

maṇḍalegnau śiśorantasādhāraṇavikalpataḥ ।
srucyadhvānaṃ samāropya prāṇasthanādvimadhyagam ।।

prāṇāpāne same kṛtvā srucādhāre vinikṣipet ।
vasudhārā prayogeṇa prakṣipejjātavedasi ।।

nābhisthānātsruco mūlaṃ nayennāsāgragocaram ।
yathā yathā(rya)jeddhārāṃ tathā prāṇaṃ samuccaret ।।

tadagnau juhuyādābhyāṃ yayā sasmitadhārayā ।
(105a)
ācāmaṃ candanaṃ dadyāttāmbūlapravṛtīrapi ।।

bhaktyā tadbhūtiṣā vandya vidadhyāt praṇatiṃ parām ।
śivaṃ visṛjya bhūrāditrayasya praṇavānvitam ।।

krameṇa yaugapadyena datvāhuti catuṣṭayam ।
tato vahniṃ samabhyarcya phaḍantāstreṇa śumbharān ।।

saṃhāramudrayā hṛtya kṣamasveti vidhāya ca ।
bhāsvarātparidhisthāṃśca purakeṇa hṛdāṇunā ।।

śraśrayā parayātmīye sthāpayeddhṛdayāmbuje ।
visarjane vidhirvahneḥ karmakāṇḍakramoditaḥ ।।

srucisruvo cājyapātre ājyalepaṃ pramārjayet ।
agnimadhye tu tatkṣiptvā yatheṣṭaṃ varamācaret ।।

tena tripuṇḍrakaṃ kuryālyalāṭe śravasorapi ।
bhujayornābhideśe tu mahadāyu(rśivarṣa)rthe ।।

praṇītāyāṃ jalaṃ yattaddikṣu sarvāsu nikṣipet ।
tena tṛpyanti bhūtāśca hotaro dikṣu saṃśritāḥ ।।

pūrvāvasāne naṣṭeyamidaṃ saṅkarṣaṇoditam ।
homānte tilakaṃ kṛtvā saptajanmāntarāśritam ।।

naśyate pātakaṃ yasmāttasmāttadbhasmacodvahet ।
na tadyajñasahasreṇa phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ।।

ṛṣīṇāṃ nandinoddiṣṭametannandīśvarāgame ।
savapākāgryamānīya kṛtvā maṇḍalakadvayam ।।
antarbahirbaliṃ dadyādāgneyyāṃ kuṇḍasannidhau ।।

oṃ hāṃ rudrebhyaḥ svāhā pūrve । agneye rakṣobhyaḥ। nairṛte nāgebhyaḥ। vāyave nakṣatrebhyaḥ।  rāśibhyaḥ viśvebhyaḥ। kṣetrapālāya koṇacatuṣṭaya madhye ।।
(105b)
praṇavavījaṃ svāhā ca sarvatra ।। dvitīyamaṇḍalake । oṃ hāṃ indrāya svāhā । evaṃ pūrvādīśāntamaṣṭau lokapālāḥ । brahmaṇe ūrdhvaṃ viṣṇave adhaḥ ।
kṣetrapālāya tanmadhye । tato maṇḍalādvahiḥ । vāyasādibhyo । vali tarpaṇaṃ kāryam ।।

sthaṇḍilercā tathā liṅge vahnikāryāvasānikā ।
darcitā madhyasandhyāyāṃ kathyatetha caro vidhiḥ ।।

carurdvividhataktosmin puroḍāśaśca śāsane ।
ekaḥ śivacaruḥ prokto vidyeśacarukastathā ।।

ekaḥ śivapuroḍāśastvanyo vidyeśvarātmakaḥ ।
carubhāṇḍasya saṃskāraḥ samaheśena kīrtitaḥ ।।

dravyāṇāṃ ca tathānyeṣāṃ pātrāṇāṃ tatra yo vidhiḥ ।
abhimantraṇamādau tu carubhāṇḍasya saptaśaḥ ।।

sadyojātena kartavyaṃ yathāvidhi niyojanam ।
kṣīraṃ tu vāmadevena japtavyaṃ toyameva vā ।।

taṇḍulāścāpyaghoreṇa japtavyā ghṛtasecitāḥ ।
veṣṭanīyaḥ kuśaiḥ kaṇṭhaścarubhāṇḍasya veṣṭitaiḥ ।।

sarpidugdhaṃ tu vaktreṇa dadhicaiva yathāvidhi ।
īśānaṃ ja * nāsakhya evaṃ śivacarorvidhiḥ ।।

sarveṣāṃ śivamantrāṇāṃ yogapīṭhasthitātmanām ।
aghoraṃ śivagāyatryā hutvodvāsya tataścaruḥ ।।

āghārasampātahuto japtaḥ kāryaḥ śivena tu ।
sarvātmadevadevena bahurūpeṇa vā tathā ।।

īśāne nāthavā kāryaḥ sa yathā sambhavāhutiḥ ।
(106a)
sruvamāpūrya gavyena sarpiṣā vimalena tu ।।

tatroparicarormantramuccārya tu yathoditam ।
svāhākāreṇa hutvāgnau carau tu sruvakaṃ dhunet ।।

eṣa sampātahomastu sarvatra vidhicoditaḥ ।
śatikaścaiva sampātaḥ kāryaḥ sambhavatopi vā ।।

vistareṇa kuśāgrastu japtavyoṣṭaśataṃ tataḥ ।
kaṇṭhapradeśe kartavyaṃ tanmaheśena mārjanam ।।

gomayenopalepaśca kartavyaḥ sakṛdeva hi ।
hṛdayena nivedyastu śivāyāṅguṣṭapīḍitaḥ ।।

tridhā vibhājyastu tatastriṣu pātreṣu saṃsthitaḥ ।
śivāya prathamo bhāgo nivedyaḥ puṣpapūjitaḥ ।।

hotavyastu dvitīyognau tṛtīyaḥ sādhakasya ca ।
suvarṇamaṇipātreṣu raupyatāmramayeṣu vā ।।

teṣāmabhāvātpātrāṇāṃ mṛnmayeṣu śubheṣu ca ।
kadalyutpalarājārka padminījalajeṣu vā ।।

palāśamadhyamaiḥ parṇairnivedyaḥ sādhakaina vā ।
saghṛtaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ śivasyāgnestathaiva ca ।।

ātmabhāgastamaghṛtaḥ kartavyo madhunāpi vā ।
uktaḥ śivacaruścaiva sarvapāpa pramocanaḥ ।।

upasarga praśamanaḥ sarvavighna praṇāśanaḥ ।
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇatulā puruṣāṇāṃ yathāvidhi ।।

śataṃ bhavati cīrṇaṃ tu sakṛdbhuktvā śivaṃ carum ।
viṣuvatyayane bhānostathā viśvapadeṣu ca ।।

ṣaḍaśīti mukhe caiva grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ ।
vyatīpāteṣu devāya nivedyaḥ sarvadā caruḥ ।।

janmanakṣatradivase caruḥ prāśyo viśeṣataḥ ।
(106b)
brahmacaryeṇa ca gṛhī pūrvaṃ śivacaruṃ sadā ।।

caruṃ prāśya paraṃ caiva labhate tatphalaṃ caroḥ ।
na kiñcidanyadaśnīyāccaruṃ prāśya vicakṣaṇaḥ ।।

madyapopi tyajenmadyamubhe bhāvena vā sadā ।
sādhakena tu bhoktavyaṃ caruṇāvartanaṃ sadā ।।

manobhilaṣitāmeva nirvighnāṃ siddhimāpnuyāt ।
vidyeśvaracaruścaiva sādhyaḥ svahṛdayena tu ।।

śālipiṣṭakanīvāra śyāmākaistaṇḍulairapi ।
carussādhyotha godhūmayavakaṅkubhireva vā ।।

eṣāmeva tu cūrṇena puroḍāśaṃ ca pācayet ।
sampūrṇājyena vidhivat kapāledvyaṅgulaiḥ samaiḥ ।।

pāśyaḥ śivapuroḍāśaḥ pāśyaḥ pañcakapālakaḥ ।
svapāsvapāṇitalamātrastu kartavyordhvakhuroṃntataḥ ।।

samapākāḥ prayatnena kartavyo vidhikalpitaḥ ।
āmorogabhayaṃ kuryāddagdho maraṇameva ca ।।
samapākaḥ puroḍāśaḥ sarvakāmārthasādhakaḥ ।
puroḍāśasya ca guṇā ye ca doṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ।।

carorapita eko * sādhakena kriyāvatām ।
navāvadānaḥ kartavyaḥ puroḍāśaḥ śivātmakaḥ ।।

vidyeśvarapuroḍāśaḥ kāryaḥ pañcāvadānakaḥ ।
dikṣu sarvāsu madhye ca avadānā navoditāḥ ।।

caturdikṣu tathā madhye hyavadānāstu pañca vai ।
hutvāvadānaṃ kramaśaḥ puroḍāśasya mantravit ।।

hutaśeṣaṃ svayaṃ cādyāddadyāttānyasya kasyacit ।
(107a)
sacaruśca puroḍāśaḥ samayajñasya mantriṇā ।।

na deyo vidhisaṃsthena guroranyatra sarvadā ।
evaṃ carupuroḍāśaḥ sakhyo vai sādhakena tu ।।

anāhuta śivegnau tu sadhvasaṃskārasaṃskṛte ।
svamattotha caruścānyaḥ paktavyaḥ sādhakena tu ।।

atramagnāḥ paramaśivātmane । sadāśivātmane । īśvarātmane । vidyātmane । māyātmane । kalātmane । niyatyātmane । puruṣātmane । pradhānātmane । praṇava namaskārau sarvatra । īśvara । vidyā । māyā । puruṣa । pradhāna । evameva ।। ।।

śrīpauṣkare carossiddhiḥ puroḍāśasya ca dvidhā ।
tattoya madhunā vacmi bhikṣāṭana vinirṇayam ।।

।। brahmovāca ।।

kuśavatsādhakānāṃ tu daridrāṇāṃ tapasvinām ।
kṛtāsyāścaruṇāvṛttiḥ kimanyadvartanaṃ vibho ।।

।। parameśvara uvāca ।।

śākāni karmamūlāni phalāni vividhāni ca ।
vartanaṃ sādhakendrāṇāṃ daridrāṇāṃ tapasvinām ।।

annasevāśnatāṃ pūrvamabhyasetaśanaiḥ śanaiḥ ।
yāvadgrāsānvardhayetaḥ śākādīnāṃ tu sādhakaḥ ।।

bhāvato - mayedgrāsānannasya ca vicakṣaṇaḥ ।
śākasya prathamo grāso hṛnnādīnāṃ yathāsthitaḥ ।।

sauhitrādātmanontrasya grāso hrādyo yathāsthitaḥ ।
ātmībhūtaṃ tu tadgrāse dvitīyaṃ vardhayedvudhaḥ ।।

evaṃ tāvat prakartavyaṃ yāvacchākādi kevalam ।
aśanaṃ sādhakendrasya saṃpraviśya tato vanam ।।

prabhūtakandamūlaṃ yatphalaśākopavṛṃhitam ।
(107b)
anyathā sahasaivānnaṃ saṃparityajya khādataḥ ।।

śākādikādbhavecchūlaṃ kukṣau gātreṣu caiva hi ।
aṅgadāhaḥ śirobhaṅgo galadāho bhramastathā ।।

arocako vepathuśca pūtakṛcchraṃ ca jāyate ।
kandamūlaphalādīni parihṛtyāthavā budhaḥ ।।

kurvīta vartanaṃ mantrī kaṇapiṇyākasaktubhiḥ ।
teṣāmapi jayaḥ pūrvaṃ kartavyaḥ kandamūlavat ।।

kandamūlaphalaṃ śākaṃ bhaikṣyaṃ bhaikṣyaṃ dvijātiṣu ।
sulabhānya vidāhīni svādūnyapi mṛdūni ca ।।

śākādīnyādadenmantrī laghūni śleṣmalāni ca ।
śākādīni kaṇādīni parihṛtya tu mantravān ।।

bhaikṣeṇa vartayedvṛtiṃ sphītasthānānyapāśrayet ।
bhaikṣaṃ caturviṃdhaṃ proktaṃ sādhakānāṃ mahātmanām ।।

mādhūkaraṃ svathaṃ daṃttaṃ sāntānika mayā citam ।
gṛhādgṛhaṃ paryaṭato bhaikṣaṃ mādhukaraṃ smṛtam ।।

svayaṃ dattaṃ sādhakasya bhaikṣaṃ pātrasya pūraṇam ।
bhikṣāṃ mṛgayato yena kṛtaṃ pātrasya pūraṇam ।।

uktaṃ madīya bhaikṣaṃ tu grāhyametacca sarvadā ।
taddhi sāntānikaṃ nāma bhaikṣaṃ tu parikalpitam ।।

bhikṣārtha{ma}praviṣṭasya bhikṣādānamathā citam ।
etaccaturvidhaṃ bhaikṣaṃ bhikṣāvṛttiṃ samācaret ।।

lābhālābhe samaṃ cittaṃ kurvītā kaluṣātmavān ।
parihāsaparānityaṃ yatra gehe ca yoṣitaḥ ।।
(108a)
bhikṣārthī tadgṛhaṃ dūrāt prayatnena vivarjayet ।
yatra sadyāmiṣajayaḥ pramodo vartate gṛhe ।।

tadgṛhaṃ sarvathā dūrādbhikṣārthī parivarjayet ।
svadharmasthāṃ caredbhikṣāṃ caturṇāmapi varṇinām ।।

patitānāṃ dvijātīnāmabhiśaptātmanāṃ tathā ।
na gṛheṣvācaredbhikṣāṃ bhikṣārthī teṣu sādhakaḥ ।।

na paracchāyayātmānamapakrāmadvicakṣaṇaḥ ।
nānuvātamaṭaiścaiva parasya tu kadācana ।।

gurūṇāṃ śrotriyāṇāṃ ca tathā pravrajitātmanām ।
garbhiṇīnāṃ ca nārīṇāṃ bhayākrāntasya caiva hi ।।

na saṃmpannastu kartavyaḥ pareṇa tu kadācana ।
sparśātsaṃbhāṣaṇāṃścaiva vi * * thā bhavanti hi ।।

upasargāśca bādhante vyādhayaśca sudussahāḥ ।
tataḥ prayatnamāsthāya mantrī bhikṣāṃ tu paryaṭet ।।

vidyāṅgaiḥ pañcabhirvidvānsannahyata yathāvidhi ।
bhikṣāmaṭedapramattaḥ śivaṃ devamanusmaran ।।

dvitīye savane klṛpte bhikṣāpātraṃ viśuddhya tu ।
vāmadevena matimānsāṅgumastreṇa caiva hi ।।

taijasaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vāpi vaidalaṃ vaiṇavaṃ dṛḍham ।
kharjūrapuṭavikṣiptaṃ pātraṃ pārṇamabhāvataḥ ।।

aṅguṣṭhapariṇāhastu samovṛttaṛjurdṛḍhaḥ ।
vaiṇavo daṇḍa evaṃ tu pravaddhodhāryamavraṇaḥ ।।

hṛdi vidyādhipaṃ nyasya vahurūpasya mudrayā ।
mūrdhni brahmaśiraścaivaṃ īśānasya tu mudrayā ।।
(108b)
śikhāyāṃ caiva rudrāṇī īśānasya tu mudrayā ।
puruṣṭhutaṃ ca stanayorbahurūpasya mudrayā ।।

astraṃ haste ca daṇḍe ca sadyojātasya mudrayā ।
nyāsastayā mudrayā tu kuryānnyāsamimaṃ kramāt ।।

tato nivedya devāya gurave ca yathāvidhi ।
bhikṣārthī praviśedgrāmaṃ śivaṃ devamanusmaran ।।

daṇḍena tāḍyena panthānaṃ tridhaivaṃ tu vicakṣaṇaḥ ।
diśo virecya cāstreṇa nyasyeśaṃ sūryamaṇḍale ।।

sarvātmānamaghoraṃ vā gāyatrīṃ śivameva vā ।
pratyaṅgirāṃ ca manasā japanbhikṣāmaṭedbudhaḥ ।।

na śūnyena śarīreṇa bhikṣāṃ gṛhṇīta mantravit ।
pratyaṅgirāṃ ca japatā bhikṣāgrāhyā prayatnataḥ ।।

svastipratyaṅgirā proktā tryakṣarā praṇavādikā ।
sarvameva hi bhikṣārthī bhikṣāyāṃ yaḥ samudyataḥ ।।

abhakṣyaṃ yaddvijātīnāṃ na tadgrāhyaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ ।
ajñānādyatra bhikṣāyāṃ māṃsaṃ dattaṃ kadācana ।।

na tatra tu bhaveddoṣo doṣo dattaṃ tu jānatā ।
ajñātāṃ māṃsa bhikṣāṃ tu na gṛhṇīyāt kadācana ।।

nātitokaṃ nāti bahu bhaikṣaṃ mantrī samāharet ।
nātyantamupayogisyānna cāti bahuśasyate ।।

devatātithipūjāsyāddevatānāṃ ca nirvapet ।
kuryādvāstu valiṃ caiva vāyasāditya eva ca ।।

tāvadeva gṛhītavyaṃ na cāti bahuvistarāt ।
na paryuṣitabhaikṣyaṃ tu svayamadyāt kadācana ।।
(109a)
bhikṣāmaṭitvāgacchecca sasvameva niketanaṃ ।
nāmnīyādātmavidbhikṣāṃ parasya tu niketane ।।

svaṃ niketanamāsādya jaṅghāśaucaṃ vidhāya ca ।
snātvā cālabhya vā bhasma bhaikṣaśuddhiṃ samācaret ।।

saṃprokṣya vāriṇā tūṣṇīmavadyotya tatholkayā ।
ālambya pañcabhirmantrairdevasyāgre tathātmanaḥ ।।

śeṣaṃ stuvīta devānāṃ vāyasānāṃ prakalpayet ।
atithirbhakṣaṇārthaṃ tu devādīnāṃ tu kāraṇam ।।

agnau hutvāttatadbhaikṣaṃ kalpitaṃ tu yadātmanaḥ ।
śeṣaṃ yathoktapātreṣu bhuñjītodakapūrvakam ।।

nātisauhityamaśnīyānnāti kāyasya karṣaṇam ।
vastutrayasrutaṃ mantrī pivedambu sunirmalam ।।

nābhuktamātre nākāle na kṣapāyāṃ kadācana ।
annaṃ pratyagramaśnīyā sakūnparyuṣitānapi ।।

pātrabhujitakaṃ caiva pratyagrānvaṭakāṃstathā ।
khaṇḍakhādyārhaviṣpūrṇānmodakāṃstilamodakān ।।

aśokavartīssukṛtā grīveṣṭhānmadhuśīrṣakān ।
garbhotkarānsalavaṇānbahudaivasikānapi ।।

nirdoṣānbhakṣayenmantrī maṇḍakāṃścājugupsitān ।
dhūpānvarvaradhūpāṃśca vartidhūpāṃśca piṣṭhikān ।।

svastikāṃllopikāṃścaiva māṣādhmābhāṃstathaivakān ।
agnau saṃskṛtya bhuñjīta mantrī paryuṣitāni mān ।।

śaṣkulī paryaṭaṃścaiva bhakṣayeccirakālajāḥ ।
vyañjanaṃ kālikaṃ yaśca śāstrasaṃskārasaskṛtam ।।
(109b)
tadbhaikṣyamaviśaṅkena vratinā gṛhiṇāpi vā ।
madhyāhna prāptasavano yopiṃ nāśnāti dīkṣitaḥ ।।

mantrāḥ parāṅmukhāstasya santi pīṭhavyavasthitāḥ ।
muhūrtaiḥ pañcabhiryātaiḥ kṛtvā madhyāhnikā vidhim ।।

bhuñjānasya śivā mantrā bhavantyaśivahāriṇaḥ ।
devāḥ sadā navāḥ siddhā ṛṣayaśca tapasthitāḥ ।।

madhyāhnasavanaṃ yatnāttriratnāḥ paryupāsate ।
pratyakṣe tu gururbhuṅkte tato bhuñjīta sādhakaḥ ।।

parokṣaṃ devadevasya praṇamya śirasā śivam ।
bhikṣāṭanamidaṃ proktaṃ śrīmatpauṣkarabhāṣitam ।।

bhojanasya vidhiṃ brahmañśṛṇuṣvekamanā mune ।
yathānnaṃ bhujyate varṇaiḥ saṃskṛtaiḥ śivaśāsane ।।

manojñaṃ bhojanasthānaṃ sadyo gomayalepitam ।
prakīrṇapuṣpaprakaraṃ pracchannaṃ dhūpadhūpitam ।।

tatra maṇḍalakaṃ kuryādgomayena vicakṣaṇaḥ ।
pratyakṣeṇa satoyena bhasmanā vā mṛdāpi vā ।।

caturaśraṃ samaṃ kuryānnātidīrghaṃ pṛthuṃ vudhaiḥ ।
viṣamairaṅgulaiḥ kuryātpṛthutvenādhikaṃ karāt ।।

dairghyādapyardhahastaṃ vāpyadhikaṃ viṣamāṅgulaiḥ ।
triguṇena pramāṇena guroścaiva prakalpayet ।।

yathoktaistu tataḥ patraicchādayettannirantaram ।
yathoktāni ca patrāṇi tatra dadyādvahūni ca ।।

saśaṅkhaśuktibhāṇḍāni toyapātrāṇi vinyaset ।
śrīparṇī dārujaṃ pīṭhaṃ nirmitaṃ gomayena vā ।।
(110a)
vṛttaṃ vā caturaśraṃ vā samaṃ dvātriṃśadaṅgulam ।
tattribhāgacaturbhāge nādhikaṃ dairghyatopi vā ।।

dhautavastropadhānaṃ tu pūjitaṃ dhūpitaṃ tathā ।
tatra kāryaḥ parihāro bhasmanā vā mṛdāpi vā ।।

śālipiṣṭakarūpeṇa godhūmarajasāpi vā ।
ekarekhaṃ dvirekhaṃ vā trirekhaṃ vāpi kārayet ।।

śuklavastraparīdhānaḥ śuklagandhānulepanaḥ ।
rocanā carcitaścaiva śuklamālyadharastathā ।।

sakalīkṛtasarvāṅgo nyastapratyaṅgiro budhaḥ ।
tatrāsane prāṅmukhastu bhuñjīta viniveśya ca ।।

śuciḥ śuklāmbaradharaḥ snātāścārcitamūrtayaḥ ।
sa te pratyaṅgirā labdhāḥ savarṇāḥ paricārakāḥ ।।

kecittatrānnadāḥ kāryāḥ kecittoyapradāstathā ।
kecidvyañjanahastāśca tathā cetkṣepapāṇayaḥ ।।

varṇo tu mānāṃ tvavarā avarāṇāṃ tathottamāḥ ।
annapānapradāyeṣāmabhojyānnāstu te matāḥ ।।

na hastena na vastreṇa na piñchena ca vījayet ।
vījayettālavṛttena mādhureṇa ca vījayet ।।

tatra pratyaṅgirā dhyāyet praṇītāyāṃ ca tatvataḥ ।
udakasya ca vardhanyāmādhāronnādike tathā ।।

praticchet prathamaṃ vāri pātreṣu parikalpitam ।
dhūpite vāsitaṃ śītaṃ laghusvādu sunirmalam ।।

praticcheta yathāmātsyamaśanaṃ bhojyavittamaḥ ।
sakūnvāpyathavā bhakṣaṃ maṇḍakānvātha lālasān ।।

aktāghṛtena bhoktavyāssakthavaḥ saindhavānvitāḥ ।
(110b)
sauvarcalā vṛtāvāpi sāmudralavaṇena vā ।।

śarkarā guḍukhaṇḍairvā murchitāḥ phaṇitena vā ।
phālitaṃ ca guḍuṃ caiva śleṣmalaḥ parivarjayet ।।

pittalaḥ śarkarākhaṇḍe śakthanadyānsu murchitān ।
vātasvabhāvaḥ pūrvoktānsakthanvai lakṣaṇānvitān ।।

pānīyaṃ pūrvamadyāttu dadhnāvāpi śareṇa vā ।
amlvena saktanā vāpi na kṣīreṇa kadācana ।।

nātisauhityamaśnīyānna niśāyāṃ kadācana ।
punargṛhītvā nāśnīyānna ca dantānsamutkhanet ।।

adyānna coṣṇena samaṃ toyena savitena vā ।
na kāñcikena tānadyānna śukreṇa kaphaṃ vinā ।।

nādyā tu khalisatyānnairna vā khalisamanvitān ।
na māṣairna kulatthaiśca rājamāṣairna caiva hi ।।

na kalājerna caṇakairna masuraiḥ kathaṃcana ।
na murmairna mayoṣṭhai(rna) niṣpārairnato varaiḥ ।।

na śākakandamūlaiśca nāśnīyāccaiva piṇḍibhiḥ ।
nādyāśca tilatailena nābhaseneṅgudena vā ।।

na vā madhukatailena naiva siddhārthakena ca ।
parityāgānna cocchiṣṭaṃ tanmātraṃ tu parityajet ।।

evaṃ sakthanbhakṣayataḥ sāmyaṃ yasya tu śaktabhiḥ ।
prakṣālya hastau tu tato bhañjītānnaṃ madhukramāt ।।

yasya prakṛtisāmyaṃ tu bhaktenaiva hi sarvadā ।
praticchet prathamaṃ protraṃ maṇḍakānmaṇḍakasthitiḥ ।।

susvinnāṃśca tato mudgānmadgu(pū)pamathāpi vā ।
avadaṃśāṃśca vividhā nādrakena samarcitān ।।
(111a)
cidbhiṭaiḥ parpaṭaiścaiva paṭolairvividhairapi ।
svinnadṛṣṭaistathā śākairatikairadhikomalaiḥ ।।

avikāreṇa vaktrasya grāsamadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ ।
praticchetpāyasaṃ pūrvaṃ nocchiṣṭaṃ tu kadācana ।।

ghṛtapakvāṃstathā bhakṣyānnadyāddantavikhaṇḍitān ।
na daṇḍakhaṇḍitaṃ kiñcidgrasedgrāsaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ।।

maṇḍukānapyuthaivaṃ tu vyañjanaiśca samanvitān ।
tataśca pānakāḥ peyāstvantare ayathattukāḥ ।।

dravamante prarīccheta mantravādīya mā kṛtam ।
kvathitaṃ mārjitaṃ vāpi saśaraṃ dadhyathāpi vā ।।

mūrchitaṃ maricājājī lavaṇe nākhilaṃ dadhi ।
kṣīramante ca samitaṃ śarkarākhaṇḍasaṃyutam ।।

paropanītaṃ yatkiñcitpakvānnaṃ bhojanodyame ।
ardhabhuktasya dātavyaṃ paryagnikṛtameva hi ।।

pātrāṇi vāmadevena śodhayet salilena tu ।
pātre pratīcchitaṃ cānnaṃ brahmabhiḥ parimantrayet ।।

apośānaṃ gṛhītvā tu īśānena tu mantravit ।
puruṣeṇa juṣitvā tu maunī bhojanamācaret ।।

gṛhītvā vāmahastena pātramaṅguṣṭhapīḍitam ।
pañcāhutīstu prāṇādyāḥ kāyāgnau juhuyādvudhaḥ ।।

upaviṣṭosmi mantreṇa hyātmānaṃ kalpayetpurā ।
ātmānamaraṇiṃ kṛtvonyātmāgniṃ ca prabodhayet ।।

prāṇāhuti pradānena prīyante pañcakāstrayaḥ ।
mahābhūtanibaddhāstu ādhyātmādi purassarāḥ ।।
(111b)
adhyātmamadhibhūtaṃ ca adhidaivatameva ca ।
pṛ(thyumbu)tejaḥ pavanagaganānāmanukramāt ।।

aparāhuti dānena prīyante daśakāstrayaḥ ।
vākpāṇipādapāyūnāmupasthasya ca ye matāḥ ।।

śrotrarvakcakṣuṣā caiva rasanā ghrāṇayostathā ।
adhyātmamadhibhūtaṃ ca adhidaivatameva ca ।।

samānāhuti dānena prīyante pañcakāstrayaḥ ।
tanmātrapratibaddhāstu adhyātmādi purassarāḥ ।।

vyānasyāhuti dānena prīyante devatāstvimāḥ ।
brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca tathā ca parameśvaraḥ ।।

śivaḥ sadāśivaścaiva sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ ca yat ।
samastameva hi jagat sa devāsuramānuṣam ।।

bhūmisaṃsthena pātreṇa pañcabhuktvaivamāhutīḥ ।
tato yatheṣṭaṃ bhuñjīta bhūmisthenoddhṛtena vā ।।

bhakṣāstu maṇipātreṣu haimaraupyeṣu modakān ।
pāyasaṃ pārthive pātre loheṣu madhusarpiṣī ।।

tāmrapātreṣu ca payo haimaraupyamayeṣu vā ।
teṣāmalābhe bhuñjīta jalasthalabhaveṣu ca ।।

kuryānnobhayapātreṣu bhojanaṃ tu kadācana ।
yatraikamannaṃ bhuñjīta nānyo bhuñjīta tatra tu ।।

hutaśeṣaṃ tu pānīyaṃ na pātavyaṃ kadācana ।
sandhyākāle tu nāśnīyānna saṃviṣṭo na cotthitaḥ ।।

nātikalye nātisāyaṃ bhramanviṇmūtritopi na ।
viṇmūtrito vā yatkiñcit kṛtaśauconnamāharet ।।

nirmālya saṅkaro yatra bhājanaṃ tatra nācaret ।
(112a)
makṣikākīṭakeśādīndṛṣṭvānnaṃ tatparityajet ।।

hastaṃ prakṣālayeccaiva tadevaṃ ca samāśrayam ।
gṛhītvā cāpyanyadannaṃ bhuñjīta vidhisaṃsthitaḥ ।।

utkleśe tu samutpanne vidhivatsnānamācaret ।
ghṛtaṃ cānāmikāṅgulyā prāśayedgavyamātmavān ।।

vāmadevasya ca japaṃ kurvītāṣṭaśataṃ budhaḥ ।
nādyādekaṃ phalamapi vidvānmaṇḍalake kṛte ।।

bhujyatennaṃ tu rakṣobhirmantriṇo devasaṃsthitaiḥ ।
uttiṣṭhannānna sadyā tu na bhojyaṃ mantriṇāṃ bhavet ।।

āyuṣkaraṃ vāgyatasya bhañjanasya śataṃ samāḥ ।
ucchiṣṭhaḥ padamapyekaṃ yāyānmantrī na kutracit ।।

bālakānāmavighnāśca sādhakaṃ dūṣayanti hi ।
bhuktvā yathoditaṃ cānnamapadhānena śaucayet ।।

hastāvuṣṭhau ca nissnehaṃ lepagatyārthamātmavān ।
ślakṣṇaṃ gomayacūrṇaṃ tu mūtreṇābhyakrameva vā ।।

niḥsnehagandhalepārthaṃ yojyaḥ kālkastu sārṣapaḥ ।
ātmano dakṣiṇe pāde mantre nānena secayet ।।

dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhakenādbhiḥ pādāṅguṣṭhaṃ ca dakṣiṇam ।
tāmbūlaṃ prāśya ca tato dantadhāvanameva ca ।।

bhūyaḥ samyagupaspṛśya prāśayenmukhavāsakaṃ ।
upaspṛśya nivedyaṃ tu śivāya gurave tathā ।।

prāyaścittaṃ prakurvīta vāmadevena mantravit ।
baddhvāparighamudrāṃ tu navavārānudīrayet ।।

vāmahastakaniṣṭhāyāṃ samārabhya niveśayet ।
