vāmadevena saṃskārāṃścaturonukrameṇa tu ।।

aṅguṣṭhaṃ pañcamaṃ caiva sahaivānya kaniṣṭhayā ।
(112b)
dakṣiṇā nāmataḥ śeṣānnamaskārāntu veśayet ।।

nyasedvāmakaniṣṭhāṃ tu mantravānnābhimaṇḍale ।
vaktre ca dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhaṃ suśreyaṃ parighā smṛtā ।।

bhojyaṃ sarvapradhānaṃ tu bhoktā vai puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ ।
ato bhojayitā devo vijñeyaḥ parameśvaraḥ ।।

śvapākeṣvapi bhuñjānonena pāpena lipyate ।
śivadāṃ caiva gāyatrīmannadasyāgrato japet ।।

annaṃ bhuṅkte tu yasyaivaṃ tasya cāsaptamaṃ kulam ।
su(du)starāntārayeta sa * mādduratikramāt ।।

ekapaṅktyā yathājyeṣṭaṃ bhoktavyaṃ brāhmaṇādibhiḥ ।
dvātriṃśadaṅgulaṃ paṅktervyomamaṇḍalakāntaram ।।

samāpte rāja(nā)nāṃ tu bhoktṛṇāṃ pravibhāgaśaḥ ।
ācārya eko bhuñjīta sādhakaścaika eva hi ।।

putrakaḥ samayajñaśca svecchayā paṅktibhojane ।
bhoktavyaṃ paṅktisaṃsthaiśca prīyamāṇaiḥ parasparam ।।

caturṇāmeṣa varṇānāṃ gṛhiṇāṃ bhojane vidhiḥ ।
carukanyādibhikṣābhiḥ pūrvamuktaṃ tapasvinām ।।

।। arghyamantrapadāni yathā ।।

oṃ tapaviṣṇosmi somamaṇḍalake । agnyādibhyāṃ parisamantātpihito * * svāhā । ityātmakalpanā ।

oṃ ātmānamaraṇiṃ kṛtvā prāṇaṃ caivottarāraṇim ।
mathyaṃ manthena mathnāmi yathā rudreṇa manthitam ।।

(113a)
svāhetyātmāgnimudbodhyā pośānaṃ gṛhītvā nāgakūrmakṛkaradevadattadhanañjayebhya upaprāṇavāyubhyaḥ svāhā। iti bhojanamabhimantrya । oṃ amṛtopastaraṇamasīti śeṣajalaṃ prāśya ।
prāṇāya । apānāya । samānāya । udānāya । dhyānāya । etatprāṇāhuti pañcakaṃ dantairaspṛṣṭaṃ datvā bhuktvā ca amṛtāpidhānamasīnyācamet ।

ātmā vai dakṣiṇe pāde parātparataro vibhuḥ ।
tamadbhirabhiṣiñcāmi yoṅguṣṭhāgre vyavasthitaḥ ।।

śivo dātā śivo bhoktā śivassarvamidaṃ jagat ।
śivo yajati sarvatra yaḥ śivaḥ sohameva hi ।।

।। iti bhojanagāyatrī ।।

bhojanasya vidhistveṣa dṛṣṭaḥ pauṣkaraśāsane ।।
atha saṅkṣepato vacmi bhojanasya vidhiṃ param ।
ācamyoddhṛtya putātmā bhikṣāmabhyukṣayet sakṛt ।।

tāṃ śodhayitvā deveṣu vahnāvātmani kalpayet ।
agroktaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā pātrāṃstrīṃstatra nikṣipet ।।

arghapātragasaṃskārāt kṛtvā pātraṃ tu dhūpayet ।
tasminsaṅgaṃ śive nyasya candramaṇḍalasaṃpuṭe ।।

sañcintya cāmṛtaṃ pātraṃ mṛtyuñjayapuṭī kṛtam ।
nivedya hṛdi devāya saptadhā śivamantritam ।।

prāṇādīnāmīśānādyairhaviḥ pañcakamuddharet ।
mastake droṇakalaśaṃ kalpya prāṇaṃ hṛdi smaret ।।

gude pānaṃ samānākhyaṃ nābhau kaṇṭhepyudānakam ।
nābhau sandhiṣu tanmātrā na pośānaṃ pivet tataḥ ।।

mantrādevāśca gāyatrī tantrāṇyoṣadhiparvatāḥ ।
(113b)
tṛptāḥ prāṇānnaprāśena bhūrbhuvassva sthitaissaha ।।

aśvinau vāyuvāryagni devayonyaṣṭakaṃ yamaḥ ।
apānavyāhṛtī prītā bhavanti sahakinnaraiḥ ।।

saricchailābdhipātāladvipāḥ pitṛgaṇarṣayaḥ ।
tṛptāssamānayogena marudbhissaha pūritāḥ ।।

māyādho bhuvanaṃ kṣmātāstadīthāḥ paribṛṃhitāḥ ।
sarvaṃ hyudānayogena bhavantyardhena pūritāḥ ।।

ābrahmaśivaparyantaṃ śuddhāḥ śuddhātmatatvagāḥ ।
īśāvyānasya samprītyā tṛptāḥ sarvāśca śaktayaḥ ।।

lalitākhye mahātantre proktamevaṃ śivāgame ।
evaṃ bhuktvā vidhānena guruṃ vandeta bhaktitaḥ ।।

ājñāṃ tasya prakurvīta manovākkāyakarmaṇā ।
śravaṇādhyayanaṃ pāṭho vyākhyānaṃ śāstracintanam ।।

kartavyaṃ sādhakendreṇa vyānaṃ nāḍivivarjitam ।
tāvadyāvatpaṭhasandhyā samprāpteti hi bhāṣitam ।।

śrīvāmadevapaddhatyāmadhunāntaprasaṅgataḥ ।
bhojanāvasarekāṃmātmanocchiṣṭaṃ spṛśedyadi ।।

tatsajātīya bhedena prāyaścittamudīryate ।
parasyocchiṣṭasaṃsparśe * * eteḥ prasādataḥ ।।

upaspṛśyaiva śuddhiḥ syātsajātī(ya)sya ca kīrtanāt ।
īśo brāhmaṇajātestu mantrastatpuruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ ।।

aghoraṃ tu kṣatrajātervāmo vaiśyeṣu kīrtitaḥ ।
sadyojātastu śūdrāṇāṃ maheśasyādi sarvataḥ ।।

brāhmaṇastu mukhodbhutastasyāṅgaṃ puruṣātmakam ।
(114a)
bāhubhyāṃ kṣatriyo jāto ghorasyāṅgaṃ ca tatsmṛtam ।।

ūruto vaiśya utpanno vāmasyāṅgaṃ ca tadviduḥ ।
padbhyāṃ śūdraḥ samutpannassadyasyāṅgaṃ ca tatsmṛtam ।।

jñātīnāmīśvarāstveva brahmamantrāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ ।
nyūnajātestu saṃsparśādahorātramupoṣyate ।।

ekasthau tatra japtavyau jātīśau tu vijānatā ।
ekāhaṃ kṣatrasaṃsparśādvaiśyasparśāddvyahaṃ japet ।।

vāhyavarṇasya saṃsparśāt sarveṣāmeva varṇinām ।
maṇḍalasthaḥ śivaḥ pūjya jātyuddhārasya karmaṇā ।।

svajātīśena coddhāraḥ svajātessamprakīrtitaḥ ।
svāhāntena tu nikṣepa tatkṣepaḥ pha(ṅkṛ)tena tu ।।

evaṃ jātessamuddhāraṃ kṛtvā tāndīṣayedguruḥ ।
vāhyavarṇasya saṃsparśādevaṃ śuddhiḥ prajāyate ।।

dīkṣākarmaguroścaiva jātyuddharaṇapūrvakam ।
parokṣe tu guroḥ kāryaṃ svayameva vijānatā ।।

varṇatrayanivṛtopi brāhmaṇaḥ śivadīkṣitaḥ ।
dīkṣitānāṃ tu varṇānādannamadyādaśaṅkitaḥ ।।

śaucācāreṇa saṃpannā ye varṇāḥ kṣatriyādayaḥ ।
teṣāmannaṃ pariharanprāyaścitti bhaveddvijaḥ ।।

āmantrito na bhuṅktennaṃ yasya varṇasya yo dvijaḥ ।
tajjātīśasvajātīśaujastvaikatra viśuddhyati ।।

eko dvau vā trayaścaiva divasāstasya śuddhaye ।
kṣatraviṭśūdrajātīnāṃ japasyānukramaḥ smṛtaḥ ।।

jātīśvarasya vā dṛṣṭvā maṇḍalaṃ tu viśuddhyati ।
(114b)   abhinnabhedataścaiva gurutaḥ svayameva vā ।।

caturaśra caturdvāraṃ caturhastaṃ catūrajam ।
catuṣpatreṇa padmena jātīśasya tu maṇḍalam ।।

īśānaḥ karṇikāmadhye pūrve tatpuruṣo dale ।
dakṣiṇe vahurūpaśca vāmadevastathottare ।।

sadyojātaḥ paścime tu sarve jātīśvarāssmṛtāḥ ।
yajanīyamidaṃ samyagīśānasya tu maṇḍalam ।।

puruṣaḥ karṇikāmadhye īśānaścottare dale ।
śeṣā yathā sthitāścaiva vaiśyajātīya maṇḍale ।।

mantriṇānnaṃ na bhoktavyaṃ manyeṣāṃ liṅgināmiha ।
bhuktvānurodhājjaptavyaṃ śataṃ liṅgeśvarasya tu ।।

īśvaro bahurūpastu paribrājakaliṅginām ।
tathā pāśupatānāṃ tu vaktre vai malaliṅginām ।।

śvetaraktapaṭānāṃ tu vāmadevaḥ prabhuḥ smṛtaḥ ।
anyeṣāṃ liṅgināṃ caiva tathā ca gṛhaliṅginām ।।

kapālaliṅgino ye tu abhojyānnāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ।
teṣāṃ bhuktvā pramādā tu punardīkṣāṃ tu kārayet ।।

liṅgoddhāraṃ tataḥ kṛtvā kuryāddīkṣāṃ svayaṃ budhaḥ ।
yo yo yasya tu liṅgasya īśvaraḥ parikīrtitaḥ ।।

tena liṅgasamuddhāro nikṣepotkṣepakarmaṇā ।
jātīśvarasya yadyasya maṇḍalaṃ parikīrtitam ।।

tadeva maṇḍalaṃ kuryānmantrī liṅgeśvarasya tu ।
āsananyāsamātraṃ tu khyātaṃ jātīśamaṇḍalaṃ ।।
(115a)
āsanasya vahirnyāsaḥ procyate tasya cādhunā ।
antarnyāsaḥ prabhedena prokto bhedā tu kathyate ।।

brahmabhiśca tathāṅgaiśca antarnyāso vyavasthitaḥ ।
bahirmūrtidharairnyāsassarveṣāṃ tu yathoditaiḥ ।।

svaissvairmūrtidharaiḥ kāryo madhye - - hmabhistathā ।
īśāne śirasassarve sarve tatpuruṣānanāḥ ।।

aghorahṛdayāssarve vāmaguhyāśca kīrtitāḥ ।
ajñātamūrtayaḥ sarve brahmamantrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ।।

upabrahmāṇi sarveṣāṃ brahmāṇī vartakalpayet ।
śivavaścopacārastu kāryastvāvāhanādikāḥ ।।

pūjāvasānasarvastu sadyojātādibhiḥ kramāt ।
śaivasyānnaṃ na bhoktavyamavijñāya tu gocaram ।।

śivāṅkaṃ nāma yeṣāṃ tu śuddhaśaivāstubhe smṛtāḥ ।
kaumārāḥ śuddhaśaivāstu śuddhādhikaraṇodgatāḥ ।।

na teṣāṃ nāmamātreṇa śuddhaśaivatvamucyate ।
dharmasaṃvādanāmnāya tsthitiścācāravartinī ।।

yeṣāṃ tu śuddhaśaivāstu ye śivāṅkitanāmakāḥ ।
punarbhavonye vijñeyāstathā svāyambhuvo pare ।।

śaivatvakhyāpanā loke na śuddhasthitivartinaḥ ।
ye tvanyaliṅginaḥ santaḥ śaivamāśritya saṃsthitāḥ ।।

punarbhuvaste kathitāścaivāste śivaśāsane ।
svayaṃ gṛhītaliṅgāyo tepi svāyambhuvāssmṛtāḥ ।।

na gurorāgamasteṣāṃ na saṃskāraśśivātmakaḥ ।
kaleryugasya doṣāya bhaviṣyanti narādhamāḥ ।।

svayamūhyāgamaṃ śaivaṃ pustakaṃ dasyubhirhṛtam ।
(115b)   svatantrasthitisaṃbhinnā ye kalāvanyaliṅginaḥ ।।

āśrayanti śivajñānaṃ svabhāvānte svayambhuvaḥ ।
kṛtvopasadanaṃ ye tu śaivācārya * * * * ।।

bhavanti tvāgamābhijñā vijñeyāste punarbhuvaḥ ।
svayambhuvo ye kathitā dvidhā te samayasthitāḥ ।।

na teṣāṃ bhojya * * brahmañśūdraṃ caive phalohavat ।
rasavacca śivāmantrāssadyojātādayaḥ kramāt ।।

viddhāstenaiva vidhinā na punaryānti lohatām ।
purādṛṣṭaṃ yathāpātramagninā śuddhimeti hi ।।

jātiduṣṭa tathā pātraṃ śuddhaṃ syānmantravahninā ।।

।। brahmovāca ।।

pratyakṣeṇa pramāṇena dṛṣṭā lohasya hematā ।
anumānena saṃsiddhā śuddhasya dvijatātra tu ।।

siddhavadanumānā tu adṛṣṭaviṣayaṃ hi tat ।
pratyakṣeṇa tu yatsiddhaṃ nādṛṣṭa pratipādikam ।।

sudṛṣṭasādhanāyaiva pramāṇānyuditāni tu ।
pratyakṣaviṣayaṃ yattu pratyakṣeṇaiva siddhyati ।।

na kiñcitpātrasaṃsiddhau pratyakṣamiha dṛśyate ।
lohādau vastutā dṛṣṭā pratyakṣā hematā yathā ।।

vastumātramidaṃ grāhyaṃ na sambandhanidarśanam ।
lohaṃ tu kāñcanaṃ yadvacchūdrepyasti dvijanmatā ।।

manuṣyajātirekaiva kathaṃ siddhā dvijanmatā ।
samāne dehapiṇḍe tu vivekena vivarjite ।।

aśive dakṣiṇe kāye sāmānyā jātikalpanā ।
vyavasthāmātramuddiṣṭā na pratyakṣa prasādhitā ।।
(116a)
atha pratyakṣasiddhaśca sarva eva tadā dvijaḥ ।
sarvabījasamutpanno bhavatīti na tattathā ।।

na pakṣahetudṛṣṭāntaiḥ kiñcidastīha kāraṇam ।
āptāgamaṃ tu muktvaivaṃ manyase śivabhāṣitam ।।

prameyasādhanāyaiva pramāṇānyuditāni tu ।
aprameyasya devasya kiṃ pramāṇaiḥ prasādhyate ।।

anantā śaktayo yasya hyaviluptāḥ kriyāstathā ।
akṣayā viśvarūpāśca sarvāssarvārthasādhakāḥ ।।

evaṃ hi dvijatā siddhā varṇānāṃ śivaśaktibhiḥ ।
tatsiddhau siddhamevānyadādau hi kamalāsana ।।

ekaśayyāsanatvaṃ yadekayā nāśanaṃ tathā ।
sarvānugrahakartṛtva vṛttiścaivāvigarhitā ।।

svecchayācaraṇaṃ kintu na hi śāstraniyogataḥ ।
vyasthākāri śāstraṃ tu sthātavyaṃ tadvivasthayā ।।

ācāryasya vyavasthāyā tena tena tadā tayāṃ ।
ācāryasya vyavasthāyā tena tena tayā tadā ।।

sthāna saṃ * * * nāpi sādhakasya vyavasthayā ।
svayā vyavasthayā stha * putrakenāpi padmaja ।।

jagāda bhagavānevaṃ pauṣkare pārameśvare ।।

।। īśvaraḥ ।।

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi samāsādvarṇanirṇayam ।
brahmakṣatraviśādīnāṃ yathāvarṇamupasthitam ।।

na jāti vihitā tatra varṇaṃ vāpi sitādikam ।
yoniliṅgodbhavāssarve jīva ekaḥ samaḥ sthitaḥ ।।

tatra sarvagato devo dṛśyate jñānacakṣuṣā ।
ajñānapāpacittānāṃ kuśāstravihitātmanām ।।
(116b)
vākpralāpaḥ sthitasteṣāṃ yadi jātiḥ prayojanam ।
pratyakṣadarśanasyāsya na jīvasyāsti kāraṇam ।।

kriyate duṣṭacittaiśca parīkṣāvādinaḥ kvacit ।
vedopaniṣadādṛṣṭernajātirbalamokṣadā ।।

atrābrahmavibhāgajñā dvandvātītāḥ pare sthitā ।
athavā medhyapūrṇasya garbhavāsasya jantuṣu ।।

retasotpattidṛṣṭasya tatra kiṃ jātiśodhanam ।
samajīve dehavaddhe hyasthisnāyuparigrahe ।।

tādṛśe * * * dhvaste jātyā kiṃ vai prayojanam ।
sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ samaṃ yatra sṛṣṭiryonyātmakā samā ।।

tatra śuddhiḥ kathaṃ kāye yāvajjñānaṃ na nirmalam ।
na sitopavītaśikhā na vedapadamāśritaḥ ।।

paṭhamānā mukhoddeśānnirgatā brāhmaṇāḥ kvacit ।
paṭhitā yatra vai vedā upavītaṃ śikhā smṛtā ।।

kṛtrimā brāhmaṇā yatra tatra jātyā tu kiṃ bhavet ।
yā nārī puruṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā gatā kāmavaśākhurā ।।

madavihvaladevāntā kā satī parikīrtitā ।
yatra brahmādayo devā yonijāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ।।

mānuṣeṣu ca tiryakṣu kā kathā tatra manyate ।
vikarmastho jātivalā vipra ūrdhvaṃ vrajedyadi ।।

dharmasaṃsthopi mātaṅgo vrajenno muktidarśanam ।
āstāṃ tāvatpare śāstre śrutirdharme dvijāḥ sthitāḥ ।।

taponibaddho yairātmā brāhmaṇāṃstānvidurjanāḥ ।
paśupāśavidhānajñāḥ śivajñānānusāriṇaḥ ।।
(117a)
te hi devātidevasya prajākarmaṇi kīrtitāḥ ।
ityuktaṃ candrahāsākhye muktajādyāgameṣu ca ।।

samayyādiviśeṣeṇa jātirekaiva kīrtitā ।
āsunubhyo manuṣyāṇāṃ kulajātyoḥ kva śuddhatā ।।

ācāraṃ tadbhajecchuddhaṃ yacchuddhyā sarvaśuddhayaḥ ।
prasaṅgataścābhihitaṃ bhaktānāṃ jātivarṇanam ।।

atha tṛtīyasavane sandhyāṃ vindeta pūrvavat ।
mārjanādi yathāpūrvaṃ mantratṛptyavasānakam ।।

kṛtvā viśedyāgaharmyaṃ japaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi ।
tataḥ praṇamya deveśaṃ praviśenmandiraṃ śubham ।।

nirmalādiḥ pradīpāḍhyaṃ dhūpāmodamanoramam ।
śubhagandhodakairkliptaṃ prakīrṇakusumojjvalam ।।

vitānavastrasacchatraṃ kṛtaguptiṃ surakṣitam ।
nirdvandvaṃ nirbhayaṃ jñātvā tatra kalpaṃ vidhāpayet ।।

bhūmau saparihārāyāmapavartethavā śubhe ।
paryaṅkaṃ tūlikāyuktaṃ śubhavastrāvaguṇṭhitam ।।

prokṣitaṃ cāstrato yena tasyordhe kamalaṃ kṣipet ।
aṣṭapatraṃ sitaṃ śubhraṃ kesarādyaṃ sukarṇikam ।।

praṇavena namontena svanāmnāmudalaṃ śubham ।
tatrāruhya svapenmantrī mantrālaṅkṛtavigrahaḥ ।।

diśo viśodhya śāstreṇa varmaṇācchādayettataḥ ।
śirodeśe smaraṃ dhyāyetsvanāmnā praṇavādinā ।।

namaskārāntayuktena dakṣiṇena ratistathā ।
vāmabhāge tathā prītistalperatigaṇaṃ smaret ।।

kāmadevaṃ tathā tatra pādānte parikalpayet ।
ityanena vidhānena gṛhastho daiśikaḥ svapet ।।
(117b)
upasthānaṃ smṛtā śayyā tatraivātha tṛṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ ।
āśalyaviddhe pa(ṭve) vā bhasmanyatha mṛgājine ।।

padmaṃ tatra purā yadvatkalpayitvā svapettataḥ ।
naiṣṭhiko bhasmapṛṣṭhe tu dhyāyandevaṃ maheśvaram ।।

śirodeśe paṭṭe vātha pūrṇakumbhethavārcite ।
manasā vātha saṃpūjya tatpādābhyāṃ śiraḥ smaret ।।

- - naṃ sādhakendrastu tato rudramanusmaret ।
devyā sārdhaṃ gaṇeśānaṃ śubhaṃ ca tadanantaram ।।

smṛtvā pūrvāmukho mantrī svapedvā dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ।
ardharātre gate tasminnutthāya śayanādbudhaḥ ।।

kaṭiśaucaṃ tataḥ kuryādbhasmasnānaṃ yathā punaḥ ।
dinoddhṛtena toyena kuryādācamanaṃ tataḥ ।।

yasmādastaṅgate sūrye salilaṃ na praśasyate ।
pūrvodakena vā snātvā mantrābhyāṃ saṃsamācaret ।। ?

manasā pūjayeddevaṃ svabhāvasthothavā bhavet ।
japo vātha prakartavyo yogābhyāsasamādhinā ।। ?

kṛtvā bhūyaḥ svapenmantrī tāvadyāvakṣapākṣayaḥ ।
gītavādyaṃ purā yadvā tathā karmasamācaret ।।

divyaṃ māheśvaraṃ puṇyaṃ bhuktimuktipradāyakaṃ ।
atha vyavasthāṃ vakṣyāmi śivoktāṃ śivayājinām ।।

yathā yānti śivaṃ sarve varṇā varṇāvarāstathā ।
nagarīnirjharāśyambho gaṅgāṃ prāpyaikatāṃ yathā ।।

tatheśamantrasaṃparkāccāturvaṇyaṃ śivī bhavet ।
śrīdevyu * * * * * * * * * * * * * ।।

vartanaṃ daiśikādīnāṃ kathaṃ brūhi maheśvara ।
(118a)   yāyādidravyahīnānāṃ tacca syātkena hetunā ।।

।। īśvaraḥ ।।

svavṛtyāva * * rtanaṃ teṣāṃ vṛrtissyādgocaroditā ।
sugocaroditairvṛttairyāgaḥ kāryaḥ sthitiśca taiḥ ।
śivo jyotiḥ śikhā caiva sāvitraśceti gocarāḥ ।।

caturdhā bhedamāyānti punaścāśrama bhedataḥ ।
vihitaṃ samayajñānāmāśramāṇāṃ prapālanam ।।

sthityarthaṃ putrakānāṃ ca siddhyarthaṃ mantravādinām ।
anugrahādhikārāya daiśikānā * mīṣyate ।।

teṣāṃ nāmāni vakṣyāmi vṛttimāśramiṇāṃ kramāt ।
prājyāpatyā mahīpālāḥ kāpotā granthikāstathā ।।

kramādāśramiṇo jñeyā dīkṣitāḥ śivagocare ।
brahmacārī gṛhasthaśca vānaprasthaśca bhikṣukaḥ ।।

vṛtarāṣṭrāḥ śakāḥ kaṅkā gopālā jyotigocare ।
kuṭikāścātha vetālāḥ padmāhaṃsāstathaiva ca ।। ?

tuṭikā māṭharāścaiva guḍikā daṇḍasaṃjñitāḥ ।
śikhā sāvitrasaṃjñā aṣṭāvete prakīrtitāḥ ।।

eṣāṃ yā vṛtayastāśca kathayāmi samāsataḥ ।
svādhyāyo guruśuśrūṣā samastendriyasaṃyamāḥ ।।

ahiṃsā satyamasteyaṃ śaucaṃ samayapālanam ।
prājāpatyaṃ vrataṃ hyetadyaccarennaiṣṭhiko dvijaḥ ।।

bhautiko vātha siddhyarthī prājāpatya(ssa u)cyate ।
tasya vṛttiḥ smṛtā * kṣā yajanāpyāyane tathā ।।

ayācitaṃ dhanaṃ yacca dvijātipratipāditam ।
mahīpālā mahīṃ yasmācchānti vi * ṣaṇādibhiḥ ।।
(118b)
pālayanti yataste vai mahīpāḥ * prakīrtitāḥ ।
cikitsāsthāpanaṃ yacca pūrvoktaṃ prārthanā tathā ।।

sadvarṇebhyastathāpatnyo grāhyāścaiva sulakṣaṇāḥ ।
dīkṣāsaṃskāravidhinā mantrairvā vedacoditaiḥ ।।

saṃskārāśca tathāpyanye kāryā garbhādayaḥ kramāt ।
akṛṣṭe patitaṃ dhānyaṃ saṃharedyaḥ kapotavat ।।

phalaṃ vā nivapettatra kapotassa kapotavat ।
devācyatithipūjā ca trayāṇāṃ vihitā dhruvam ।।

ṛtukālābhigāmitvaṃ dvayorbhi * rasaṅgitā ।
svakhyāyādikamanyaśca vihitaṃ mānasaṃ tathā ।।

śivādipūjanaṃ pūrva karmasaṃnyāsa eva ca ।
śivadhyānaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ rātrāvuktaṃ yathepsitam ।।

sarvame * dyathoddiṣṭaṃ gocarāṇāṃ yathā kramāt ।
sāmānyaṃ vihitaṃ devi viśeṣaṃ kathayāmi te ।।

saṃyatākṣairmahāsatvaiḥ kumārabrahmacāribhiḥ ।
naiṣṭhikaiścaritaṃ nityaṃ jagatsarvaṃ carācaram ।।

sādhakaiścaiva mantrāṇāmalabdhaiśca dayālubhiḥ ।
evaṃvidhāḥ smṛtā devi vṛtarāṣṭrāstu sādhakāḥ ।।

gūḍhāścaranti ye nityaṃ vratadharmamathāpi vā ।
caranti svārthamevaikaṃ ekāste nibhṛtā bhuvi ।।

saritsarasta ekaṃ vā samāśritya vyavasthitāḥ ।
tatkṛṣṭasiddhayo ye vai tepi kaṭvaḥ prakīrtitāḥ ।।

gobhiḥ sārdhaṃ vrajaṃstiṣṭhedbhikṣāhāro jitendriyaḥ ।
goṣṭhe ca śivasennityaṃ sagopālo muniḥ smṛtaḥ ।।

sapatnīkāśca ye śubhrā brahmacaryaṃ caranti vai ।
asidhā(rāvrata)sthāste kuṭikāssamudāhṛtāḥ ।।
(119a)
tīrthayātrāṃ sapatnīkāryathālabdhopajīvinaḥ । ?
yathāvasye prakurvanti vetālāste prakīrtitāḥ ।।

adṛṣṭvārkaṃ na bhuñjanti na vadanti divāpi ye ।
rājyakāmāḥ smṛtāḥ padme padmahomaratāśca ye ।।

haṃśāścādvaitino proktā utpannajñānayoginaḥ ।
* * kāṇḍe punasteṣāṃ jñānaṃ vakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ ।।

kṣapayanti sadātmānaṃ kṛcchacāndrāyanādibhiḥ ।
tuṭimātrāśinastepi tuṭikāssādhakottamāḥ ।।

saṭhe kṛṣimayīṃ patnīṃ kṛtvā ye gṛhamedhinaḥ ।
gṛhakarmāśritāstepi khyātā māṭharasaṃjñayā ।।

ye tu grāsapramāṇābhirguḍikābhi tathāṣṭabhiḥ । ?
kandamūlaphalorthābhirvartante guḍikāstu te ।।

daṇḍino daṇḍavadye vai rātrau vīrāsane ratāḥ ।
vyomavyāpiratā nityaṃ daṇḍinastepi sādhakāḥ ।।

evaṃ ṣoḍaśabhedāstu śaivānāṃ parikīrtitāḥ ।
svagocaroditā tābhistairvartivyaṃ svavṛtibhiḥ ।।

yena ye dīkṣitāstepi tadgocarasamākhyayā ।
khyātāstvāśramadharmāśca svecchāsaṅkalpato bhavet ।।

etāssa(jñā) dvijāgryāṇāṃ rājādīnāṃ gaṇāṅkitāḥ ।
śaktisaṃjñāstathā strīṇāṃ sarvāsāṃ parikīrtitāḥ ।।

yāvajjīvaṃ samākhyātā vṛttissaṅkṣepatastathā ।
sāmānyā daiśikādīnāmahorātragatāṃ śṛṇu ।।

gṛhe hassnānayogau ca sthiti vyākhyā tathāhnikā ।
yāminyo vṛttayaḥ khyātā gurordhyānastutī śiśi ।।
(119b)
japaḥ pūjāgnikāryaṃ ca sthitiśāstrekṣaṇaṃ tathā ।
mantriṇāṃ grahaṇaṃ pūjā sthitiśāstrekṣaṇaṃ tathā ।।

putrakasyāpyaparasyāpi svādhyāyaṃ gurukarma ca ।
yāgādiśravaṇaṃ rātrau sarvairdhyānastutikriyā ।।

kāryā svaśaktito vṛttirinyākhyātā samāsataḥ ।
naimittikī yathāyogaṃ prayoktavyā samāsataḥ ।।

iti vidyā purāṇoktā grāhyā śivasamāśritaiḥ ।
viśeṣatopi niśśvāsāddevyāyāmalakādapi ।।

śāṅkārabhairavādyuktā śivajātirna vaidikī ।
mātāpitṛmayīyonissarvabhūteṣu sā samā ।।

śukraśoṇitasaṃyogātṣaṭkośakṛtavigrahā ।
aśucissā tu vijñeyā saṃskārātsaguṇojjhitā ।।

tadā mantrātmikā yonirniṣekādau vyavasthitā ।
tato jātāḥ smṛtā viprāḥ ṣāṭkośī cānyathā śuciḥ ।।

tatrāpyātmaguṇāśśreṣṭhāstairvinā nāpi dṛśyate ।
guṇasaṃskārahīnatvānna śabdabrāhmaṇaṃ na ca ।।

ayuktaḥ śivatatvena brahmatatvena vartate ।
brahmaṇo brahmalokāttu vitāpatitonyathā ।।

brātyaśśaktakṛtī hīno hyapārto brahmabandhukaḥ ।
brahmatatvapradhānātmā na caivaṃ śivatatvagaḥ ।।

pañcadhā praṇavāṅgatvācchaivaḥ pañcaguṇo dvijaḥ ।
brahmadvijatvaṃ saṃyojya {hutvā saṃskāramāditaḥ ।।

niṣekādiśmaśānāntaṃ hutvā sarvaṃ guṇaissaha ।
catvāriṃśāṣṭakaṃ hutvā hyāśramāṃśca pṛthakpṛthak ।।

saṃśodhya saptalokāṃśca brahmatatve niyojayet ।
hutvā brahmāṣṭakaṃ śodhya} yojyaṃ pañcaṣṭakādiṣu ।।

uttarottarayogena śivayukto hyanuttaraḥ ।
tasya tatra ca kiṃ dūraṃ paśyatādhvanilakṣaṇaṃ ।।

tasmādvicārya vaktavyāśśivavipre dvijentaram ।
(120a)
ānantyāpramāṇe tu yuktyākāreṇa dṛśyate ।
śivadvijasya viprasya cāntaraṃ mahadantaram ।।

vedadharmaiḥ pramucyante devāśca munayaḥ kramāt ।
kimarthaṃ dīkṣitāssarve brahmādyā ṛṣisattamāḥ ।।

vedapīṭhaḥ śivo devo vedāśca śivasevakāḥ ।
vedavicca tataśśaivo vedavedyaṃ sanātanam ।।

brahmaviṣṇvindra saṃpūjya śśivāḥ kāraṇakāraṇam ।
tatra yuktaḥ śivībhūto dīkṣitāśśivatatvajaḥ ।।

tasmācciravratī nityaṃ jātirāgamimaṃ tyajet ।
tyaktajātimado maunī śivavratadharaśśivaḥ ।।

jñātvaivaṃ mukuṭātsarvaṃ sādhakassiddhibhāgbhavet ।
itīmaṃ vākyaratnaughaṃ śāstrasindhubhya uddhṛtam ।।

kaṇṭhe kuruta sadbhaktā bhaktikāntā samāgame ।
kleśāṃstakṣnu hi viśvaga pratihataṃ jñānaṃ vibho।।

darśayanvatteṣā hṛdayepi * * * * * *।
* * * * * * karuṇā sindho vaco me śṛṇu ।।

yatkleśā bilujantu santatimimau prakṣālya bhaktyaughataśśīghraṃ prāpayatanni * * * jamajatvatsevakā yānti yat ।
evaṃ takṣakavartabhaktivihitāṃ vijñaptimākarṇya bho he śambhossātprasudhāra bharitāṃ dṛṣṭiṃ niveśya drutam ।
vaktreṇenmuśatāmalena subhagāṃ vāṇīmudīryāryamāṃ yuṣmaddhyānajapārcanādya vahitāṃllokāṃśca saṃpādaya ।।

।। iti rājānakatakṣaka(varta) viracitamarcanaprakaraṇaṃ paripūrṇamiti śivam ।। ।।
(120b)
atha sādhakasiddhyarthaṃ mantrārādhanamuttamam ।
śāntyādau mantraviṣayadīpanādimajātikam ।।

śāstrāntarebhya uccitya vakṣyate sādhakocitam ।
yasmāddhi - - ke loke dṛśyante siddhihiṃsakāḥ ।।

kīlanaṃ caiva mantrāṇāṃ bhedanaṃ mohanaṃ tathā ।
santrāsaṃ tāsranaṃ caiva jambhanaṃ stambhanaṃ tathā ।।

riputvakāraṇaṃ cānyatpratyaṅgaratvameva ca ।
sarvahānividhāyitvaṃ sṛjyante duṣṭamantribhiḥ ।।

evaṃ daśaprakāreṇa prayatante hi hiṃsakāḥ ।
tasmātsarvaprayatnena suguptena * ṇopari ।।

svagṛhe kārayedyatnādyāgaṃ homaṃ japaṃ tathā ।
athaiṣāṃ dīpanādīnāṃ yathā siddhyanti sādhakāḥ ।।

paraprayuktā naśyanti kṛtyāḥ khārkhadakādayaḥ ।
pratyaṅgirā prayogena hanti duṣṭānanekaśaḥ ।।

vādā tāmeva sarveṣāṃ mantravādamihottamam ।
jñātvā niyojayenmantrī mantraliṅgāni suvrate ।।

dīpanaṃ bodhanaṃ caiva tāḍanaṃ cābhiṣecanam ।
vimalīkaraṇaṃ caiva tathendhananiveśanam ।।

santarpaṇaṃ guptibhāvamāpyāyaṃ navamaṃ tathā ।
evaṃ navaprakāreṇa mantravādamaśeṣataḥ ।।

yo jānāti sujānāti mantrasādhanasādhanam ।
ekādaśavidho mantro jñātavyaśca punaḥ priye ।।

yena samyaṅniyogena siddhyante sādhakeśvarāḥ ।
bhedaṃ caiva pravakṣyāmi sarvaśāstreṣu sammatam ।।
(121a)
sampuṭaṃ grathitaṃ grastaṃ samastaṃ ca vidarbhitam ।
ākrāntamatha cādyantagarbhasthaṃ sarvatoddhṛtam ।।

tathāyuktividarbhaṃ ca vidarbhagrathitaṃ tathā ।
ityekādaśadhā mantrā niyuktāssarvasiddhidāḥ ।।

siddhaṃ sādhyaṃ svasiddhaṃ ca tathaivāritvameva ca ।
jñātvā sarvamaśeṣaṃ tu mantravādaṃ samācaret ।।

udayāstamayavyāptiṃ dhyānaṃ mudrāṃ svarūpataḥ ।
yo vettyevaṃ sa sarvajñassarvakṛtsādhakottamaḥ ।। ?

evamanyairviśeṣaiśca bhāvabhedai(sva)reśvari ।
bhāvitavyā mahāmantrā bhavanti phaladāḥ priye ।।

tasmātsarvaprayatnena jñāne sarvaṃ niyojayet ।
asya vai mantrarājasya nāsti devi vicāraṇā ।।

sarveṣāmeva mantrāṇāṃ bṛṃhakaḥ parameśvaraḥ ।
anena grathitā mantrāssūtre maṇigaṇā iva ।।

asya garbhe sthitā mantrā jāyante siddhimokṣadā ।
paraṃ sarvagataṃ devaṃ sarvasiddhiphalodayam ।।

vyāpakaṃ sarvatobhadraṃ sarvatomukhamavyayam ।
pūraṇaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ sarvato balam ।।

mantrāṇāṃ yonibhūtaṃ tu mokṣadaṃ siddhidaṃ śivam ।
yatastasya mantrasya nāsti devi vicāraṇā ।।

etairdoṣasahasraistu cchidritāssādhakā yadi ।
vināyakaiśca ye grastā ādivyādhiprapīḍitāḥ ।।

rājyabhraṣṭāśca rājānaḥ pararāṣṭraprapīḍitāḥ ।
(121b)   mṛtaputrāsmṛtadārāssubhayā vigataśriyaḥ ।।

ācāryassādhakā vāpi mantrasiddhiparāṅmukhāḥ ।
viputrā durbhagā nāryo vandhyāviddhiṣṭabhartṛkāḥ ।।

evamādisahasraistu duḥkhadoṣaissamanvitāḥ ।
dṛṣṭvā tāndānavāṃlloke hyuktadoṣaiśca dūṣitāḥ ।।

evamuddeśataḥ proktaṃ mṛtyujitsārasaṅgrahe ।
vistareṇa punarvakṣye dīpanādisvarūpakam ।।

dīpanaṃ ca mahābhāge praṇavobhayayojanam ।
bodhane tu namaskārassvāhākāro male bhavet ।।

vauṣaḍantargataṃ mantramabhiṣeke niyojayet ।
phaṅkārobhayasaṃyuktaṃ tāḍane viniyojayet ।।

lākāreṇa tu bījena tathaivāsvaritena tu ।
balavāñjāyate mantro guptibhāvaṃ tu rakṣaṇam ।।

mṛtyujitsampuṭībhūtaṃ japettadayutaṃ punaḥ ।
japte cānena vidhinā mantre rakṣā kṛtā bhavet ।।

ekāntaritayogena vāṃkāreṇa tu sarvadā ।
bhavedāpyāyano mantro * * * * * * * * ।।

śrīmaducchuṣmatantrādau dīpanādinirūpitam ।
oṃkārādyantasaṃruddhaṃ guhyaśaktipradīpitam ।।

dīpanaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ sarvasiddhikaraṃ param ।
oṅkārādyantasaṃruddhaṃ hūṃkāreṇa vidarbhitam ।।

bodhanaṃ dīpanaṃ teṣāmasiddhānāṃ tu siddhidam ।
oṅkārādyantasaṃruddhamoṅkāreṇa vidarbhitam ।।

(122a)
tāḍanaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ garvitānāṃ vināśanam ।
ṛkārādyantasaṃruddhaṃ vīṃkāreṇa vidarbhitam ।।

amṛtīkaraṇaṃ sekaṃ mantrāpyāyanakāraṇam ।
hakārādyantasaṃruddha huṃkāreṇa vidarbhitam ।।

amalīkaraṇaṃ cānyatsarvamantreṣu sādhakam ।
ṛkārādyantasaṃruddhaṃ vīṃkāreṇa vidarbhitam ।। ?

dahanaṃ sarvamantrāṇāmamalīkaraṇaṃ param ।
mantro yasya na siddhyeta japatassādhakasya tu ।।

vidhinā śāstradṛṣṭena yasye saṃkārayedvidhim ।
taṃ japecchivasāmiśraṃ māṃsamiśraṃ tu homayet ।।

uttiṣṭhata manomantro brahmaghnasyāpi siddhyati ।
ṛkārādyantasaṃruddhaṃ vīṃkāreṇa vidarbhitam ।।

kīlitāścaiva dagdhāśca cchinnā vitrāsitāścaye ।
amṛtīkaraṇaṃ teṣāṃ * * * vaṃ paraṃ viduḥ ।।

āpyāyanaṃ tato vakṣye mantrāṇāṃ sarvasiddhidam ।
dagdhā cchinnā vīryyahīnā ākṛṣṭāḥ kīlitāstathā ।।

pracaṇḍajapavidyāśca kṣubdhā lubdhāḥ prakopitāḥ ।
avamānavaśātkruddhā anyeṣāṃ dīptatejasām ।।

parasparaviruddhānāṃ mantravīryyāpahāriṇām ।
tadguhyāguhyamantrāṇāṃ caturthaṃ * mahābalam ।।

kliṃ bījaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ yuktamāpyāyakārakam ।
ācāryaissādhakaiśśiṣyaissamayajñairviśeṣataḥ ।।
(122b)
japato vidhinā mantrassarvajña * * carcitaḥ ।
tasya japyaṃ purākalpya vidhivattaṃ pradīpayet ।।

pradīpto jāyate mantro rakāraṣṣaṣṭhamūrtimān ।
tasyādyantaṃ niruddhyādvai sahasrapañcakaṃ japet ।।

dīpanaṃ yayate devi dhruvametanna saṃśayaḥ ।
japtavyonena vidhinā rephādyantaniyojitaḥ ।।

āgneyīṃ dhāraṇāṃ dhyātvā nidrāyogaṃ jahāti saḥ ।
tatparaṃ sādhakaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhavetsiddhiphalapradaḥ ।।

bodhito nātrasandehassādhakaiḥ kṛtaniścayaiḥ ।
tāḍitaśśaktidaṇḍena rephādyantaniyojitaḥ ।।

sattāhūtā vivecyante tadā yānti phalapradāḥ ।
yadā mantragaṇāssarve bhogādvivṛtalālasāḥ ।।

dhyānoktaṃ rūpakaṃ jñātvā uśīrapratimāṃ kuru ।
siṃhāsane niveśyaitān kalaśenābhiṣecayet ।।

paramākṣaradevena sahasramabhimantrayet ।
yāgaṃ kṛtvā pūrvesyā ācāryaścābhiṣecayet ।।

vidyābhiṣeka evaṃ syānmahāsukhaphalapradaḥ ।
malaṃ hitvāpyaśeṣaṃ tatsādhakasya varānane ।।

mantrassamalatāṃ yāti vimalīkaraṇaṃ śṛṇu ।
varṇāntamadhyagaṃ japtvā vimalīkaraṇaṃ bhavet ।।

sahasrajapamātreṇa sādhakaiḥ kṛtaniścayaiḥ ।
yāgahīnāstu ye mantrā homahīnāstathaiva ca ।।

jvālā mālā pradīptārṇā mantrā jñeyā niritvattāḥ ।
(123a)   svāhā vauṣaḍviniṣkrāntāḥ japtavyāḥ parvakarmataḥ ।।

sahasrajapamātreṇa oṅkāreṇa pratarpaṇam ।
māyādyantaniyogena jāyate managocaraḥ ।।

śatārdhajapamātreṇa jāyate gopanaṃ puram ।
pañcamaṃ balavarṇaṃ tu bindusthamamṛtākṣaram ।।

sahasrajapasādhyoyaṃ bhavatīti na saṃśayaḥ ।
mantravidyāgaṇasyaivaṃ prayogassādhakasya tu ।।

vyākhyāto vidhinānena sādhakassiddhibhāgbhavet ।
devyāyāmalaśāstre ca prāha śītāṃśuśekharaḥ ।।

aiśvaryamadadarpeṇa mudirā mūrcchitā iva ।
paśyantopi na paśyanti jānantopi vimūḍhakāḥ ।।

atorthāddevadevena svājñā yuktā niyāmikā ।
tathā tu dīpitā mantrā bodhavṛddhiṃ prayānti ca ।।

yathā tantrānusāreṇa japaṃ svābhāvikaṃ priye ।
kṛtvā karma ca mantraśca tathā dīpanamācaret ।।

praṇavobhayasaṃyuktaṃ japenmantraṃ mahātapāḥ ।
tataḥ karoti karmāṇi sādhakasya prayogataḥ ।।

dīpanaṃ prāha deveśa umāyāmalaśāsane ।
dīpanairdīpito mantro jātiṃ prāpya pradīyate ।।

anyajātiṃ tiraskṛtya siddhiṃ cāpi prakāśayet ।
bodhito buddhyate ka * sādhakasya yathākṛtam ।।

bījataḥ phaladaḥ cāpi bhavenmantro na saṃśayaḥ ।
(123b)   dīpanaṃ daśabhāgena śatabhāgena bodhanam ।।

japātsahasrabhāgena bhavenmantrasya bījanam ।
vidyāpurāṇe kathitaṃ jātibhedena dīpanam ।।

sarveṣāmeva mantrāṇāṃ saptasroto vinirgatam ।
karmabhedaṃ paraṃ guhyaṃ siddhimūlamanuttamam ।।

pravakṣyāmi samāsena saṃpuṭādi yathārthataḥ ।
vinā yena varārohe karmasiddhirna jāyate ।।

mantramādau likhetsarvaṃ sādhyasajñāmanantaram ।
punaścaivaṃ likhenmantraṃ saṃpuṭassamudāhṛtaḥ ।।

śāntipuṣṭikaro jñeyastrailokyaiśvaryasādhakaḥ ।
sādhyā nāsārṇamekaikaṃ mantrārṇaissaṃpuṭīkṛtam ।।

grathitaśca samākhyāto vaśyākṛṣṭikaraḥ param ।
adhordhve ca tathādyante mantraṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ।।

madhye cāsya bhavetsajñā grastamityabhidhīyate ।
abhicāreṣu sarveṣu yojayenmāraṇādiṣu ।।

abhidheyaṃ likhetsarvaṃ paścānmantraṃ mahādhipe ।
evametaddvidhā * * samastamabhidhīyate ।।

vidveṣoccāṭanārthaṃ tu yojayedaviśaṅkitaḥ ।
abhidheyaṃ bhavetpūrvaṃ tato mantrassakṛdbhavet ।।

vidarbhitaśca sa jñeyassarvaduṣṭanivarhaṇaḥ ।
rakṣārthaṃ sarvalokānāṃ jagadvaśyakarastathā ।।

mantreṇākramitassādhyassamantādyatra tiṣṭhati ।
(124a)   ākrāntassa bhavettasmātsadyaḥ sarvārthasiddhidaḥ ।।

sakṛtpūrvaṃ likhenmantramante caivaṃ tridhā punaḥ ।
madhye cāsya likhetsaṃjñāmādyantamitaṃ viduḥ ।। ?

ādāvantepyadhaścordhve mantraṃ caiva samālikhet ।
sādhyānāmamanurmadhye garbhastha eṣa kathyate ।।

māraṇoccāṭane vaśyaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣe ।
hetinausainyadhīgarbhādivyastambhanake tathā ।।

saṃyuktaḥ kurute hyāśu satyaṃ devi na cānyathā ।
tridhā mantraṃ likhetpūrvaṃ tathaivānte punarlikhet ।।

sakṛtsajñā yadā madhye taṃ vidyātsarvato mukham ।
sarvopasargaśamanaṃ mahāmṛtyuvināśanam ।।

sarvasaubhāgyajananaṃ mṛtānāmamṛtapradam ।
vidhinā yojitaṃ bhadre sarvakāryasusādhakam ।।

mantramādau tato nāma punarmantraṃ samālikhet ।
evametattridhā kṛtvā bhavedyuktivicāritāḥ ।।

sarvajvaraharaḥ prokto bhūtāpasmāramardanaḥ ।
sarvavyādhipraśamano bhuktidaḥ paramaḥ smṛtaḥ ।।

ekaikaṃ sādhyavarṇaṃ tu kṛtvā mantraṃ vidarbhitam ।
pūrvavadgrathito cānyattasyādyante prakalpayet ।।

vidarbhagrathite caitanmantralakṣaṇamuttamam ।
sarvākṛṣṭikaraṃ proktaṃ sarvasiddhiphalapradam ।।

jarādāridryaduḥkhaughamṛtyudaurbhāgyanāśanam ।
śāntipuṣṭitathā vaśyaṃ vidveṣoccāṭamāraṇam ।।

stobhastambhanamāveśamohanāpyāyanādikam ।
(124b)   prayuktaṃ sādhayenmantraṃ yatkiñcinmanasepsitam ।।

uktānuktamaśeṣaṃ vai nānāśāstraprayojitam ।
etadguhyatamaṃ proktaṃ sāmānyaṃ bhuktimuktidam ।।

lakṣaṇaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ kathitaṃ kubjikāmate ।
garbhasthaṃ ca tathā grastaṃ yaśvoktaṃ sarvato vṛtam ।।

{ākrāntaṃ ca dvidhā proktaṃ tajjñeyaṃ sarvato vṛtam ।}
eṣa likhyavidhiḥ prokto likhyate paṭhyate tathā ।।

ākrāntaṃ sarvato nāma mantreṇa pariveṣṭayet ।
garbhasthe ca tathā graste mantraṃ dikṣu niveśayet ।।

ākrāntaṃ tu yathā proktaṃ tajjñeyaṃ sarvato vṛtam ।
eṣa likhyavidhiḥ prokto japasya vidhirucyate ।।

ādyantayoryato mantro madhye saṃjñā sasampuṭā ।
mantrākṣaraṃ bhavetpūrvaṃ sādhyanāmākṣaraṃ punaḥ ।।

evaṃvidhaṃ japenmantraṃ grathitassa udāhṛtaḥ ।
samastaṃ prakṛterjñeyaṃ praṇavādinamontagam ।।

mantramādau tatassādhyaṃ sakṛjjñeyaṃ vidarbhitam ।
sādhyamādau niyoktavyaṃ praṇavādisamanvitam ।।

tataḥ karma tato mantramākrāntaṃ paramaṃ bhavet ।
ādyantayossampuṭavanmadhya santamathāpi vā ।।

ādadīta tato madhyaṃ tridhā yojyaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ ।
vidarbhagrathito hyeṣa vijñeyaḥ karmasiddhidaḥ ।।

tridhā mantraṃ tridhāsādhyaṃ praṇavādinamontagam ।
eṣayukti vidarbhākhyaḥ prayogaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ ।।

sāmānyamadhyamānāṃ tu śāmbhavādau niyojayet ।
ativīryyaprabhāvāste svayaṃdīptā mahābalāḥ ।।
(125a)
ityekādaśadhā mantro yena jñātassa siddhibhāk ।
anyadvācanikaṃ sarvaṃ na tu vākyaṃ virudhyate ।।

ūrdhvādharatiromātrā mantrāṇāṃ lakṣayeddvija ।
ekākṣaro trayo mantro bahvakṣarasamanvitaḥ ।।

tatonye malinā jñeyā * * * * samanvitāḥ ।
uttamādhamamadhyāstu vijñeyā mantradevatāḥ ।।

ūrdhvamātrāssvarā jñeyā adhaḥmātrāssadāsurāḥ ।
tiryaṅmātrāḥ smṛtā yakṣā gandharvāssamudāhṛtāḥ ।।

ūrdhvamātraiḥ khecaratva sāsurairāsuraṃ param ।
tiryaṅmātrairmanuṣyatvaṃ vividhaḥ siddhisaṃgrahaḥ ।।

uttamairuttamā siddhirmadhyamairmadhyamā bhavet ।
sāmānyairmānuṣīsiddhistasmāduttamamāśrayet ।।

yathācchinnaśirassatyaṃ neha bhūyaḥ prarohati ।
udvatpaiśācamātrāṇāṃ siddhiragryā na vidyate ।।

svapnalabdhaṃ japenmantraṃ prasenoktamathāpi vā ।
svapnakarmāṇi siddhyanti mantraiścaiva śivoditaiḥ ।।

tasmāt svapnaśiśuṃ kuryāt prasenāmavatārayet ।
bhūmau ca likhitaṃ prāptamāgaṃta vā kulakramāt ।।

bhāvāṃśako vā yatra syādbhaktissyāddevatāṃ prati ।
āsiddhessādhayedekaṃ śabalaṃ na samācaret ।।

bhūtānāmanukampārthamanyaṃ vā sādhayet pṛthak ।
īpsitaṃ guruṇā * paṃ gaṇayitvātha taṃ japet ।।

iti proktaṃ maheśena hāṭakeśvaraśāsane ।
atha jātivibhāgaśca pṛthakkarmasvavasthitaḥ।।
(125b)
procyate pravibhāgārthaṃ mantrāṇāmanupūrvaśaḥ ।
jātiyogena mantratve sannidhānaṃ vrajanti te ।।

hrasvo dahati pāpāni dīrghaścoccāṭayeddhruvam ।
āpyāyane plutau yojyo muktikarmaṇi caiva hi ।।

hrasvaścādyantasaṃyogāt phaṅkārāntaśca mārayet ।
yadi krodhasamāviṣṭo japaṃ cāyatalocanaḥ ।।

hūṃkārādyantasaṃyukto dīrghaścoccāṭayeddhruvam ।
vidveṣosau prayoktavyaḥ phaṅkārādisamanvitaḥ ।।

plutena tu punaḥ kāryaṃ śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ tathā ।
dīkṣākāle tu kurvīta tatvasandhānakarma ca ।।

dīrghenākārṣayetsādhyaṃ phaṅkārādiniyojanāt ।
svamahatpātako bhūtvā sādhu mūrdhani kalpayet ।। ?

nigarbhodayamārgeṇa viśettenātha tasya vai ।
tadvadaikatvamāviśya tenaivākarṣayetpunaḥ ।।

aṅgānyaṅgeṣu sandhāya cetaścetasi yojayet ।
padmakośvasthitaṃ tantu sādhyasyātmānamāharet ।।

yadeva kriyate karma tannāmopapadaṃ smṛtam ।
japettatsthānasaṃyuktaṃ nāmayuktaṃ vidarbhitam ।।

binduyuktaḥ sapraṇavo na mantro hyarcane smṛtaḥ ।
home svāhāntagaḥ śāntā vākarṣe vaśyakarmaṇi ।।

pauṣṭike manumaskāro hrīṃkārādyantasaṃpuṭaḥ ।
ākṛṣṭāvatha vidveṣo hūṃkārādyantasaṃyatam ।।

phaḍantaṃ pha(ṭpra)yogaṃ tu māraṇe saṃprayojayet ।
jambhane stambhane mohe daṇḍanigrahabandhane ।।

virodhonmādaduḥkhādau viprītau kāryanāśane ।
(126a)   virodhadṛṣṭibandhe ca bādhiryendhatvakarmaṇi ।।

vicittatā manobhede mahāvyādhiprakopane ।
phaṭkārādyantasaṃruddhaṃ mantrī mantraṃ samuccaret ।।

stambhane savisargāntaṃ cchindidantaṃ tu cchedane ।
śoṣaṇe bahudhāśūnyaṃ rāgasañjanane tathā ।।

kīlane tiṣṭhatipadaṃ dhāraṇābījasaṃyutam ।
ekārādyantago mantro mukhabandhaṃ karotyalam ।।

sāntasthito vā yavarṇaśca staścoccāṭane gataḥ । ?
vidhilābhe makārānto nivṛttau svetisaṃyutaḥ ।।

dāhe rephadvayākrānto mūlamantro niyojitaḥ ।
bhūpreṣye ghakārānta ākarṣeṣṭadaleṣu ca ।।

saṃpuṭe syādikārotra vidveṣe īprakīrtitaḥ ।
kṣorakṣo duṣṭasatvādimardane śāntipauṣṭike ।।

makāro viṣanāśāya viṣe sthāvarajaṅgame ।
āpyāyane jakārotra rephassyāddāhakarmaṇi ।।

huṃkāro māraṇe yojyaṣṭakāro viṣadūṣitaḥ ।
suravarṇādiraṣṭāre lekhyamānaḥ svakāryakṛt ।।

jājvalyamānassañcintya pādāṅguṣṭhāttu mastakam ।
praviśyādhomukhaṃ kṛtvā hyapānasthānamātmanā ।।

dagdhakāṣṭhasamaprakhyaṃ sādhyasa * * * * * ।
tyaktadehe vicintyaivaṃ mantracchedaḥ kṛto bhavet ।।

kīlanaṃ mohanaṃ caiva tejohānistathā bhavet ।
rakṣācchedaśca bhavati satyaṃ nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।।
(126b)
sarvadviṣṭhaśca bhavati mantrastasya na siddhyati ।
kakārapuṭayormadhye yasya nāma samālikhet ।।

tadvāhye vahnibhuvanaṃ rephāṣṭakavibhūṣitam ।
likhitvā sthāpayedyatra kapālo bhayasaṃpuṭe ।।

yasya nāmnā na sandeho mantracchedastu tasya vai ।
jāyate siddhi hāniśca vidviṣṭassarvato bhavet ।।

uttarasthāssadā mantrā lekhyāssāmukhyamāgatāḥ ।
nāyakābhimukhāssarve madhyastho yassa nāyakaḥ ।।

tadā bhavanti phaladā vāsācārasthitāssadā ।
viparītena likhitā vidveṣoccāṭanādikam ।।

kurvanti dakṣiṇe cāre hyariṣṭaṃ viparītagāḥ ।
itthaṃ jātiprabhedena yatheṣṭaṃ viniyojayet ।।

mantrī mantramiti prāha śrīmadvarmaśivābhidhaḥ ।
tadekaṃ bahudhā devi dhyānaṃ caiva svasiddhidam ।।

dvaitādvaite vimiśre vā hyekavīretha yāmale ।
sarvaśāstraprakāreṇa yathā siddhiprado bhavet ।।

cintāratnaṃ yathāloke cintitārthaphalapradam ।
tathaiva mantrarājastu cintitārthavidhāyakaḥ ।।

mantrāṇāṃ saptakoṭīnāmālayaḥ paramo balī ।
bhāvahīnāstu ye mantrāśśaktihīnāstu kīlitāḥ ।।

varṇamantrādihīnāstu gurvāgamavivarjitāḥ ।
{trasvāmnāyā vinaṣṭā ye hyāgamojjhitavighnitāḥ ।।

na siddhyanti yadā devi japtā iṣṭāssahasraśaḥ ।
asiddhā ripavo ye tu sarvāṃśakavivarjitāḥ ।।}

ādyantasampuṭenaivamādyarṇena tu rodhitaḥ ।
mantreṇānena deveśi hyamṛteśena jīvitāḥ ।।
(127a)
siddhyanti hyaprayatnena japtā iṣṭānusaṃśayaḥ ।
khyātāssarvapradā devi bhavanti navaśonṛtam ।।

tadevaṃ paramaṃ devamamṛteśamanāmayam । ?
svabhāvasthaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ vyāpakaṃ * * * dhruvam ।।

na tasya rūpaṃ varṇo vā paramārthena vidyate ।
yasmāt sarvamayo devassarvāgamamayaśśubhaḥ ।।

vyāpakaḥ sarvamantrāṇāṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ ।
nirmalaḥ spaṭiko yadvattantau protassitādike ।।

pratibimbeta sarvatra yena yena hi rañjitaḥ ।
yathā tathaiva deveśaḥ sarvāgamaniyojitaḥ ।।

phalaṃ dadāti sarveṣāṃ sādhakānāṃ yathepsitam ।
tasmāt sarveṣu srotassu cintāmaṇirivojjvalaḥ ।।

bhāvabhedena vai dhyātassarvāgamaphalapradaḥ ।
śivassadāśivaścaiva bhairavastumburustathā ।।

somasūryasvarū(pe vā) vahnirūpadharo vibhuḥ ।
yato jñānamayo devo jñānaṃ tu bahudhā sthitam ।।

niyantritānāṃ baddhānāṃ  (trāṇā)ttattetramucyate ।
mṛtyoruttārayedyassānmṛtyujittena śocyate ।।

amṛtatvaṃ dadātyevamamṛteśa iti smṛtaḥ ।
yena yena hi bhāvena tasya tatphalado bhavet ।।

yassadāśivarūpeṇa sadā dhyāyati sādhakaḥ ।
sadāśivatanu(rya)sya bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ ।।

sarvasādhāraṇo hyeṣa sarvānugrahakārakaḥ ।
sadyovāso hyaghoraśca puruṣeśānavigrahaḥ ।।

anena hṛdayādīni nyastavyāni varānane ।
(127b)   anenaiva tu mantreṇa svāsanaṃ parikalpayet ।।

evaṃ hi mṛtyujittantre jagāda parameśvaraḥ ।।

।। śrīdevyuvāca ।।

pūrvamuktaṃ tvayā deva mantraṃ śuddhāṃśakaṃ yathā ।
taṃ tathācakṣva śiṣyāṇāṃ hitārthaṃ tripurāntakaṃ ।।

evaṃ cāturdaśavidhaṃ saṃsāraṃ * * * ditam ।
tatra cāṣṭavidhassargo devānāṃ samudāhṛtaḥ ।।

ābrahmādipiśācāntaṃ tadaṃśā manujāḥ priye ।
tadā śāstreṣu saṃsiddhaṃ kathitaṃ naganandini ।।

kathayāmi samāsena piśācādiyathoditam ।
piśācāṃśo mahāmādhvī dāmbhikaḥ pāpasaṃyutaḥ ।।

devadvijagurudrohī mitrāṇāṃ ca bhayaṅkaraḥ ।
bhayāntacchādmiko nityaṃ sarvāśī sarvavikrayī ।।

aśuddhamārganirataṃ sarvakālaṃ sugarhitaḥ ।
na tasya siddhyate kiñcidityāha bhagavāñśivaḥ ।।

nairṛtyāṃśasamudbhūtaḥ chalaprekṣī bhavetsadā ।
kanyāpahārako nityaṃ krodhanaścapalastathā ।।

saṃsārī vahnipūjāsu niratassarvadā bhavet ।
atimānī sadātīkṣṇacchadmanā caiva ghātayet ।।

krūramantrāṃśake siddhistasya nityaṃ bhavetpriye ।
dhanārjanaparo nityaṃ yakṣāṃśassvāṅganāpriyaḥ ।।

matsyamāṃsāmiṣārthī ca lubdho garvitaceṣṭitaḥ ।
surāsavaṃ tathā sadya satitīkṣṇaṃ niṣevate ।।

āyudha saṅgrahā * * rthī śāstrasevī sadā bhavet ।
tasyāpi siddhissubhage hyavicārasamāśritā ।।
(128a)
gāndharvo gītakuśalo lubdhacchadmaparāyaṇaḥ ।
sauryābhimānī satataṃ śāstrabhīrurvarānane ।।

rahasye vāṅganāsevī sarvabhāvaspṛhālukaḥ ।
anāsthā paramo nityaṃ na nadi * * sauhṛdaḥ ।।

māyāvī satataṃ dhūrto devatāyatane rataḥ ।
asthiraḥ sarvakāryeṣu gandharvāṃśo bhavennaraḥ ।।

māṃsāṃśī darpitaḥ krūrakrodhanotyaśanapriyaḥ ।
mahāhave sadāsaktaḥ śatrūṇāṃ vijaye rataḥ ।।

āyudhāndhārayennityaṃ sarvamevābhimānayet ।
asahiṣṇu balī nityaṃ devadveṣaparāyaṇaḥ ।।

śūro daityāṃśajo nityaṃ pātālādiṣu bhājanam ।
indrāṃśo taijaso nityaṃ sarvakāryāṇi sādhayet ।।

devadvijarato nityaṃ vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ ।
sarvaśāstrapravartā ca kāvyakartā bhavet sadā ।।

āstiko bhāvasaṃyukto brahmacaryaparāyaṇaḥ ।
svaśīlo dharmanirataḥ indrāṃśosau bhavennaraḥ ।।

svaveśo bahuputraśca brahmaṇyaśśāstraparagaḥ ।
dharmajñaśca kṛtajñaśca dātā ca svajanapriyaḥ ।।

devatāyatane sakto gurubhaktaśca śāstravit ।
prājāpatyo mahādevi sargasiddhipradāyakaḥ ।
somāṃśo darśanīyaśca samastajanavatsalaḥ ।।

devadvijārcanarataḥ somāṃśo bhavate naraḥ ।
tapasvī saṃyato nityaṃ sitavaktro jitendriyaḥ ।।

japārcanarato dāntaḥ paṇḍitaśśāstrasevakaḥ ।
brahmāṃśo brahmabhaktaśca sidhyate tu narottamaḥ ।।
(128b)
devārcanarato vāgmī brahmaṇya(ssatya)saṅgiraḥ ।
yajñaśīlassadādakṣaḥ piṅgalo māyayā * * ।।

śatruhānispṛho nityaṃ daityānāṃ dveṣaṇe rataḥ ।
nigrahānugrahe sakto viṣṇvaṃśo bhavate naraḥ ।।

rudrāṃśaḥ kekarākṣaśca piṅgalo dambhavarjitaḥ ।
māyāvi(da)stvamāyīyo devatārādhane rataḥ ।।

na kasyacit prahasate garvitaḥ krodhavānsadā ।
vedavedāṅgavidvettā tejasvī mantratatparaḥ ।।

mantrasiddhisamāyukto japaniṣṭhaḥ kriyānvitaḥ ।
yogī siddhyati deveśi sarvamantreṣu bhāvitaḥ ।।

vipi * * ceṣṭāvyāmiśrā jāyante varavarṇini ।
evaṃ jñātvāṃśakaṃ devi gurustantraviśāradaḥ ।।

anugrahaṃ prakurute svabhāvasadṛśeṃ budhaḥ ।
tathā siddhyati deveśi sādhako mantratatparaḥ ।।

yatra bhaktissthirā vāpi tadaṃśosau mahādhiye ।
bhāvanāyāṃ tu deveśi aṃśaṃkaṃ tadbhavetsphuṭam ।।

siddhasādhyavibhāgena mantraṃ vāpi vicārayet ।
siddha ādyantasaṃsthastu madhyasiddhiprado bhavet ।।

sādhyastristhānago devi neha siddhiprado bhavet ।
svasiddhaḥ sādhakendrāṇāṃ siddhyante hyavicārataḥ ।।

avināśakaro jñeyo nityameva gaṇāmbike ।
tatrāpi vidhimākhyāsye yathā tatpravicārayet ।।

nāmapūrvaṃ samālikhya mantravarṇāvagaṃ budhaḥ ।
- -  -  - vinirbhinnaṃ na tu mantraṃ tu pārvati ।।
(129a)
pūrayenmantravarṇāni nāmavarṇaistu daiśikaḥ ।
siddhasādhyasvasiddhārīngaṇayedgaṇitena tu ।।

kūṭaśaṇṭhavihīnāṃ ca mātṛkāṃ gaṇayedbudhaḥ ।
phalaṃ tatra vicāryātha mantradānaṃ samārabhet ।।

siddhamaripradiṣṭaṃ tu siddhyate naiva pārvati ।
vicāryāriṃ tathā sādhyaṃ pārśvagaṃ yadi sundari ।।

tataḥ siddhipradaṃ proktaṃ siddhādyantargato yadi ।
sādhyobhayasthitaṃ siddhaṃ madhyagaṃ taṃ vidhīyate ।।

siddhau sthānadvaye yatra hyarirmadhye vyavasthitaḥ ।
madhyasiddhipradaṃ taṃ tu hyarerduṣṭaṃ gaṇāmbike ।।

svasiddhavahalaṃ mantraṃ sādhyasaṃsthaṃ tu siddhidam ।
svasiddhaṃ bhayadaṃ śatruṃ siddhihīnaṃ vicārayet ।।

svasiddhaṃ śatrupārśvasthaṃ siddhidaṃ nātrasaṃśayaḥ ।
evaṃ vicārya mantraṃ tu śatrumādhyasvasiddhakaiḥ ।।

svasiddhaṃ yatra cāntaḥstha madhyagaṃ cottamaṃ smṛtam ।
tataḥ siddhyati deveśi sādhako nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

ityaṃśakaṃ vicāryādāvuktaṃ vīṇāśikhottare ।
vidhānaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yathoktamāgamāntare ।।

mātṛkāṃ prastaredādau kūṭaśaṇṭhavivarjitām ।
pūjayitvā vidhānena tadadho mantramālikhet ।।

saṃyogānāṃ tathā mātrā pṛthagajjālabhedataḥ ।
adhasthānnāma cālikhya mantrārṇa kramabhedataḥ ।।

lokaprasiddhaṃ yannāma janmanāmāthavāpi ca ।
(129b)   yatīnāṃ puṣpapātena guruṇā yatkṛtaṃ bhavet ।।

sādhyasādhakayorjñeyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ cāṃśakaṃ ca yat ।
siddhasādhyasvasiddhāni caturdhā paripaṭhyate ।।

nāmākṣaraistu mantrāṇāṃ mātṛkāntavisarpitam ।
mantrārṇaṃ gaṇayedyāvatsādhakaḥ siddhikāṃkṣikaḥ ।।

sarvasiddhaḥ prasiddhoto dṛṣṭvādṛṣṭaphalaṃ prati ।
sarvasādhya udāsīnassvasiddhassiddhido mataḥ ।।

tathāriḥ kṛtakaḥ prokto sādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ ।
ādisiddhontasiddhaśca sarvasiddhiphalapradaḥ ।।

sādhyādyanto bhavedyastu kṛcchrasādhyastu sammataḥ ।
ādyante tu sasiddho yaḥ kṣiprameva sa sidhyati ।।

ādyante tu ripuryatra savai sārayate kṣaṇāt ।
ādisiddhāntasādhyastu dviguṇena svasiddhadaḥ ।।

ādau svasiddhassādhyantaścaturguṇamapekṣate ।
ādyantayoryathā sādhyo madhye siddho yadā bhavet ।।

sādhyobhaye svasiddhānte japādhikyamapekṣate ।
arisaṃpuṭitassiddhassvasiddhastu tathaiva ca ।।

sarvanāśakaro jñeyassādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ ।
siddhāntaritasādhyo yaḥ svasiddhāntaritopi vā ।।

nirvighnaṃ siddhyate mantrassādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ ।
siddhenāntaritaḥ śatrussvasiddhenāthavāpi ca ।। ?

na ripurbhavate tatra kintu kṛcchramapekṣate ।
sādhyāntaritasiddhastu svasiddhāntaritopi vā ।
(130a)   atikṛcchrena siddhyeta varjanīyassa sarvadā ।।

ripuṇāntaritassiddhassvasiddhastu yadā bhavet ।
īdṛśe lakṣaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā dūrataḥ parivarjayet ।।

ripuṇā dūṣito mantro nedayastu kathaṃcana ।
siddhaṃ śaivasvasiddhaṃ cāpyekīkṛtya vidhānataḥ ।।

punaḥ sādhyaripū caiva guṇayeddaśabhistataḥ ।
guṇite tu dvidhā rāśau paścādbhāgaṃ haredatha ।।

siddharāśau tu vasvabhirbhānubhistvarirāśitaḥ ।
arirāśau tu yaśśeṣo māsāṃstāntu vinirdiśet ।।

siddharāśau tu yaśśeṣaḥ praharāste bhavanti ca ।
tatprahare tu tanmāse mantragrahaṇamācaret ।।

tatkālāhnikamiṣṭaṃ ca caruṃ prāśya samāhitaḥ ।
tanmāse siddhimāyāti sādhakastu na śaṃśayaḥ ।।

evaṃ mantrāṃśakaṃ jñātvā samyagjñātvātmaniścayam ।
mantrasyārghaḥ pradātavyaḥ yadīcchetsiddhimuttamām ।।

akacaṭatādyaivapo yaśāḥ pārthivāḥ smṛtāḥ ।
ā ai kha cha ṭha thādyairahāṣā vāyavā smṛtāḥ ।।

ī ū gha jha ḍha dhādyaiva bhabahānābhasāssmṛtāḥ ।
o au aṃ astu vargāntāstaijasāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ।।

catvāriṃśattathā pañca varṇā ekatra saṃkhyayā ।
gaṇastu navabhirjñeyo mātṛkāyāṃ na saṃśayaḥ ।।

yasya mantrasya pūrvārṇaṃ māhendraṃ dṛśyate kvacit ।
sādhakasya tathaiveha svakulaṃ tasya tadviduḥ ।।

(130b)
evameva ca vāyavyaṃ vāruṇaṃ nābhasaṃ tataḥ ।
jñātavyaṃ taijasaṃ vāpi mantraṃ pañcavidhaṃ śubham ।।

pārthive vāruṇamitamāgneyasyāpi mārutam ।
māhendra vāruṇābhyāṃ ca śatrutve mārutaḥ sthitaḥ ।।

āgneye vāruṇaḥ śatruḥ vāruṇe taijasaḥ tathā ।
sarveṣāmeva tatvānāṃ samānatvena nābhasaḥ ।।

parasparaviruddhānāṃ melāpaṃ pañcavaktrajam ।
varjayenmantravarṇānāṃ yadi syācchreyase matiḥ ।।

na kevalaṃ hi mantrāṇāṃ dehināṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ।
śatrujñātisutairsārairbandhusādhyahitāhitaiḥ ।।

kartavyassaṅgraho jñātvā dravyauṣadhirasāyanaiḥ ।
ekatripañcabījānāṃ dvātriṃśārṇe tathaiva ca ।।

saptadaśākṣare mantre naitatkāryaṃ parīkṣaṇaṃ ।
ekavīrā mahākūṭā śivaśaktyātmakāstu ye ।।

saurā baudhā hatāścaiva vaiṣṇavā nārasiṃhikāḥ ।
siddhasādhyavicāroyaṃ teṣāṃ pakṣe na vidyate ।।

catvāriṃśākṣarasyo vāmekaivākṣaravṛddhitaḥ ।
triṣaṣṭisaṅkhyayā prānte mantraṃ tatsarvakāmadam ।।

ājñāsiddhāstu ye mantrā yoginīnāṃ prasādataḥ ।
labdhā yepi ca te mantrāssarvakāmaphalapradāḥ ।।

sarvametatsamākhyātaṃ paścime mantralakṣaṇam ।
jñātvaivaṃ siddhyati kṣipraṃ mantrastasya na saṃśayaḥ ।।
(131a)
sādhakassātviko dhīrassarvaśāstravidhānavit ।
siddhakṣetraṃ samāsādya vakṣyamānaṃ yathepsitam ।।

svasahāyo vigatabhīḥ kuryālliṅgaparigraham ।
yajetpaścānmukhaṃ liṅgamalābhātprāṅmukhaṃ budhaḥ ।।

yasya kauvīravaktraṃ tu siddhyarthaṃ na praśasyate ।
uparyupariliṅgaṃ ca mukhaliṅgaṃ vivarjayet ।।

ṛṣibhiḥ sthāpitaṃ grāhyaṃ devairvā pūrvapūjitam ।
svayambhumathavā devaṃ spāṭikaṃ vā samarcayet ।।

raukmaṃ maṇimayaṃ vāpi chatrākāraṃ ca siddhidam । ?
kukuṭāṇḍanibhaṃ vāpi mantrasiddhipradāyakam ।।

sthāpitaṃ vidhivadvāpi daiśikena mahātmanā ।
tatrasthaṃ pūjayelliṅgaṃ sāṅgaṃ mantragaṇānvitam ।।

pūrvoktena vidhānena liṅgayogavidhau sadā ।
kṣetraveśmadṛḍhaṃ kuryātpūrvottaramukhaṃ śubham ।।

pratyagdvāramalābhena varjayeddakṣiṇāmukham ।
vidiṅmukhaṃ prayatnena varjayetsādhakottamaḥ ।।

madhyadvāraṃ bhavedvāpi vāstudvāranidarśanāt ।
svabhadraṃ maṅgalaṃ bhadraṃ vardhamānaṃ krameṇa tu ।।

pūrvādidikṣu kramaśo dvārāṇyetāni lakṣayet ।
devālayeṣu sarveṣu vahnirājagṛheṣu ca ।।

sthānānyetāni kāryāṇi siddhidāni viśeṣataḥ ।
dvātriṃśaddvārasaṃjñāstu proktā nakṣatranāmabhiḥ ।।

gṛhakāryeṣu kānteṣu sarvataivopayujyete ।
(131b)   tantrāntare vidhātavyaṃ dikṣu caiva śarāṣṭakam ।।

ūrdhvaśūlamadhaścakraṃ mahāstraparimantritam ।
bandhayetpañcaratnena chāgarajjvāthavā punaḥ ।।

khādiraiśca * {bhirvāpi} sarṣapairdikṣubandhanam ।
āsanaṃ tūlasaṃpūrṇaṃ mṛgacarmāvaguṇṭhitam ।।

kṛṣṇājinadharastatra yogapaṭṭākṣasūtradhṛt ।
dhūpapuṣpaphalairyuktaṃ tadveśma svadhayojjvalam ।।

haisīṃ kuryādakṣamālāṃ padmarāgamayīṃ ca vā ।
putrajīvakapadmākṣaissarvakarmasvaśasyate ।।

kuryānmaṇimayīṃ vātha spāṭikīṃ vā svasiddhidām ।
śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ kuryāttadardhaṃ pādameva vā ।।

madhye merustu kartavyastajjātīyothavānyajaḥ । ?
astrābhimantritāṃ kuryāddhyāyeddiptāṃ tu vahnivat ।।

adhobhāgena merostu satataṃ parivartayet ।
na merūparivarteta na ca śabdamayī bhavet ।।

ātmānaṃ devatāṃ caiva dīptametattrayaṃ sadā ।
abhyarcitaṃ tu gṛhṇīyādastreṇābhyukṣya sādhakaḥ ।।

ākarṣayetkaniṣṭhādi śāntikādiṣu karmasu ।
anāmikā tu vaśyādi madhyamākarṣaṇe smṛtā ।।

tarjanyākrūrakarmāṇi hyaṅguṣṭhenākhilāni tu ।
na vilambitasampaṣṭaṃ na cāmbukṛtasadrutam ।। ?

nāsaṅkhyaṃ na manobhrāntaṃ japaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ।
upāṃśurvācikaścaiva mānasastrividho japaḥ ।।

sakṛcchatānantaguṇaṃ phalameṣāṃ prakīrtitam ।
(132a)   pūrvottaramukhaṃ mantrī japaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ।।

mūlamantraṃ samuccārya mūlamantrasamanvitam ।
mantrākṣarāṇi ma * vacchaktau protāni bhāvayet ।।

tāṃ ca śaktiṃ pare vyomni paramāmṛtavṛṃhitām ।
tadantastho bhavenmantrassarvakāmaphalapradaḥ ।।

hṛtpadmakarṇikāntassthā nādaśaktiranāmayā ।
tatra saṃgrathitā mantrāstantau maṇigaṇā iva ।।

parāmṛtarasākrāntā snānovanto bhavanti hi ।
vidite tu pare tatve sarvajñe sarvage vyaye ।।

kiṅkaratvamupāyānti mantrā mantrādhipaissaha ।
praśāntāyāmayogena yadā mantraṃ japedbudhaḥ ।।

yogī sagaṇamadhyastho mṛtyuṃ jayati līlayā ।
nānyo mantro vinā nādaṃ nādo vāyu vidhīyate ।।

yā vāyorvāhinī śaktiścinmātrasparśarūpiṇī ।
sā mantraśaktiḥ kathitā na mantronyo japo na hi ।।

brahmā viṣṇustathā rudraṃ īśvaraśśiva eva ca ।
śivabhedāṣṭakaṃ yaśca vidveṣo bhairavastathā ।।

evamādyāstu ye mantrā śāmbhavāste prakīrtitāḥ ।
umādyāśśaktayaḥ proktā lakṣmī gaurī sarasvatī ।।

jayādyāścaiva raktādyāḥ kālāntakaraṇī tathā ।
evamādyā mahādevyaśśāktāstā dvijasattama ।।

dūtyaśca mātaraścaiva kiṅkaraśca purassarāḥ ।
piśācāśca tathā yakṣā āṇavāssaṃprakīrtitāḥ ।।


(132b)
gacchaṃstiṣṭhansvapañjāgransādhakaḥ kṛtaniścayaḥ ।
śaktisaṃsthitamantrātmā siddhibhāgbhavati dhruvam ।।

yasya mantrasya yā śaktistasthaṃ taṃ vicintayet ।
āṇavaḥ pārthive tatve tatrāsāvupalabhyate ।।

hṛtpadmakarṇikāntassthaṃ samudgaṃ sarvadehinām ।
jānīyāt sādhako nityamāṇavānāṃ tu kāraṇam ।।

tadūrdhvaṃ śāktamityāhuścittasambodhalakṣaṇam ।
hṛtpadmādyāvadūrdhvaṃ tu śaktitatvaṃ mahojjvalam ।।

raktāstatra sthitā mantrā nādāntaṃ yāvadeva hi ।
dvādaśāntāddhruvormadhye mūrdhni jyotīṃṣi caiva hi ।।

sūkṣmanādastathā bindau śaivā nityaṃ pratiṣṭhitāḥ ।
evaṃ mantrodayaḥ prokto japasyaiva vidhiḥ paraḥ ।।

yugasya rūpabhāvena japaṃ kuryādanukramam ।
kṛtāvadhītimātraṃ tu tretāyāṃ kalpacoditam ।।

dviguṇaṃ dvāpare proktaṃ japaṃ svaravarārcite ।
kalau daśaguṇaṃ devi japaṃ mantrī prasiddhyati ।।

homaṃ japānte tattulyamardhameva tu vācikam ।
daśabhāgaṃ śatāṃśaṃ vā jñātvā vibhavasātmanaḥ ।।

pañcāṅgikaistu kartavyaṃ tilairvāpyatha taṇḍulaiḥ ।
ājyena vā samidbhirvā gugguloṅgulikādibhiḥ ।।

puṣpairāraṇyakairvāpi śrīphalaiḥ kamalotpalaiḥ ।
nājaptvā sidhyate mantrī nāhutvā siddhibhāgbhavet ।।
(133a)
nāpūjyaṃ pūjyate loke krīḍādhyānādṛte kutaḥ ।
tapobhissidhyate mantrarvrataiścāndrāyaṇādibhiḥ ।।

kṛcchrātikṛcchracaryābhistulā puruṣacaryayā ।
evamuktaṃ tu mantrāṇāṃ sādhanaṃ pañcalakṣaṇam ।।

siddhiliṅgāni jāyante yasya siddhistu saṃmukhā ।
devatādarśanaṃ svapne tābhissaṃbhāṣaṇaṃ mahat ।।

bhavate cāntarikṣe ca candrādityau ca bhakṣayet ।
gaṅgāṃ vāpi pibet svapne samudraṃ vā mahānadam ।।

śiro vā chidyate yasya rudhireṇābhiṣicyate ।
rudhiraṃ vā pibet svapne gṛhṇīyādvā mahāvratam ।।

sevedvarāṅganāṃ vāpi vṛkṣāgramadhirohati ।
āru * * * * * * * * * * * * * *।।
(133b)
nijānandarasāsaktāścoditāḥ parameśinā ।
tatparvadinamākhyātaṃ sakṛtrimaphalapradam ।।

jñānayogavihīnopi kulaparvaṇi yoyajet ।
labhate sasvasaṃvittimityājñā pārameśvarī ।।

vīravrataṃ śaktilābhaḥ priyamelāpanaṃ tathā ।
māraṇoccāṭavidveṣavaśyākarṣaṇakarma yat ।।

tatsarvaṃ parvadivase svayaṃ tenaiva sidhyate ।
viśeṣapūjanaṃ kuryādvidhinā kulaparvaṇi ।।

puṣpadhūpopahārādyaiḥ bhakṣyabhojyairanekaśaḥ ।
toyavyomasthalodbhūtairāsiṣairāsavaistathā ।।

yāmalena vidhānena pūjayet parameśvaram ।
kṛṣṇāyāṃ mārgaśīrṣasya navamyāṃ rajanīmukhe ।।

ādityottaraphalguṇyaiḥ pūrvārdhapraharadvayam ।
pauṣamāsanavamyāṃ ca kṛṣṇāyāmardharātragam ।।

citrācandramasoryoge dvitīyaṃ parva pārvati ।
pūrṇāyāṃ pañcadaśyāṃ ca māghasyārdhaniśāgame ।।

yoge maghābṛhaspatyostṛtīyaṃ parva kaulikam ।
tiṣyacandramasoryoge dvādaśyāṃ phālguṇasya ca ।।

caturthaṃ parva kathitaṃ nabhomadhyagate ravau ।
candre uttaraphālgunyā dinamadhyagate ravau ।।

caitraśuklatrayodaśyāṃ pañcamaṃ parva pārvati ।
vaiṣākhamāsasyāṣṭamyāṃ budhaśravaṇasaṃgame ।।

madhyāhna kṛṣṇapakṣe ca ṣaṣṭhaṃ parva varānane ।
(134a)    jyeṣṭhamāsyasite pakṣe navamyāṃ madhyavāsare ।।

candravāruṇayoryoge saptamaṃ parva pārvati ।
āṣāḍhamāsapratipadyarke madhyāhnage site ।।

mūlabhāskarayoryoge parvāṣṭa samudāhṛtam ।
śrāvaṇe rohiṇīśukrayoge caikādaśehani ।।

kṛṣṇapakṣe prabhāte ca navamaṃ parva bhāmini ।
viṣākhājīvasaṃyoge ṣaṣṭhyāṃ bhādrapade site ।।

dinanāthodaye devi daśamaṃ parva kaulikam ।
yā śuklā navamī māsi bhavedāśvayuji priye ।।

tasyāṃ bhagrahanakṣatravelākālo na gaṇyate ।
tadā vidrāviṇī devī bhaginī vīravatsalā ।।

māteva siddhikāmānāṃ śaśvatsiddhiṃ prayacchati ।
etadekādaśaṃ parva kulasiddhimahodayam ।।

kārtike māsi śuklāyāṃ navamyāṃ rajanīmukhe ।
śravaṇendusamāpattau dvādaśaṃ parikīrtitam ।।

nakṣatraṃ ca graho velā divasaṃ pakṣameva ca ।
pañcarātramidaṃ prāpya kaulikīṃ siddhimāvahet ।।

pañcasveteṣu divaso mukhyastamanucintayet ।
viśeṣaparvāṇyuktāni sāmānyānyatha me śṛṇu ।।

māsasādhyaṃ caturthaṃ tu pañcamaṃ navamaṃ tathā ।
caturdaśaṃ dvādaśaṃ ca pakṣayorubhayorapi ।।


(134b)
gurvātmanorjanmadinaṃ saṃskāradinameva ca ।
śrāddhamāpatpratīkāro devatādbhutadarśanaṃ ।।

laukikotsavasaṅkrānti svapnājñā lābha eva ca ।
rahasyaśāstravyākhyāyāmārambhāntaranirgamāḥ ।।

gurorvā guruvargasya svagṛhāgamanaṃ tathā ।
jñānalābhaśśāstralābhassamayācāraniṣṭhibhiḥ ।।

yogini melanaṃ svāṃśa santānādyaiśca saṃgatiḥ ।
iti viṃśatidhā proktaṃ parvasāmānyalakṣaṇam ।।

viśeṣavadvṛttiratra śasyate kulayoginām ।
kṛtvā parvavidhiṃ kuryānmūrtiyāgaṃ yathāvidhi ।।

mūrtiyāgāsambhave ca kumārīreva tarpayet ।
stanapuṣpavatīrnaiva navyaṅgānāmanoramāḥ ।।

kulayogeṣu sarveṣu kalāḍhyaḥ kulapūruṣaḥ ।
yenātmā jñāyate devi sodhikārī sa siddhibhāk ।।

itthaṃ cakreśvaramate proktaḥ parvavidhirdvidhā ।
kulasiddhāntabhedena sādhakānāṃ yathāruci ।।

nityapūjādyapūrṇatvaṃ pūrayetsarvapūjayā ।
pavitravidhinātrāpyapūrṇatvaṃ * prapūrayet ।।

vittābhāve prakurvīta kāśairapi kuśairapi ।
sati vitte na catkuryānnarakavyādhibhā kutaḥ ।।

pavitrakavilope tu prāyaścittaṃ japetsadā ।
ucchuddhaḥ sanpunaḥ kuryādityājñā parameśituḥ ।। ?
(135a)
vṛthā dīkṣā vṛthā jñānaṃ vṛthā ca gurusevanam ।
pavitrakaṃ ca na cetkuryātkuryāccetsaphalaṃ tu tat ।।

।। śrīdevyu ।।

pavitrārohaṇaṃ deva śrotumicchāmi tatvataḥ ।
tadutpattividhānaṃ ca saṃkṣepādvada śūlabhṛt । ?

।। bhairavaḥ ।।

sādhu sādhu mahāprājñe lokānugrahakāriṇi ।
pavitraṃ nāma yatproktaṃ dharmakarmārthasādhanam ।।

devadānavagandharvapiśācoragakinnaraiḥ ।
bhūtaiḥ ṛṣigaṇaiścānyaiḥ kṣetrapālādirākṣasaiḥ ।।

pavitraṃ tu kṛtaṃ sarvaṃ pavitrārthaṃ varānane ।
ācāryaiḥ sādhakairvāpi samayasthaipi putrakaiḥ ।।

dīkṣito nityanirato yo na kuryāt pavitrakam ।
saṃvatsarakṛtāṃ pūjāṃ vighnastasya hareddhruvam ।।

pūjāsaṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ tu pavitraṃ vihitaṃ mayā ।
sthaṇḍile maṇḍale vāpi vidadhīta pavitrakam ।।

kanīyānvāsukerbhrātā tasya nāma pavitrakam ।
tenāhaṃ toṣitaṃ devi tapasogreṇa bhaktitaḥ ।।

tata uktaṃ mayā devi varamicchasva pannaga ।
saṃvatsarakṛtā pūjā japahomakriyādikā ।।

tasmai dattā mayā devi yo na kuryāt pavitrakam ।
āṣāḍhe śrāvaṇe vāpi kuryā - - padepi vā ।।

āṣāḍhe cottamaṃ proktaṃ śrāvaṇe madhyamaṃ smṛtaṃ ।
(135b)   nabhasye kanyasaṃ māsi pavitraṃ parikīrtitam ।।

anyathā naiva kartavyaṃ yadīcchecchreya ātmanaḥ ।
kṛtau yuge hemamayaṃ tretāyāṃ raupyasambhavam ।।

dvāpare tāmrajaṃ kuryāt kalau karpāsasūtrajam ।
sudṛḍhaṃ sunika(knaṃ) ca kartitaṃ dvijakanyayā ।।

triguṇaṃ triguṇīkṛtya sūtraṃ kuryāt pavitrakam ।
etena kṣālayet sūtraṃ vāmadevena granthayet ।।

aghoreṇa tu saṃśodhya puruṣeṇāpi rañjayet ।
sthāpayecca tadiśāne naivametatkrameṇa tu ।।

navatantuṣu ye devāstanme nigadataśśṛṇu ।
oṅkāraḥ prathamo devi dvitīyaḥ somasajñakaḥ ।।

tṛtīyo vahnidaivatyaścaturtho brahmasajñakaḥ ।
pañcamo nāgadaivatyaṣṣaṣṭhaścaiva śikhidhvajaḥ ।।

saptamassūryadaivatyo hṛṣṭastu sadāśivaḥ ।
navame sarvadevāśca ityete navatantavaḥ ।।

aṣṭottaraśataṃ kuryāt pañcāśatpañcaviṃśatiḥ ।
uttamaṃ madhyamaṃ proktaṃ kanyasaṃ ca tataḥ param ।।

siddhimuktipradaṃ proktamaṣṭottaraśataṃ tu yat ।
pañcāsatsiddhidaṃ proktamāyurlābhāya suvrate ।।

pavitrārthaṃ tu kartavyaṃ pañcaviṃśatisaṅkhyayā ।
granthayo daśa dātavyā antaraṃ caturaṅgulam ।।

prathamaśca kriyā nāma dvitīya pauruṣī smṛtā ।
tṛtīyastu bhavedvīrā caturthaścāparājitā ।।
(136a)
pañcamastu jayā nāma ṣaṣṭhastu vijayā tathā ।
ajitā saptamo jñeyo hyaṣṭamastu sadāśivaḥ ।।

manonmanīśca navamo daśamaḥ sarvatomukhī ।
etāni granthināmāni samāsāt kathitāni tu ।।

atha liṅgasya mānena kartavyaṃ tu pavitrakam ।
ajāteṣṭau kalā jñeyā vāmadeve trayodaśa ।।

aghore ca kalā aṣṭau catasraḥ puruṣe kalāḥ ।
īśāne ca kalāḥ pañca sarva evamudāhṛtāḥ ।।

aṣṭātriṃśat kalā devi yo jānīte pavitrake ।
athavā nava tatvāstu granthaya * varānane ।।

aṅguladvayamānena hyathavaikāṅgulena tu ।
kuṅkumena viliptastu gairikeṇa haridrayā ।।

ātmavittānusāreṇa rañjanīyo vipaścitā ।
pakṣadvaye saptamī yā athavāpi trayodaśī ।।

adhivāsapavitraṃ tu tatrāropyaṃ yathāvidhi ।
jalādinā tataḥ snātvā mantrasannaddhavigrahaḥ ।।

payodadhighṛtaiḥ liṅgaṃ snāpayitvā virūkṣayet ।
yavagodhūmajaiścūrṇaiḥ kaṣāyairgandhayojitaiḥ ।।

snāpayitvā vilimpeta karpūrāgurucandanaiḥ ।
pūjayettadvidhānena śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā ।।

puṇyāhajayaśabdaiśca śaṅkhavāditrakaiśśubhaiḥ ।
mantrapūtakaro devi - - nmantrī pavitrakam ।।

sadyo vāmastathā ghoraḥ puruṣa īśa eva ca ।
(136b)   pañcaitāni tu brahmāṇi tedyai dadyāt pavitrakam ।।

dantakāṣṭhaṃ tu pūrveṇa mṛttikā paścimena tu ।
bhasmāpi dakṣiṇe sthāpya kṣipedudakamuttare ।।

puṣpaṃ caiva tathaiśānyāmāgneyāṃ rocanāṃ nyaset ।
aiśānyāṃ candanaṃ nyasya ceśānyā sa guruṃ kṣipet ।।

catussamaṃ ca vāyavyāṃ sarvāṅgeṣu vinikṣipet ।
piṇḍikāveṣṭanaṃ kuryāt prasādasya maṭhasya ca ।।

abhāve jalabhāṇḍeṣu cakrādinkiraṇaissaha ।
caṇḍīśaṃ ca mahākālaṃ bhṛṅgīśaṃ ca gaṇādhipam ।।

vṛṣabhaṃ ṣaṇmukhaṃ cāpi nandirudraṃ tathaiva ca ।।
viṣṇuṃ tathā devīṃ sarvatassodhivāsayet ।
anyāśca devatā * * * * * ṇena tuṣṭayet ।।

dikpālakādīnsaṃpūjya mantrī dadyā * * * * ।
gāyanairnartakaissārdhaṃ yāgaṃ tu racayecchubham ।।

* * * * * * devi pūjāṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ ।
kṛtvā vittānusāreṇa dadyāddevi pavitrakam ।।

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā hyaghorasya mṛgekṣaṇe ।
liṅgamādau tathātmānaṃ prāsādaṃ caiva veṣṭayet ।।

janaiḥ māheśvaraissārdhaṃ dīkṣitaiḥ prokṣitairapi ।
evaṃ sarveṣu deveṣu kartavyaṃ vai pavitrakam ।।

yatra ye saṃsthitā mantrāstāṃstatraiva niyojayet ।
ācāryeṇa tu kartavyaṃ sarvadaiva pavitrakam ।।

evaṃ vyāptimavijñāya yastu kuryāt pavitrakam ।
(137a)   phalaṃ na kiñcidāpnoti vṛthā tasya pariśramaḥ ।।

athavā dhanalobhena yo na kuryāt pavitrakam ।
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ yāvadābhūmisaṃplavaṃ ।।

tadante ca punardevi tiryagyoniśataṃ vrajet ।
tuṣānsukhaṇḍayenmūḍho suṣṭināhanti vai nabhaḥ ।।

niṣphalaṃ jāyate sarvaṃ yo na lobhāt samācaret ।
vidhinā mantrayuktena pavitreṇa varānane ।।

sarvāssiddhīravāpnoti śivalokaṃ ca gacchati ।
saptasāgaraparyantaṃ pṛthivīṃ karibhūṣitām ।।

yo dadāti dvijātibhyo rāhugraste divākare ।
tattulyaṃ phalamāpnoti vidhiyukte pavitrake ।।

sākaṃ kulaikaviṃśatyā śivalokamavāpnuyāt ।
gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt putrake sādhakepi ca ।।

tapaśvinetha bhaktāya śivabhakte dvijepi ca ।
yo nindati śivaṃ śāstraṃ guruṃ caiva tapasvinam ।।

tasmai dānaṃ na dātavyaṃ taddvayaṃ narakaṃ vrajet ।
sarvasvaṃ gurave dadyāt praṇipatya kṣamāpayet ।।

tena tuṣṭena deveśi prāpyate paramaṃ padam ।
na gurossadṛśī mātā na pitā na ca bāndhavāḥ ।।

tārayeddhelayā martyānghorātsaṃsārabandhanāt ।
ācāryasya śivajñāne mantrasya ca śivasya ca ।।

nānātvaṃ yodhigaccheta vidveṣaṃ yaḥ karoti vā ।
tasmai dattaṃ ca bhuktaṃ ca sarvaṃ bhavati niṣphalam ।।
(137b)
evaṃ jñātvā mahāprājñāssadguruṃ vai samāśrayet ।
ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā pañcarātramathāpi vā ।।

athavā saptarātraṃ ca gurorvāpi yadṛcchayā ।
pūjāṃ kṣamāpayeddevaṃ saṃhṛtya ca pavitrakam ।।

homayedastramantreṇa śivamantreṇa mantravit ।
pavitre saṃhṛte devi guruṃ caiva kṣamāpayet ।।

tasmai ca dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādyathāśakti varānane ।
kandamūlaphalairvāpi kartavyaṃ gurupūjanam ।।

nimitteṣu ca sarveṣu saṅkrāntigrahaṇeṣu ca ।
(aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ) aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ pakṣayorubhayorapi ।।

ācāryo mantriṇā pūjyo bhojanīyaḥ prayatnataḥ ।
tena bhuktena bhavati mayā bhuktaṃ sureśvari ।।

pitarāstasya nṛtyanti tṛptāssvarge vrajanti ca ।
guroścenna samīpasthastatrādau pañcayona * ।।

ānetavyaḥ prayatnena na kuryāttena nigraham ।
taṃ vā tuṣṭena deveśi labhate śāśvataṃ padam ।।

evaṃ jñātvā yadā kaścidavajñāṃ kurute naraḥ ।
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ punarjanma yato na hi ।।

pavitrārohaṇamidaṃ vāmadevyāṃ śivoditam ।
caturviṃśatisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāmathocyate ।।

viśeṣotra yathoddiṣṭaśśrīmatā somaśambhunā ।
sarvapūjāvidhiśchidrapūraṇāya pavitrakam ।।

kartavyamanyathā mantrī siddhibhraṃśamavāpnuyāt ।
āṣāḍhādicaturdaśyāmatha śrāvaṇabhādrayoḥ ।।
(138a)
sitāsitāsu kartavyaṃ caturdaśyaṣṭamīṣu ca ।
eteṣu triṣu māseṣu jyeṣṭhamadhyādhamaṃ kramāt ।।

śambhoḥ pavitramuddiṣṭamanyathā doṣakārakam ।
āṣāḍhādiṣu triṣveva supta eva janārdanaḥ ।।

pavitraṃ vidadhītaiva sautraso niyamastridhā । ?
pratyahaṃ na vidheyatvānnāyaṃ nityavidhirmataḥ ।।

pavitraṃ kālanaiyatyānnaimittikamidaṃ smṛtam ।
vighnāpahāramūrtyādi phalamasyopagīyate ।।

mohasurottaressābhirmṛṣṭaṃ cedaṃ śivāgame ।
tasmānnaimittikaṃ nāma pavitrakamidaṃ sphuṭam ।।

nityaṃ tu nityapūjāyāṃ mantrapuṣpaphalādibhiḥ ।
pavitramanyakālepi pratyahaṃ parikalpayet ।।

vahnibrahmāmbhokebhāsyanāgaskandārkaśūlinām ।
durgāyametsugovinda smaraśambhu sudhā bhujām ।।

amīṣāṃ sarvadevānāṃ vidhātavyaṃ pavitrakam ।
pratipādassamārabhya yāvatpañcadaśīṃ kramāt ।।

sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ kṛtādiṣu yathākramaṃ ।
kalau karpāsajaṃ cāpi yathāśakti pavitrakam ।।

kartitaṃ dvijakanyābhirdviguṇaṃ triguṇīkṛtaṃ ।
dhautaṃ śuṣkaṃ śubhraṃ sūtramanyadapyupayujyate ।।

praṇavaścandramā vahni brahmā nāgaḥ śikhī raviḥ ।
sādākhyassarvadevāṃśca krameṇa navaturtuṣu ।।

aṣṭottaraśatenaikaṃ sūtrāṇāṃ syāt pavitrakam ।
(138b)   itthaṃ trīṇi tadardhena viṃśatyāvasa saptayā ।।

ekāśītyathavāsūtraistriṃśatā vāṣṭayuktayā ।
pañcāśatāya kartavyaṃ tulya - - ntarālakam ।।

dvādaśāṅgulamānāni vyāsādaṣṭāṅgulāni ca ।
liṅgavistāramānāni caturaṅgulakāni vā ।।

tathaiva piṇḍikāsparśidattataṃ sārvadaivatam ।
gaṅgāvatārakaṃ kāryaṃ pavitramatisundaram ।।

dhautaṃ sūtramajātena grathitaṃ guhyakena tu ।
śodhitaṃ bahurūpeṇa vaktramantreṇa rañjayet ।।

raktacandanakāśmīra kastūrī candrarocanā ।
haridrāgairikaṃ caiṣāṃ rañjedekatamena tat ।।

granthayo daśa kartavyā athavā tatvasaṃkhyayā ।
antaraṃ vā yathāśobhamekadvicaturaṅgulam ।।

prakṛtiḥ pauruṣī bījā caturthītyaparājitā ।
jayantī vijayā ṣaṣṭhī ajitātha sadāśivā ।।

manmonī tataḥ proktā daśamī sarvatomukhī ।
adhikā yathā śobhāḥ kartavyā grantha(ya)ssamāḥ ।।

ratnaje sthaṇḍile vāṇe cale rohe svayambhuvi ।
yathocitaṃ pavitrāṇāmekaṃ kāryaṃ yathāruci ।।

caṇḍīśa ravi vahnīnāṃ śivavatsūtrasaṃpadi ।
anyathaikaikameteṣāṃ vidadhīta pavitrakam ।।

nijamūrtau gaṇādhīśe gurupādeṣu pustake ।
* * * * * * * radikpālakalaśādiṣu ।।
ekādinavahastāntaṃ liṅgānāṃ syātpavitrakam ।
(139a)   aṣṭāviṃśatito vṛndhyā daśabhirdaśabhiḥ kramāt ।।

dvyaṅgulādyantarā sūtrakramādekā(ṅgulā)ntarāḥ ।
granthayo mānasapyevaṃ liṅgavistārasa - -  ।।

saptamyāṃ vā trayodaśyāṃ kṛtanityakriyaśśuciḥ ।
bhūṣayetpaṭṭavastrādyaissayāhne yāgamandiram ।।

kṛtvā naimittikīṃ sandhyāṃ viśeṣeṇa tu tarpaṇam ।
parigṛhīte bhūbhāge sūtrite sūryamarcayet ।।

ācamya sakalīkṛtya praṇavārghakaro guruḥ ।
dvārānyastrennāsaṃprokṣya pūrvādikramaśorcayet ।।

iti pratidvāraśākhāṃ dvau dvau saṃpūjayed gaṇau ।
nityavaddvārapālādīnabhyarcya vidhinākṣaram ।।

praviśya paścimadvāre vāstunāthaṃ prapūjayet ।
bhūtaśuddhiṃ viśeṣārghaṃ svaśiraḥ prokṣaṇādikam ।।

vidhāya yajñasaṃbhāraṃ pūrvavat parimantrayet ।
āpaḥ kṣīraṃ kuśāgrāṇi taṇḍulāssumanāstilā ।।

arghoṣṭāṅga iti prokto ya vai ssiddhārthakaissaha । ?
darbhadūrvākṣataiḥ puṣpaiścandanena hṛdādibhiḥ ।।

śivahastaṃ vidhāyetthaṃ * * * * * * * * ।
śivo amādi sarvajño samayajñe pradhānatā ।।

atya * bhāvayedevaṃ jñānakhaḍgadharo guruḥ ।
nirṛterdeśamāsādya prakṣipedudagānanaḥ ।।

arghāmbu pañcagavyaṃ ca samantānmakhamaṇḍale ।
catuṣpathādyaiḥ saṃskārairvīkṣaṇādyaissusaṃskṛtaiḥ ।।
(139b)
nikṣipya vikirāṃstatra kuśakūrcopasaṃharet ।
tāni śaśiśivārdhānyāmāsanāyopakalpayet ।। ?

nirṛterdhātugīrvāṇāndvāre lakṣmīṃ ca pūjayet ।
paścimābhimukhaṃ kumbhaṃ sarvadhānyoparisthitam ।।

praṇavena vṛṣārūḍhaṃ siṃhasthāṃ vardhanīṃ tataḥ ।
kumbhe sāṅgaṃ śivaṃ devaṃ vardhanyāmastramarcayet ।।

dikṣu śakrādidikpālānviṣṇubrahmāvasānakam ।
vardhanīṃ samyagādāya ghaṭapṛṣṭhānugāminīm ।।

śivājñāṃ śrāvayenmantrī pūrvādīśānagocaram ।
avicchinnapayodhārāṃ mūlamantramudīrayan ।।

samantādbhrāmayedenāṃ rakṣārthaṃ śastrarūpiṇīṃ ।
pūrvaṃ kalaśamāropya śastāluṃ tasya vāmataḥ ।।

{samagrāsanake kumbhe} yajeddevaṃ varānane ।
vardhanyāṃ praṇavasthāyāṃmāyudhaṃ tanudvayoḥ ।।

bhagaliṅgasamāyogaṃ vidadhyālliṅgamudrayā ।
kumbhe nidhāya bodhāsiṃ mūlamantrajapaṃ tataḥ ।।

taddaśāṃśena vardhanyāṃ rakṣa vijñāpayediti ।
gaṇeśaṃ vāyudigbhāge pūjayitvā caturbhujam ।।

padminīpatrasaṃkāśaṃ sāyudhaṃ vighnavāraṇam ।
pañcāmṛtādibhirdevaṃ snāpayitvā vidhānataḥ ।।

yajetpaṃcopacāreṇa pūrvoktavidhinā tataḥ ।
kṛtvā kuṇḍādisaṃskārānabhyarcya śiva - vakam ।।
(140a)
vidhivaccarukaṃ kṛtvā saṃpātahutiśodhitam । ?
devāgnyātmavibhedena rurdhyānnaṃ vibhajetridhā ।।

datvā bhāgaṃ śivāgnibhyāṃ saṃrakṣedbhāgamātmani ।
devaṃ ca kṣīravṛkṣotthaṃ dvādaśāṅgulamāyataṃ ।।

nareṇa varmaṇā vāpi pūrvato dantadhāvanam ।
dakṣiṇe bhasma dātavyaṃ rūpiṇā śikhayāthavā ।।

mṛttikā paścime deyā sadyojātena vā hṛdā ।
uttare devadevasya dadyādāmalakīphalam ।।

jalaṃ ca vāmadevena śirasā vāpi pāvanam ।
aiśānyāmīśamantreṇa mūlamantreṇa vā jalam ।।

sugandhi pañcagavyaṃ ca palāśapuṭasaṃsthitam ।
aiśānyāṃ kusumaṃ dadyādāgneyyāṃ diśi rocanām ।। ?

aguruṃ nairṛtāśāyāṃ vāyavyāṃ ca catussamaṃ ।
homadravyāṇi sarvāṇi ajātena kuśaissaha ।।

daṇḍākṣasūtrakaupīnabhikṣāpātrāṇi rūpiṇā ।
añjanaṃ kuṅkumaṃ tailaṃ śalākāṃ keśaśodhanīṃ ।।

tāmbūlaṃ darpaṇaṃ dadyāduttare rocanāmapi ।
āsanaṃ pāduke pātraṃ yogapaṭṭātapatrakam ।।

aiśānyāmīśamantreṇa dadyādīśāya tuṣṭaye ।
pūrvasyāṃ carukaṃ sājyāṃ māntragandhādikaṃ nare ।।

yadasampannameteṣāṃ manasā tatprakalpayet ।
pavitrāṇi samādāya prokṣitānyastravāriṇā ।।
(140b)
saṃhitāmantrapūtāni nītvā pāvṛkasannidhim ।
kṛṣṇājinādinācchādya smaransaṃvatsarātmakaṃ ।।

sākṣiṇaṃ sarvakṛtyānāṃ goptāraṃ śivamavyayam ।
sveti heti prayogena mantrasaṃhitayā punaḥ ।।

śodhayecca pavitrāṇi varaṇāmekaviṃśatim ।
yāgadhāmavṛṣasthānaprāsādānatha saṃśrayan ।।

kārakau pañcasūtreṇa varmaṇā pariveṣṭayet ।
pūjitāya(va)sūryāya datvā gandhapavitrakam ।।

praṇipatya samācamya kṛtanyāsavratādikaḥ ।
nandyādidvārapālebhyo dadyāt kuśapavitrakam ।।

praviśya vāstunāthāya brahmaṇepi dadīta tat ।
sāstrebhyo lokapālebhyaḥ pavitrāṇi svanāmabhiḥ ।।

śivakumbhāya vārdhānyai dadyāllambho - rāya ca ।
vighnanāyakamabhyarcya gurupādāmbujau tataḥ ।।

svātmanaṃ pavitreṇa gandhapūrveṇa bhūṣayet ।
atha sarvauṣadhīliptaṃ mānātmānaṃ vivarjitam ।।

dhūpitaṃ puṣpadhūpāḍhyamekagranthyalpatantukam ।
caturvargaphalopāyasāmantraṇapavitrakam ।।

vidhāyāñjalimadhyastha sāmantraṇapadaṃ paṭhet ।

। tadyathā ।

oṃ samastavidhicchidrapūraṇeśamakhaṃ prati ।
prabho āmantrayāmi tvāṃ tvadicchāvāptakāraka ।।

tatsiddhimanujānīhi ye jātaścidacitpade ।
(141a)   sarvathā sarvadaśambhau namastripta prasīda me ।।

। athavā ।

āmantritosi deveśa saha devyā gaṇeśvaraiḥ ।
mantraiśca lokapālaiśca sahitāḥ parivārakaiḥ ।।

nivedayāmyahaṃ tubhyaṃ prabhāte tu pavitrakam ।
niyamaṃ ca kariṣyāmi parameśa tavājñayā ।।

ityevaṃ devamāmantrya recakenāmṛtīkṛtam ।
śivāntaṃ mūlamuccārya tacchivāya nivedayet ।।

japaṃ stotraṃ praṇāmaṃ ca kṛtvā śambhuṃ kṣamāpayet ।
hutvā carospatīyāśaṃ taddadīta śivāgnaye ।।

tato diṅnāmabhedena sarvadikṣu bahirvalim ।
dadyādanye tu vāyavyāṃ kṣetrapālavaliṃ jaguḥ ।।

samācamya vidhicchidrapūrakaṃ homamācaret ।
pūrṇāvyāhṛtihomaṃ ca kṛtvā rundhīta pāvanam ।। ?

tato dadyādvidhānena āhutīnāṃ catuṣṭayam ।
agnau kuṇḍārcitaṃ devaṃ maṇḍalābhyarcitaṃ śive ।।

nāḍisandhānarūpeṇa vidhinā yojayettataḥ ।
patre vaṃśādijopātra vinyasyāstramakaṅkaṭam ।।

hṛdaye ca tatastatra pavitrāṇyadhiropayet ।
kalābhirvāṣaruṅgena mantrairvā brahmavācakaiḥ ।।

athavā mūlamantreṇa pavitrāṇyabhimantrayet ।
hṛdayaṃ kavacaṃ śastraṃ teṣāmupari yojayet ।।

ityevaṃ saṃpuṭīkṛtya vidadyādanyavastunā ।
(141b)   saṃveṣṭya varmaṇāsūtraiḥ pūjayitvāṅgasambhāraiḥ (śambadhe) ।।

rakṣārthaṃ jagadīśāya bhaktinamrassamarpayet ।
pūjite puṣpadhūpādyairdatvā siddhāntapustake ।।

guroḥ pādāntikaṃ natvā bhaktyā dadyāt pavitrakam ।
nirgatya vāhirācamya gomaye maṇḍalatraye ।।

pañcagavyaṃ caruṃ dantadhāvanaṃ ca kramādbhajet ।
ādānto mantrasannaddhaḥ kṛtasaṃgītajāgaraḥ ।।

svapadena smarannīśaṃ bubhukṣurdarbhasaṃstare ।
anenaikaprakāreṇa mumukṣurapi saṃviśet ।।

kevalaṃ bhasmamayyāyāṃ sopavāsassamāhitaḥ ।
atha prātassamutthāya kṛtasnānassamāhitaḥ ।।

kṛtasandhyārcano mantrī praviśyamakhamaṇḍapam ।
samādāya pavitrāṇi avisarjitadaivataḥ ।।

aiśānyāṃ bhājane śuddhe sthāpayet kṛtamaṇḍale ।
tato visarjya deveśaṃ nirmālyamapanīya ca ।।

pūrvavadbhūtale śuddhe kṛtvā nityāhutidvayam ।
ādityadvāradikpālakumbhe sthālau śivānale ।।

naimittikīṃ suvistārāṃ kuryāt pūjāṃ viśeṣataḥ ।
mantrāṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ prāyaścittahomaṃ śarāṇunā ।।

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā dadyāt pūrṇāhutiṃ śanaiḥ ।
pavitraṃ bhānave dattvā samācamya dadīta tat ।। ?

dvārapālādidikpāla kumbhavardhanikāsu ca ।
sannidhāne tataśśambhorupaviśya nijāsane ।।
(142a)
pavitramātmane dadyādgaṇāya gurupaṅktaye ।
pavitrakamathādāya kiṃcit kalaśasaṃmukhaḥ ।।

smarankālātmakaṃ devaṃ prabruyāditi śaṅkaram ।
oṃkālātmane tvayā deva yaddṛṣṭemāmake vidhau ।।

kṛtaṃ kliṣṭaṃ samutsṛṣṭaṃ dhṛtaṃ guptaṃ ca yatkṛtam ।
sarvātmanāsunā śambho pavitreṇa tvadicchayā ।।

oṃ pūraya pūraya makhavrataṃ niyameśvarāya svāhā ।
ātmatatve prakṛtyante pālite padmayoninā ।
mūlaṃ māyāntamuccārya pavitreṇārcayecchivam ।।

vidyātatve ca vidyānte viṣṇukāraṇapālite ।
īśvarāntaṃ samuccārya pavitramadhiropayet ।।

śivānte śivatatvetha rudrakāraṇapālite ।
śivāntaṃ mantramuccārya tasmai deyaṃ pavitrakam ।।

sarvakāraṇapālyeṣu sarvatatveṣu suvrataḥ ।
mūlaṃ layāntamuccārya datvā gaṅgāvatārakam ।।

puṣpāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā bhaktyā vijñāpayecchivam ।
tvaṃ gatissarvabhūtānāṃ saṃsthitastvaṃ carācare ।।

antaścāreṇa bhūtānāṃ draṣṭā tvaṃ parameśvara ।
karmaṇā manasā vācā tvatto nānyā gatirmama ।।

mantrahīnaṃ kriyāhīnaṃ dravyahīnaṃ ca yatkṛtam ।
japahomārcanāhīnaṃ kṛtaṃ nityaṃ mayā tava ।।

akṛtaṃ vākyahīnaṃ ca tatpūraya maheśvara ।
(142b)   svapūtastvaṃ maheśāna pavitraṃ pāpanāśana ।।

tvayā pavitritaṃ sarvaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅghamam ।
khaṇḍitaṃ yanmayā deva vrataṃ vaikalyayogataḥ ।।

ekībhavatu tatsarvaṃ tvadājñāsūtragumphitam ।
japaṃ nivedya devasya bhaktyā stotraṃ vidhāya ca ।।

praṇasyācalacittena santuṣṭaṃ bhāvayecchivam ।
caturastrīnatha dvau vā māsaṃ māsārdhameva vā ।। ?

saptāhaṃ pañcarātraṃ tryahamekāhameva vā ।
guruṇā vā yathoddiṣṭaṃ gṛhītvā niyamaṃ tathā ।।

praṇamya kṣamayitveśaṃ gatvā kuṇḍantikaṃ vratī ।
pāvakasthe śivepyevaṃ pavitrāṇāṃ catuṣṭayam ।।

dikpatibhyastato datvā svapavitraṃ bahirbalim ।
siddhāntapustake dadyāt svapramāṇaṃ pavitrakam ।।

hutveti vyāhṛtīrdadyāccaturāhutayo yathā ।
guruśśiva iryārcyotha vastravittavibhūṣaṇaiḥ ।।

tuṣṭena gurunāthena tuṣṭo bhavati śaṅkaraḥ ।
samagraṃ saphalaṃ tasya karmakāṇḍādivārṣikam ।।

yasya tuṣṭo gurussamyagityāha parameśvaraḥ ।
itthaṃ guroḥ samāropya hṛdālambi pavitrakam ।।

liṅgino bhojayedbhaktyā dvijāndhakṛpaṇānapi ।
vastrairdakṣiṇayā śaktyā pūjayitvā vadediti ।।

dānenānena deveśaḥ prīyatāṃ me sadāśivaḥ ।
(143a)   prātaḥ snānādikaṃ kṛtvā pavitrāṇyāha(redbudhaḥ) ।।

aṣṭapuṣpikayā devaṃ pūjayitvā kṣamāpayet ।
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kṛtvā vistareṇa yathā purā ।।

pavitrāṇi samāropya praṇamyāgnau śivaṃ yajet ।
prāyaścittaṃ tatostreṇa kṛtvā pūrṇāhutiṃ yajet ।।

muktikāmotha devāya kuryāt karmasamarpaṇaṃ ।
tvatprasādena me karma phalaṃ sādhakamastviti ।।

bhuktikāmastu karmedaṃ māstu me nātha bandhakam ।
vahnisthaṃ nābhiyogena śivaṃ saṃyojayecchive ।।

hṛdi nyasyāṇusaṅghātaṃ pāvakaṃ ca visarjayet ।
samācamya praviśyāntaḥ kumbhālugataśambharān ।।

śive saṃyojya sākṣepaṃ kṣamasveti visarjayet ।
visṛjya lokapālādīnādāyeśātpavitrakam ।।

sati caṇḍeśvare pūjāṃ kṛtvā datvā pavitrakam ।
tannirmālyādikaṃ tasmai sapavitraṃ samarpayet ।।

athavā sthaṇḍile caṇḍaṃ pīṭhe vā pūrvavadyajet ।
yatkiñcidvārṣikaṃ karma kṛtaṃ nyūnādhikaṃ mayā ।।

tadastu paripūrṇaṃ me caṇḍanātha tavājñayā ।
iti vijñāpya caṇḍeśaṃ datvā stutvā visarjya ca ।।

andhakūpetha vāto ye tannirmālyādikaṃ kṣipet ।
(143b)   itthaṃ saṃpūjya caṇḍeśaṃ bhūmiṃ gomaya vāriṇā ।।

viśodhya śuddhadehastu snāpayitvā visarjayet ।
pañcayojanasaṃsthopi pavitraṃ gurusannidhau ।।

kurvīta vidhinānena labhate vāñchitaṃ phalam ।
pavitrakavidhiḥ । atha kṛṣṇa caturdaśī vidhiḥ ।।

kailāsapīṭha madhyasthaṃ bhagavantaṃ maheśvaram ।
praṇamya kārtikeyaḥ prākpapraccha vinayānvitaḥ ।।

।। kārtikeyaḥ ।।

bhagavanparameśāna tvayā snāyāḥ purā vibho ।
ukto vratavidhiḥ puṇyaśśivārādhanasaṃjñayā ।।

tathā kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ tatra pūjyo sivaṅkara ।
samākhyā hi tamīśāna bhaktānāmīhitecchayā ।।

।। śrībhairavaḥ ।।

sādhu vatsa samāsena bhaktānāṃ hitakāraka ।
śṛṇu vratavidhānaṃ tvaṃ nāmamantrasamanvitam ।।

prasādaḥ paramo mantro vyomavyāpyavatārakaḥ ।
bahurūpī mṛtyujidvā bhaktyā vātha ṣaḍakṣaraḥ ।।

dīkṣākālethavau diṣṭo guruṇā yastu mantrarāṭ ।
yatrāhnikaprakaraṇaṃ japadhyānavidhikriyā ।।

eṣāmuccāraṇādeva sannidhiṃ kurute śivaḥ ।
mahāpātakinā me te proktāḥ pāpaharā api ।।

śuddhābhaktiṃ samālambya pūjya ebhirvibhuśśivaḥ ।
caturdaśīṣu kṛṣṇāsu manorathaphalepsubhiḥ ।।

phālguṇādiṣu sāmeṣu nāmabhiryaiśśṛṇuṣva tat ।
(144a)   śivaḥ phalguṇamāsyuktaścaitre rudro vṛṣākapiḥ ।।

vaiśākhe ca haro jyeṣṭha āṣāḍhe tryambakaḥ smṛtaḥ ।
śrīkaṇṭhaḥ śrāvaṇe vatsa bhavo bhādrapade vibhuḥ ।।

īśānaścaivāśvayuje śarvaḥ kārtikasaṃjñake ।
mahādevo mārgaśīrṣe pauśe paśupatiḥ prabhuḥ ।।

māghe maheśvara iti nāmabhiḥ pūjya īśvaraḥ ।
mumukṣubhirmahādevo mārgaśīrṣādito haraḥ ।।

ārādhyo nāmabhiryaistu śṛṇu tatputra bhaktitaḥ ।
haṃsaśśucirjyotirūpaḥ kapardī brahma conmanaḥ ।।

mahāhaṃso visargaśca mahāpuruṣa eva ca ।
prāṇaścaiveśvaraścaiva śivonte dvādaśa smṛtaḥ ।।

tatra phālguṇamāsasya śuddhātmā niyatendriyaḥ ।
saṃbhūya kṛṣṇadvādaśyāmarcayet parameśvaraṃ ।।

liṅge snānaistu vividhaistoyaistīrthāvṛtairyajet ।
sugandhibhirdadhikṣīraiḥ ghṛtena madhunā tathā ।।

pañcagavyena puṣpaiśca dhūpadīpaissugandhibhiḥ ।
bhakṣyairbhojyairyathāśakti phalamūlaiśca pānakaiḥ ।।

japyaiḥ stotrirnamaskārairgītanṛttādibhirvibhum ।
arcayitvā praṇamyeyaṃ bhaktyā vijñāpayecchivam ।।

prātastvāṃ pūjayāmīśa tatra me sannidhiṃ kuru ।
bhaktasya deva vātsalyāttathā dīnānukampayā ।।

evamastviti tenoktaṃ śraddhayā bhāvayeddhiyā ।
bhuktvā tataḥ smarandevaṃ bhakṣayeddantadhāvanam ।।
(144b)
svapyātsmaraṃstaṃ bruyāścakṣutapraskhalanādiṣu ।
prātarutthāya tenaiva vidhinā pūjayecchivam ।।

vijñāpayedidaṃ liṅgaṃ tvaṃ tu sannihitastadā ।
upoṣyādya maheśāna tatastvāṃ pūjayāmyaham ।।

tatra vighnā(ñca)maya me śraddhābhaktī prayaccha me ।
liṅgesminbhagavañchambho tathā sannihito bhava ।।

gṛhāṇārcāmimāṃ deva kṛpayā bhaktivatsala ।
evaṃ bhavatviti proktaṃ bhāvayedvibhunā dhiyā ।।

tataḥ prātassamutthāya viśeṣeṇa samarcayet ।
liṅgasthamagnisthamatho guruvidyāsthitaṃ yajet ।।

dānaṃ dadyādyathāśakti gobrāhmaṇatapaśvinām ।
santarpya tānvadatteṣāṃ bhagavanprīyatāmiti ।।

devaśśivaste prītostu tairukte śraddhayānvitāḥ ।
bhaktyā vijñāpayeddevaṃ nātheyaṃ me caturdaśī ।।

pūjitā tvatprasādena dehi mebhimataṃ phalam ।
japyo japo japayitā pūjyaḥ pūjātha pūjakaḥ ।।

dhyānaṃ dhyeyaṃ tathā dhyātā yatastvaṃ parameśvaraḥ ।
visarjya bhaktyā deveśaṃ bhuñjīta kṣīrabhāvitam ।।

munyannaṃ brahmacārī syāttryahametatsamāhitaḥ ।
liṅgābhāvet sthale pūjya pātre vā jalapūritam ।।

snānāni dadyādādarce bhaktyoktaṃ sarvamācaret ।
evaṃ māsadvādaśakaṃ māghānte pārayedvratam ।।

(145a)
pāraṇe parameśānaṃ punarvijñāpayedidam ।
pāritaṃ tvatprasādena śivārādhanamīśvara ।।

jahi me sāmayāṃllopānpūrṇānkāmāṃśca me kuru ।
vijñāpyaivaṃ yathāśakti dadyādvipreṣu dakṣiṇām ।।

evaṃ kṛtebhilaṣitaṃ yadasya manasā bhavet ।
tatprāpnuyādavitathaṃ gacchecca brahma śāśvatam ।।

varṇe ṇaikena putrai tatphalaṃ proktaṃ vratasya tu ।
yāvajjīvaṃ kṛtavatāṃ na phalaṃ vaktumutsahe ।।

maṇḍalaṃ vā pūjayatāṃ te madaṃ śānasaṃśayaḥ ।
sarvatīrthatapoyajñairvratadhyānaiśca yatphalaṃ ।।

tadaśaṅkhyeyaguṇitaṃ vijñeyaṃ vratapāraṇam ।
nārī śraddhāsamāyuktā kṛtvaitadvratapāraṇaṃ ।।

strītvaṃ vihāya devasya gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt ।
manaso yadabhīṣṭaṃ syāttadayatnena cāpnuyāt ।।

mumukṣuḥ mārgaśīrṣādau kārtike vratapāraṇaṃ ।
phalaṃ tvanabhisandhāya kṛtvā brahmaiti śāśvatam ।।

eṣā eva vidhiḥ kṛtsnaḥ śuklāsu dvādaśīṣvapi ।
tatrāpyetatphalaṃ proktaṃ nāmānyetānyapi prabhoḥ ।।

paurṇamāsīṣu cāpyevaṃ caturthīnavamīṣu ca ।
upavāsavidhiḥ kāryatata vā naktabhojanam ।।

yādṛśe tādṛśe vāpi vatso poṣyadine naraḥ ।
bhaktyārcayenmahādevaṃ liṅge yātyavyayaṃ padam ।।
(145b)
dine dine yo deveśamarcayitvā yathāvidhi ।
bhuṅkte pibati nūnaṃ samadaṃśo bhuvi tiṣṭhati ।।

tasya darśanasaṃbhāṣāt smaraṇāt sparśanādapi ।
mahāpātakināṃ puṃsāṃ pāpaṃ yātyapi bhasmasāt ।।

tasmāt putra sadā bhaktyā śraddhayā vidhipūrvakaṃ ।
arcanīyaḥ śivo devassarvasiddhiphalepsubhiḥ ।।

ṛtiniśśvāsa śāstrāntaścaturdaśyāṃ śivodite ।
vrate vidhissamuddiṣṭaśśivarātryāmathocyate ।।

।। atha śivarātri vidhiḥ ।।

।। śrīdevyuvāca ।।

bhagavandevadeveśa nirāmaya maheśvara ।
śrutaṃ mayā tvatprasādācchivadharmā anekaśaḥ ।।

adyāpi saṃśayo mesti taṃ cchindi parameśvara ।
aryaśca yāminī deva nityaṃ sannihite tvayi ।।

śivarātrīti yā nāmnā kathaṃ sā tithiruttamā ।
kasminkāle samutpannā kṛtvā tāṃ kiṃ bhavetphalam ।।

anuṣṭhānaṃ ca kiṃ tasyāṃ vratacaryā ca kīdṛśī ।
yāgaṃ homaṃ vrataṃ japyaṃ mūrtiyāgamathārcanam ।।

dānaṃ ca kīdṛśaṃ tasyāḥ kasminkāle pradīyate ।
pāne goṣṭhīkṛtaṃ kāryaṃ sabāhyābhyantaraṃ tathā ।।

cakrayāgaṃ ca vai nātha kaissārdhaṃ tatra kārayet ।
etat praśnavaraṃ guhyaṃ vadasva parameśvara ।।

। īśvaraḥ ।

śṛṇu devi paraṃ guhyaṃ mantrāṇāṃ mokṣmadaṃ ca yat ।
{śrīmadujjayinīpīṭhe kṛtaṃ cakraṃ parāparam ।।

prārthitassa ca pīṭhastha yoginībhirahaṃ purā ।
tvayāhaṃ tu yathādṛṣṭaḥ kimarthaṃ prāthito vibhuḥ ।।

tadā mayā tava proktaṃ yāgārthaṃ tu nimantritaḥ ।
tvayā punarapi proktamāgacchāmi tvayā samam ।।

tava rūpamahaṃ dṛṣṭvā vismayaṃ tu paraṃ gataḥ ।
yadi neha ca tatraiva vistāraṃ dustaro bhavet ।।

ato hetoḥ śarīrasthāṃ kṛtvā tāṃ gopayantataḥ ।}
śrīmadujjayinīpīṭhe cakrayāgaṃ tu ḍāmaram ।।
(146a)
mahāsaṃbhīṣaṇaṃ bhadre yatra līnaṃ jagattryam ।
rajobhirvividhākāraiḥ pānaiścaivottamottamaiḥ ।।

upahairarbahuvidhaistaccakraṃ paripūritam ।
yāgānuyāgaṃ tatraiva kṛtvā saṃpūrṇatāṃ gatāḥ ।।

tataścānanditāssarvā nṛttalagnāśca devatāḥ ।
madhye śāsamahaṃ devi nṛttamāno vyavasthita ।। ?

mayi nṛtyati devīti tatra cchidraṃ prakalpitam ।
mātṛbhiḥ tvapahṛtya tvāṃ devī cakre niveditā ।।
bhakṣitā yoginībhiśca tato hṛṣṭāśca devatāḥ ।
nṛtyantiśca mayā sārdhaṃ yāvadeva dinadvayam ।।

tatassamastā viśrāntā hṛdi tvaṃ cintitā mayā ।
na paśyāmi ca devi tvāṃ vismayaṃ paramāgataḥ ।।

dhyānena cintitā yāvattāvattābhiśca bhakṣita ।
tataḥ kruddhena ca mayā cakreśaṃ gṛhya bhūtalāt ।।

tadbhrāmyotkṣipya gaganaṃ prakṣiptamatha bhūtale ।
bhagraṃ samastaṃ cakraṃ ca khaṇḍaśaśca kṛtaṃ mayā ।।

yoginyo hatavidhvastā gatāścaiva diśo daśa ।
kasyāśca na śirobhagnaṃ kasyā bāhūrujaṅghakam ।।

evaṃ cakre krudhā bhagne pīṭhānāṃ nāśanodyate ।
mayi tā devatāḥ sarvā cakībhūtā samāgrataḥ ।।

prahvāḥ prāñjalayo bhūtvā stutiṃ cakrūrmahādbhutāḥ ।
(146b)
gītavādyādinṛttaiśca pānairbahuvidhaistathā ।
valyopahārairvividhaistoṣitohaṃ varānane ।।

tuṣṭe mayi ca yoginyaḥ papracchanmāmidaṃ vacaḥ ।
kimarthaṃ nātha ruṣṭosi ājñāpaya mahāprabho ।।

uktaṃ tatra vai tāsāṃ devī kutra gatā mama ।
kasminsthāne gatā sā ca devatāḥ punarabruvan ।।

tava deva śarīrāntarāsitā parameśvarī ।
gopitā tu kimarthaṃ sā jaganmātā sureśvarī ।।

sukumārā ca subhagā tadarthaṃ gopitā mayā ।
evamuktāstu yoginyaḥ punarvaktuṃ samudyatāḥ ।।

īdṛktvayā kṛtaṃ kasmādasmābhiḥ punarīdṛśam ।
kṛtaṃ śambho tadarthaṃ ca kopaṃ tyaja maheśvara ।।

prasīda deva deveśa sarvānugrahado hyasi ।
mayā tāsāṃ tu vai proktaṃ sarvāsāṃ vacanaṃ tvidam ।।

devīṃ samarpayata me yena tuṣyāmyahaṃ punaḥ ।
tatassamagradevībhissandhitā parameśvarī ।।

umeti sandhitāṃ kṛtvā dadustāśca samāgrataḥ ।
samarpitā yadā sā tu tāsāṃ tuṣṭo hyahaṃ tataḥ ।।

cakreṇāpi yathādiṣṭaṃ tathā saṃpāditaṃ mayā ।
tatastithiraṣau devi martyalokevabhāritā ।।

mayā vidhānapūrveṇa vidhinā parikalpitā ।
yoginībhivarodattasteneyaṃ śivayāminī ।।

śarīrasyāsamarthatvāddeśabhaṅgātkadācana ।
bhuktānāṃ ca kriyāṇāṃ tu guruṇādiṣṭameva yat ।।
(147a)
tasyārcājapahomāderlopo yasmācca pārvati ।
dveṣāttapasvināṃ vāpi samayasya ca laṅghanāt ।।

jāyate sumahāndoṣastasmātkuryādidaṃ vratam ।
yā phālguṇasya māsasya kṛṣṇapakṣe trayodaśī ।।

śivasajñā niśā devi kṛtā sā tu mayā purā ।
snātaḥ śuklāmbaradhara ekacittassamāhitaḥ ।।

sarvadvandvavinirmuktaḥ śivadhyānaikatatparaḥ ।
upoṣya kṛtvā naktaṃ vā kuryādyāśādikaṃ kramāt ।।

ādau maṇḍalakaṃ tatra vidhāya parikalpitaṃ ।
rekhākarmamayaṃ kāryaṃ kramātsvadarśanārcanam ।।

kalaśānsuvicitrāṃśca sarvauṣadhiphalairyutān ।
hiraṇyena samāyuktānkarpaṭairapi veṣṭitān ।।

candanenānuliptāṃstu puṣpamālāvibhūṣitān ।
vitānavastraśobhāḍhyā ghaṇṭācāmarabhūṣitān ।।

kāṇḍapratisarairyuktānpūrṇapātrasamanvitān ।
kuryādvicitraśobhāṃśca puṣpairnānāvidhaiśśubhān ।।

vidhāyaivaṃvidhaṃ yāgaṃ pūjāṃ tatra samārabhet ।
sandhyāyāmarcanaṃ kāryaṃ yathāvibhavavistaraiḥ ।।

arghadhūpādiratnaiśca mṛṣṭadhūpaiśca dhūpayet ।
dīpamālāḥ prabhūtāśca kṛtvā tatra nivedayet ।।

naivedyāni vicitrāṇi bhakṣabhojyānyanekaśaḥ ।
(147b)   naivedya bhaktipūrveṇa upahārānanekaśaḥ ।।

mahāpaśūṃśca mahiṣānajānmeṣāṃśca śobhanān ।
mīnānuṣṭhānkharāṃścaiva naivedyāṃśca yathādhanam ।।

atha piṣṭamayānvāpi paśūnyāge nivedayet ।
mahāpaśūnkrodhalobhakāmānevaṃ nivedayet ।।

evaṃ yāgaṃ tu saṃtarpya japaṃ paścāt samārabhet ।
kuryāddhomaṃ japānte tu tato mūrtau yajedvibhuṃ ।।

santarpya digbaliṃ datvā kṣetreśānviranāyakān ।
gurvādiyoginassarvāndevatāstarpayettataḥ ।।

daiśikāssādhakā ye ca taissārdhaṃ tu niśāṃ kṣipet ।
mūrtiyāge kṛte paścājjāgaraṃ tu samācaret ।।

gītavādyanamaskārairjapahomahuḍukkaraiḥ ।
avicitraṃ deharūpaṃ ghaṇṭāṃ cālya punaḥ punaḥ ।।

vādyāni vividhānyeva nṛttānyapi tathaiva hi ।
geyāni caiva ramyāṇi suragrāmayutāni ca ।।

kāryāṇi caiva rūpasya parivartānyanekaśaḥ ।
cakrācārāṇi sarvāṇi tatvāni paribhāvya ca ।।

ekatāṃ taissamaṃ kṛtvā sāmarasyaṃ tataḥ paraṃ ।
śeśakālakalātītaṃ sarvavyāpinamakṣayam ।।

nityānandamayaṃ śāntaṃ cintayecca paraṃ śivam ।
parāmṛtasamudbhūtā kalā saptadaśī tvayā ।।

mahābhairavarūpaṃ tu galitaṃ tatparāmṛtaṃ ।
tena madyena taissārdhaṃ cakragoṣṭhīṃ tu kārayet ।।

paramānandarūpeṇa svānandānanditātmanaḥ ।
(148a)   bhojayitvā tu tānsarvānevaṃ kuryānmahotsavam ।।

trayodaśyāṃ yathā kuryāttathā kuryāddinatrayam ।
arcanaṃ tu japaṃ homaṃ dānaṃ vai bhojanādikam ।।

athavā pañcasaptāhaṃ yathāśaktyā mahotsavam ।
śivaikabhāvitaḥ kuryādathavaikāṃ tu yāminīṃ ।।

ekāṃ trayodaśīṃ puṇyāṃ śivasāyujyadāyikām ।
kurvīta bhaktibhāvena vittaśāṭhyavivarjitaḥ ।।

ye kecicchivabhaktāstu kurvantīmāṃ tithiṃ kramāt ।
te yānti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatrāhamumayā saha ।।

matsamasta pumānnāsti tvatsamā nāsti cāṅganā ।
śivarātrisamo yāgo na bhūto na bhaviṣyati ।।

pratisaṃvatsaraṃ yāgaṃ dinarātriṃ karoti yaḥ ।
bhaktibhāvasamopetaśśivasāyujyatāṃ vrajet ।।

śivabhaktisamāyuktaśśivadharmaparāyaṇaḥ ।
kulaikaviṃśamuddhṛtya rudralokaṃ sa gacchati ।।

sarvayajñeṣu yatpuṇyaṃ sarvadāneṣu yatphalam ।
tatphalaṃ labhate martyo yaḥ kuryācchivayāminīṃ ।।

janmamadhye kadācittu krīḍārthaṃ kurute tu yaḥ ।
vasetsarudralo * tu yāvadācandratārakam ।।

śivarātrividhissoyaṃ tvatprītyā kathito mayā ।
śṛṇoti ya imāṃ bhaktyā sa gacchecchivamandiram ।।

yāvattiṣṭhati martyo sāvaṇisādyāśca ṛddhayaḥ ।
bhavanti tasya viduṣo hyantarāyavivarjitāḥ ।।
(148b)
snānena sarvatīrtheṣu yatphalaṃ tadavāpnuyāt ।
caturvargaṃ samāpnoti puṇyabhāgijanaśca saḥ ।।

eṣa devyāssamākhyātaśśivarātrivinirṇayaḥ ।
dūtiḍāmaraśāstresyānmāhātmyaṃ ca śivena tu ।।

anyatra bahudhā proktaṃ phalaṃ kāmānurūpataḥ ।

।। śivarātrividhiḥ ।।

samāpteyaṃm nityādisaṅgrahābhidhānapaddhatiḥ kṛtistatrabhavataḥ mahāmāheśvarācāryaśrīmattakṣakavartasyeti śivam ।
oṃ namaśśivādibhyo gurubhyaḥ śivamastu sarveṣām ।। ।।
